Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

|| śrīgurubhyo namaḥ ||
|| śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
|| śrīsarasvatyai namaḥ ||
|| śrī reṇukāyai namaḥ ||
|| oṃ namo bhagavate kṛṣṇacandrāya ||
|| atha maharṣiśrīvedavyāsapraṇītaṃ śrīgaṇeśapurāṇam ||

<adhyāya 1 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
oṃ namastasmaigaṇeśāya brahmavidyāpradāyine |
yasyāgastyāyatenāma vighnasāgaraśoṣaṇe ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
sūtasūtamahāprājña vedaśāstraviśārada |
sarvavidyānidhetvattovaktānyaunaupalabhyate ||1||
janmajanmātarīyaṃnaḥ sthitaṃpuṇyaṃ mahattaram |
tena saṃdarśanaṃ jātaṃ sarvajñasyasatastava ||2||
vayaṃ dhanyatamāloke jīvitaṃ naḥ sujīvitam |
pitaro vedaśāstrāṇi tapāṃsyāśrama eva ca ||3||
aṣṭādaśa purāṇāni vistarācchrāvitāninaḥ |
anyānyapihinaḥ śrotumicchāstidvijasattama ||4||
śaunakeye mahāsatre saktā dvādaśavārṣike |
tvatkathāmṛtapānānto nānyadviśrāmakāraṇam ||5||
sūta uvāca ||
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃṃ mahābhāgā bhavadbhiḥ puṇyakarmabhiḥ |
sādhūnāṃ samacittānāṃ matirlokopakāriṇī ||6||
mamāpi paritoṣosti kathānāṃ kathane dvijāḥ |
atohaṃ sādhuvṛttebhyaḥ kathayiṣye viśeṣataḥ ||7||
anyānyupapurāṇāni vartante'ṣṭādaśaiva ca |
gāṇeśaṃ nāradīyaṃ ca nṛsiṃhādīnyathāpi ca ||8||
gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ yattatrādau kathayāmyaham |
durlabhaṃ śravaṇaṃ yasya matryaloke viśeṣataḥ ||9||
yasya smaraṇamātreṇa kṛtakṛtyo bhavennaraḥ |
prabhāvamasya gadituṃ neśaḥśeṣaścaturmukhaḥ ||10||
saṃṃkṣepatonnabravīmyetattathāpi bhavadājñayā |
bahujanmārjitaiḥ puṇyairasyatu śravaṇaṃ bhavet ||11||
pākhaṇḍināṃ nāstikānāṃ nabhavetpāpakarmiṇām |
nityatvānnirguṇatvācca anāditvācca tattvataḥ ||12||
gaṇeśasya svarūpaṃ tu vaktuṃ kenāpineṣyate |
tathāpyupāsanāsaktaiḥ saguṇaṃ tannirūpyate ||13||
oṃkārarūpī bhagavān yo vedādau pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
yaṃ sadā munayodevāḥ smarantīndrādayohṛdi ||14||
yaṃ pūjayanti satataṃ brahmeśānendraviṣṇavaḥ |
yo hetuḥ sarvajagatāṃ sarvakāraṇakāraṇam ||15||
yadājñayā kaḥ sṛjatetha viśvaṃ yadājñayā pālayate ca viṣṇuḥ |
yadājñayā saṃharate haropi yadājñayā saṃbhramate dineśaḥ ||16||
yadājñayā vātisamīraṇopi yadājñayāpaḥ pravahanti dikṣu |
yadājñayā bhāni patanti bhūmau yadājñayāgnijrvalati triloke ||17||
tasya yaccaritaṃ guptaṃ kasyāpi na niveditam |
tadahaṃ vaḥ pravakṣyāmi sādaraṃ śrūyatāṃ dvijāḥ ||18||
brahmaṇā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ vyāsāyāmitatejase |
bhṛgave kathitaṃ tena somakāntāya tena ca ||19||
vratairyajñaistapobhiśca dānaistīrthaiśca koṭayaḥ |
bhavanti yeṣāṃ puṇyānāṃ teṣāṃ buddhiḥ prajāyate ||20||
gaṇeśākhya purāṇasya śravaṇe dvijasattamāḥ |
māyā yeṣāṃ na saṃsāre na dārāpatyabhūmiṣu ||21||
mayūreśa kathāyāṃ te sādarā munisattamāḥ |
śrūyatāmasya mahimā somakānta prasaṅgataḥ ||22||
saurāṣṭre devanagare somakānto bhavannṛpaḥ |
vedaśāstrārthatattvajño dharmaśāstrārthatatparaḥ ||23||
daśanāgasahasrāṇi hayānāṃ dviguṇāni ca |
rathānāṃ ṣaṭsahasrāṇi yaṃyīta manuyānti ca ||24||
padātayopyasaṃkhyātā agniśastradharāstathā |
kodaṇḍadhāriṇaścānye niṣaṅgadvayadhāriṇaḥ ||25||
buddhyābṛhaspatiṃ jigye saṃpadā dhanadañcayaḥ |
kṣamayā pṛthivīṃ jigye gāmbhīryeṇa mahodadhim ||26||
sūryācandramasau jigye bhāsākāntyācayonṛpaḥ |
pratāpenānalaṃ jigye saundaryeṇa manobhavam ||27||
yasyāmātyāḥ prabalinaḥ pañcāsandṛḍhavikramāḥ |
nītiśāstrārthatattvajñāḥ pararāṣṭravimardinaḥ ||28||
rūpavānprathamastatra vidyādhīśastathāparaḥ |
kṣemaṃkaro jñānagamyaḥ subalaḥ pañcamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||29||
etairnānāvidhādeśā ākrāntāḥ svaparākramāt |
eteti sundarā nānā bhūṣāvastrairalaṃkṛtāḥ ||30||
rājakārya karānityaṃ rājñaḥ priyatamābhṛśa |
tasya rājño bhavadbhāryā sudharmā guṇaśālinī ||31||
tadrūpamavalokyaiva ratirambhātilottamāḥ |
lajjitā na sukhaṃ kvāpi lebhire nacamenire ||32||
anekaratnaracitetāṭaṅke kāñcane śubhe |
bibhratīkarṇayoḥ kaṇṭhe niṣkaṃmuktāphalāni ca ||33||
kaṭauratnamayīṃkāñcīṃ tādṛśe nūpureṅghrige |
aṅgulīyānyuttamāni karapādāṅgulīṣu ca ||34||
vāsāṃsyanekavarṇāni mahārhāṇi sahasraśaḥ |
bhagavadbhajanedānesaktācātithipūjane ||35||
sevaneharniśaṃ bhatrturvacane caratā sadā |
hemakaṇṭha itikhyātaḥ putro bhūvanayoḥ śubhaḥ ||36||
nāgāyutabalodhīmān vikramī śatrutāpanaḥ |
evamāsītsomakāntaḥ pṛthivyāṃ rājasattamaḥ ||37||
sarvān rājño vaśekṛtvā cakre rājyaṃ dharātale |
nityaṃ dharmarato yajvādātātyāgīdvijottamāḥ ||38||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe somakāntavarṇanaṃ nāma prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||1||

<adhyāya 2 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
sūta uvāca ||
śrūyatāmṛṣayaḥ sarve somakāntasya duṣkṛtam |
akasmādabhavettasya galatkuṣṭhotiduḥkhadaḥ ||1||
dharmaśīlasyarājñothapūrvakarmavipākataḥ |
śubhaṃvāpyaśubhaṃ karma namuñcati naraṃ kvacit ||2||
yasyāṃyasyāmavasthāyāṃ kṛtaṃ bhavati karma yat |
tasyāṃtasyāmavasthāyāṃ bhujyate prāṇibhirdhruvam ||3||
duḥkhasāgaramagnobhūdaplavojaladhau yathā |
vedanāmalabhadbahvīṃ bhujaṅgeneva daṃśitaḥ ||4||
nānākṣatasamākīrṇaḥ sravadraktastatastataḥ |
pūyaśoṇitavān rājā kṛmibhirvihvalīkṛtaḥ ||
asthiśeṣobhavadrājā yakṣmaṇe vasamāvṛtaḥ ||5||
cintayā vyākulībhūtaḥ sarvendriyarujānvitaḥ |
tatomātyānjagaurājā manaḥ saṃruddhyayatnataḥ ||6||
rājovāca ||
dhiṅme rājyaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca dhigbalaṃ jīvitaṃ dhanam |
kenedaṃ karmabījena vyasanaṃ samupasthitam ||7||
kāntyāsomojito yena somakāntastatobhavam |
yena me sādhavo dīnāḥ saṃta sanyāsinopi ca ||8||
pālitāḥ putravajjānapadālokāstathāpare |
yena me nirjitā bāṇaiḥ śatravoghorarūpiṇaḥ ||9||
yena me sakalāpṛthvī kṛtāsvavaśavartinī |
samyagārādhitodevaḥ paramātmā sadāśivaḥ ||10||
duṣṭasaṅgatihīnena cittanigrahakāriṇā |
yena me vapuṣāpūrvamiṣṭagandhāniṣevitāḥ ||11||
tadidānīṃ pūtigandhamatome jīvitaṃ vṛthā |
atohaṃ kānanaṃ yāsye sarveṣāmabhyanujñayā ||12||
hemakaṇṭha sutaṃ sarve buddhivikrama saṃyutam |
siñcanturājyahetorme pālayantu parākramaiḥ ||13||
idānīṃ na pradarśiṣye mukhaṃ loke kathañcana |
na me rājyairnadārairvā jīvitena śriyāpivā ||14||
prayojanaṃ mahāmātyāḥ kariṣye svahitaṃ vane |
sūta uvāca ||
ityuktvā nipapātorvyāṃ tarurvātahatoyathā ||15||
pūyaśoṇitadharmaughairvyāptosau dvijasattamāḥ |
kolāhalo mahānāsīdamātyānāṃ ca yoṣitām ||16||
hāhākāraśca lokānāṃ kṣaṇamāsītsudāruṇaḥ |
vastraproṃchanavātādyairauṣadhaiḥ kṣiprakāribhiḥ ||17||
mantri mantra prayogaistaṃ sacetanamakārayan |
svasthe jāte nṛpe tasminnamātyāḥ prābruvannidam ||18||
amātyāūcuḥ ||
tava prasādādamarendratulyaṃ bhuktaṃ sukhaṃ sarvajaneṣvasiddham |
kathaṃ tvidānīṃ tvadṛtevasāmaḥ kathaṃ jīvema paśughnatulyāḥ ||19||
katotu rājyaṃ tava sūnurekaḥ prabhūta kośo balabān ripughnaḥ |
vihāya sarvaṃ sukhamadyarājansahaivayāmastuvanāyagantum ||20||
sūta uvāca ||
tataḥ sudharmā vacanaṃ jagāda vane nṛpaṃ sevitumekavīrā |
vrajesahānena pradhānavaryā praśiṣṭarājyaṃ sahamesutena ||21||
duḥkhasya bhoktānaparosti naiva sukhasya vā pūrvakṛtasya jantoḥ |
yathā yathā karmaphalaṃ prasaktaṃ tadeva bhogyaṃ svayameva tādṛk ||22||
mayāpi nānāvidha bhogavatyā sukhena rājyaṃ paribhuktamasya |
strīṇāṃ hi bhattrrā gamanaṃ sahaiva paratraloke munibhiḥ pradiṣṭam ||23||
tataḥ putro hemakaṇṭho vinītaḥ śokasaṅkulaḥ |
uvāca tasminsamaye somakāndamidaṃvacaḥ ||24||
hemakaṇṭha uvāca ||
nanurājyena dāraiśca prāṇairdhanacayairapi |
tvāṃ vinā nṛpaśārdūla kṛtyaṃ naivāsti yatkvacit ||25||
vināsnehaṃ yathā dīpo vināprāṇaṃ yathā tanuḥ |
vṛthārājyaṃ tathā rājaṃ stvāṃ vinā dharmapālaka ||26||
sūta uvāca ||
amātyānāṃ sudharmāyāḥ putrasya vacanāmṛtam |
śrutvā hṛṣṭamanā rājā putraṃ provāca dharmataḥ ||27||
rājovāca ||
piturvākyaratonityaṃ śraddhayā śrāddhakṛttathā |
piṇḍadoyo gayāyāntu sa putraḥ putra ucyate ||28||
dharmaśāstrārthatattvajño nītijñokhiladoṣakṛt |
pitṛṛnuddharateyastu putravānputra ucyate ||29||
atonītyāsamāyuktaḥ kururājyaṃ mamājñayā |
amātyayuktaḥ śādhitvaṃ putravaddhākhilāḥ prajāḥ ||30||
ahaṃ vanaṃ gamiṣyāmi galatkuṣṭho'tigarhitaḥ |
patnyāsudharmayāsārdhamanujānīhi suvrata ||31||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||2||

<adhyāya 3 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
sūta uvāca ||
tata utthāya rājā sa gṛhitvā dakṣiṇekare |
putraṃ viveśa harmyāgraṃ yatra mantrayate sadā ||1||
yatra siṃhāsanaṃ divyaṃ kāñcanaṃ bahuratnayuk |
muktāpravālaracitaṃ bhātiśakrapadaṃ yathā ||2||
tatrāsatuḥ pitāputrau bhātodvāvapyanekadhā |
pratiratnaṃ gatau yasmātsumadāyavṛtāviva ||3||
ācāraṃ prathamaṃ prāha nītiṃ nānāvidhāṃ nṛpaḥ |
putrāya karuṇāyukto yaśase svakulasya ca ||4||
somakānta uvāca ||
yāmamātrāvasiṣṭāyāṃ niśāyāṃ jāgṛtaḥ pumān |
tyaktvā śayyāṃ śucisthāne upaviśya guruṃ smaret ||5||
devatāṃ cintayitveṣṭāṃ praṇametstutipūrvakam |
prārthya pṛthvīṃ kṣamasveti pādasparśaṃ jaganmaye ||6||
prātarnamāmi gaṇanāthamaśeṣahetuṃ brahmādidevavaradaṃ sakalāgamāḍhyam |
dharmārthakāmaphaladaṃ janamokṣahetuṃ vācāmagocaramanādimanantarūpam ||7||
prātarnamāmi kamalāpatimugravīryaṃ nānāvatāranirataṃ nijarakṣaṇāya |
kṣīrābdhivāsamamarādhipabandhumīśaṃ pāpāpahaṃ ripuharaṃ bhava muktihetum ||8||
prātarnamāmi girijāpatimindumauliṃ vyāghrājināṃvṛtamudastayaṃmanoje |
nārāyaṇendravaradaṃ surasiddhajuṣṭaṃ sarpāṃstriśūlaḍamarūdadhataṃ purārim ||9||
prātarnamāmi dinanāthamaghopahāraṃ gāḍhāndhakāraharamuttamalokavandyam |
vedatrayātmakamudastasurārimāyaṃ jñānaikahetumuruśaktimudārabhāvam ||10||
prātarnamāmi girijāṃ bhavabhūtihetuṃ saṃsārasindhu parapārakarīṃ trinetrām |
tattvādikāraṇamudastasurārimāyāṃ māyāmayīṃ suramunīndranutāṃ sureśīm ||11||
evamanyāṃśca saṃsmṛtya devānmunigaṇāṃstathā |
mānasairupacāraiśca pūjayitvā kṣamāpayet ||12||
tato gacchetsodapātro grāmasya nairṛtīṃ diśam |
mṛdaṃ gṛhitvā suśvetāṃ brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyoruṇām ||13||
vaiśyaśūdrau tathā śyāmāṃ nanadītīrataḥ khanet |
norvarāyā na valmīkād brāhmaṇāgārataḥ kvacit ||14||
tṛṇādinācchādayitvā dharāṃ mūtrapurīṣayoḥ |
kṛtvotsargaṃ divārātrāvudagdakṣiṇadiṅmukhaḥ ||15||
ādau gudaṃ pramṛjyaiva tṛṇakāṣṭhādinā naraḥ |
pañcavāraṃ mṛdādbhiśca kṣālayettadanantaram ||16||
daśavāraṃ vāmahastaṃ saptavāramubhaukarau |
liṅgatathaikavāraṃ tu vāmahastaṃ trivārataḥ ||17||
mūtrotsarge kṣālayīta dvau karau ca dvivārataḥ |
pādausadaikavāraṃ tu gṛhasthasyaivamīritam ||18||
vratavān dviguṇaṃ kuryāstriguṇaṃ vanagocaraḥ |
yatiścaturguṇaṃ kuryādgātrāvarddhantumaunavān ||19||
arddhaṃpādaṃ divārātrau śaucaṃ strīśudra eva ca |
tata ācamya kāṣṭhaṃ tu kṣīrakaṇṭakavṛkṣayoḥ ||20||
gṛhītvā prārthanāpūrvaṃ dantāñjihvānviśodhayet |
balamojoyaśastejaḥ paśubuddhidhanāni ca ||21||
medhāṃ brahmadhiyaṃ caiva tvaṃ me dehi vanaspate |
tataḥ śītajalaiḥ snānaṃ kṛtvā malaharaṃ purā ||22||
tato mantraiśca gṛhyoktaiḥ sandhyopāstiṃ tataścaret |
japaṃ homaṃ ca svādhyāyaṃ tarpaṇaṃ devapūjanam ||23||
vaiśvadevaṃ tathātithyaṃ bhojanaṃ dvijasākṣikam |
purāṇaśravaṇaṃ dānaṃ paranindāvivarjanam ||24||
paropakāraṃ kuryācca dravyaprāṇavacomṛtaiḥ |
parāpakāraṃ nokuryādātmastavanameva ca ||25||
gurudrohaṃ vedanindāṃ nāstikyaṃ pāpasevanam |
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇaṃ caiva paradāraniṣevaṇam ||26||
svadāravarjanaṃ caiva kuryādṛtugatiṃ caret |
mātāpitṛguruṇāṃ ca gavāṃśuśrūṣaṇaṃ sadā ||27||
dīnāndhakṛpaṇebhyaśca dadyādannaṃ savastrakam |
prāṇātyayepi satyasya tyāgaṃ naiva samācaret ||28||
īśvarānugrahoyeṣāṃ sādhūnāṃ ca prapālanam |
aparādhānusāreṇa dharmaśāstraṃ vilokya ca ||29||
pṛṣṭvā vā paṇḍitāndaṇḍaṃ kuryānnītiviśāradaḥ |
viśvāso yasya naivasyāttatra no viśvasetkvacit ||30||
viśvastetyanta viśvāso nakartavyobubhūṣatā |
kṛtavairetha viśvaste kadāpi na ca viśvaset ||31||
ṣaḍguṇānāṃ prayogeṇa vardhayedrāṣṭamātmanaḥ |
dānaṃ svaśaktyā kurvīta kṣīṇatāmanyathā vrajet ||32||
pare vyākuliteyānamadhamaṃ paricakṣate |
cāradṛṣṭirdūtavaktraudyadaṇḍo nṛpo bhavet ||33||
daṇḍasyaiva bhayāllokāḥ sve sve dharme vyavasthitāḥ |
anyathā niyamo nasyātpārakyaṃ svīyamityadaḥ ||34||
adhamo yadi nindetastuvīta yadi vā kvacit |
nakruddhennacatuṣyecca kiṃ tayā kiṃ tayāpi ca ||35||
pūrvāpakārī yadi ca punarvā śaraṇaṃ vrajet |
pūrvaṃ ca dhaniko yaḥ syātparipālyaḥ sadaiva hi ||36||
mantraguptiḥ sadākāryā tanmūlaṃ rājyamucyate |
kāmādi ṣaḍripūnhitvā tatonyānvijayīta ca ||37||
vṛtticchedaṃ prajocchedaṃ devatocchedameva ca |
ārāmacaityayośchedaṃ na kuyānnṛpasattamaḥ ||38||
parvakāle dadeddānaṃ yaśase tyāgameva ca |
na mitravañcanāṃ kuryātstrīṣu gopyaṃ vadenna ca ||39||
ṛṇato brāhmaṇaṃ caiva paṅkato gāṃ samuddharet |
anṛtaṃ na vadetkvāpi satyaṃ kvāpi na hāpayet ||40||
amātyānāṃ prajānāñca bhṛtyānāṃ cittahṛdbhavet |
brāhmaṇebhyo namaskuryāddevatābhyaḥ sadaiva ca ||41||
sūta uvāca ||
ityanyadapi śikṣāpya hemakaṇṭha sutaṃ nṛpaḥ |
ācāraṃ sahadharmeṇa nītiśāstraṃ yathā śrutam ||42||
kṣemaṃ karaṃ rūpavantaṃ vidyādhīśaṃ tathaiva ca |
āhvayāmāsa cāmātyānsumuhūrtaṃ vilokya ca ||43||
saṃbhārānupakalpyaiva nānāsthānagatānnṛpaḥ |
brāhmaṇanvedaviduṣo yajñakarma suniṣṭhitān ||44||
rājñothamahatorājapatnīśca suhṛdaḥ svakān |
āhvayāmāsa nṛpatiḥ śreṇīmukhyāṃśca nāgarān ||45||
abhiṣekāvalokāya putrasya ripughātinaḥ |
gaṇeśaṃ pūjayitveṣṭadevatāṃ ca yathāvidhi ||45||
vācayitvāsvastyayanaṃ mātṛpūjanapūrvakam |
kṛtvābhyudayikaṃ śrāddhaṃ brāhmaṇāṃstarpyacāndhasā ||47||
kārayitvā mantraghoṣairabhiṣekaṃ sutasya saḥ |
uvāca somakāntastrīnmantrimukhyānidaṃ vacaḥ ||48||
rājovāca ||
mamāyaṃ suta ityeva mamātyābuddhirastuvaḥ |
bhavatāmevahasteṣu suta eṣa nirūpitaḥ ||49||
mamānuśāsanaṃ yadvatkṛtaṃ nītiviśāradaiḥ |
tathāsya śāsanaṃ kāryaṃ śreṇīmukhyasamanvitaiḥ ||50||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe ācārādinirūpaṇaṃ nāma tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||3||

<adhyāya 4 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||
jātṛbhiṣekerājāsau cakāra dvijapūjanam |
maṇimuktāpravālāni dadau sāṅgaṃ gavāyutam ||1||
toṣayāmāsa sarvāṃstān gajagośvadhanāṃśukaiḥ |
nānādeśyānivāsāṃsisuvarṇāṃtaritāni ca ||2||
nānāvarṇāni kāśmīra deśajāni mahānti ca |
rājabhyorājapatnībhyo grāmamukhyebhya eva ca ||3||
tadṛtyebhyo guṇibhyaśca dadau rājā yathārhataḥ |
amātyebhyodadagvanyān grāmān bahudhanānyapi ||4||
tato vanaṃ yayau rājā duḥkhaśokasamanvitaḥ |
pūrvajanmārjitairdoṣairatyantamalino'śuciḥ ||5||
tasminprayāte lokānāṃ hāhākāromahānabhūt |
svaṃ svaṃ kāryaṃ vihāyaiva pratijagmurjanādhipam ||6||
amātyārājabhāryāśca hemakaṇṭhaḥ suhṛdvṛtaḥ |
uttiṣṭhanprapatanyātaḥskhalandhāvanrudannapi ||7||
amātyānāgarāstantu duḥkhitāapyavārayan |
gavyūtidvitayaṃgatvā tasthau rājā śramānvitaḥ ||8||
dṛṣṭvā vāpīṃ śītajalāṃ nānāvṛkṣasamanvitam |
uvāca nāgarānsarvānamātyānsvajanānapi ||9||
aparāddhaṃ mayāyadyacciraṃ rājyaṃ prakurvatā |
tatkṣantavyaṃ namāmyeṣa badhvoṃñjalipuṭaṃ janāḥ ||10||
kṛpāvidheyā putre me yadi daivātsamāgate |
vijñāpayāmivaḥ sarvānmayisnehonahīyatām ||11||
gacchantu nagaraṃ sarve sastrīvṛddhāḥ samāgatāḥ |
putreṇa pālyamānā me tiṣṭhantu vigatajvarāḥ ||12||
anujānaṃ tu māṃ sarve sucittoyāmyahaṃ vanam |
yuṣmāsutu prayāteṣu mano me niścalaṃ bhavet ||13||
upakāraṃ mahāntaṃ me kurvantu kṛpayākhilāḥ |
duḥkhitoniṣṭhuraṃ vaktuṃmartukāmopinotsahe ||14||
mahatpāpaṃ mayaivaitajjanmajanmāntarārjitam |
yadviyogo hi rājyasya lokānāṃ hitakāriṇām ||15||
parantu kiṃ karomyeṣa yadgalatkuṣṭhavānaham |
sarvopi svakṛtaṃ bhuṅte sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ tathā ||16||
sūta uvāca ||
iti śrutvā vaco rājño mūrcchitāḥ suhṛdastathā |
ghnantaḥ kecitpāṇitalaiḥ śirāṃsi bhṛśaduḥkhitāḥ ||17||
kecitparasparaṃcakrurabhijñāḥ paṇḍitāśca ye |
sāntvanaṃ pūrvajātānāṃ nṛpāṇāṃ caritairnṛpe ||18||
anirvācyāmavasthāṃ tatsamīkṣyeyurathāpare |
yathāsvarūpevijñāte yogino jñānarattayaḥ ||19||
duḥkhitaṃ somakāntaṃ taṃ vanaṃ gantuṃ samutsukum |
niyamya duḥkhaṃ dhairyeṇa dhīrāḥ kecidathābruvan ||20||
janā ūcuḥ ||
poṣayitvā pālayitvā nanastvaṃ tyaktumarhasi |
yathā na tyajate śaityaṃ jalamuṣṇaṃtanūnapāt ||21||
maryādāṃ jaladhirvāpi sūryaḥprakāśyameva ca |
kathaṃ vā nagaraṃ yāmastvāṃ vinā janavatsala ||22||
yathoḍupatināhīnaṃ vyomatārānvitaṃ nṛpa |
puraṃ tadvannaśobheta tvāṃ vinā śatrukarṣaṇa ||23||
sahai vayāmastīrthāni dvitrāṇi vayamapyuta |
rūpaṃ te kāntimatkānta bhavitā tīrtha sevanāt ||24||
tataḥ sahaivayāsyāmo nagaraṃ dhvajasaṃkulam |
harṣeṇa mahatā yuktā vādyabandipuraḥsarāḥ ||25||
sūta uvāca ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā krodhadūḥkhasamanvitaḥ |
uvāca rājā tānnatvāmaivaṃmaivaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ||26||
tato bravīddhemakaṇṭhaḥ sāmātyovatsalaṃ nṛpam |
snehakāruṇyabhāvena vinayena samanvitaḥ ||27||
putra uvāca ||
vinā tvāṃ notsahe gantuṃ kartuṃ rājyaṃ ca jīvitum |
namayā dṛṣṭa pūrvaste virahastaṃ kathaṃ sehe ||28||
rājovāca ||
etadarthaṃ mayā pūrvaṃ dharmaśāstraṃ sunītimat |
upadiṣṭaṃ tava śubhaṃ na vṛthākartumarhasi ||29||
śrūyate jāmadagnyena jananī nihatāpurā |
pitṛvākyānurodhena nītijñena sudhīmatā ||30||
rāmastu rājyaṃ tyaktvaiva vanaṃ yātaḥ sahānujaḥ |
apṛṣṭvā kāraṇaṃ sītāṃ tattyāja lakṣmaṇo vane ||31||
hemakaṇṭha puraṃ śīghramamātyaistribhiranvitaḥ |
ato mamājñayā gaccha kuru rājyaṃ mayārpitam ||32||
vijñeyathākāryasakte cittaṃ tasya parātmani |
yathāvasthāpite vitte cittaṃ lokasya varttate ||33||
tathāmayi vanaṃ yāte svāntaṃ me tvayivarttate |
daivayogena subhago jātoyāsye punargṛham ||34||
bhaviṣyati tathā dharmo madvākyaṃ kāriṇastava |
na tathā sahayā tena tasmādgaccha vrajāmi ca ||35||
sūta uvāca ||
amātyānāgarāputraḥ prayātuṃ kṛtamānasāḥ |
kaṣṭena mahatāyuktā namaścakrurnṛpaṃ tadā ||36||
anajñātāstatastena āśīrbhibhinandya ca |
pradakṣiṇī kṛtya nṛpaṃ nivṛttā nagaraṃprati ||37||
agrekṛtvā mahāsenāṃ gajāśvarathapattinām |
chatradhvajayutomānī hemakaṇṭho'gamatpurīm ||38||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||4||

<adhyāya 5 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
tato mātaramabhyetya snehakātarayādhiyā |
tāṃ jagāda kathaṃ mātastyajase'naparādhinam ||1||
putra uvāca ||
sahaivayātu putroyamiti vācyastvayā pitā |
tavavākyānurodhena yadimāṃsahaneṣyati ||2||
tadāvāṃ sevanaṃ kuryāṃ na me rājye matistathā |
kiṃ tadrājyaṃ sukhaṃ tadyādbhavadbhyāṃrahitasyame ||3||
sudharmovāca ||
etāvadduḥkhaśokābhyāṃ yuktorājānameva ca |
kariṣyati mahābāho tasmādgaccha mamājñayā ||4||
pātivratyena dharmeṇa paravatyasmi bālaka |
bharturanyo na devosti yoṣitāṃ mānyaeva ca ||5||
sūta uvāca ||
ityākarṇya namaścakre mātaraṃ sasuhṛtsutaḥ |
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tatonujñāṃprāpya puraṃ yayau ||6||
alaṅkṛtairnāgarikaiḥ patākādhvajapallavaiḥ |
siktamārgaṃ sugandhāḍhyaṃ yathendranagaraṃ tathā ||7||
dattvātāmbūlavāsāṃsi visṛjya svajanānnṛpaḥ |
praviveśa gṛhaṃ śrīmaddharṣaśokasamanvitaḥ ||8||
cakārarājyaṃ dharmeṇa sutavatpālayanprajāḥ |
dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu yathā śikṣaṃ manodadhe ||9||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
kathaṃ rājā somakāntaḥ kiṃ vanaṃ samupāgataḥ |
kiṃ sahāyaśca kiṃ cakre karmano vadavistarāt ||10||
sūta uvāca ||
haṃtavaḥ kathayiṣyāmi somakānto yathā vanam |
gatvā cakārayatkāryaṃ sādaraṃ śrṛṇutānaghāḥ ||11||
subala jñānagamyāmamātyābhyāṃ sudharmayā |
dharmapa yāyutoraṇyaṃ praviveśadurāsadam ||12||
agrataḥ prakṛtiyāto madhyerājāthapṛṣṭhataḥ |
dharmapatnī sudharmāgātsītevarāmapṛṣṭhataḥ ||13||
ekāśanāśca catvāra ekacittāsanālayāḥ |
samaduḥkhasukhāścāpi vanādvanamupāyayuḥ ||14||
kṣudhātṛṣāśramairvāpi nimnonnatagatādhvabhiḥ |
atyantakarṣitaśchāyāmāśrityopāviśankvacit ||15||
punarvanāntaraṃ gatvā dadṛśuste saromahat |
yatra mātaṅgasadṛśā nakrābhāntisakacchapāḥ ||16||
tālāstamālāḥ saralāḥ priyālābakulāḥ śubhāḥ |
saralāḥ panasājambunimbāśvatthavaṭādayaḥ ||17||
vṛkṣānānālatājālairveṣṭitāḥ paritobabhuḥ |
yatrāste timiraṃ gāḍhaṃ giridaryudare yathā ||18||
yatravāyuḥ sukhasparśaḥ kumudotpala gandhavān |
yasmānnayanti munayo visāsana ca phalāni ca ||19||
yatra haṃsā bakāḥ śyenāḥ sukāḥ kākāścakokilāḥ |
sārikāścakravākāśca nānārāvānvikurvate ||20||
nayatraśītoṣṇakarāṃśu saṃdhirnānālatāpuṣpakujāśritānām |
kṣuttṛḍbhayaṃ yatra na caiva mṛtyuḥ svarge yathā puṇyavatāṃ dvijendrāḥ ||21||
tatragatvā jalaṃ sarve papuḥ śītaṃ śramāpaham |
snātvā nityakriyāṃ kṛtvā phalāni bubhujuśca te ||22||
suṣvāpacakṣaṇaṃrājākacchekomalavāluke |
dharmapatnī sudharmā ca pādasaṃvāhanesthitā ||23||
tasyānumatamājñāya dvāvamātyāvagacchatām |
ādratuṃ kandamūlāni phalāni ca visāni ca ||24||
dadarśa tatrādbhutarūpamugraṃ bālaṃ sudharmā prabhayājvalantam |
utkṛṣṭarūpasyakṛtesmarantaṃ jātaṃ purā bālamitismamene ||25||
dṛṣṭvaiva sotaṃ jahṛṣe sudharmā mene ca taṃ sāhitakāriṇaṃ ca |
kṣubdhaṃ prasannaṃ hṛdayaṃ samīkṣyāpakāriṇaṃ copakaraṃ hi vakti ||26||
papraccha taṃ kaśca kutaścayātaḥ kasyāsi putro jananī ca kā te |
vadasva śabdāmṛtadhārayāme sakhevakarṇau paratoṣayāśu ||27||
sūta uvāca ||
itismapṛṣṭaḥ sa jagāda bālastāṃ rājaputrīṃ sudhayāgirāme |
pitā bhṛgurbhāmini me pulomā mātā jalārthī svagṛhādihāgām ||28||
cyavanaḥ khalu nāmnāhaṃ piturājñākaraḥ śubhe |
tvaṃ ca kā katamoyaṃte kathaṃ vanamidaṃ gataḥ ||29||
sravanti kathamaṃgāni varṣākālegireriva |
ati durgandhatā cāsya kena vā vada karmaṇā ||30||
kṛmibhāra samākīrṇamamuṃvāsevase katham |
svayaṃcārutarābhūtvā sukumārī sulocanā ||31||
cāru prasannavadanā cārusarvāṃgaśobhinī |
kaccitpūrvaṃ na pitrā te suhṛdbhirbhrātṛbhirdvijaiḥ ||32||
vijñātoyaṃ varaḥ kuṣṭhīkṛmibhārākulopi ca |
kathamenaṃ vṛtavatī durgamaṃ cāgatā vanam ||33||
sūta uvāca ||
iti pṛṣṭā sudharmā sā muniputreṇa dhīmatā |
vyācaṣṭe sakalaṃ tasmai śokaharṣasamanvitā ||34||
sudharmovāca ||
saurāṣṭradeśe vikhyātaṃ devatākhyaṃ puraṃ mahat |
tatrāyaṃ somakānto me bharttārājyaṃ cakāra ha ||35||
atimānīvadānyaśca śūrodṛḍhaparākramaḥ |
asaṃkhyātabalopeto ripurāṣṭravimarddanaḥ ||36||
yajvāti sundaraḥ śrīmānsuhṛdānaṃdakārakaḥ |
vivektāsarvakāryāṇāṃ nītiśāstraviśāradaḥ ||37||
rājyaṃ svaṃ bubhuje rājā bahukālaṃ dvijottama |
imāmavasthāṃ saṃprāptaḥ pūrvakarma vipākataḥ ||38||
amātyadvayasaṃyukto vanametatsamāgataḥ |
bhramantīpṛṣṭhalagnāsya putrerājyaṃ prayacchataḥ ||39||
subalajñānagamyābhyāṃ prakṛtibhyāmihāgatā |
rājñonujñāṃ samādāya phalārthaṃ tau vane gatau ||40||
ihāsmān rākṣasāḥ pretābhūtānimṛgapakṣiṇaḥ |
nānāvidhābhīṣayanti kathaṃ nobhakṣayantina ||41||
najāne duḥkhabhogāya sthāpayantyagratopi ca |
duḥkhasyāntaṃ napaśyāmi karmaṇo duṣkṛtasya ca ||42||
kaṭutiktāmlalavaṇamadhurasnigdhabhojane |
na tathā rucirasyābhūtparītasya dvijātibhiḥ ||43||
yathedānīṃ kandamūla kaṣāyāmlaphalādiṣu |
daridrāṇāṃ mahāhāraḥpākopi bhakṣitasya ca ||44||
na tathā śrīmatāṃ śakti raśane pāka eva ca |
yaḥ śete komale divye śayanesma manorame ||45||
idānīṃ yatrakutrāpi paśyakāla viparyayam |
yasyadigvyāpinaścāsannānāparimalāḥ śubhāḥ ||46||
ya ānandamaye sindhau magnobhūtpaṇḍitairvṛtaḥ |
pūyaśoṇitadigdhasya pūtigandhopisāṃpratam |47||
sa idānīṃ duḥkhamaye kṛmibhiḥ parivāritaḥ |
kathantarema duḥkhābdhiṃ najāne bhṛgunandana ||48||
bhavapotaivāgādhe majjantaṃ naurivārṇave ||49||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe sudharmācyavanasaṃvādo nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||5||

<adhyāya 6 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ ||
sūta uvāca ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā cyavano bhṛgunandanaḥ |
tvarayāsvamupādāya kalaśaṃ jalasaṃbhṛśam ||1||
duḥkhita paraduḥkhena tūṣṇīmevā gamadgṛham |
taṃ bhṛguḥ paripapraccha vilaṃbakāriṇaṃ sutam ||2||
bhṛguruvāca ||
kimapūrvaṃ tvayā dṛṣṭaṃ lakṣyase ca kito yathā |
vilambaśca kathaṃ jāto vada putra mamāgrataḥ ||3||
putra uvāca ||
saurāṣṭra deśe vikhyāte daivate nagare mune |
somakānta itikhyāto rājā rājīvalocanaḥ ||4||
cakre rājyaṃ bahutithaṃ dharmeṇa pālayanprajāḥ |
durbhagatvaṃ gato daivāt putra rājyaṃ niveśyasaḥ ||5||
patnyāsudharmayāyukto bharturājñā bhṛtāpitaḥ |
subala jñānagamyābhyāṃ prakṛtibhyāmihāgataḥ ||6||
galatkuṣṭhī kṛmiyuto vibhramandurgamaṃ saraḥ |
sahasrabhagatāprāpto yathendro gautamānmuneḥ ||7||
kvasā sudharmā cārvaṅgī kva galatkuṣṭhavānpatiḥ |
iti me pṛcchato vṛttaṃ tadīyaṃ gatavānkṣaṇaḥ ||8||
tadīyaiḥ karuṇāvākyairmano me kalilaṃ tvabhūt |
tatohaṃ kalaśaṃ pūrṇaṃ kṛtvā śīghramupāgataḥ ||9||
sūta uvāca ||
sarvaṃ cā kathayattasmai tayāyatkathitaṃ vacaḥ |
śrutvātacca punaḥ putraṃ cyavanaṃ bhṛgurabravīt ||10||
bhṛguruvāca ||
sarvānānayatān śīghraṃ putra gaccha mamājñayā |
drakṣyāmi kautukaṃ teṣāṃ svīyaṃ vā darśayāmi tān ||11||
sūta uvāca ||
itthaṃ sa coditaḥ pitrā cyavanaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ |
jagāma saraso bhūmiṃ sudharmāṃ draṣṭumutsukaḥ ||12||
tasminneva kṣaṇemātyau phalakandabharānvitau |
subala jñānagamyau tāvāgatau rājasaṃnidhim ||13||
tataūce sa tu muniḥ sudharmāṃ cārulocanām |
pitā me svāśramaṃ sarvānākārayati suvrate ||14||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā sudharmā śokavihvalā |
tadaiva sāvadhānāttanuḥprāṇaivāgate ||15||
suśīlā rājapatnī sā pītvā tadvacanāmṛtam |
agamatsāsucārvaṅgī prakṛti dvaya saṃyutā ||16||
somakāntena patinā muni putrasaraḥsarā |
gaṇeśaskandasahitā śivevaśivasaṃyutā ||17||
śuśubhe mārgamadhye sā vācaspatipuraḥsarā |
mantraghoṣayutaṃ prāpa bhṛgorāśrama maṇḍalam ||18||
nānāpuṣpalatākīrṇaṃ nānāpakṣivināditam |
mārjārānakulāḥ śyenāgajāgāvomayūrakāḥ ||19||
bhujaṃgā pakṣiṇaḥ siṃhāvyāghrāḥ krīḍanti yatra ca |
na ca vāyurbhṛśaṃ vāti na sūryastapate bhṛśam ||20||
na varṣati bhṛśaṃ megho varṣate ca tadicchayā |
viviśuste harṣayutā muniputra puraḥsarāḥ ||21||
tatrādbhutaṃ vyāghramṛgājinasthaṃ dadarśa sūryapratimaṃ bhṛguṃ tam |
rājātha patnī prakṛti dvayaṃ ca nanāma baddhāñjalirabravīcca ||22||
rājovāca ||
adyāśiṣo me saphalā dvijendra dvijeritā dharmacayastapopi |
ājanmatohaṃ paripūta eṣa piturjananyāśca sujīvitaṃ me ||23||
bhavadṛśiḥ saṃprati hantyayainaḥ pūrvārjitaiḥ puṇyacayauḥ kṛtāpi |
āgāmi kalyāṇakarī munīndra kālatraye janma karoti pūtam ||24||
saurāṣṭradeśe khalu devatākhye pure kṛtaṃ rājyamamoghadṛṣṭe |
bhītena pāpādvijadevatādi pūjākṛtā nītimatā munīndraḥ ||25||
ākasmikaṃ me duritaṃ kimetat prādurbabhūvogrataraṃ durantam |
yenāhamīdṛk kudaśāṃ praṇīto jāne na kiṃcitpratikāramatra ||26||
apāyatāṃ yānti kṛtā upāyā yuṣmatkṛtopāyamupāyamīhe |
nirvairatāṃ yānti hi jātivairāstavāśramevāṃ śaraṇaṃ prapannaḥ ||27||
sūta uvāca ||
śrutveti vacanaṃ tasya bhṛgustu karuṇāyutaḥ |
uvāca somakāntaṃ taṃ dhyānenālokya suvrataḥ ||28||
bhṛguruvāca ||
upāyaṃ vacmi nṛpate nacintāṃ kartumarhasi |
mamāśramagatāduḥkhaṃ nahirvindanti janminaḥ ||29||
janmāntara kṛtaṃ caiva duritaṃ te nṛpottama |
yene māṃ gamitovasthāṃ kathayiṣye tadapyaham ||30||
kurvantu bhojanaṃ sarve cirakālaṃ bubhukṣitāḥ |
vanādvanāntarāṃ yātāḥ śrāntāḥ klāntānanābhṛśam ||31||
sūta uvāca ||
ityuktvā snāpayāmāsa sutailābhyaṅgapūrvakam |
bhojayāmāsa cānnāni ṣaḍrasāni tvanekaśaḥ ||32||
bubhujustepi viśrāntā bhṛgoramitatejasaḥ |
ājñayā munivaryasya susnātāḥ suṣṭhvalaṃkṛtāḥ ||33||
suṣupurmṛduśayyāyāṃ hitvā cintāṃ duratyayām |
muninā kalpitāyāṃte svarājyaṃ gamitāiva ||34||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe bhṛgorāśramāgamanaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ ||6||

<adhyāya 7 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha saptamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ||
tatragatvā kimakarotsomakānto nṛpastadā |
upāyaḥ kathitaḥ ko vā bhṛguṇā sarvavedinā ||1||
kathayasva kathāmetāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ no dvijottama |
natṛptimadhigacchāmaḥ pītvā tvadvacanāmṛtam ||2||
sūta uvāca ||
samyakpṛṣṭaṃ mahābhāgā yūyaṃ tu jñānasāgarāḥ |
yaḥ kathāṃ taṃ naprayāti śrotāvaktāthavādvijāḥ ||3||
likhyamānaṃ vācayati pustakaṃ haratepi vā |
nṛpṛcchati cayaḥ śiṣyo guruḥ pṛṣṭo na vakticet ||4||
ubhau tau mūkabadhirau lokedṛṣṭau dvijottamāḥ |
śrṛṇvantu kathayiṣyāmi somakānta kathāmataḥ ||5||
tasyāṃ rātryāṃ vyatītāyāṃ dinanāthe tathodite |
snānaṃ sandhyāṃ japaṃ homaṃ kṛtvā sa bhṛgusattamaḥ ||6||
snātāya kṛtajapyāya patnīprakṛtisaṃyuje |
pūrvajanmakathāṃ tasmai rājñe kathitumārabhat ||7||
bhṛguruvāca ||
kolhāranagare ramye vindhyācalasamīpataḥ |
cidrūpa iti vikhyāto vaiśyo bahudhano bhavat ||8||
tasya bhāryā tu subhagā nāmnā khyātā sulocanā |
suśīlā dānaśīlā ca pativākyaparāsatī ||9||
tasyāstvaṃ putratāṃ yāto bhave pūrve nṛpottama |
kāmaṃda iti te nāma cakratustau dvijeritau ||10||
atisnehaṃ divārātrāvatilālanamekake |
vārdhakyakāla jetau tu pitarau tyayi cakratuḥ ||11||
vivāhaṃ cakratustātu dhanakautukamaṅgalaiḥ |
mṛgākṣī sukumārāṅgī nāmnā khyātā kuṭumbinī ||12||
anuraktā tvayi sadā dvijadevātithipriyā |
babhūva yoṣidyoṣitsu ratnabhūtādi sundarī ||13||
kuṭumbinīti svaṃ nāma vidadhe sārthakaṃ tu sā |
saptaputrā pañcakanyā pañcabāṇapriyānukim ||14||
tato bahutithe kāle pañcatvamagamatpitā |
mātā tava satī tena sahadagdhā divaṅgatā ||15||
tatastvaṃsakhivargaistaddravyaṃ nāśitavānbahu |
nītaṃ naṣṭaṃ bhakṣitaṃ ca vināśamagavaddhanam ||16||
cintayā dharmapatnī tvāṃ nirākṛtavatī bhṛśam |
na gṛhītaṃ tu tadvākyaṃ tato vikrītavān gṛham ||17||
gatā sā bālakaiḥ sārdhamanujñātā piturgṛham |
apatyapoṣaṇakṛte ṛtetvāṃ vaṃśakaṇṭakam ||18||
tata uddhṛttatāṃyāta unmatta iva madyapaḥ |
anyāyakārī nagare jātomatta iva dvipaḥ ||19||
parasvahāro vanitāsujāro grāmeṣucorojanatāpakāraḥ |
dyūteṣuvīro vṛjinaughasāro hiṃsāvihāro balahīna śūraḥ ||20||
ye ye janāste sukhasaṅgatṛptāstebhyo gṛhītvā bahuvittamādaḥ |
nikṣepataḥ pitṛkatātsahṛdbhyomiṣātkutaścinagarasthitebhyaḥ ||21||
śapathānanṛtāṃścakarthanūnamanṛtānāṃ pramadā janeṣu sākṣyam |
iti sarvajanāḥ subhītimāpururagātsadmagatādivogravīryāt ||22||
asahyatāṃ tena janasyayāto gokṣūrakaḥ pāyasagoyayaiva |
rājño gṛhitvānumatiṃ janāste nirvāpayāmāsuratopurāttvām ||23||
vanesthitastvaṃ bahujantughātī strībālavṛddhānvinihaṃsi nityam |
mahājanaṃ vīkṣya palāyase ca siṃhaṃ nirīkṣyevavṛkomṛgovā ||24||
matsyānbakānsārasakukkuṭāṃśca vṛkānmṛgānvānarakokilāṃśca |
hatvātsikhaḍgānśaśakāṃścagotrāmudhāsvadeśaṃ vṛjinādapoṣaḥ ||25||
siṃhānvyāghrāṃśca jambūkānniḥsārya girigahvarāt |
nānāsthānagatāścerānmelayitvā sudurdharān ||26||
kāṣṭhairloṣṭaiśca pāṣāṇaiścakartha gṛhamuttamam |
viśālaṃ krośavistāraṃ nānākautuka maṇḍitam ||27||
pitrābhītenapatnī sā prāpitā bālakaiḥ saha |
guhāgataṃ te sadanaṃ rājatojanato'pi ca ||28||
nānālaṃkāravasanā bālakā api tejasā |
devāṅganeva śuśubhe tvaṃ cacorasamanvitaḥ ||29||
tvaṃ ca mārge janāndīnān hatvā hatvā guhāṃ gataḥ |
coraiḥ sabālayoṣidbhirbhāsirājevatadgataḥ ||30||
kadācid brāhmaṇo vidvān vikhyātoguṇavardhanaḥ |
madhyāhne mārgamadhye tu tvayādṛṣṭaḥ sa ekakaḥ ||31||
gṛhītvā dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇiṃ taṃ vipraṃ dhṛtavānasi |
sa vepamānastvadbuddhiṃ jānandharṣaṇayātayā ||32||
ūce tvāṃ svāṃtakaṃ matvā mūrcchito jīvitecchayā |
atyantakaruṇāvākyairbodhayaṃ svāṃsahetukaiḥ ||33||
guṇavardhana uvāca ||
dhanavānsubhagobhūtvā kathaṃ māṃ hantumicchasi |
dvijaṃ naboḍhā bhartāraṃ śāntaṃ cānaparādhinam ||34||
durvuddhivāsanāṃ tyaktvā saddharmeṣu matiṃ kuru |
prathamāmegatā kāntā prāptā kāntatarāparā ||35||
svācārāparamodārā sādhvī sarvaguṇākarā |
pitṛṛṇāmanṛṇārthāya dharmasantānavṛddhaye ||36||
kṛtāsāti prayatnena gārhasthaṃdharmamicchatā |
madvinātadvināvāṃhi janmanī bhavato vṛthā ||37||
pitāmehi bhavatvaṃ ca mātāputropyahaṃ tava |
jīvadātā bhayatrātā pitāśāstre nigadyate ||38||
dasyavopihi rakṣanti dvijaṃ vā śaraṇāgatam |
dvijaṃ yogyaṃ śaraṇyaṃ māmatatstvaṃ moktumarhasi ||39||
nocetkalpasahasraṃ tvaṃ nirayānpratipadyase |
bhoktāraḥ sarva evaite strīputrāḥ suhṛdojanāḥ ||40||
na pāpabhāginastve te sukhinastvatpratārakāḥ |
kiyadbhirjanmabhiḥ pāpaṃ bhokṣyase navitarkaye ||41||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe somakāntapūrvajanmakathanaṃ nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ ||7||

<adhyāya 8 prāraṃbhaḥ>

||athāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
bhṛguruvāca ||
iti tenoditaṃ vākyaṃ muhurglāniṃ prakurvataḥ |
karuṇāyutamākarṇya nodbhinnaṃ hṛdayaṃ tava ||1||
vrajātsārataraṃ kiṃ nu brahmaṇā tyayi nirmitam |
hiṃsanād bahujantūnāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ sahasraśaḥ ||2||
atiniṣṭhuratāṃ prāptaṃ kṛtaghnasyeva mānasam |
avocastvaṃ tatastantu kṛtānta iva niṣṭhuraḥ ||3||
cora uvāca ||
kiṃ te vākyacayairvipraḥ vṛthāmayi niyojataiḥ |
aśrocaritapāṇḍityaṃ nyubjekumbhe yathā jalam ||4||
kveyaṃ mama matirmūḍha kvopadeśastavāpyayam |
tattva cinteva madhupe sāmprataṃ me na rocate ||5||
arthāsaktaya na kvāpi pitṛbandhuvicāraṇā |
yathākāmāturasyāpi na bhītirhṛśca vidyate ||6||
śuddhiḥ kākedyūtakāre satyaṃdhairyaṃ napuṃsake |
kāmaśānti striyāṃ sarpe kṣamā dṛṣṭvā tvayānukim ||7||
antiketvaṃ nisṛṣṭosi vyāpārarahitasya me |
vidhātrā daivayogena na tvāṃ muñcāmi karhicit ||8||
bhṛguruvāca ||
ituktvā śitiṃ khaḍgaṃ hastaādāya dakṣiṇe |
cakartātha śirastasya biḍāla iva mauṣakam ||9||
evaṃ te bahmahatyānāṃ saṃkhyāṃ kartuṃ na śakyate |
strībālavṛddhajantūnāṃ hatyānāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ ||10||
saṃkhyātāparapāpānāṃ vibhāgī jāyate yataḥ |
tato bahutithe kāle gate kāmaṃdate jarā ||11||
agatā kaphoglāniḥ svedehohikkāthavepathuḥ |
abhūttandrīcopaviṣṭe prasuptena ca sā bhavat ||12||
anādaraṃ ca cakruste putrādāsyothasevakāḥ |
suhṛdaśca sutāścāpinaptārothāpi mitrakāḥ ||13||
ekaevābhavattatra dṛṣṭaāpto rahasyakṛt |
anivārya gatirvipraḥ sa cāpi preṣitastvayā ||14||
ākārayituṃ sarvāṃstān munīn kānanavāsinaḥ |
te cāpi tvadbhayā deva dvijavākyātsamāgatāḥ ||15||
abravīstānnamaskṛtyamattodānānigṛśyatām |
te bruvanna ca gṛhṇimo dānāni patitasya te ||16||
yajanādhyāpanādyaunātsambandhādbhāṣaṇādapi |
paraṃ sañcarate pāpaṃ sahayānāsanāśanāt ||17||
iti te bhāṣaṇāstvāṃ gatvā ca nijamāśramam |
sasnuḥsacailasarvepi pāvamānīstato japan ||18||
tatonutāpo manasi jātaḥ kāmaṃdate bhṛśam |
āmayātsvajanaistyāgādbrāhmaṇaiḥḥ parivarjanāt ||19||
jīrṇadevālayoddhāre bhavatte matirūrjitā |
dhanaṃ svaṃ vipulaṃ vīkṣya kupyaratnādi saṃyutam ||20||
tatonādiḥ parāmūrtirgaṇeśasya vane śubhā |
jīrṇa devālaye kṣudre sthitā te kathitā dvijaiḥ ||21||
tato'ti vistṛtaṃ dīrghaṃ catustoraṇasaṃyutam |
caturdvāraṃ suruciraṃ catuḥśikhara śobhitam ||22||
nānāstambhabhitaṃ nānā vedibhiḥ parivāritam |
muktāpravālaratnādyaiḥ racitaṃ rucirāṅgaṇam ||23||
nānāpuṣpadrumayutāṃ nānāphaladrumaiścitam |
caturdikṣucāruvārimayavāpī virājitam ||24||
prāsādaṃ nirmitavatastaddravyaṃ te vyayaṃ gatam |
kiñcitstriyā ca putraiśca suhṛdbhirbandhubhirhṛtam ||25||
tato nāti cirekāle pañcatvaṃ ca gato bhavān |
yāmyadūtaiḥ kaśāghātairbaddhaḥ santāḍito bhṛśam ||26||
kaṇṭakairviddhasarvāṅgaḥ śilāyāṃ pothitopi ca |
majjito narake ghore pūyaśoṇitakardame ||27||
evaṃ nītastutairdūtaiścitraguptayamāntike |
yamena pṛṣṭaḥ kiṃ puṇyamutabhokṣyasi pātakam ||28||
tvamavocaḥ puṇyamahaṃ pūrvaṃ bhokṣyāmi sūryaja |
tataḥ saurāṣṭradeśe tvaṃ rājatvena vinirmitaḥ ||29||
iti te kathitaṃ pūrvajanma pāpakaraṃ mayā |
śaraṇāgata kāruṇyāttapobala samāśrayāt ||30||
kānta prāsādakaraṇātsomakānto'bhavonṛpaḥ |
kāntayā kāntatarayā bhāsikāntyā śaśī yathā ||31||
sūta uvāca ||
bhṛguṇābhihitaṃ śrutvā somakānto nṛpādhamaḥ |
tadvākye saṃśayāpanna āsīdaśmeva niṣkriyaḥ ||32||
vedaśāstrārthaviduṣo bhūtabhāvibhavadvidaḥ |
tapasvino bhṛgorvākye yataḥ sa saṃśayaṃ dadhau ||33||
niragankṣaṇamātreṇa tasyāṅgātpakṣiṇo bhṛśam |
nānāvarṇākṛtidharā babhakṣurnṛpatiṃ tadā ||34||
uḍīyoḍḍīyacañcograirdadaṃśurnṛpaśuṃ dṛḍhaiḥ |
utkṛtyotkṛtya māṃsāni nijakṣuste munisannidhau ||35||
tatotiduḥkhita tanuḥ śaraṇaṃ punarāgataḥ |
avadaddīnayā vācā bhṛguṃ jñānataponidhim ||36||
rājovāca ||
tvadvane jātivairāṇāṃ paraspara bhayaṃ na hi |
tvatsamakṣaṃ kathamime mṛtaṃ māṃ mārayanti ca ||37||
tvatpāda pravaṇaṃ dīnaṃ sakuṣṭhaṃ śaraṇāgatam |
idānīṃ mocaya mune sarvasatvā bhayaṅkara ||38||
sūta uvāca ||
ituktaḥ punarapyāha bhṛgustaṃ dīnavatsalaḥ |
madvākyasaṃśayāditthamanubhūtaṃ nṛpatyayā ||39||
pratīkāraṃ vadiṣyāmi svastho bhava kṣaṇaṃ nṛpa |
mama huṃkāra mātreṇa gamiṣyanti dvijā ime ||40||
sūta uvāca ||
dvijasya huṃkṛtiṃ śrutvā dvijāścāntarhitāstadā |
tutoṣa nṛpatiścāpi patnyamātyasanvitā ||41||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe nānāpakṣinivāraṇaṃ nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ ||8||

<adhyāya 9 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha navamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
sūta uvāca ||
tato bhṛguḥ kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā punastaṃ prāha bhūpatim |
atyanta vihvalo duḥkhaṃ vīkṣyatatpūrvakarmajam ||1||
bhṛguruvāca ||
kva pāpa nikarāstetu kvopāyāḥ kathitā mayā |
tathāpyevaṃ bravīmitvāmupāyamaghanāśanam ||2||
gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ tvaṃ śroṣyase yadisatvaraḥ |
tadaiva mokṣyaseduḥkhasāgarānnātrasaṃśayaḥ ||3||
ityuktvā taṃ gaṇeśasya nāmāṣṭaśatamuttamam |
japtvā jalaṃ mantrayitvā siṣeca nṛpatiṃ tadā ||4||
siktamātre jale tena tannāsāraṃ dhrato laghuḥ |
kṛṣṇāsyaḥ prāpatadbhūmau vavṛdhe tatkṣaṇādasau ||5||
saptatālapramāṇobhūdvyāditāsyo bhayaṅkaraḥ |
karālajihvoraktākṣo dīrghabāhurjaṭādharaḥ ||6||
mukhāduruvamannagniṃ kṣaṇācca pūyaśoṇite |
netrāṇāmandhatāṃ kurvannandhakāra ivāparaḥ ||7||
ālokya dudruvuḥ sarve tadāśrama nivāsinaḥ |
daṃṣṭrārāvairdaśadiśaḥ pūrayantaṃ tamadbhutam ||8||
apṛcchattaṃ dvijavarastatsamakṣaṃ tu pūruṣam |
vidvānapītikositvaṃ nāma te kiṃ vadasvame ||9||
pratyuvāca muniṃ taṃ tu paripṛṣṭo dvijenasaḥ |
prāṇimātraśarīrastho nāmnāhaṃ pāpapūruṣaḥ ||10||
tvanmatrajalapātena niḥsṛto rājadehataḥ |
kṣudhāturo bhakṣyamīhedehimahyaṃ na cettadā ||11||
bhakṣayiṣye munelokān somakāntaṃ tavāgrataḥ |
vada me vasatiṃ ramyāmitoniḥsāritasya ca ||12||
tatastu bahirabhyetya tamuvāca muniḥ punaḥ |
asmin rasātale sarale virase vasa koṭare ||13||
galitāni ca patrāṇi bhakṣayasva mamājñayā |
nocetvāṃ bhasmasātkuryāṃ nānṛtaṃ me vacodhama ||14||
sūta uvāca ||
iti tanmuvākyānte śuṣkaṃ sa vṛkṣamaspṛśat |
bhasmasādabhavadvṛkṣodvijāstatsparśamātrataḥ ||15||
muneḥ saṃdarśanādbhīta tasmin bhasmanyalīyata |
tasmin viline sa muniḥ somakāntaṃ punarjagau ||16||
bhṛguruvāca ||
purāṇaśravaṇādyatte jātaṃ puṇyaṃ nṛpottama |
ayaṃ yāvaccyūtataruratracotiṣṭhate punaḥ ||17||
tāpatsaṃpādayasmiṃstadbhasmanyeva dine dine |
vivṛddhesmiṃstaraurājanniṣpāpastvaṃ bhaviṣyasi ||18||
rājovāca ||
gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ yannadṛṣṭaṃ na ca vai śrutam |
kutra tallabhyate brahmanvyākartā tasya vā mune ||19||
muniruvāca ||
brahmaṇā kathitaṃ pūrvaṃ vedavyāsāya dhīmate |
vyāsācca viditaṃ mahyaṃ purāṇaṃ pāpanāśanam ||20||
ahaṃ ca te pravakṣyāmi tīrthesnānaṃ samācara |
śroṣye purāṇamiti ca saṅkalpaṃ kuru suvrata ||21||
sūta uvāca ||
bhṛguṇānoditaḥ snātvā bhṛgutīrtheti viśrute |
somakāntomudāyuktastataḥ saṅkalpamācarat ||22||
gaṇeśasya purāṇaṃ yattacchroṣyedyaprabhṛtyaham |
saṅkalpamātre tu kṛte babhau rājā nirāmayaḥ ||23||
bhṛguprasādānnīraktaḥ kṛmikṣatavisarjitaḥ |
gṛhītvā taṃ bhṛguragādvismitaṃ harṣitaṃ nṛpam ||24||
upaviśyāsanesvīye dāpayattasyacāsanam |
upaviṣṭo babhāṣe taṃ divyakāntirnṛpottamaḥ ||25||
rājovāca ||
bhavatprasādātsakalā gariṣṭhā saṅkalpa mātreṇa gatā vyathā me |
āścaryabhūtaṃ sakalaṃ vadasva purāṇametad dviradānanasya ||26||
bhṛguruvāca ||
śrṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā tatpurāṇaṃ vadāmyaham |
anantapuṇyanicayairyasya śravaṇagocarā ||27||
matirutpadyate puṃsāṃ nānyathā pāpakāriṇām |
yasyākarṇanamātreṇa saptajanmārjitānyapi ||28||
laghuśuṣkārdrasthūlāni pāpāni ca mahāntyapi |
tatkṣaṇādvilayaṃ yānti gaṇeśasya prasādataḥ ||29||
avyayasyā prameyasya nirguṇasya nirākṛteḥ |
manovāganirūpyasya kevalānandarūpiṇaḥ ||30||
yasya svarūpaṃ navidurbrahmeśānādayaḥ surāḥ |
sahasra vadano yasya mahimānaṃ na cakṣamaḥ ||31||
yāvadviśeṣavidapi pravaktuṃ rājasattama |
mayā yathā śrutaṃ pūrvaṃ tatpurāṇaṃ supuṇyadam ||32||
atīndriyajñānavato vyāsāccāmitatejasaḥ |
yajñavidhvaṃsaśokārto dakṣaḥ suśrāvamudgalāt ||33||
yasya bhaktirdṛḍhā rājan gaṇeśe sarvasiddhide |
sa etacchṛṇuyānnityaṃ na vācyaṃ tāṃdṛśetare ||34||
yadi sarvepi kurvīran vighnarājasya sevanam |
kva tadā vighnanicayā vicareyuryathāsukham ||35||
dukhānyanubhaveyuḥ kenānāvirahajānyuta |
cakāredaṃ purā vyāso bhūtabhavyabhaviṣyavit ||36||
vedārthajñānarahitān vedādhyayanavarjitān |
varṇāśramācāraśūnyān jātisaṃkarakāriṇaḥ ||37||
kalau vilokya tu janān kuṭilān pāpakāriṇaḥ |
aṣṭādaśa purāṇāni kṛtavān dharmaguptaye ||38||
tāvantyupapurāṇāni tatorthān bubudhurjanāḥ |
tata eva gaṇeśasya rūpaṃ jānantyamījanāḥ ||39||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe rājopadeśakathanaṃ nāma navamo'dhyāyaḥ ||9||

<adhyāya 10 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha daśamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
bhṛguruvāca ||
nārāyaṇāṃśa saṃbhūtaḥ pārāśaryomahāmuniḥ |
atītānāgata jñānī vedaśāstrārthatatvavit ||1||
kṛtvā vedaṃ caturbhāgaṃ tadartha jñānasiddhaye |
svavidyāmadagarveṇaṃ purāṇaṃ kartumārabhat ||2||
samāpti sādhanaṃ naiva kṛtavān maṅgalaṃ purā |
gaṇeśasya natiṃ vāpi stutiṃ vā tasya vā kvacit ||3||
tatovighnābhibhūtaḥ sannarthaṃ kaṃcananāsmarat |
bhrāntirevā bhavattasya laukike'laukikepathi ||4||
nityanaimittikekāmyeśrautesmārte ca karmaṇi |
vyākhyāturvedaśāstrāṇāṃ sarvajñasya sa tepi ca ||5||
auṣadhībhiśca mantraiśca bhagnavīryaivāhirāṭ |
tastaṃ bhesvātmanibhṛśaṃ taddhetuṃ nābhyagacchata ||6||
tatogacchatsatyalokaṃ dhātāraṃ praṣṭumādarāt |
vismayākrāntahṛdayo hrīṇaḥ pārāśaro muniḥ ||7||
namaskṛtvā devagaṇāndevarṣīn kamalāsanam |
pūjito brahmaṇādatte niṣasādāsane śubhe ||8||
pāṇinācāspṛśatpādau pārāśaryo mahāmuniḥ |
praṇayāvanato bhūtvā brahmāṇaṃ praṣṭumārabhat ||9||
vyāsa uvāca ||
brahmannadbhuta me taddhi kiṃ me daivādupasthitam |
vedārthāni purāṇāni kartuṃ me buddhirādṛtā ||10||
āvīkṣatokhilām̐llokān jñānācāra vivarjitān |
kalaukarmajaḍāṃstabdhān nāstikān vedanindakān ||11||
vidhiṃ caiva niṣedhaṃ ca jñāsyanti mama vākyataḥ |
mamaivatugataṃ jñānaṃ bhrāntaḥ kṣībaivābhavam ||12||
nahetuṃ tatra paśyāmi sphūrtiḥ kāpi na jāyate |
taddhetuṃ sphūrtihetuṃ ca praṣṭuṃ tvāmahamāgataḥ ||13||
anyaṃ kaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāmi vinā tvāṃ caturānana |
sarvajñaḥ sarvakartā tvaṃ mama bhrāntiṃ nivāraya ||14||
vadahetuṃ mama bhrānternārāyaṇasvarūpiṇaḥ |
nityācāravatobrahman sarvajñasya satopi ca ||15||
sūta uvāca ||
evamākarṇya tadvākyaṃ vicārya kamalāsanaḥ |
praṇataṃ munimabhyāha prahasan vismayanniva ||16||
brahmovāca ||
haṃta te kathayiṣyāmi gatiṃ sūkṣmāṃ hi karmaṇām |
vicārya myakkartavyaṃ karmasādhvitaraccayat ||17||
anyathā kurvataḥ puṃsaḥ kṛtaṃ bhavati cānyathā |
buddhyāyuktyārjavenāpi gurūṇi ca laghūni ca ||18||
kāryāṇi sādhayeddhīmān na garvān na ca matsarāt |
vāhana tvaṃ samāpanno garveṇa tu khageśvaraḥ ||19||
matsarān nāśitaṃ sarvaṃ ābikeya sutena ca |
matsarādeva rāmeṇa kṣatramutsāditaṃ purā ||20||
yonādi nidhanodevo jagatkartā jaganmayaḥ |
jagaddhātā jagaddhartā sadasadvyaktamavyayam ||21||
yo kartumanyathākartuṃ śaktaḥ kartuṃ ca sarvadā |
yasyājñā vaśagā nityaṃ devā indrapurogamāḥ ||22||
ahaṃ viṣṇuśca rudraśca sūryāgnivaruṇādayaḥ |
yo vighnahartā bhaktānāṃ vighnakartetarasya ca ||23||
tasmiṃstvaṃ kṛtavān garvaṃ svavidyābalasaṃśrayāt |
sarvajñatābhimānena na kṛtaṃ pūjanaṃ tvayā ||24||
smaraṇaṃ vā gaṇeśasya prārambhenyasya vā tathā |
na kṛtaṃ ca tvayā vyāsa tena bhrāntistavānagha ||25||
ārambhe sarvakāryāṇāṃ praveśe vāpi nirgame |
śrautesmārttelaukikeyosmṛtovighnaṃ karoti ca ||26||
yamāhuḥ paramānandaṃ yamāhuḥ paramāṃ gatim |
yamāhuḥ paramaṃ brahma vedaśāstrārthadarśinaḥ ||
taṃ gaccha śaraṇaṃ vatsa dviradānanamādarāt ||27||
sa cetprasanne bhagavān vāñchitaṃ te kariṣyati |
nocedvarṣasahasreṇa na svavāñchāmavāpsyati ||28||
vyāsa uvāca ||
ko sau gaṇeśaḥ kiṃ cāsya rūpaṃ vedyaṃ kathaṃ ca tat |
kasya vāyaṃ prasannobhūtpūrvaṃ hi caturānana ||29||
kativāhyavatārāśca kiṃ kiṃ kāryaṃ ca vai kṛtam |
pūrvaṃ ca pūjitaḥ kena kasmin kāle smṛtopi ca ||30||
mama vikṣipta cittasya pṛcchataḥ prapitāmaha |
etatsarvaṃ suvistārya brūhi me karuṇānidhe ||31||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe vyāsapraśnavarṇanaṃ nāma daśamo'dhyāyaḥ ||10||

<adhyāya 11 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| athaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
bhṛguruvāca ||
jagādabhūyaścaturānanastamevaṃ kṛta praśna vivakṣayātha |
krameṇa sarvaṃ kathayāmitehaṃ gaṇeśa mantrānbahudhā vicārya ||1||
brahmovāca ||
anantā hi mune mantrā gaṇeśasya mahātmanaḥ |
upāsanaṃ vadiṣyāmi śīghraṃ siddhikarasya te ||2||
saptakoṭi mahāmantrā gaṇeśasyāgamesthitāḥ |
tadrahasyaṃ śivovedakiñcikiñcidahaṃ mune ||3||
ṣaḍakṣaraikākṣarayoḥ śreṣṭhatvaṃ teṣu vidyate |
yayoḥ smaraṇamātreṇa sarvasiddhiḥkare bhavet ||4||
jīvanmuktābhavantyeva yadupāsanayā mune |
ta eva dhanyāste pūjyāstenamasyāḥ surairapi ||5||
yadupāsanayādāsyaṃ yeṣāṃ kurvanti siddhayaḥ |
icchāvihārāḥ sarvajñā nānārūpā bhavanti te ||6||
ye kurvanti gaṇeśasya bhaktiṃ bhāvasamanvitāḥ |
na yeṣāṃ bhaktileśopi teṣāṃ janma nirarthakam ||7||
nālokayen mukhaṃ teṣāṃ vimukhā ye gajānane |
teṣāṃ darśanamātreṇa vighnānisyuḥ pade pade ||8||
vighnānyupaśamaṃ yānti tadupāsaka darśanāt |
namasyanti ca bhūtāni sthāvarāṇi carāṇi ca ||9||
atastehaṃ pravakṣyāmi mantramekākṣaraṃ śubham |
tadanuṣṭhāna mātreṇa vāñchitaṃ samavāpsyasi ||10||
anuṣṭhānaṃ pravakṣyāmi yathoktaṃ śambhunā mama |
snānaṃ kṛtvā dhautavastre paridhāya śucirnaraḥ ||11||
cailājinakuśaiḥkṛtvā svāsanaṃ sādhakaḥ sudhīḥ |
tasmin sthitvā bhūtasuddhiṃ prāṇānāṃ sthāpanaṃ tathā ||12||
antarbahirmātṛkāṇāṃ nyāsaṃ kṛtvātvatandritaḥ |
prāṇāyāmaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā mūlamantraṃ japan hṛdi ||13||
mantra sandhyāmupāsīta āgamoktāṃ yathāvidhi |
āpādamastakaṃ devaṃ dhyātvāniścalacetasā ||14||
mānasairupacāraistaṃ pūjayetsusamāhitaḥ |
tato japedyathāśakti puraścaraṇamārgataḥ ||15||
anukūlobhavedyāvadvaraṃ dātuṃ gajānanaḥ |
svarūpaṃ darśayedyāvattāvajjapaparo bhavet ||16||
bhṛguruvāca ||
evamuktvā muniṃ brahmā vilokya śubhavāsaram |
ekākṣaraṃ mantrarājamekārṇaṃ gaṇapasya ca ||17||
aśikṣayattadā sarvaṃ sambhrāntaṃ munisattamam |
brahmovāca ||
sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ varaṃ dātuṃ samāgamat ||18||
yadādrakṣyasitaṃ devaṃ tadācittaṃ sthirī kuru |
mamaiva hṛdaye nityaṃ sthiro bhava gajānana ||19||
yācasveti varaṃ tasmāt sa te dāsyatya saṃśayam |
hṛdisthite tatra deve divyaṃ jñānamavāpsyasi ||20||
bhūtaṃ bhāvi bhavadghaiva jñāsyasi tvamaśeṣataḥ |
hitvā bhrāntiṃ dṛḍhāṃ vatsa nānāgranthān kariṣyati ||21||
vyāsa uvāca ||
gatā bhrāntirmamapitarupadeśe kṛte tvayā |
anuṣṭhānaṃ kariṣyāmi pitāmaha tavājñayā ||22||
brahmovāca ||
ekānte nirjane deśe vyagratā hetu varjite |
anuṣṭhānaṃ kuruvibho gajānanamanusmaran ||23||
nāstike nindake krūre nācāre ca khale śaṭhe |
na vaktavyo mantrarājastādṛśe śaraṇāgate ||24||
dṛḍhabhaktau śraddadhāne vinīte vedavādini |
sākāṃkṣeniranukrośe śāstrajñe ca prakāśaya ||25||
prāpayatyeva narakān daśapūrvān daśāparān |
mantrarājastu vaktāramaprakāśyeprakāśitaḥ ||26||
yojayedbhaktipūrvaṃ tu sa labhedīpsitaṃ phalam |
putrapautrasamāyukto dhanadhānyasamanvitaḥ ||27||
ekadanta prabhāvena labdhvājñānaṃ sunirmalam |
iha bhuktvākhilān bhogānante mokṣamavāpnuyāt ||28||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe mantrakathanaṃ nāmaikādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||11||

<adhyāya 12 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
sūta uvāca ||
iti śrutvā tu vacanaṃ nirgataṃ brahmaṇomukhāt |
harṣeṇa mahatāyukto muniḥ papraccha taṃ punaḥ ||1||
vyāsa uvāca ||
avadhānaṃ mayā prāptaṃ pītvāvāgamṛtaṃ tava |
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi mantrarājamimaṃpitaḥ ||2||
ko jajāpa kathaṃ siddhimavāpa ca gajānanāt |
etaṃ me saṃśayaṃ cchindhi tvattonyonāsti me guruḥ ||3||
bhṛguruvāca ||
ityevaṃ muninā pṛṣṭosau jagāda vadatāṃ varaḥ |
kṛpayāvanataṃ brahmā taṃ vyāsaṃ rājasattama ||4||
brahmovāca ||
sādhusādhu tvayā pṛṣṭaṃ puṇyavānasi sāmpratam |
nahyapuṇyavatāṃ brahman kathāyāḥ śravaṇeratiḥ ||5||
upāsanāmārgamimaṃ samyaktvāṃ bodhayāmyaham |
snigdhe prajñādhike śiṣye yato gopyaṃ na kiñcana ||6||
oṃkārarūpībhagavānuktaste gaṇanāyakaḥ |
yathā sarveṣu kāryeṣu pūjyate sau vināyakaḥ ||7||
nirvighnakāmanāvadbhirnocedvighnaṃ karoti saḥ |
tathoṃkārabījayukta oṃkārapallavānvitāḥ ||8||
mantrāḥ sarvāgameproktā anye ye niṣphalāśca te |
sadasadvyaktamavyaktaṃ sarvaṃ hi gaṇanāyakaḥ ||9||
evaṃ sarve devasiddhamunirākṣasakinnarāḥ |
gandharvāścāraṇānāgāyakṣaguhyakamānavāḥ ||10||
upāsakā gaṇeśasya sarvelokāścarācarāḥ |
ataeva gaṇeśāddhi paraṃ kiñcinnavidyate ||
idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi kathāmekāṃ purātanīm ||11||
mantrarāja japenāyaṃ yathā tuṣṭo gajānanaḥ |
kadāciddaivayogena pralaye samupasthite ||12||
vāyubhiḥ parvatābhinnāḥ patitāḥ paritodiśam |
tapanti dvādaśādityāḥ śoṣayitvā jalaṃ mahat ||13||
jvālāmālī mahāvahnirakhilaṃ jvalayatyapi |
saṃvartakā mahāmeghā varṣanti paritojalam ||14||
hastihastopamābhistu dhārābhirdvijasattama |
ullaṅghayanti maryādāṃ sāgarāḥ saritopi ca ||15||
evaṃ sarve vinaśyanti ābrahmasthāvarādayaḥ |
evaṃ māyāmaye naṣṭe vikāresau gajānanaḥ ||16||
aṇubhyoṇutaraṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvā kvāpi vyavasthitaḥ |
tataḥ kāle bahutaregatendha tamasāvṛte ||17||
ekākṣaraṃ punarbrahma nādayuktamajāyata |
vaikārikaṃ punārūpaṃ yadānandamayaṃ sthitam ||18||
māyāvikāramāsādyatadevābhūdgajānanaḥ |
tata eva ca saṃbhūtā guṇāḥ satvaṃrajastamaḥ ||19||
tatastrayaḥ samutpannā viṣṇurbrahmā haropi ca |
māyayā racitaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācaram ||20||
tatastanmāyayā bhrāntā babhramuste surāstrayaḥ |
tameva janakaṃ svasya draṣṭuṃ praṣṭuṃ samutsukāḥ ||21||
kimasmābhiḥ karmakāryamiti jijñāsayā mune |
ekaviṃśati svargāṇi pūrvamūrdhvaṃ vilokyate ||22||
antarīkṣaṃ tathā tiryak paścāt pātālamāyayuḥ |
adṛṣṭvā paramātmānaṃ tapaścerustato bhṛśam ||23||
nirāhārā japaparā divyavarṣa sahasrakam |
śrāntāḥ khedamanuprāptāstataste pṛthivīṃ punaḥ ||24||
vicinvato yayurdraṣṭuṃ vanānyupavanāni ca |
saritaḥ sāgarān śailān śikharāṇi guhāpi ca ||25||
tato jalāśayaṃ te tu mahāntaṃ dadṛśuḥ surāḥ |
nānājalacarairvṛkṣaiḥ pakṣibhirvividhairyutam ||26||
balākābhiścakravākairhaṃsaiḥ kāraṇḍakairapi |
nāditaṃ nalinījālaṃ bisakhaṇḍāśanerataiḥ ||27||
snātvā viśramya tattīre samuttīrya puro yayuḥ |
nānāvīci samāyuktaṃ mahāntaṃ taṃ jalāśayam ||28||
sudustarataraṃ puṃsāṃ jhaṣanakrasamākulam |
īkṣāṃcakru sudurdarśaṃ pralayānala sannibham ||29||
koṭisūryapratikāśaṃ tejārāśiṃ puro mune |
cintāmāpuḥ parāṃ te tu tejasā hṛtadṛṣṭayaḥ ||30||
tato gagana mārgeṇa tejo madhyādvinirgatāḥ |
kṣudhātṛṣāpariśrāntā niśvasaṃto muhurmuhuḥ ||31||
nindantaśca śapantaśca svātmānaṃ jātasādhvasāḥ |
tatoti karuṇāviṣṭo lokādhyakṣo khilārthavit ||32||
darśayāmāsa tān rūpaṃ manonayana nandanam |
pādāṅgulīnakhaśrībhirjitaraktābjakesaram ||33||
raktāmbara prabhāvāttu jitasandhyārkamaṇḍalam |
kaṭisūtraprabhājālairjitahemādriśekharam ||34||
khaḍgakheṭadhanuḥśaktiśobhicārucaturbhujam |
suvāsaṃ pūrṇimācandra jitakānti mukhāmbujam ||35||
aharniśaṃ prabhāyuktaṃ padmacāru sulocanam |
anekasūryaśobhājinmukuṭabhrājimastakam ||36||
nānātārāṅkita vyoma kāntijiduttarīyakam |
varāhadaṃṣṭrāśobhājidekadanta virājitam ||37||
airāvatādi dikpāla bhayakārisu puṣkaram |
dṛṣṭvaiva sahasā devaṃ praṇemustemudāmune |
pādāmbujamupaspṛśya tatastotraṃ pracakramuḥ ||38||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe gajānanadarśanaṃ nāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||12||

<adhyāya 13 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
vyāsa uvāca ||
pañcāsya caturāsyaśca sahasramastakopi ca |
gajāsyaṃ varadaṃ devaṃ kathamete pratuṣṭhuvuḥ ||1||
brahmovāca ||
prasādonmukha vighneśa kṛpāpāṅga nirīkṣaṇāt |
prāptabuddhi prasādāste keśāstaṃnunuvustadā ||2||
brahmaviṣṇumaheśā ūcuḥ ||
ajaṃ nirvikalpaṃ nirākāramekaṃ nirānandamadvaitamānanda pūrṇam |
paraṃ nirguṇaṃ nirviśeṣaṃ nirīhaṃ parabrahmarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ bhajema ||3||
guṇātītamādyaṃ cidānandarūpaṃ cidābhāsakaṃ sarvagaṃ jñānagamyamam |
munidhyeyamākāśarūpaṃ pareśaṃ parabrahmarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ bhajema ||4||
jagatkāraṇaṃ kāraṇajñānarūpaṃ surādiṃ sukhādiṃ yugādiṃ gaṇeśam |
jagadvyāpinaṃ viśvavandyaṃ sureśaṃ parabrahmarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ bhajema ||5||
rajoyogato brahmarūpaṃ śrutijñaṃ sadākāryasaktaṃ hṛdācintyarūpam |
jagatkārakaṃ sarvavidyānidhānaṃ parabrahmarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ natāsmaḥ ||6||
sadāsattvayogaṃ mudākrīḍamānaṃ surārīnharantaṃ jagatpālayantam |
anekāvatāraṃ nijājñānahāraṃ sadāviṣṇurūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmaḥ ||7||
tamoyoginaṃ rudrarūpaṃ trinetraṃ jagaddhārakaṃ tārakaṃ jñānahetum |
anekāgamaiḥsvaṃ janaṃ bodhayantaṃ sadāśarvarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmaḥ ||8||
tamastomahāraṃ janājñānahāraṃ trayīvedasāraṃ parabrahmapāram |
munijñānakāraṃ vidūrevikāraṃ sadābrahmarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmaḥ ||9||
nijairoṣadhīstarpayantaṃ karoghaiḥ saraughānkalābhiḥ sudhāsrāviṇībhiḥ |
dineśāṃśu santāpahāraṃ dvijeśaṃ śaśāṅkasvarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmaḥ ||10||
prakāśasvarūpaṃ nabhovāyurūpaṃ vikārādihetuṃ kalākālabhutam |
anekakriyānekaśaktisvarūpaṃ sadāśaktirūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmaḥ ||11||
pradhānasvarūpaṃ mahatatvarūpaṃ dharāvārirūpaṃ digīśādirūpam |
asatsatsvarūpaṃ jagaddhetubhūtaṃ sadāviśvarūpaṃ gaṇeśaṃ natāsmaḥ ||12||
tvadīye manaḥsthāpayedaṅghriyugme janovighnasaṅghān napīḍāṃ labheta |
lasatsūryabimbe viśāle sthitoyaṃ janodhvānta pīḍāṃ kathaṃ vā labheta ||13||
vayaṃ bhrāmitāḥ sarvathā jñānayogādalabdhātavāṅghri bahūnvarṣapūgān |
idānīmavāptāstavaiva prasādāt prapannān sadā pāhi viśvaṃbharādya ||14||
brahmovāca ||
evaṃ stuto gaṇeśastu saṃtuṣṭobhūnmahāmune |
kṛpayā parayopetobhidhātuṃ tān pracakrame ||15||
gaṇeśa uvāca ||
yadarthaṃ kleśitā yūyaṃ yadarthamihacāgatāḥ |
prītohamanayāstutyā varaṃmattovṛṇītatam ||16||
kṛtaṃ ca mama yat stotraṃ bhavadbhirbhāvitātmabhiḥ |
stotrarājamitikhyātaṃ bhaviṣyati mamājñayā ||17||
idaṃ yaḥ paṭhet prātarutthāyadhīmāṃ strisandhyaṃ sadā bhaktiyukto viśuddhaḥ |
saputrān śriyaṃ sarvakāmān labheta parabrahmarūpo bhavedantakāle ||18||
brahmovāca ||
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā santuṣṭāste tamabruvan |
tadīkṣayā rajaḥsattvatamoguṇasamudbhavāḥ ||19||
trayaūcuḥ ||
yadi tuṣṭosi deveśa sṛṣṭisaṃhārakāraka |
tavāṅghri kamalenostu bhaktiravyacibhāriṇī ||20||
kiṃ cāsmāṃbhiḥ prakartavyamājñāpayatu no bhavān |
ayameva varosmākaṃ vāñchitodviradānana ||21||
iti śrutvā vacasteṣāṃ punaḥ prāha gajānanaḥ |
bhaviṣyati mahābhāgā mayibhaktirdṛḍhāhivaḥ ||22||
saṃkaṣṭāni yathā yūyaṃ tariṣyatha mahāntyapi |
bhavat khyātyai pravakṣyāmi kāryāṇi ca pṛthak pṛthak ||23||
sṛṣṭikartā bhava brahman rajoguṇa samudbhavaḥ |
pālanaṃ kuru viṣṇotvaṃ vyāpakaḥ sattvasaṃśrayaḥ ||24||
hara saṃhara sarvaṃ tvaṃ tamoguṇa samudbhavaḥ |
brahmovāca ||
vedaśāstrapurāṇāni sṛṣṭisāmarthyameva ca ||25||
dadau gaṇeśonyāṃ vidyāṃ brahmaṇe paramātmane |
viṣṇave bhagavān prādāt yogātsvacchanda rūpatām ||26||
ekākṣaraṃ ṣaḍarṇaṃ ca mantraṃ sarvāgamāṃstathā |
harāya bhagavānprādācchaktiṃ saṃharaṇepi ca ||27||
tatobrahmādīnamanāstrailokyeśaṃ jagadgurum |
uvāca prāñjalirbhūtvā varadaṃ tamibhānanam ||28||
brahmovāca ||
yathāgṛhītaśaktistu vācyāvācyaṃ vivaktina |
tathānekavidhāṃ sṛṣṭimadṛṣṭvā kvāpi kiñcana ||29||
karttuṃ vāpi vibhorājñā vibhedaṃ kathamutsahe |
itaḥ kūpa itovāpītyevaṃ prāptaṃ mayā katham ||30||
tato gajānanaḥ prāha divyaṃ cakṣuḥ pradāyatam |
brahmāṇaṃ vedaśāstrajñaṃ tathā vyākulitaṃ prabhuḥ ||31||
gajānana uvāca ||
bahirantaḥ śarīreme saṃkhyātītāni padmaja |
brahmāṇḍāni tvamadyaiva vibhramanti vilokaya ||32||
brahmovāca ||
tato gajānanenā sau svodare śvāsavāyunā |
nīto dadarśa dhātāpi brahmāṇḍāni tvanekaśaḥ ||
audumbarāṇīvatarau teṣu vā maśakāniva ||33||
bhedayāmāsa tatraikaṃ parameṇa svatejasā |
tadantaḥ sakalāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ dadarśa kamalāsanaḥ ||34||
brahmāṇaṃ cā paraṃ tatra viṣṇumindraṃ prajāpatim |
śaṃkaraṃ bhāṣkaraṃ vāyuṃ vanāni saritombupam ||35||
sāgarān yakṣagandharvānapsaraḥ kinnaroragān |
ṛṣīnpuṇyajanānsādhyānmanuṣyānparvatāndrumān ||36||
udbhijānjārajānjantūn svedajānaṇḍajānapi |
pṛthvīṃ ca saptapātālānyavyayānyekaviṃśatim ||37||
dadarśa viśvaṃ cāhaṃ vai bhāvābhāvaṃ carācaram ||38||
yadyadbrahmā bhedayāmāsa cāṇḍaṃ tasmiṃstasmin sarvamevaṃ dadarśa |
dṛṣṭvā bhrāntiṃ pūrvavatsaṃprapede teṣāmantaṃ sarvathā nādhyagacchat ||39||
na sthātuṃ naiva vā gantuṃ śaśāka kamalāsanaḥ |
upaviśya tato brahmā tuṣṭāvadviradānanam ||40||
brahmovāca ||
vande devaṃ devadevaṃ gaṇeśaṃ brahmaṇḍānāṃ naiva saṃkhyāyadaṃge |
abhroḍunāṃ sāgare vā jhaṣāṇāṃ kaḥ saṃkhyātā śarkarāṇāṃ ca tīre ||41||
lajjā nametrāsti surendravandya pādāravindaṃ tava yadvilokya |
bhrānto bhavaṃ jñānanidhau prasanne mokṣopi tucchaḥ kimutānyavārttā ||42||
dṛṣṭaṃ ca nānārtha yutaṃ sureśa brahmāṇḍakūṭaṃ jaṭhare tvadīye |
sthātuṃ bahirgantumapīhaneśastvadanyadevaṃ śaraṇaṃ navedmi ||43||
tataḥ prasanno bhagavānananto vai gajānanaḥ |
bahirniṣkāsayāmāsa brahmāṇaṃ khinnamānasam ||44||
nāsikārandhra mārgeṇa hariṃ tadanuyāyinam |
tāmasaṃ ca haraṃ devo brahmaṇāsaha saṃgatam ||45||
niḥsārayāmāsa vibhuḥ śrutirandhrādgajānanaḥ |
śayātesmatadaṃgetā bubhauhariharausukham ||46||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe brahmastuti varṇanaṃ nāma trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||13||

<adhyāya 14 prāraṃbhaḥ>

|| atha caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
rājovāca ||
tataḥ kimakarod brahmā vīkṣyāṃḍāni sahasraśaḥ |
kathaṃ ca sṛṣṭimakarodājñāṃ prāpya gajānanāt ||1||
bhṛguruvāca ||
abhavadgarvasaṃpannaḥ svātmanyevaṃ vicārayat |
vedmiśāstrāṇivedāṃśca purāṇānyāgamānapi ||2||
jñānavijñānasaṃpannaḥ śāpānugrahaśaktimān |
brahmāṇḍāni ca dṛṣṭāni sṛṣṭīnāṃ racanāstathā ||3||
idānīṃ sṛṣṭikaraṇe'śakyaṃ me naiva kiñcana |
ityevaṃ garvasampanne sṛṣṭyarthaṃ kamalodbhave ||4||
vighnā nānāvidhā bhūpa prādurāsansahasraśaḥ |
taṃ veṣṭayitvā brahmāṇaṃ sthitāḥ paramadāruṇāḥ ||5||
yathāmattāḥ puṣparasaiḥ saradhā madhujālakam |
trinetrāḥ pañcahastāśca kūpāsyāḥ saptapāṇayaḥ ||6||
tryandhrayaḥ pañcatuṇḍāśca saptatuṇḍāḥ ṣaḍaṅghrayaḥ |
daśāsyāḥ pañcapādāśca tāladantā vṛkodarāḥ ||7||
nānārūpā mahāsatvāḥ saṃkhyātuṃ na ca te kṣamāḥ |
cakampe druhiṇasteṣāṃ nānārāvānniśamyatān ||8||
kecittaṃ muṣṭibhirjaghnurnemuścanunuvuḥ pare |
catuḥśikhāsu saṃgṛhya dolayāmāsurādarāt ||9||
catvāryapi mukhānyasya jahasustaṃ tathāpare |
nininduśca praśaṃsāṃ ca cakruḥ kecinsiṣevire ||10||
mocayāmāsurapare babandhurapare punaḥ |
mocituṃ punarapyanye vicakarṣuritastataḥ ||11||
āliliṃguśca taṃ keciccucumbuḥ śiśuvatpare |
aṣṭaśmaśruṣutaṃ dhṛtvā nanarte koṣṭahastavān ||12||
evaṃ sa paravān brahmā cintāśoka samanvitaḥ |
akharvaṃ sṛṣṭigarvaṃ taṃ tatyāja hṛdisaṃgatam ||13||
jagāma mahatīṃ mūrchāṃ nirāśaḥ svasyajīvane |
muhūrtamātre tu gate sasmāra manasā vibhum ||
prārthayāmāsa kāruṇyādrudanniva gajānanam ||14||
brahmovāca ||
na cāyuṣyaṃ svalpaṃ vividha janane sakta manaso |
na me tattvajñānaṃ bhavajaladhitāraṃ suvimalam |
janurbhūmau labdhvā tava bhajanato yāmi paramāṃ |
kadā bhuktiṃ muktiṃ nirupama sukhāṃ vā khilaguro |15||
tvatkaṭākṣāmṛtenākto bhaktaḥ sīdati te vibho |
iyaṃ lajjātavaivāstāṃ na me mṛtyuścirāyuṣaḥ ||16||
bhṛguruvāca ||
ityasau prārthayanneva śuśrāva gaganeritām |
giraṃ tapasveti tataḥ prārthayāmāsa tāṃ punaḥ ||17||
antarhitāśca te vighnāḥ śrutāyāṃ nabhasogiri |
nānārūpā mahāvīryā muktvā taṃ kamalāsanam ||18||
sa muktaścintayāmāsa padmayonirmahāyaśāḥ |
vināmantraṃ vināsthānaṃ kathaṃ tapsye tapo mahat ||19||
iti vyākula cittosau babhrāma jalamadhyataḥ |
ananya manasā dhyāyan gajānanamanāmayam ||20||
mukuṭena virājantaṃ muktāratnayujā śubham |
raktacandana liptāṅgaṃ sindūrāruṇamastakam ||21||
muktādāmalasatkaṇṭhaṃ sarpayajñopavītinam |
anarghya ratnaghaṭita bāhubhūṣaṇabhūṣitam ||22||
sphuranmarakatabhrājadaṃgulīyaka śobhitam |
mahā hi veṣṭita bṛhannābhiśobhimahodaram ||23||
vicitra ratnakhacita kaṭisūtra virājitam |
suvarṇasūtra vilasadraktavastrasamāratam ||24||
bhālacandraṃ lasaddantanta śobhārājatkaraṃ param |
evaṃ dhyāyati tasmiṃstu punareva nabho vacaḥ ||25||
udiyāya vaṭaṃ paśya vaṭaṃ paśyeti sundaram |
śrutveti vacanaṃ brahmā punaścintāmavāpa saḥ ||26||
|| iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṇḍe brahmacintāvarṇanaṃ nāma caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||14||

<adhyāya 15 prāraṃbhaḥ>

bhṛguruvāca ॥
vyāsāyākathayaccāgre brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ |
somakāṃta pravakṣyāmi kathāṃ śṛṇuta sādaram ॥1॥
brahmovāca ॥
tato'haṃ sumahatsvapnaṃ dadarśa munisattama|
bhramatā bhramatā vyoma jale dṛṣṭo vaṭo mahān ॥2॥
mahāvātātapādbhistu naṣṭe sthāvarajaṃgame |
avaśiṣṭaḥ kathamayameka eva vaṭo mahān ॥3॥
evaṃ saṃśayamāpannastat patre bālakaṃ laghum |
caturbāhuṃ samukuṭaṃ kuṃḍalābhyāṃ virājitam ॥4॥
maṇimuktāmayaṃ dāma bibhratkaṃṭhe suśobhitam |
ardhacandra raktavastraṃ kaṭisūtraṃ tathaiva ca ॥5॥
ekadantaṃ nakhapurgajāsyaṃ tejasā jvalat |
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ tarkayāmāsa bālakaṃ kathamatra vai॥6॥
puṣkareṇa ca tadbālaṃ jalaṃ manmastake'kṣipat |
tato'hamājahāsoccaiściṃtānaṃda samanvitaḥ ॥7॥
mayyā hasati bālaḥ sa uttatāra vaṭāttataḥ |
sa mamāṃkaṃ samāgatya maṃjuvākyaṃ jagāda ha॥8॥
bāla uvāca ॥
laghorlaghutaro'si tvaṃ vṛthā vṛddho'pi mūḍhadhīḥ |
vighnairabhihato'si tvaṃ sṛṣṭiciṃtā samāvṛtaḥ ॥9॥
tapaściṃtā paro nityaṃ bibhramaṃ jalamadhyagaḥ |
sarvaṃ ciṃtāharaṃ te'hamupāyaṃ caturānana ॥10॥
upādiśāmi te maṃtramekākṣaramimaṃ mama |
puraścaraṇa mārgeṇa daśalakṣaṃ japasva tam ॥11॥
tataḥ pratyakṣatāṃ yāsye dāsye sāmarthyamuttamam |
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ svapnamāścaryaṃ pratibuddho'bhavaṃ hyaham ॥12॥
śuśoca ca kadā me syāddarśanaṃ parameśituḥ |
dṛṣṭasvapno'bhavaṃ magna ānaṃdamaya sāgare ॥13॥
tataḥ snātvā paraṃ maṃtraṃ jajāpa bahuvāsaram |
ekapādena kamale tiṣṭhan dhyāyan gajānanam ॥14॥
jitendriyo jitāhāraḥ kāṣṭhapāṣāṇavat sthiraḥ |
divyavarṣasahasraṃ sa tapastepe paraṃ mahat ॥15॥
tato me mukhato jvālāḥ prādurāsan durāsadāḥ |
tābhiḥ sarvāṇi bhūtāni pīḍāmāpuḥ sudāruṇām ॥16॥
tato gajānano dṛṣṭvā tāṃ niṣṭhāṃ mama sudṛḍhām |
tutoṣa parayā bhaktyā prādurāsīttataḥ puraḥ ॥17॥
koṭi sūrya samāna śrīrjvālāmālīva havyabhuk |
dahanniva trilokīṃ sa saṃharanniva rodasī ॥18॥
paraśukamaladhārī divya māyā vibhūṣaḥ |
sakala duritahārī sarva saundarya kośaḥ |
karivara mukha śobhī bhaktavāṃchā prapoṣaḥ |
sura manuja munīnāṃ sarva vighnaika nāśaḥ ॥19॥
tejorāśimahaṃ dṛṣṭvā cakampe vyāsa sanmune |
ciṃtāmavāpa paramāṃ vyagracitto japāccyutaḥ ॥20॥
ācchāditānīkṣaṇāni smṛtiśca vilayaṃ gatā |
dṛṣṭvedṛśīmavasthāṃ tāṃ śīghraṃ prāhātha vighnarāṭ ॥21॥
gaṇeśa uvāca ॥
na bhayaṃ kuru lokeśa sa evāhaṃ samāgataḥ |
yena te mantra ādeśi svapna ekākṣaraḥ śubhaḥ ॥22॥
tena siddhiranuprāptā varaṃ dātuṃ samāgataḥ |
saumyabhāvaprapanno'smi varaṃ varaya suvrata ॥23॥
dāsyāmi te prasanno'haṃ yadyatte hṛdi vartate |
mayi prasanne sarvaṃ tad bhavitā nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥24॥
muniruvāca ॥
itthaṃ niśamya gaṇanātha vaco viśuddhaṃ brahmā jaharṣa paramaprasamīkṣamāṇaḥ |
natvā carācaragurūṃ sakalaiḥ śirobhiḥ prāha prasannahṛdayaḥ saphalaṃ janurme ॥25॥
brahmovāca ॥
yo vedānāṃ saśāstrāṇāṃ jñānināṃ yogināmapi |
sarvopaniṣadāṃ caiva gocaro na kadācana ॥26॥
sa puṇyanicayairme'dya yātaḥ pratyakṣatāṃ vibhuḥ |
anādi nidhano'naṃto'prameyo nirguṇo'pi ca ॥27॥
yadi prasanno deveśa vighneśa karuṇākara |
tava bhaktiṃ dṛḍhāṃ dehi yayā duḥkha na naḥ spṛśet ॥28॥
idānīṃ dehi sāmarthyaṃ nānā nirmāṇane mama |
vighnāni ca śamaṃ yāntu yadi tuṣṭo'si me prabho ॥29॥
smṛtamātraḥ sadā me tvaṃ sarvaṃ kāryaṃ samāpaya |
ante muktiṃ sthirāṃ dehi jñānaṃ ca vimalaṃ mama ॥30॥
gajānana uvāca ॥
evamastu kuruṣva tvaṃ sṛṣṭiṃ nānāvidhāṃ bahuma |
māṃ smṛtvā sarva vighnāni nāśaṃ yāsyanti sarvataḥ ॥31॥
dṛḍhā bhaktiḥ śubhaṃ jñānaṃ matprasādā dbhaviṣyati |
niḥśaṃkaṃ kuru kāryāṇi sarvāṇi caturānana ॥32॥
munirūvāca ॥
evaṃ labdhavaro brahmā pūjayāmāsa taṃ vibhum |
yadyaccite 'ciṃtayat sa tattadevopatiṣṭhate ॥33॥
pūjārthaṃ devadevasya gaṇeśasya prasādataḥ |
dakṣiṇāvasare dve tu kanyake samupasthite ॥34॥
cāruprasanna nayana vadanaiḥ suvirājate|
aneka ratnakhacita nānālaṃkāra śobhite ॥35॥
divyagandhayute divya vastra mālā vibhūṣate |
te tasmai dakṣiṇārthaṃ sa kalpayāmāsa padmabhūḥ ॥36॥
raṃbhāgarbheṇa nīrājyaṃ divyapuṣpāṃjaliṃ dadau |
sahasranāmabhiḥ stutvā pradakṣiṇamathākarota ॥37॥
namasya prārthayāmāsa dīnānāṃ śaṃkaro bhava |
evaṃ saṃpūjitastena brahmaṇā parameṣṭhinā ॥38॥
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān vighnahartā gajānanaḥ |
siddhibuddhī gṛhītvā te antardhānamagādvibhuḥ ॥39॥
kastataḥ kārayāmāsa sṛṣṭiṃ pūrvavadāyatām |
ājñayā parameśasya prasādācca prasannadhīḥ ॥40॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe gajānanapūjānirūpaṇaṃnāma paṃcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥15॥

<adhyāya 16 prāraṃbhaḥ>

rājovāca ॥
śrutvā kathāṃ gaṇeśasya harṣaścetasi jāyate |
punaśca vada viprarṣe na tapyāmi kathāmṛtam ॥1॥
antarhite bhagavati gaṇeśe paramātmani |
kathaṃ vinirmame sṛṣṭiṃ brahmā tadvarṇaya prabho ॥2॥
yaccāgre kathayāmāsa vyāsāyākhyānakaṃ vidhiḥ |
tadahaṃ vistareṇaiva śrotumicchāmi sanmune ॥3॥
bhṛguruvāca ॥
siddhakṣetrasya māhātmyaṃ vyāsāyākathayatprabhuḥ |
ānupūrvyeṇa tatte'haṃ kathayiṣye'sṛjacca yat ॥4॥
sa brahmā nirmame pūrvaṃ manasā sapta putrakān |
āha tān sṛṣṭisāhāyyaṃ sṛjadhvaṃ svasvabuddhitaḥ ॥5॥
te tu tadvacanaṃ śrutvā tapase kṛta niścayāḥ |
tapastaptvā suvipulaṃ paraṃ brahma samāgatāḥ ॥6॥
tato'nyān kalpayāmāsa saptaputrān prajāpatiḥ |
te'tyanta jñānasaṃpannā na cakruḥ sṛṣṭimuttamām ॥7॥
mattaḥ svayaṃ samārebhe sanakādīn vilokya tān |
mukhato brāhmaṇānagnimasṛjat kamalāsanaḥ ॥8॥
bāhurupādato'nyāṃ strīn varṇāścandramasaṃ hradaḥ |
cakṣubhyāṃ ca raviṃ śrotrān mārutaṃ prāṇameva ca ॥9॥
antarikṣaṃ tathā nābheḥ śiraso divameva ca |
bhūmiṃ padbhyāṃ diśaḥ śrotrādanyām̐llokānakalpayat ॥10॥
anyaduccāvacaṃ viśvaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṃgamaṃ ca yat |
samudrān saritaḥ śailāṃ tṛṇa gulma mahīruhān ॥11॥
tataḥ katipayāhaḥ su gateṣu munisattamāḥ |
nidritasya mahāviṣṇoḥ śrotrājjātau mahāsurau ॥12॥
madhu kaiṭabha nāmānau vikhyāto bhuvanatraye|
daṃṣṭrākarālavadanau piṃgākṣau dīrghanāsikau ॥13॥
mahākāyau mahāsārāvatyuccau parvatāviva |
varṣā mudira śabdau tau garjantau bhṛśagarvitau ॥14॥
vacobhirbahubhirduṣṭo dhikkāraṃ tasya cakratuḥ|
viprān devān ṛṣīn sādhūn śāstrāṇi ca ninindatuḥ ॥15॥
tayoḥ śabdena dharaṇī cakampe śeṣa eva ca |
udvignabhāsit sakalaṃ brahmāṃḍaṃ svanastayoḥ ॥16॥
tatastau krodha raktākṣau kaṃ taṃ khāditumudyatau|
tato'sau varadāṃ devīṃ viṣṇu mohanakāriṇīm ॥17॥
viṣṇunetragatāṃ nidrāṃ tuṣṭāva kamalāsanaḥ |
madhukaiṭabhanāśāya prabodhāya harerapi ॥18॥
gajānana prasādena viṣṇu hastāttayorvadham |
jñātvā samyag vicāryaiva ciṃtā harṣa samanvitaḥ ॥19॥
brahmovāca ॥
svāhā svadhā rūpadharā sudhā tvaṃ mātrārdhaṃ mātrā svarūpiṇī ca |
kartrī ca hartrī jananī janasya sato'sataḥ śaktirasi tvameva ॥20॥
śrutiḥ svarā kālarātriranādi nidhanā kṣapā|
jaganmātā jagaddhātrī sṛṣṭi sthityaṃtakāriṇī ॥21॥
sāvitrī ca tathā saṃdhyā mahāmāyā tṛṣā kṣudhā |
sarveṣāṃ vastujātānāṃ śaktistvamasi pārvati ॥22॥
trailokyakartā tvannātho daityadānavasūdanaḥ |
nidrayā vyāptalittau'sau jñānavijñānavānhariḥ ॥23॥
jagadutpādyate yena pālyate hiyatepi ca |
so'pi tvayā'vatārāṇāṃ saṃkaṭe viniyojyate ॥24॥
duṣṭātmānau mohayaitau tvaṃ daityau madhukaiṭabhau |
hantumetau durādharṣau jñānamasya pradīyatām ॥25॥
ahamārādhitaścābhyāṃ pūrvajanman yatadritam |
varān bahuvidhān prādāmavadhyau mama tāvubhau ॥26॥
tayoruccāvacāṃ śabdā tato'hamasahaṃ bahu|
māmeva haṃtukāmau tau stutau nānāvidhai stavaiḥ ॥27॥
tathāpi madvadhāttau na nivṛttau duṣṭabhāvataḥ |
atastvāṃ prārthaye devi viṣṇu bodhana hetave ॥28॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe devīprārthanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥16॥

<adhyāya 17 prāraṃbhaḥ>

yāvattiṣṭhate viṣṇustāvattābhyāṃ triviṣṭapam |
ākrāṃtamindrasadanam yāmyaṃ kauberameva ca ॥1॥
tau dṛṣṭvā sarvato devāḥ palāyanaparā babhuḥ |
nipeturbabhramuḥ kecin mumūrcchuśca skhaluḥ pare ॥2॥
tato devyā vinirmukto nidrayā harirīśvaraḥ |
āśvāsya sarvaṃ devāṃstāṃ stāṃbhyāṃ yuddhaṃ cakāra ha ॥3॥
ākramaṃ sarvadevānāṃ kṛtaṃ tābhyāṃ nivāritum |
śeṣādi sa vai nāgānāṃ munīnāṃ yakṣarakṣasām ॥4॥
śuśubhe sa haristatra śaṃkhacakragadādharaḥ|
kirīṭa kuṃḍaladharo nīlamegha ghanacchaviḥ ॥5॥
tataḥ sa bhagavān śaṃkhaṃ pradadhmau bhṛśa vegavān |
tena śabdena mahatā kṣobhayāmāsa rodasī ॥6॥
pāṃcajanyasvanaṃ śrutvā bibhidai hṛdayaṃ tayoḥ |
tatastau bhayasaṃvignau parasparamathocatuḥ ॥7॥
bhūmaṃḍalaṃ ca pātālaṃ svargāṇāmekaviṃśatiḥ |
ākrāntāḥ samyagāvābhyāṃ tadā'yaṃ na śrutaḥ svanaḥ ॥8॥
vajrasāramayaṃ yena cakaṃpe hadayaṃ dvayoḥ |
tasmādanena yoddhavyaṃ puruṣeṇa balīyasā ॥9॥
raṇakaṃḍūti śāṃtyarthaṃ vijayāyetarāya vā |
ripumenaṃ haniṣyāvo gacchāvo vā punarbhavam ॥10॥
evaṃ tau niścayaṃ kṛtvā yuyutsuṃ harimūcatuḥ |
raṇakaṃḍūti śāṃtyarthaṃ dṛṣṭo'si purūṣottama ॥11॥
kathamuttamatāṃ yāsi āvayordṛṣṭigocaraḥ |
muniruvāca ॥
iti vākyaṃ tayoḥ śrutvā jagāda viṣṭaraśravāḥ ॥12॥
hariruvāca ॥
samyaguktaṃ mahādaityau yatheṣṭaṃ yudhyataṃ mayā|
na hi kāmayate kaścinmaraṇaṃ svayamā manaḥ ॥13॥
tāvūcatuḥ |
caturbhujo'si deveśa bāhuyuddhaṃ dadasva nau |
muniruvāca ॥
evamukto haristābhyāṃ tathetyāha mudānvitaḥ ॥14॥
tyaktvāyudhāni yuyudhe dvābhyāmekaccaturbhujaḥ |
jaghnatustau śiro mūrdhnā jaṃghābhiratha jaṃghayoḥ |
kūparo kūparaiḥ svīyairbāhu bāhubhireva ca ॥15॥
gulphau gulphaiḥ kaṭiṃ tābhyāṃ nāsikābhyāṃ ca nāsikām |
muṣṭibhyāṃ muṣṭideśaṃ ca pṛṣṭhābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhameva ca ॥16॥
āsphoṭanavikarṣābhyāṃ bāhubhirmaṃḍalairapi |
evaṃ parasparaṃ yuddhaṃ prāpartata ciraṃ tadā ॥17॥
sahasraṃ paṃcaguṇitamatikrāntaṃ mahāmune|
varṣāṇāṃ na tu tau jetuṃ śaśāka harirīśvaraḥ|
tato dadhāra rūpaṃ sa gāṃdharvaṃ gītakovidam ॥18॥
gatvā vanāṃtaraṃ cārū vīṇāgānaṃ cakāraḥ saḥ |
hariṇāḥ śvāpadā lokā deva gandharva rākṣasāḥ ॥19॥
sva sva vyāpārarahitā sarve tatparatāṃ yayuḥ |
ālāpaṃ tasya giriśaḥ kailāse śrutavānmuhuḥ ॥20॥
nikuṃbhaṃ puṣpadaṃtaṃ ca jagāda bhaganetrahā |
etamānayataṃ śīghraṃ yo'sau gāyati kānane ॥21॥
tau gatvā darśanaṃ tasya gṛhītvā vai tamūcatuḥ |
tava gītasvanaṃ śrutvā śaṃkaro harṣanirbharaḥ ॥22॥
tvāṃ samāhvayate devo gānaṃ śruṃtuṃ tavānagha |
āvābhyāṃ saha yāhi tvaṃ śīghra yāma tadaṃtikam ॥23॥
tayorvākyamiti śrutvā gaṃdharvo haribhaktimān |
tābhyāṃ saha yayau tatra yatra devo maheśvaraḥ ॥24॥
dadarśa pārvatīkāṃtaṃ caṃdrārdhakṛtaśekharam |
gajacarma parīdhānaṃ ruṃḍamālā vibhūṣitam ॥25॥
rājat piṃgaṭajaṭābhāraṃ sarpayajñopavītinam |
nanāma bhuvi viśveśaṃ praṇatārtivināśanam ॥26॥
utthāpya giriśaḥ svena pāṇinā tadadhokṣajam |
āsanaṃ ca dadau tasmai pūjayāmāsa śaṃkaraḥ ॥27॥
tato jagāda sa hariradya me saphalaṃ januḥ |
yato'dya darśanaṃ te'bhūddharmakāmārthamokṣadam ॥28॥
toṣayāmāsa taṃ devaṃ gaṃdharvo gānatatparam |
vīṇāravaiḥ kalapadairālāpairvividhairapi ॥29॥
skaṃdaṃ gaṇeśvaraṃ devīṃ pārvatīṃ ca surānṛṣīn |
tato maheśvaraḥ prītyā liliṃga prakaṭaṃ harim ॥30॥
śaṃkha cakra gadā padma pītāṃbaradharaṃ śubham |
uvāca ca hare matto vṛṇu kāmānaśeṣataḥ ॥31॥
dāsyāmi tava gānena parā mudamupāgataḥ |
bhṛguruvāca ॥
tata ūce svabhūstaṃ tu vṛttāntaṃ daityayoḥ param ॥32॥
hariruvāca ॥
mayi kṣīrābdhiśayane nidrite madhukaiṭabhau |
utpannau karṇamalato brahmāṇaṃ bhakṣituṃ gatau ॥33॥
tena nidrā stutā bharga tayā'haṃ pratibodhitaḥ |
yuddhaṃ ca kṛtavāṃstābhyāṃ malla līlāmupāgataḥ ॥34॥
nāsmi śaktāviletuṃ tau tata etatkṛtaṃ mayā |
idānīṃ tadvadhopāyaṃ vada me karuṇānidhe ॥35॥
bharga uvāca ||
vināyakamanarcyaiva gato'si raṇabhūmikām |
śaktihīnaśca tenāsi subhṛśaṃ kleśavānapi ॥36॥
gaṇeśaṃ pūjayitvaiva vraja yuddhāya māriṣa |
sa ca tau māyayā mohya vaśatāṃ prāpayiṣyati ॥37॥
matprasādena duṣṭau tau vadhiṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ |
hariruvātra ||
kathaṃ vināyakaṃ devamupāse bharga tadvada ॥38॥
īśvara uvāca ॥
uktā gaṇeśvarasyaiva maṃtraṇāṃ saptakoṭayaḥ |
tatrāpi ca mahāmaṃtrāsteṣvapyekākṣaro mahān ॥39॥
ṣaḍakṣaraśca bhagavaṃ stayorekaṃ vadāmi te |
tata ekākṣaraṃ tyaktvā siddhāricakrayogataḥ ॥40॥
ṛṇaṃ dhanaṃ śodhayitvā taṃ jagāda ṣaḍakṣaram |
mahāmaṃtraṃ gaṇeśasya sarvasiddhipradaṃ śubham ॥41॥
asyānuṣṭhānamātreṇa kāryaṃ te siddhimeṣyati |
tato jagāma sa hariranuṣṭhānāya satvaraḥ ॥42॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe maṃtropadeśonāma saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||17॥

<adhyāya 18 prāraṃbhaḥ>

somakāṃta uvāca ॥
kathaṃ jajāpa hariḥ kutra vā maṃtramuttamam |
siddhiṃ cāvāpa sa kathaṃ vada ne tat savistaram ॥1॥
bhṛguruvāca ॥
siddhikṣetramiti khyātaṃ bhuvi siddhikaraṃ param |
tatra gatvā mahāviṣṇustatāpa paramaṃ tapaḥ ॥2॥
dhyāyan vināyakaṃ devaṃ ṣaḍakṣaravidhānataḥ |
ārādhayāmāsa tadā jitvā khāni prayatnataḥ ॥3॥
bāṇāstreṇa tu kṛtvaiva pūrvaṃ digbaṃdhamādṛtaḥ |
bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāyaiva prāṇānāṃ sthāpanaṃ tathā ॥4॥
kṛtvāṃtarmātṛkā nyāsamādhārādi krameṇa tu |
bahiśca mātṛkā nyāsaṃ mastakādi krameṇa ca ॥5॥
prāṇānāyamya mūlena dhyātvā devaṃ gajānanam |
āvāhanādi mudrābhiḥ pūjayitvā manomayaiḥ ॥॥
dravyairnānāvidhaiścaiva ṣoḍaśaiścaupacārakaiḥ |
jajāpa paramaṃ maṃtraṃ viṣṇuryogeśvareśvaraḥ ॥7॥
gate varṣaśate kāle paramātmā gaṇādhipaḥ |
pratyakṣatāṃ yayau tasya koṭisūryāgnisannibhaḥ ॥8॥
ati prasannahṛdayo babhāṣe garuḍadhvajam |
yācasva tvaṃ varānmatto yāṃstvaṃ kāmayase hare ॥9॥
dadāmi tānahaṃ sarvāṃstapasā'nena toṣitaḥ |
pūrvamevārcitaḥ syāṃ cedvijayaste dhruvaṃ bhavet ||10॥
hariruvāca ॥
brahmeśānāvindramukhyāśca devāṃ yaṃ tvāṃ draṣṭuṃ naiva śaktāstapobhiḥ |
taṃ tvāṃ nānārūpamekaskarūpaṃ paśye vyaktāvyakta rūpaṃ gaṇeśam ||11॥
tvaṃ yo'ṇubhyo'ṇu svarūpo mahadbhyo vyomā dibhyastvaṃ mahānsatvarūpaḥ |
sṛṣṭiṃ cāntaṃ pālanaṃ tvaṃ karoṣi vāraṃvāraṃ prāṇināṃ daivayogāt ॥12॥
sarvasyātmā sarvagaṃ sarvaśaktiḥ sarvavyāpī sarvakartā pareśaḥ |
sarva draṣṭā saṃhārakartā pātā dhātā viśvanetā pitā'pi ॥13॥
etādṛśasya te deva darśanānmama siddhayaḥ |
saṃbhaviṣyanti sarvatra tathā'pyekaṃ vadāmi te ॥14॥
mamaiva yoganidrānte śrutermalasamudbhavau|
madhukaiṭabhau mahāsatvau kaṃ khāditumudyatau ||15||
tābhyāmahaṃ tato yuddhaṃ kṛtavān bahuvatsaram |
tataḥ kṣīṇabalaṃ svā'haṃ śaraṇaṃ samupāgataḥ |
ato yathā tayormatto vadhaḥ syāttadvicāryatām ॥16॥
anyeṣāmapi daityānāṃ jayena yaśa uttamam |
dehi me parameśāna bhaktiṃ te hyanapāyinīm ॥17॥
yayā me kīrtiratulāṃ trailokyaṃ pāvayiṣyati |
gaṇeśa uvāca ॥
yadyatte prārthitaṃ viṣṇo tattatte bhavitā dhruvam ॥18॥
yaśo balaṃ parā kīrtiravighnaśca bhaniṣyati |
muniruvāca ॥
evamuktvā mahāviṣṇuṃ tatraivāntardadhe vibhuḥ ॥19॥
tata ānaṃdapūrṇo'sau mene tāvasurau jitau|
prāsādaṃ nirmame tatra sphāṭikaṃ bhūri ratnakam ॥20॥
lasatkāṃcana śikharaṃ catuḥdvāraṃ suśobhanam |
pratimāṃ sthāpayāmāsa gaṃḍakīyopalaiḥ kṛtām ॥21॥
devāśca munayaḥ siddhirvināyaka iti prathām |
cakruratra yataḥ siddhiḥ prāpteyaṃ hariṇā śubhā ॥22॥
siddhakṣetraṃ tatastattu paprathe bhuvi sarvaśaḥ |
tato jagāma sa hariryatra tau madhukaiṭabhau ॥23॥
dṛṣṭvā tau harimāyāṃtaṃ jahasaturninindatuḥ |
meghaśyāmaṃ mukhaṃ te'dya darśitaṃ nau kutaḥ punaḥ ॥24॥
āvāṃ tu te mahāmuktiṃ dāsyāvo'taḥ punaḥ kila |
laghutāṃ ca prayāto'si kimarthaṃ raṇamāgataḥ ॥25॥
hariruvāca ॥
sahasā dahate sarvaṃ laghureva hutāśanaḥ |
laghudīpo mahadrātrau yathā saṃharate tamaḥ ॥26॥
tathā vāmahamadyaiva śakto nāśāya durmadau |
muniruvāca |
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā'ti kruddhau madhukaiṭabhau ॥27॥
sahasā jaghnaturviṣṇuṃ muṣṭibhyāṃ hṛdaye bhṛśam |
tataḥ punarmallayuddhaṃ tayostasya vyavardhata ॥28॥
yuddhvā bahudinaṃ tābhyāṃ varaṃ dātuṃ sumutsukaḥ |
uvāca ślakṣṇayā vācā haristau madhukaiṭabhau ॥29॥
mama prahārānhi yuvāṃ sahārtha bahulāḥ samāḥ |
yuvayoḥ puruṣārthena prīto'haṃ daityapuṃgavau ॥30॥
yuvayoḥ sadṛśau naiva saṃbhūtau na bhaviṣyataḥ|
tāvūcatuḥ |
asmattastvaṃ varān brūhi dāsyāvastānhare bahūn ॥31॥
āvāṃ hi tava yuddhena saṃtuṣṭau ca bhṛśaṃ tvayi |
muniruvāca॥ tayorvacanamākarṇya māyāmohitayorhariḥ ॥32॥
śrutvā babhāṇa saityau me varāndātuṃ samudyatau |
tadā me vadhyatāṃ yātaṃ vara eṣa vṛto mayā ॥33॥
tadā sarvaṃ jalamayaṃ dṛṣṭvā tau madhukaiṭabhau|
ūcatuḥ paramaprītau tava hastānmṛtiḥ śubhā ॥34॥
ante tvaciṃtanātsadyo muktiṃ yānti sanātanīm |
yatra norvī jalamayī tatra nau jahi mādhava ॥35॥
sarvaṃ tyajāvo no satyaṃ satye sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhitam |
muniruvāca ॥
tayoritthaṃ vacaḥ śrutvā jaghane tau dadhāra ha ॥36॥
cakreṇa kṣuradhāreṇa ciccheda śirasī tayoḥ |
tato devā mumudire vavarṣuḥ kusumāni ca ॥37॥
gandharvā nanṛtuḥ sarve jagurapsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ |
tatastu viṣṇurabhyetya brahmāṇaṃ parameṣṭhinam ॥38॥
kathayāmāsa vṛttāttaṃ sarvaṃ harṣavinirbharaḥ |
hariruvāca ॥
yadā'śakto vijetuṃ tau tadāhaṃ giriśaṃ gataḥ ॥39॥
śaṃkareṇopadiṣṭo'haṃ ṣaḍarṇamantramuttamam |
ārādhayamahaṃ tena devaṃ vighneśvaraṃ vibhum ॥40॥
tena dattā varā mahayaṃ nānā kāmaphalapradāḥ |
tatprabhāvān mayā duṣṭau nihatau madhukaiṭabhau ॥41॥
stutaḥ saṃpūjitaḥ so'tha tadaivāntardadhe vibhuḥ |
vijñāto mahimā tasya gaṇeśasya mahātmanaḥ ॥42॥
śaṃkarasya prasādena haniṣye daitya dānavān |
devāśca munayaḥ sarve stutvā devaṃ gajānanam ॥43॥
brahmāṇaṃ śakaraṃ māṃ ca yayuḥ svaṃ svaṃ niketanam |
ya idaṃ śṛṇuyānnityaṃ māhātmyaṃ pāpanāśanam ॥44॥
kasmādapi bhayaṃ na syāllabhate sārvakāmikam ॥45॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe siddhakṣetrotpattikathanaṃnāmāṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥18॥

<adhyāya 19 prāraṃbhaḥ>

bhṛguruvāca ॥
śrutvākhyānamidaṃ vyāsaḥ ṣaḍarṇavidhimapyatha |
punarūce vidhātāraṃ nāti pūrṇāśayo yathā ॥1॥
vyāsa uvāca ॥
siddhakṣetrasya māhātmyaṃ gaṇanāthasya caiva hi|
śrutaṃ pāpaharaṃ sarvaṃ kāmadaṃ puṇyavarddhanam ॥2॥
punaśca vada me brahman vināyaka kathānakam |
tṛptiṃ na yāti paramāṃ pibannapi kathāmṛtam ॥3॥
brahmovāca ॥
pārāśarya mahākhyānaṃ kathayāmi tavāgrataḥ |
sarvadevādhidevasya gaṇeśasya vibhoḥ śubham ॥4॥
vidarbhadeśe rājā'bhūddānaśūro mahābalī |
nāmnā bhīma iti khyāto dātā bhīma parākramī ॥5॥
kauṃḍiṇyanagare tasya nivāso'bhūnmahāmate |
sāmantāḥ karadā yasya karadā itare nṛpāḥ |
vājināṃ vāraṇānāṃ ca pattīnāṃ koṭayo daśa ॥6॥
rathānāmagrato yāntaṃ yānti tatpṛṣṭato'pi ca |
brāhmaṇānāṃ sahasrāṇi yamāśritya mudaṃ yayuḥ |
tasya bhāryā mahābhāgā nāmnā'bhūccāruhāsinī ॥7॥
vikasat padmavadanā mṛgaśāvākṣilocanā |
brahmaṇyā daivataparā nityaṃ dharmaparāyaṇā |
pativratā patiprāṇā pativākyaratā sadā ॥8॥
aputrā daivayogena jātā sā varavarṇinī|
tāṃ nirīkṣya tadā bhīmaścārusarvāṃgaśobhinīm ॥9॥
avadadduḥkhito vākyaṃ putrahīno nṛpottamaḥ |
sarvaṃ rājyaṃ parityajya gantavyaṃ vanamuttamam ॥10॥
aputrasya gatirnaiva svargo vā sukhameva ca |
devā havyaṃ na gṛhṇanti pitaraḥ kavyameva ca ॥11॥
vṛthā me jananī janma pitā gehaṃ dhanaṃ kulam |
viphalaṃ sakalaṃ karma vinā putreṇa niścitam ॥12॥
sa eva niścayaṃ kṛtvā dvāmātyau samāhvayat |
manoraṃjana nāmaikaḥ sumanturnāmako'paraḥ ॥13॥
ānvīkṣikīṃ trayīṃ vārtāṃ jānatau ṣoḍaśīḥ kalāḥ|
āgatya tatkṣaṇādeva nṛpaṃ taṃ nematurmune ॥14॥
tāvuvāca tato bhīmo rājyaṃ me paripālyatām |
mama vā mama patnyā vā duritaṃ pūrvajanmajam ॥15॥
tena nau nāsti santānamubhayaṃtra sukhāvaham |
yadyahaṃ punarāgantā tadā tanmana dīyatām ॥16॥
no cedubhābhyāṃ me rājyaṃ vibhajya parigṛhyatām |
evaṃ niścitya sa nṛpaḥ svastivācanapūrvakam ॥17॥
kṛtvā dānāni bahuśo brāhmaṇebhyo yayau purāt |
sapatnīko nṛpavaro'mātyābhyāṃ saha nāgaraiḥ ॥18॥
gavyūtimātraṃ gatvā tu visasarjākhilāṃstadā |
amātyāvūcatu rājannāvāṃ yāvaḥ sahaiva te ॥19॥
suhrado nāgarāścainaṃ rurudurbhṛśaduḥkhitāḥ |
tānuvāca na bhīḥ kāryā'mātyau vo'dhipatī kṛtau ॥20॥
yathā'haṃ vastathaivaitau pālanaṃ sa kariṣyataḥ |
iti tān samanuśvāsya tāvuvāca punarnṛpaḥ ॥21॥
dattaṃ rājyaṃ bhavadbhyāṃ me rakṣataṃ sarvathā puram |
iti sarvān visṛjyaiva sapatnīko yayau purāt ॥22॥
bhramamāṇo dadarśaikaṃ kāsāraṃ kamalairyutam |
puṣpitaṃ drumasaṃśobhi nānā jalacarānvitam ॥23॥
tato'sya nikaṭe ramyamāśramaṃ paśyataḥ śubham |
rājā ca rājapatnī ca sarvānandavivardhanam ॥24॥
yatra vairaṃ na cakruste jātivairā gajādayaḥ |
siṃhāśca nakulāḥ sarpā viḍālā mūṣakādayaḥ ॥25॥
dadarśatustatra muniṃ viśvāmitraṃ kuśāsane |
śiṣyaiḥ parivṛtaṃ śāntaṃ vedādhyayanatatparaiḥ ॥26॥
praṇipatya mahātmānaṃ kṛtāṃjalipuṭāvubhau |
punaḥpunarnamaskārān pādau dhṛtvā pracakratuḥ ॥27॥
utthāpya muniśārdūlo viśvāmitrastaponidhiḥ |
uvāca ślakṣṇayā vācā vijñātārtho nṛpaṃ tadā ॥28॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
bhavitā te suto rājan guṇayukto sahāyaśāḥ |
kutastvamāgataḥ kiṃ te nagaraṃ nāma tadvada ॥29॥
tataste pāpanāśārthaṃ yatiṣye nṛpasattama |
bhīma uvāca |
vidarbha viṣaye svāmin kauṃḍinyaṃ nagaraṃ mama ॥30॥
bhīma ityeva nāma patnīyaṃ cāruhāsinī |
putrārthaṃ ca kṛto yatnastapo dānavratādibhiḥ ॥31॥
nāgatā karuṇā daive pūrvajanmakṛtādaghāt |
tyaktvā rājyaṃ vane yātau dṛṣṭvau tvaccaraṇau mune ॥32॥
bhraman vanāni bahuśo daivenehopasāditaḥ |
sādhūnāṃ saṃgatiḥ sadyo padāti phalamuttamam ॥33॥
ato me bhavadāśīrbhirjātaḥ putro na saṃśayaḥ |
vidyā vrata tapodāna yajña svādhyāyakāriṇaḥ ॥34॥
dayā damavataste'dya nāśīrvyarthā bhavenmune|
paraṃtu kaluṣaṃ me kiṃ pūrvajanma kṛtaṃ mune ॥35॥
vada tatra pratikāraṃ sarvajño'si mato mama |
brahmovāca ॥
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā viśvāmitro mahāmuniḥ |
tasmai rājñe pūrvakathāṃ kathayāmāsa sādaram ॥36॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
kuladharmāstvayā tyaktāḥ śrīmadāndhena durmate |
vede śāstre purāṇe ca laukike ca pratiṣṭhitāḥ ॥37॥
te sarve pūrvajā nityaṃ pupūjurgaṇanāyakam |
tasya krodhavaśānnaiva santatistava jāyate ॥38॥
yena te kuladevatvaṃ saṃprāpto'sau gajānanaḥ |
bhaṇe tatte mahāvīra śṛṇu sādaramāditaḥ ॥39॥
saptamaḥ puruṣa svattaḥ pūrvaṃ bhīmo nṛpo'bhamat |
rūpavān dhanasaṃpanno vallabho nāma sattamaḥ |
tasyāpi bahukālena śiśureko'bhavannṛpa ॥40॥
atha tasyābhavaddehau mūko badhira eva ca |
ati durgaṃdhavat pūya srāvavān kubja eva ca ॥41॥
taṃ dṛṣṭvā jananī tvasya kamalā duḥkhitā bhṛśam |
acintayat svāṃtamadhye loke samyagaputratā ॥42॥
naitādṛśaṃ putravatvaṃ ślāghyaṃ duḥkhakaraṃ bhṛśam |
maraṇaṃ me vidhātrā kiṃ nāsya vā kriyate'dhunā ॥43॥
kathaṃ vā mukhadyāhaṃ darśayiṣye suhṛjjanān |
evaṃ vilapatī duḥkhādruroda bhṛśadāruṇam ॥44॥
śrutvā tadruditaṃ bhartā sūtikāgāramāgamat |
dṛṣṭvā tathāvidhaṃ gālaṃ bhṛśa patnīṃ ca duḥkhitām ॥45॥
babhāṇa mṛduvākyena sāṃtvayan karma mārgavit |
mā duḥkhaṃ kuru kalyāṇi karmaṇāṃ gatirīdṛśī ॥46॥
pūrvajanmakṛtāt pāpājjāyate duḥkhabhāṅnaraḥ |
duḥkhavānsukhamāpnoti sukhavānapi tatpunaḥ ॥47॥
na hi śocyo hi bālo'yaṃ samīcīno bhaviṣyati |
yathā'sya pūrvavṛttaṃ syāttathaivāsya bhaviṣyati ॥48॥
vayaṃ ca prayatiṣyāmo maṇi maṃtra mahauṣadhaiḥ |
tapobhiśca japairdevapūjā yātrā vidhānataḥ ॥49॥
samyak kartuṃ śiśuṃ subhru prastutaṃ yatsamācara |
evaṃ prabodhitā sādhvī tyaktvā śokaṃ śiśuṃ tataḥ ॥50॥
kṣālayitvā jalaistaṃ tu jaharṣa sakhibhiḥ saha |
sarvamābhyudayaṃ cakre putrasya jananocitam ॥51॥
pūjayāmāsa viprān sa sā tat patnīranekaśaḥ ॥52॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe kamalāputra varṇanaṃ nāmaikonaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥19॥

<adhyāya 20 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca ॥
tataḥ sa brāhmaṇān sādhūn daivajñān vedaniṣṭhitān|
ākārya pratipūjyādau ratnavāsodhanādibhiḥ ॥1॥
