pṛṣṭvā tāndakṣa ityeva nāma cakre sutasya saḥ |
japa maṃtra prayogāṃśca cakārauṣadha saṃvayān ॥2॥
svayaṃ ca dvādaśābdāni cakāra paramaṃ tapaḥ |
putrarogavimuktyarthaṃ svasaṃtānasya vṛddhaye ॥3॥
nāpaśyadroganirmuktaṃ putraṃ taṃ sa sadā nṛpaḥ |
tadā nirvedamāpanno jagāda krodhamūrchitaḥ ॥4॥
gaccha me bhavanādrājñi kamale putrasaṃyutā|
nemaṃ putraṃ na ca tvāhaṃ draṣṭuṃ śakta itaḥparam ॥5॥
iti nirbhartsitā tena vallabhena tadā sutam |
ādāya kamalā tasmānnagarāt prasthitā vanam ॥6॥
rudatī śokasaṃvignā duḥkhitā'śrupramārjatī|
vahantī pṛṣṭhabhāge taṃ kṣuttṛṭśamavikaśitā ॥7॥
grāmādgrāmāntaraṃ yātā bhikṣācārā bhṛśāturā|
taskarairluṃṭhitaṃ tasyā vasanaṃ bhūṣaṇādikam ॥8॥
tato grāmāntaraṃ gatvā nyasya putraṃ śivālaye|
bhikṣāyai puramadhye sā babhrāma nṛpavallabhā ॥9॥
kadācit saha putreṇa bhikṣituṃ sā puraṃ yayau |
kasyacid dvijamukhyasya vāyusparśena so'rbhakaḥ ॥10॥
bhakteratiśayāt sākṣātkṛto laṃbodareṇa saḥ |
cakṣuṣmān śrutisaṃpanno divyadeho'bhavattadā ॥11॥
jaharṣa kamalā tyaktvā sarvaṃ duḥkhaṃ nirīkṣya tam |
maṃjulāṃ sukhadāṃ vācamuccarantaṃ muhurmuhuḥ ॥12॥
tarkayāmāsa kamalā maṇimaṃtramahauṣadhaiḥ |
anuṣṭhānairmahāhomairna samyagyo babhūva ha ॥13॥
sa kathaṃ mārutasparśāt samīcīno'bhavatsutaḥ |
atha taṃ puruṣaṃ kvāhaṃ drakṣye duṣkarmanāśanam ॥14॥
ityuktvā sutamāliṃgya sā tasthau vigatajvarā|
tamādāya gatā bhikṣāṃ kartuṃ tannagare punaḥ ॥15॥
nāgarā bhojayāmāsurubhāvāmantrya sādaram |
nānā pakvānnaśākādyaiḥ śarkarāghṛtapāyasaiḥ ॥16॥
evaṃ pratidinaṃ tau tu bhuktvā'nudivasaṃ mudā |
āpaturdhanavastrāṇi nūtanāni varāṇi ca ॥17॥
tamapṛcchannāgarikaḥ pituste nāma vai vada |
ko deśaḥ kiṃ puraṃ jñātiḥ kā ca te vṛttameva ca ॥18॥
iti tadgiramākarṇya dakṣo nāma tamabravīt |
tato mātaramāgamya pitaraṃ nagaraṃ kulam ॥19॥
vṛttaṃ ca paripapṛccha tatastaṃ punarabravīt |
karnāṭe bhānunagare vallabho me pitā mahān ॥20॥
kṣatriyo balasaṃpannaḥ khyāto ripubalārdanaḥ |
tasyeyaṃ kamalā jāyā suto'haṃ dakṣasaṃjñakaḥ ॥21॥
utpanno'haṃ yadā brahmannandho badhira eva ca |
aneka kṣatasaṃpanno mātā tyaktuṃ tadodyatā ॥22॥
pitrā niṣedhitā sā ca yatnaṃ bahu tathā'karot |
dvādaśābdaṃ tapastaptvā pitā puṇyaṃ nyavedayat ॥23॥
na ca me pāṭavaṃ dehe paśyati sma pitā yadā|
tadā visarjayāmāsa mātaraṃ māṃ ca nāgara ॥24॥
iti sarvaṃ jagau tasmai dakṣo mātṛmukhodgatam |
atrāhaṃ subhago jāto vāyusparśena kasyacit ॥25॥
śrutvaivaṃ tu gate tasmin puruṣe puravāsini |
dakṣo jagāma tvarito mātaraṃ prati nandayan ॥26॥
tatastannagare tau dvāvupadiṣṭau dvijanmanā |
gaṇeśārādhanavidhau karuṇāyuta cetasā ॥27॥
tataḥ sā kamalā dakṣo nirvāṇaṃ paramāsthitau |
ekāṃguṣṭhena tapasā gaṇeśārādhane ratau ॥28॥
devanāma caturthyantamoṃkāra pallavānvitam |
aṣṭākṣaraṃ paraṃ maṃtraṃ japantau bhaktitatparau ॥29॥
vāyubhakṣau śuṣkatanū nirīkṣya bhagavāṃstadā|
āvirāsīttayoragre karūṇābdhirvināyakaḥ ॥30॥
caturbhujo mahākāyo vāraṇāsyo'tisuṃdaraḥ |
aneka sūryasaṃkāśo niśi sūrya ivoditaḥ ॥31॥
ratnakāṃcanamuktāvan mukuṭabhrājimastakaḥ |
pītakauśeyavasano hāṭakāṃgadabhūṣaṇaḥ ॥32॥
ekajānu nipātena sanniviṣṭo mahāsane |
kaṭisūtra kāṃcanīyaṃ mudrikāṃ ratnasaṃyutām ॥33॥
mahārhi jaṭhare bibhradekadaṃtaṃ gajārdhakam |
evaṃ rūpaṃ dadṛśatuḥ punaśca dvijarūpiṇam ॥34॥
jagāda sa dvijastau tu bhavannirvāṇatoṣitaḥ |
āgato'haṃ varaṃ dātuṃ vṛṇutaṃ manasepsitam ॥35॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
evaṃ prasanne vighneśe pratyakṣe dvijarūpataḥ |
nanāma parayā bhaktyā'bhaṇad baddhāṃjaliśca tam ॥36॥
dakṣa uvāca ॥
pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ phalitaṃ me dvijottama |
yanmayā'darśi rūpaṃ te dvividhaṃ paramaṃ mahat ॥37॥
vaināyakaṃ ca vaipraṃ ca janma me'jani sārthakam |
kāraṇānāṃ paraṃ tvaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ chandasāmapi ॥38॥
paraṃ jñeyaṃ paraṃ brahma śrutimṛgyaṃ sanātanam |
tvameva sākṣī sarvasva sarvasyāntarbahistathā ॥39॥
tvameva kartā kāryāṇāṃ laghusthūla śarīriṇām |
nānā rūpyekarūpī tvaṃ nirūpaśca nirākṛtiḥ ॥40॥
tvameva śaṃkaro viṣṇustvamevendro'nalo'ryamā|
bhūvāyukhasvarūpo'pi jala somarkṣa rūpavān ॥41॥
viśvakartā viśvapātā viśvasaṃhārakārakaḥ |
carācara gurorgoptā jñānavijñānavānapi ॥42॥
bhūtaṃ bhāvi bhavaccaiva tvamevendriyadevatāḥ |
kalā kāṣṭhā muhūrtāśca śrīrdhṛtiḥ kāntireva ca ॥43॥
tvameva sāṃkhya yogaśca śāstrāṇi śrutireva ca |
purāṇāni catuḥṣaṣṭi kalā upaniṣattathā ॥44॥
tvameva brāhmaṇo vaiśyaḥ kṣatriyaḥ śūdra eva ca |
deśo videśastvaṃ kṣetraṃ puṇyakṣetrāṇi yānyuta ॥45॥
tvaṃ prameyo'prameyaśca yogināṃ jñānagocaraḥ |
tvameva svargaḥ pātālaṃ vanānyupavanāni ca ॥46॥
oṣadhyo'tha latāvṛkṣa kandamūlaphalāni ca |
aṃḍajā jārajā jīvāḥ svedajā udbhijā api ॥47॥
kāmaḥ krodhaḥ kṣudhā lobho dambho darpo dayā kṣamā |
nidrā tandrī vilāsaśca harṣaḥ śokastvameva ca ॥48॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
iti dakṣavacaḥ śrutvā suprasanno vināyakaḥ |
meghagaṃbhīrayā vācā smayanniva jagāda tam ॥49॥
gajānana uvāca ॥
prīto'haṃ te mahābhāga stutyā gaṃbhīrayā'nayā |
varaṃ dātuṃ samutkaṃṭhastathāpi na dadāmi te ॥50॥
yadyahaṃ varadaḥ syāṃ te krudhyed bhakto mamopari|
sa eva dāsyati varaṃ yadaikasya prabhaṃjanāt ॥51॥
divyaṃ dehamanuprāptaścakṣu śrotra samanvitaḥ |
jātastvaṃ tasya nāmāpi vadāmi mudgaleti ca ॥52॥
sa dhyātamātro vipreśaḥ svarūpaṃ darśayiṣyati |
yānyān kāmayase kāmān sa te sarvān pradāsyati ॥53॥
ityuktvā paramātmā'sau tatraivāntaradhīyata |
tasminnantarhite dakṣo ruroda bhṛśaduḥkhitaḥ ॥54॥
daridreṇa nidhau labdhe gate tasminyathaiva saḥ |
gatāyāṃ gavi vatso vā roravīti yathā bhṛśam ॥55॥
muṃcannaśrūṇi netrābhyāṃ papāta dharaṇītale|
kva gataḥ kva gata iti muhurjalpanvināyakaḥ ॥56॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe dakṣastutirnāma viṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥20॥

<adhyāya 21 prāraṃbhaḥ>

viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
itastato bhraman dhāvan vallabhasyātmajastadā |
na videha gataṃ vastraṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ cātivihvalaḥ ॥1॥
papraccha brāhmaṇānmārge vṛkṣāṃścaiva vināyakam |
vināyako gataḥ kutra kvacid dṛṣṭo gadaṃtu me ॥2॥
sa evaṃ bhrāntahṛdayo bhramaccakṣu nṛpottama |
nipapāta dharāpṛṣṭhe kṣaṇaṃ vigatacetanaḥ ॥3॥
etasminnaṃtare svapne'paśyad brāhmaṇamagrataḥ |
dattaṃ tavepsitaṃ bāla mudgalena mayā hi tat ॥4॥
yattvayā yācitaṃ pūrvaṃ vighneśād dṛṣṭigocarāt |
ityuktvā prasthite tasmin brāhmaṇe nṛpasattama ॥9॥
suptotthita iva prāyo dakṣo hṛṣṭamanā'bhavat |
kasmaicit paripapraccha tvaritāya dvijātaye ॥6॥
antikaṃ darśitaṃ tena bahuśiṣyopaśobhitam |
mudgalasyātibhaktasya gajānanaratasya ca |
āśramaṃ paramaṃ divyaṃ sarvasatvābhayapradam ॥7॥
mudgalaṃ manasā dhyāyan bhramanyātastadāśramam |
nānāścaryayutaṃ ramyamalakānandanātigam ॥8॥
dadarśa mudgalaṃ tatra parārdhyāsanagaṃ dvijaṃ |
vedavedāṃgatatvajñaṃ sarvaśāstraviśāradam ॥9॥
yogābhyāsabalairnānā rūpiṇaṃ sūryatejasam |
vaināyakīṃ mahāmūrtiṃ ratnakāṃcananirmitām ॥10॥
caturbhujāṃ trinayanāṃ nānālaṃkāra śobhinīm |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pūjayaṃtaṃ vidhānataḥ ॥11॥
nanāma dakṣastaṃ dṛṣṭvā daṃḍavat pṛthivītale|
muṃcannaśrūṇi netrābhyāṃ niśvasaṃśca muhurmuhuḥ ॥12॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
papraccha mudgalastaṃ tu kastvaṃ kasmādihāgataḥ |
nudāmi duḥkhaṃ kiṃ te'sti vada sarvamaśeṣataḥ ॥13॥
evaṃ vipravacaḥ śrutvā kamalānandanastadā|
sāvadhānamanā bhūtvā jagāda dvijapuṃgavam ॥14॥
dakṣa uvāca ॥
satyameva vade brahmannabhiprāyaṃ tavāntike |
karṇāṭadeśe rājā'bhūnnagare bhānusaṃjñike ॥15॥
vallabho nāma nītijño jñānī dātā dayānvitaḥ |
tasya patnī tu kamalā janayāmāsa māṃ yadā ॥16॥
tadā durgandhikṣatayuk śoṇitasrāvināsikaḥ |
aṃdhaḥ kubjaḥ karṇahīnaḥ śabdahīnaḥ śvasanbahu ॥17॥
abhavannāgarā dṛṣṭvā tyajainamiti cābruvan |
pitā me dvādaśābdāni nānāyatnamathākarot ॥18॥
śarīrapāṭavārthaṃ me nālabhattanmaheśvarāt |
upāyaṃ sa tato māṃ ca mātaraṃ kamalāṃ ca me ॥19॥
bahirniṣkāsayāmāsa nirdayenāntarātmanā |
tato me jananī khedādaṭamānā puraṃ puram ॥20॥
āgatya kauṃḍinyapuraṃ mahā saha kṣudhārditā |
bhikṣāṭane kriyamāṇe pūrvapuṇyaprabhāvataḥ ॥21॥
abhavaddarśanaṃ teṣāmandhasyeva yathā dṛśiḥ |
tava gātrabhavādvāyoḥ sparśāddoṣā gatā mama ॥22॥
raghunāthāṃghrisaṃsparśādahalyāyā yathā purā|
divyadehaṃ samāpannaḥ prasādāttava suvrata ॥23॥
na mayā viditaṃ kicijjananyā me niveditam |
ahaṃ ca vismito bhūtvā niścayaṃ kṛtavān hṛdi ॥24॥
yadaṃga vāyusparśena divyadehamahaṃ gataḥ |
taddarśanaṃ yadā syānme tadā dehaṃ dharāmyaham ॥25॥
evaṃ bhramanbahudinaṃ tato'sau karuṇānidhiḥ |
āvirāsīn matpurataḥ koṭisūryasamaprabhaḥ ॥26॥
ubhayostapasā tuṣṭo devadevo gajānanaḥ |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā kamalā kāmān saṃprāptā manasīpsitān ॥27॥
tato māmabravīddevaḥ prasanno maṃjulāṃ giram |
yadarthaṃ niyamaṃ kṛtvā bhramankhedamupāgataḥ ॥28॥
so'haṃ te darśanaṃ yāto mudgalo brāhmaṇottama |
śrutvā tadvacanaṃ hṛṣṭamabhavanmānasaṃ mama ॥29॥
tato gajānanamahaṃ stutavān vividhaiḥ stavaiḥ |
uvāca suprasannātmā varaṃ vṛṇu mahāmate ॥30॥
ahaṃ cākathayaṃ tasmai yadyanme manasīpsitam |
tato brāhmaṇarūpaṃ tat tyaktvā'nyadrūpamādadhe ॥31॥
caturbhujaṃ mahākāyaṃ mukuṭāṭopamastakam |
paraśuṃ kamalaṃ mālāṃ modakānāvahatkaraiḥ ॥32॥
divyāmbaradharaṃ bhrājadviṣāṇa karapuṣkaram |
kuṃḍale prāvahacchrutyoḥ sūryabimbe ivāpare ॥33॥
divyālaṃkārasaṃyuktaṃ bhujaṃgavalayodaram |
devarṣi gaṃdharvagaṇaiḥ kinnarairupaśobhitam ॥34॥
tata ānandapūrṇo'haṃ dṛṣṭvā rūpaṃ tathā'bhavam |
pūrṇacaṃdraṃ yathā dṛṣṭvā pūrṇo bhavati vāridhiḥ |
tenoktaṃ mudgalaḥ sarvān kāmāṃste paripūryati ॥35॥
tadevāṃtarhitaṃ rūpaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ yāvanna sādaram |
yathā svapnagataṃ sarvaṃ jāgare naiva dṛśyate ॥36॥
tato'haṃ nitarāṃ khinno nyapataṃ bhuvi mūrcchitaḥ|
pratilabhya tataḥ saṃjñāṃ varaṃ vṛṇviti saṃsmaran ॥37॥
yayāce devadeveśaṃ sarvavyāpinamīśvaram |
gṛhe me susthirā lakṣmīrbhaktirme tādṛśī tava ॥38॥
dṛṣṭaścet pūrvapuṇyena dvayametat pradīyatām |
tato'haṃ śrutavāndattamiti vācaṃ nabhogatām ॥39॥
tato hṛṣṭamanā vipra tava sānnidhyamāgataḥ |
mudgalastvaṃ dvipāsyo'sau dvipāsyastvaṃ sa mudgalaḥ ॥40॥
iti me manasi spaṣṭaṃ bhāti sarvaṃ gajānanaḥ |
bhṛguruvāca॥
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā mudgalo vākyamabravīt ॥41॥
sabhāgyaḥ kṛtakṛtyo'si bhaktimān kamalāsuta |
mahimā tava bhakterhi vaktuṃ śakyo na kenacit ॥42॥
tapāmi sudṛḍhaṃ cāhaṃ daśavarṣaśataṃ tapaḥ |
na ma etādṛśo devo dṛṣṭa āsīt kadācana ॥43॥
yaḥ sarvajagatāṃ nāthaścarācara gurorguruḥ |
yo rajaḥ satva tamasāṃ nenā nityaṃ guṇāśrayaḥ ॥44॥
yo brahmaśivaviṣṇūnāṃ śarīrāṇi karoti hi |
bhūtānāṃ ca vibhūtīnāṃ mātrendriya dhiyāmapi ॥45॥
yaṃ na devā viduḥ samyak na vedā narṣayo'pi ca |
evaṃ gajānanaṃ tvaṃ hi pratyakṣaṃ dṛṣṭavān sphuṭam ॥46॥
ahaṃ tvaccaraṇau vande yatastvaṃ bhakmiānparaḥ |
tataḥ parasparaṃ tau tu nematuścāliliṃgatuḥ ॥47॥
gurubaṃdhū ciraṃkālamekacittau samāgatau|
tata ekākṣaraṃ maṃtraṃ saṃjapa dhyānapūrvakam ॥48॥
mudgalo rājaputrāya namrāyopadideśa ha|
uvāca cainaṃ bhūyo'pi maṃtrasyāsya dinedine ॥49॥
anuṣṭhānaṃ kuruṣva tvaṃ prasannaste bhaviṣyati |
gajānano dadet kāmān manasā vāṃchitāṃstvayā ॥50॥
enaṃ cettyajase mantraṃ sarvaśo nāśameṣyasi |
yadyasya bhakti suciraṃ loke'smin vicariṣyasi ॥51॥
indrādilokapālānāṃ gaṇo vaśyatvameṣyati |
iha bhuktvā'khilān bhogānante mokṣamavāpsyasi ॥52॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe mantropadeśavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥21॥

<adhyāya 22 prāraṃbhaḥ>

rājovāca ॥
āścaryabhūtaṃ kathitaṃ dakṣaputrasya ceṣṭitam |
vismayo me mahāṃstatra saṃjāto munisattama ॥1॥
aṃdhaḥ kubjaḥ sravadraktaḥ pūtigandhasamanvitaḥ |
vācā vihīno'medhyaśca śvāsamātrāvaśeṣitaḥ ॥2॥
kathaṃ mudgala dehottha vāyunā divyadehabhāk |
saṃjātaḥ kena puṇyena mukto vā pātakāt kutaḥ ॥3॥
divyavarṣasahasraṃ yastatāpa paramaṃ tapaḥ |
kathaṃ tasyābhavannaiva darśanaṃ devakāṃkṣitam ॥4॥
kathaṃ vallabhaputrāya devadeva ibhānanaḥ |
pratyakṣo'bhūdvinā kleśaḥ sa ca kaḥ pūrvajanmani ॥5॥
etatsaṃśaya jātaṃ me nuda sarvajña te namaḥ |
na tṛpyāmi pibannityaṃ gajānana kathāmṛtam ॥6॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
samyak pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā rājan saṃśayacchedanāya te |
vadāmi nikhilaṃ samyak śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa ॥7॥
siṃdhudeśe'ti vikhyātā pallī nāmnā 'bhavatpurī|
tasyāmāsīddhanī vaiśyaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ kalyāṇasaṃjñakaḥ ॥8॥
vadānyaḥ kuśalo dhīmān dvijadevaparāyaṇaḥ |
indumatīti vikhyātā patnī tasya śubhānanā ॥9॥
pativratā patiprāṇā pativākyaparāyaṇā |
tayoḥ kālena saṃjāto guṇavān putra uttamaḥ ॥10॥
kalyāṇaḥ pradadau dhenūrvastrālaṃkaraṇāni ca |
brāhmaṇebhyo'tha ratnāni kāṃcanaṃ bhūridakṣiṇām ॥11॥
jyotirvidbhirnigaditaṃ nāma cakre sutasya saḥ |
ballāla iti vikhyātaṃ baladevyācchubhaṃ tadā ॥12॥
sa ca kālena mahatā vayasyaiśca samanvitaḥ |
devapūjārato nityaṃ grāmād bahirayānmadā ॥13॥
ekadā te vanaṃ yātā ballāla pramukhāḥ sutā |
nānā krīḍāratāḥ snātā upale sthāpya sundaram ॥14॥
gaṇeśabuddhyā saṃpūjya durvākuraṃ supallavaiḥ |
keciddhyānaratāstasya nāma jāpyaṃ pracakrire ॥15॥
kecicca nanṛtustatra yatheṣṭaṃ devabhaktitaḥ |
kecicca gānakuśalā jagurdevasya tuṣṭaye ॥16॥
kecitkāṣṭhaiḥ pallavaiśca maṃḍapaṃ cakrurojasā|
kecidbhittiparīveṣaṃ kecitprāsādamuttamam ॥17॥
kecinmānasapūjābhiḥ kecitpuṣpalatādibhiḥ |
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ phalatāṃbūladakṣiṇāḥ ॥18॥
nivedya pupujustasmai mudā paramayā yutāḥ |
kecicca paṃḍitā bhūtvā purāṇānyabruvaṃstathā ॥19॥
vyācakrurdharmaśāstrāṇi graṃthānanyāṃśca kecana |
evaṃ devaratāḥ sarve ninyurvai divasānbahūn ॥20॥
na kṣudhā na tṛṣāṃ ko'pi jānandevasya bhaktitaḥ|
ekadā janakāsteṣāṃ kalyāṇavaiśyamanvayuḥ ॥21॥
abruvan roṣitāḥ sarve svaballālaṃ nivāraya |
śiśūn sarvān samāhūya vanaṃ yāti dine dine ॥22॥
nāyānti prātarāśāya madhyāhne niśāmukhe |
kṛśā no bālakā jātāḥ svasutaṃ śikṣāyādhunā ॥23॥
no cedvayaṃ nibaddhvainaṃ tāḍayāmaḥ itaḥ param |
purādhipamathābhyetya tvāṃ bahiḥ karavāmahi ॥24॥
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā'śrutapūrvaṃ kadācana |
roṣāveśātsa kalyāṇo japākusumalocanaḥ ॥25॥
mahāntaṃ daṃḍamādāya tāḍanārthaṃ sutaṃ yayau|
daṇḍāghātena gatvaiva maṃḍapaṃ prababhaṃja ha ॥26॥
bālakāśca tataḥ sarve jagmurdaśa diśo nṛpa |
eka eva sa ballālo dṛḍhabhaktyā sthiro'bhavat ॥27॥
dṛḍhamuṣṭyā sa taṃ dhṛtvā daṃḍenātāḍyad bhṛśam |
yathā sarvaśarīrāttu dhārāḥ śoṇitasaṃbhavāḥ ॥28॥
prādurāsan vāridhārā varṣākāle yathā gire |
cikṣepa dūrataḥ paścāddevaṃ siṃdūrasuṃdaram ॥29॥
babaṃdha taṃ sutaṃ vṛkṣe vallīpāśaśatairdṛḍham |
putrasnehaṃ parityajya yamadūta ivāparaḥ ॥30॥
mukto dantairna hastābhyāṃ pādābhyāṃ na yathā bhavet |
uvāca paścāttaṃ putraṃ devastvāṃ mocayiṣyati ॥31॥
sa dātā bhojanaṃ pānaṃ gopanaṃ ca kariṣyati |
yadi ced gṛhamāyāsi tadā satyaṃ mariṣyasi ॥32॥
muniruvāca ॥
vibhajya devālayamāśu yātaḥ kalyāṇavaiśyo'tiruṣāniviṣṭaḥ |
svamandiraṃ badhya vane svaputraṃ daivasya yogādabhavat suduṣṭaḥ ॥33॥
tasminprayāte sa ca vaiśyaputraḥ śuśoca devaṃ manasā vicintya |
vighnārirityeva kathaṃ nu deva nāmābhavat sarvajaneṣu gītam ॥34॥
duṣṭāṃśca vighnāni na nāśayestvaṃ duṣṭāntakatvena kathaṃ prasiddhaḥ |
śeṣo'vaniṃ vā savitā'tha dīptiṃ candro'mṛtaṃ vahnirathoṣṇatāṃ ca ॥35॥ tyajetsvabhaktānna ca saṃtyajethā vedeṣu śāstreṣu kathaṃ prasiddhiḥ |
evaṃ vilapyātha nijaṃ śaśāpa kalyāṇasaṃjñaṃ pitaraṃ suduṣṭaṃ ॥36॥
yenedaṃ mama vidhvastaṃ devālayamanuttamam |
gaṇeśamūrtivikṣepaṃ tāḍanaṃ ca mamākarot ॥37॥
sa cāndho badhiraḥ kubjo mūko bhavati niścitam |
yadi me sudṛḍhā bhaktirdviradānana uttamā ॥38॥
tadoktaṃ mama satyaṃ syāttasya sarvaṃ mayoditam |
na baddhe bhaktimanasī śakto'sau dehabandhane ॥39॥
ananyabuddhyā paricintya devaṃ, tyajāmi dehaṃ vijane vane'smin |
palāyanaṃ naiva kṛtaṃ yadaiva, tadaiva deho'rpita eṣa deve ॥40॥
tasyaivaṃ niścayaṃ buddhvā prādurāsīdgajānanaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya svarūpeṇa ballālasya prabhāvataḥ ॥41॥
yathā niśātamopāye cchrīmūrya udayācale|
tathaiva bandhanānyasya tejasā śithilāni ca ॥42॥
jātāni tata enaṃ sa namaścakretha daṃḍavat |
dehasya cārutā jātā na kṣataṃ na ca śoṇitam ॥43॥
jñānaṃ ca nirmalaṃ jātaṃ devadevasya darśanāt |
tuṣṭāva vividhairvākyairyathāmati gajānanam ॥44॥
ballāla uvāca ॥
tvameva mātā'si pitā'si bandhustvameva kartā'si carācarasya |
nirmāsi duṣṭāṃśca khalāṃśca sādhūnyonau viyonau viniyuṃkṣyathāpi ॥45॥
tvameva dik cakra nabhodharābdhi, girīndra kālānala vāyurūpaḥ |
ravīndu tārāgraha lokapāla varṇendriyārthoṣadhi dhāturūpaḥ ॥46॥
muniruvāca ॥ iti stutiṃ samākarṇya suprasanno gajānanaḥ |
āliṃgya nijabhaktaṃ tamuvāca ghananisvanaḥ ॥47॥
gajānana uvāca ॥
prāsādo yena bhagno me narake sa patiṣyati |
tava śāpo'pi tasyaivaṃ bhaviṣyati mamājñayā ॥48॥
andho'tha badhiraḥ kubjo mūko'sṛksrāvasaṃyutaḥ |
mama śāpaṃ samāsādya bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ ॥49॥
pitā samātṛkaṃ cainaṃ kariṣyati gṛhād bahiḥ |
anyatte vāṃchitaṃ brūhi duṣprāpamapi te dade ॥50॥
muniruvāca ॥
ballālo'thābravīddevaṃ tvayi bhaktirdṛḍhā'stu me |
asminkṣetre sthiro bhūtvā lokān rakṣasva vighnataḥ ॥51॥
gaṇeśa uvāca ॥
tvannāmapūrva mannāma bhaviṣyati jane śubham |
ballāla vināyaketi nagare supratiṣṭhitam ॥52॥
mayi cittaṃ sthiraṃ te syād bhaktiravyabhicāriṇī|
yātrā mama kariṣyanti nagare pallisaṃjñike ॥53॥
bhādrapadaśuklacaturthyāṃ ye teṣāṃ kāmāndadāmyaham ॥54॥
bhṛguruvāca ॥
iti datvā varaṃ devastatraivāntaradhīyata |
tato vināyakaṃ sthāpya ballālo brāhmaṇaiḥ saha ॥55॥
prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa nānā śobhāsamanvitam|
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
ballālavaināyaketi kathitaṃ te kathānakam ॥56॥
yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo muktaḥ kāmānavāpnuyāta ॥57॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe ballālavināyakakathanaṃ nāma dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥22॥

<adhyāya 23 prāraṃbhaḥ>

bhṛgurūvāca ॥
viśvāmitravacaḥ śrutvā bhīmaḥ papraccha taṃ punaḥ |
vaiśyacaritavārtāyāḥ śravaṇe paramotsukaḥ ॥1॥
somakānta nṛpaśreṣṭha kathayāmi śṛṇuṣva tat |
bhīma uvāca ॥
śrutaṃ dakṣasya caritaṃ viśrāntaṃ mama mānasam ॥2॥
kathaṃ kalyāṇavaiśyasya gatirāsīdvadasva tām |
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
śṛṇuṣvaikamanā bhīma kathāmetāṃ vadāmi te ॥3॥
ballālaśāpāt kalyāṇadehāt susrāva śoṇitam |
asaṃkhya kṣatasaṃyuktaḥ srāvo vādhiryamaṃdhatā ॥4॥
mūkatā pūtigandhitvaṃ jātaṃ tasya durātmanaḥ |
dadarśendumatī tasya daśāmākasmikīṃ tataḥ ॥5॥
kimidaṃ kimidaṃ kasmājjātamevaṃ śuśoca tam |
jñānino'sya vadānyasya devadvijaratasya ca ॥6॥
dharmaśāstrārthaniṣṭhasya svadāraparitoṣiṇaḥ |
avastheyaṃ kathaṃ jātā mama bharturnirenasaḥ ॥7॥
muniruvāca ॥
evaṃ vilapya bahudhā niśvasantī punaḥ punaḥ ॥8॥
tena baddhaṃ vane putraṃ niśamya rudatī muhuḥ|
paureḥ saha yayau tatra yatra baddho vane sutaḥ ॥9॥
tatra devālayaṃ devaṃ dadarśa gaṇanāyakam ॥10॥
caturbhujaṃ trinayanaṃ sindūrāruṇavigraham |
ballālaṃ ca sutaṃ tatra pūjayantaṃ gajānanam ॥11॥
muktaṃ nirvraṇamavyaṃgaṃ nirīkṣya puravāsinaḥ |
uvācendumatī kruddhā bhartsayantī punaḥpunaḥ ॥12॥
kathaṃ pratāritā tathyavādibhirbhartṛsannidhau |
tādṛśaṃ taṃ parityajya putrasnehādihāgatā ॥13॥
paśyaṃtu devabhaktiṃ me putraṃ kurvantamīdṛśīm |
muniruvāca ॥
vismitāḥ sarva evaite na kiṃcit procuraṇvapi ॥14॥
kecidūcurmahābhaktermahimā kena gamyate |
vīkṣya siṃdūraraktāṃgaṃ raktacandanacarcitam ॥15॥
raktāmbaraṃ raktapuṣpamālābhirupaśobhitam |
nirmamaṃ nirahaṃkāramaśuṃḍamiva vighnapam ॥16॥
nirīkṣya tādṛśaṃ putraṃ tyaktvā śokaṃ nananda sā |
āliliṃga tadā taṃ tu snehanutapayodharā ॥17॥
āha taṃ svagṛhaṃ yāva pituste śyasanaṃ mahat |
utthitaṃ tatra kicittaṃ kurūpāyaṃ mahāmate ॥18॥
āvāṃ dhanyatamau loke yayoḥ putrastvamīdṛśaḥ |
sarvāṃga kṣatavān raktasrāvavān pūtigaṃdhavān ॥19॥
śyāmāsyaḥ kṛśatāyukto badhiro'thāndha eva ca |
evaṃvidhaḥ pitā te'sti taṃ niveditumāgatā ॥20॥
pitṛdharmeṇa tena tvaṃ tāvito'narthakṛdyapi |
tatrāparādho naivāsti śrutismatipurāṇataḥ ॥21॥
putradharmānnirīkṣasva kurvārogya vicāraṇam |
tvayā ślāghyatamo loke pitā te pitṛvatsala ॥22॥
mātā pitṛvacaḥ kāryaṃ satputreṇa yaśasvinā |
pūjanaṃ ca tayoḥ kāryaṃ poṣaṇaṃ pālanaṃ tathā ॥23॥
auṣadhānmantrataścāpi devatāprārthanādapi |
upāyaṃ kuru putra tvaṃ mayi dṛṣṭiṃ niveśaya ॥24॥
yaśaste bhavitā loke saubhāgyaṃ mama bālaka |
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā ballālo vākyamabravīt ॥25॥
ballāla uvāca ॥
kasya mātā pitā kasya kasya putro'thavā suhṛt |
vighnarāja kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ mānuṣasyakhilaṃ smṛtam ॥26॥
tasmānmama pitā bhadre mātā devo vināyakaḥ |
yo yathā kurute karma sa tathā phalamaśnute |
mayā samarpito jīvo devadeve gajānane ॥27॥
tena jīvastathā jñānaṃ mama dattaṃ subhaktitaḥ |
prāsādabhaṃjanāddevakṣepaṇāttāḍanānmama |
vināyakātibhaktasya tathā prāptaṃ phalaṃ śubhe ॥28॥
vicāryamāṇe na tvaṃ me mātā vā sa pitā'pi ca |
sarvasya hi pitā devo mātā devo gajānanaḥ ॥29॥
sa eva jñānastrātā saṃhartā kālarūpavān |
sarvasvarūpo devendro brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakaḥ ॥30॥
vṛthā mayena duṣṭena tāḍanaṃ nirghṛṇena ca |
kṛtaṃ devasya vikṣepaḥ prāsādasyāpi bhaṃjanam ॥31॥
tasyāsya darśane doṣaḥ patitasya mahānbhavet |
snehaṃ tyakvā madīyaṃ tvaṃ patiṃ svaṃ pariṣevaya ॥32॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
iti putravacaḥ śrutvā sā taṃ punarathābravīt |
mātovāca ॥
kṛpayā'nugrahāt snehāducchāpaṃ vaktumarhasi ॥33॥
putra uvāca ॥
bhavāntare'sya jananī bhavitāsi varārthinī|
ayametādṛśaḥ putro bhavitā tava suvrate ॥34॥
kalyāṇa nāmā bhavitā vallabhaḥ kṣatriyarṣabhaḥ |
kamaleti ca vikhyātā nāmnā tvaṃ ca bhaviṣyasi ॥35॥
dakṣa ityeva putrasya nāma khyātaṃ bhaviṣyati |
tato dvādaśa varṣāṇi vallabhastapsyate tapaḥ ॥36॥
andhatāṃ badhiratvaṃ ca kṣatāni mūkatāṃ tathā |
vyapanetuṃ tu dakṣasya paraṃ niyamamāsthitaḥ ॥37॥
alabdhvā sa phalaṃ tasmātsuputrāṃ tvāṃ śubhānane |
nirākariṣyate gehādvideśasthā bhaviṣyasi ॥38॥
kasyacid dvijavaryasya dviradānanacetasaḥ |
daivāt sparśena bhadre te samyakputro bhaviṣyati ॥39॥
tatraiva gaṇanāthasya darśanaṃ ca bhaviṣyati |
tadā sa divyadehatvaṃ prāpsyate dviradānanāt ॥40॥
iti sarvaṃ samākhyātamucchāpakāraṇaṃ śubhe|
bhaviṣyaṃ kathitaṃ te'dya yatheṣṭaṃ gamyatāṃ tvayā ॥41॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
evaṃ nirākṛtā tena jananī niragāttadā |
duḥkhaśokasamāyuktā kiṃciddharṣayutā'pi ca ॥42॥
sa cāruroha taddivyaṃ vimānaṃ bhaktibhāvitam |
gajānanena vihitam ballālo'thāgamaddivam ॥43॥
iti te sarvamākhyātaṃ yat pṛṣṭo'hamiva tvayā|
yā gatistena vaiśyena saṃprāptā janmanordvayoḥ ॥44॥
yathā jagāda ballālaḥ sarvaṃ tadabhavattadā |
sā jajñe kamalā bhūtvā so'bhavat kṣatriyarṣabhaḥ ॥45॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe bhaviṣyakathanaṃ nāma trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥23॥

<adhyāya 24 prāraṃbhaḥ>

bhīma uvāca ॥
dakṣeṇa muniśārdūla rājaputreṇa dhīmatā |
anuṣṭhānaṃ kṛtaṃ kutra kathaṃ vā kasya vā mune ॥1॥
etadvistārya brūhi tvaṃ śṛṇvaṃstṛpyāmyahaṃ na ca |
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
avidūre purastasyāḥ kauṃḍinyasya mahāvanam ॥2॥
ramyaṃ nānāvṛkṣayutaṃ nānāśvāpadasaṃkulam |
nānāpakṣigaṇairyuktaṃ latājālavirājitam ॥3॥
sanmano nirmalapayaḥ sarovāpīvirājitam |
jīrṇaprāsādamadhyastha gajānana virājitam ॥4॥
tatra sthitvā tapaścakre gajānanasutoṣakam |
mudgalenopadiṣṭena mantreṇaikākṣareṇa saḥ ॥5॥
toṣayāmāsa taṃ devaṃ varṣairdvādaśasaṃmitaiḥ |
snānairvastraiḥ sugaṃdhaiśca mālyairdhūpaiśca dīpakaiḥ ॥6॥
naivedyaṃ kandamūlaiśca bhakṣaṇīyairakalpayat |
manasā kalpayāmāsa dakṣiṇāṃ kṣatriyarṣabhaḥ ॥7॥
evaṃ tasyāgamanbhūpa dināni tvekaviṃśatiḥ |
tataḥ prabhātasamaye svayamevaṃ dadarśa saḥ ॥8॥
ekaṃ mahāmataṃgaṃ ca siṃdūrāktaṃ suśobhitam |
madasrāvisugaṃḍābhyāṃ suṃdaraṃ girisannibham ॥9॥
cāruprasannavadanaṃ dantaśobhimahatkarama |
bhramarālisamākīrṇaṃ gajānanamivāparam |
tena tatkaṃṭhadeśe tu ratnamālā samarpitā ॥10॥
tata utthāpya taṃ skandhe sthāpayāmāsa dantirāṭ |
sa dantī nagaraṃ yātaḥ patākā dhvajaśobhitam ॥11॥
tataḥ sa pratibuddhaḥ sanpapṛccha jananīṃ nijām |
kamale vada mātastvamasyābhiprāyamadya me ॥12॥
ārohaṇaṃ gajaskandhe śubhaṃ vā yadi vā'śubham |
kamalovāca ॥
dhanyastvamasi dṛṣṭo'yaṃ gajarūpī vināyakaḥ ॥13॥
ārohaṇaphalaṃ rājyaprāptireva na saṃśayaḥ |
dakṣa uvāca ॥
yadi rājyasya prāptiḥ syāttadā tubhyaṃ dadāmyaham ॥14॥
narayānaṃ tathā grāmān dīdikāṃ mauktikaṃ srajam |
dharmaṃ ca kārayiṣyāmi gavāṣṭā padadānataḥ ॥15॥
vratāni niyamāṃścaiva dānānyanyānyanekaśaḥ |
muniruvāca ॥
śrutvetthaṃ kamalā hṛṣṭā jagāda tanayaṃ prati ॥16॥
tvayi rājyasthite putra paramānandatā mama |
saddharme ramatāṃ cetaḥ sataḥ sārabhṛtastava |
āyuṣya vipulaṃ te'stu dvijadevārcane ratiḥ ॥17॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe svapnakathanaṃ nāma caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥24॥

<adhyāya 25 prāraṃbhaḥ>

viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
śṛṇu rājan mahāścaryaṃ daivakālakṛtaṃ śubham |
kauṃḍinye nagare rājā caṃdraseno mahāmatiḥ ॥1॥
svakarmaparipākena nidhanaṃ kālayogataḥ|
agamaddivyayānena dharmabāhulyato divam ॥2॥
tacca nāgarikāḥ śrutvā hāhākāraṃ pracakrire|
dhāvamānā yayustatra tyaktvā kāryāṇyanekaśaḥ ॥3॥
śirāṃsi ca vinighnaṃtaḥ pāṇibhiḥ śokakarśitāḥ |
caskhaluśca patantaśca dadṛśuḥ pretabhūpatim ॥4॥
nemuḥ pādau pragṛhayaiva duḥkhamohavaśaṃ gatāḥ |
keciddhastaṃ gṛhītvaiva svasvamūrdhninyaveśayan ॥5॥
ruruduḥ susvaraṃ kecitpāṇipṛṣṭamukhasvanāḥ |
mṛtā iva nipetuśca snehātiśayato'pare ॥6॥
tasya patnī tu sulabhā ruroda karuṇasvarā |
pāṇibhyāṃ nighnatī svīyaṃ hṛdayaṃ bhṛśaduḥkhitā ॥7॥
vikīrṇabhūṣaṇā mūrcchāmāgatā patitaḥ bhuvi |
samānavyasanābhiḥ sā purastrībhirdhṛtā tadā ॥8॥
vilalāpa tadā kāntā candrasenasya suṃdarī|
nirlajjā niranukrośā nātha nātheti jalpatī ॥9॥
re dhātarna dayā te'sti bālavaccaritaṃ tava |
yunakṣi snehabhāvena viniyuṃkṣya kṛtārthakam ॥10॥
rājanna pṛṣṭvā kva gato vada māṃ karuṇānidhe |
dine dine vadasyeva yāmi bhadrāsanaṃ priye ॥11॥
adya niṣṭhuratāṃ kena gamito'syāgasā mama |
tatkṣamasva namāmyeṣā janamadhye gatatrapā ॥12॥
naya māṃ yatra yāto'si priyāṃ te priyakāriṇīm |
trilokīṃ paśyatīṃ śūnyāmaputrāṃ patitā vinā ॥13॥
muniruvāca ॥
sumantuḥ prakṛtistasya manoraṃjana eva ca |
dvāvapyavoditāṃ tā kiṃ rājasya bhaviṣyati ॥14॥
āvābhyāvicāryaiva kva gato nṛpasattama |
kasmānna vadase rājan kimarthaṃ maunamāsthitaḥ ॥15॥
na paśyasi priyāṃ bhāryāmanāthāmiva vihvalām |
sahaiva yāvo nṛpate tyaktvā sarvaṃ gṛhāśramam ॥16॥
anāthaṃ nagaraṃ te'dya rāṣṭraṃ vā ko'tha pāsyati ॥17॥
muniruvāca ॥
etasminnaṃtare tatra babhāṇa brāhmaṇaḥ sudhīḥ ॥18॥
vedaśāstrārthatatvajñaḥ prastutaṃ niṣṭhuraṃ vacaḥ |
sarve svārthaparā yūyaṃ nāptaḥ kaścana vidyate ॥19॥
suhṛdāṃ rodanāśrūṇi mukhe pretasya yānti hi |
pṛthivyāṃ bhāratāṃ yāti prāṇahīnaṃ kalevaram ॥20॥
brahmāṃḍagolake ko nu mṛtamanyo'nugacchati |
iyaṃ ca sulabhā rājñī rodate jīvitāśayā ॥21॥
yasyā mano'nugatyarthaṃ na sā roditi karhicit |
yūyaṃ sarve nāgarikāḥ svakāryagamanākulāḥ ॥22॥
sūryavaṃśyāḥ somavaṃśyā ye rājāno mṛtā na kim |
tasmādutthāya sarve'pi rājñaḥ kurvantu saṃskṛtim ॥23॥
mṛtasaṃskārakārī yaḥ sa evāpto na cāparaḥ ||
etadarthaṃ hi lokasya jyāyasī putragṛdhnutā ॥24॥
tasmādānīyatāṃ dharmaputro vā'pyanya eva ca |
sa hi kriyāmārabhatu sarvairdeyastilāṃjaliḥ ॥25॥
muniruvāca ॥
tataste nāgarāḥ sarve dvāvamātyau striyaśca tāḥ |
prabodhitā brāhmaṇena cakrustasyaurdhvadehikam ॥26॥
sumaṃtunā kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ sarvairdattastilāṃjaliḥ |
punaḥ snātvā tu te sarve nagaraṃ viviśuścirāt ॥27॥
aśnanti picumandasya patraṃ natveśvaraṃ janāḥ |
sāntvayitvā tu sulabhāṃ yayuḥ svaṃ svaṃ niketanam ॥28॥
trayodaśāhe nirvṛtte rājñyai dattvā'mbarāṇi te|
cakruste bhojanaṃ prītyā pratyahaṃ bahuvāsaram ॥29॥
ekadā nāgarāḥ sarve dvāvamātyau nṛpapriyā |
sarve saṃśayitā āsan prajāpālana karmaṇi ॥30॥
etasminnaṃtare tatra mudgalo munirāyayau|
uvāca sarvāśayavidasya rājño mahāgajaḥ ॥31॥
gahano nāma gṛhṇātu mālāṃ puṣkaranirmitām |
samāje yasya kaṃṭhe tāṃ nikṣipetsa nṛpo bhavet ॥32॥
tatheti ca tadā sarve sādhu sādhvityapūjayan |
atīndriya jñānavato vaco mudgalaśarmaṇaḥ ॥33॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe paṃcaviśo'dhyāyaḥ॥25॥

<adhyāya 26 prāraṃbhaḥ>

viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
ekasmindivase śubhagrahayute lagne śubhe vāsare|
yoge cāruphale jane ca nagare nānāvidhe melite |
mālāṃ ratnamayīṃ dadau narapate rājñī kareṇoḥ kare |
saṃprārthya dviradaṃ kuruṣva nṛpatiṃ lokeṣu yaste mataḥ ॥1॥
tāmājñāṃ pratigṛhaya vāraṇapatiḥ saṃraṃjito dhātubhirbabhrāma dvijabandicāraṇagaṇairāśāsitaḥ sarvataḥ |
nānāvādyakarairvṛto nṛpabhaṭai rājyārthibhiḥ pūrūṣai|
jighran sarvajanaṃ sabhāparigataṃ yāto bahiḥ pattanāt ॥2॥
nāryastannagare śiśūnatha patīn kṛtvā puro'vasthitān |
sajyārthaṃ vividhā narāśca bahudhā śreṇīmukhāśca sthitāḥ |
te sarve vimanaskatāṃ parigatāḥ svaṃ svaṃ gṛhaṃ cāgatā |
yāte'smin gahane kariṇyatha puro yātā bahiścāpare ॥3॥
gajastu yātaḥ kamalāsutaṃ taṃ saṃpūjayantaṃ dviradānanaṃ tam |
dṛṣṭvaiva tasminnidadhe sa mālāṃ paśyatsu lokeṣu sureṣu nāke ॥4॥
vastrāṇi mālyāni ca bhūṣaṇāni dakṣāya dattāni tadaiva lokaiḥ |
jñātvā mataṃ paurajanasya rājā rājñaḥ prakṛtyorubhayostadaiva ॥5॥
vavaṃdurvādyasaṃghāśca divyā bhaumā anekaśaḥ|
mumucuḥ puṣpavarṣaṃ te devā harṣayutāḥ śubham ॥6॥
upaviṣṭāstadā lokā yathāsthānaṃ yathākramam |
nemuśca taṃ dakṣanṛpamamātyadvaya saṃyutam ॥7॥
tāmbūlāni ca vastrāṇi janebhyaḥ pradadau nṛpaḥ |
brāhmaṇān pūjayitvaiva dattvā dānānyanekaśaḥ ॥8॥
saṃpūjya mātare tāṃ tu vastrālaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ|
dāpayāmāsa dānāni brāhmaṇebhyo yathāvidhi ॥9॥
narayāne samāropya svayamāruruhe gajam |
alaṃkṛtaṃ siktamārgaṃ patākādhvajasaṃyutam ॥10॥
aśvāruḍhāvamātyau tau puraskṛtya yayau puram |
stuvanti bandinaḥ paurā nṛtyantyapsarasaḥ puraḥ |
gaṃdharvā gānaniṣṇātā dhāvitvā yāntyamuṃ puraḥ ॥11॥
jayaśabdairnamaḥ śabdo vādyaśabdo'gamaddivam |
nṛpadvāraṃ samāsādya natvā kecidgṛhaṃ yayuḥ ॥12॥
sabhāyāṃ viviśuḥ sarve saṃkhyātītā nṛpāstadā |
nṛyānaṃ preṣayāmāsa mudgalāya mahāmatiḥ ॥13॥
chatraṃ dhvajaṃ cāmaraṃ ca sumantuṃ prakṛtiṃ tathā |
āyāntaṃ mudgalaṃ dṛṣṭvā svāsanātpurato yayau ॥14॥
sakirīṭena śirasā pādayoḥ praṇanāma tam |
nyaveśayat svāsane taṃ tenājñapto'nya āsane ॥15॥
upaviṣṭo rājasaṃghaiḥ pūjayāmāsa taṃ munim |
gāṃ cāpi pradadau tatra brāhmaṇāya mahāmatiḥ ॥16॥
uvāca taṃ nṛpo dakṣo bho mudgala mahāmune |
adya te mahimā jñāto lokairetairmahānayam ॥17॥
śarīre cārutā rājyaprāptiśca tvatprasādataḥ |
kva sā'vasthā pūrvatanoḥ kvedṛgarājyaṃ mahāmune ॥18॥
vināyakamahaṃ jāne tvāmeva munisattama |
punarme mastake brahmannidhehi karapaṃkajam ॥19॥
yenāhaṃ sarvakāmānāṃ bhājanaṃ syācciraṃ mune |
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
ākarṇya vacane tasya mudgalo'thābravīcca tam ॥20॥
na te bhayaṃ ripukṛtaṃ bhaviṣyati kadācana |
yaṃ yaṃ kāmayase kāmaṃ sarvataste bhaviṣyati ॥21॥
tatastasmai dadau grāmān vāso ratna dhanādikam |
anyeṣāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca godhanānyaṃśukāni ca ॥22॥
āśīrbhirabhinandyātha yayuste brāhmaṇā gṛham |
amātyābhyāṃ gṛhebhyaśca dadau grāmānanekaśaḥ ॥23॥
prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa gaṇeśasya bṛhattaram |
kuṃḍine nagare tasmin prāktanaṃ saṃsthitaṃ laghum |
visarjayāmāsa sabhāṃ praviveśa gṛhaṃ nṛpaḥ ॥24॥
āyayau vallabhastatra śrutvā vārtāṃ janeritām |
vīraseno narapatiḥ svāṃ sutāṃ patimicchatīm ॥25॥
dadau tasmai svapnagataṃ gaṇeśasyājñayā śubham |
dakṣāya rājñe mahate trilokaśrutakīrtaye ॥26॥
tasmāttasyā'bhavatputro bṛhadbhānuriti śrutaḥ |
tato'bhavat khaḍgadharaḥ sulabhastat suto'bhavat ॥27॥
padmākarastattanayo vapurdīptastu tatsutaḥ |
citrasenastatsuto''bhūccitrasenāttvameva hi ॥28॥
brahmovāca ॥
viśvāmitramukhātsarvaṃ śrutvā vaṃśaparaṃparām |
tataḥ sa naraśārdūlo bhīmo nāma mahīpatiḥ ॥29॥
saṃtoṣya taṃ muniṃ prārthya papraccha dvijapuṃgavam |
bhīma uvāca ॥
vināyakaḥ kadā me syāt suprasanno mahāmune ॥30॥
tamupāyaṃ vada vibho yena me tadanugrahaḥ |
kadā'haṃ kṛtakṛtyaḥ syāṃ dṛṣṭvā devaṃ gajānanam ॥31॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanā khaṃḍe paraṃparāvarṇanaṃ nāma ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥26॥

<adhyāya 27 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca ॥
ka upāyastu muninā viśvāmitreṇa dhīmatā|
kṛpāvatā tu bhīmāya kathitastaṃ vadasva me|
manmanomohapaṭalaṃ mṛtyoriva viniḥsṛtam ॥1॥
tatkathāmṛtapānena pitāmaha mṛtā yathā| brahmovāca ॥
bhīmāya kathitastena ya upāyaḥ śṛṇuṣva tam |
ekākṣaraṃ mahāmaṃtraṃ bhīmāyākathayanmuni ॥2॥
pārāśarya muniśreṣṭha kathayāmi śṛṇuṣva tam |
uvāca taṃ prasannātmā viśvāmitraḥ sudharmavit ॥3॥
viśvāmitra uvāca ॥
anenārādhaya vibhuṃ daivataṃ gaṇanāyakam |
anuṣṭhānaṃ kuruṣva prāsāde dakṣanirmite |
vināyakaḥ prasannaste sarvānkāmān pradāsyati ॥4॥
dharmamarthaṃ tathā kāmaṃ mokṣamanyadapekṣitam |
gaccha vā svapuraṃ bhīma cintāṃ kāmapi mā kuru ॥5॥
brahmovāca ॥
ityuktaḥ sa tadā tena taṃ praṇamya yayau nṛpaḥ |
sapatnīko dadarśātha pattanaṃ svaṃ jaharṣa ca ॥6॥
amātyau senayā sārddhaṃ nāgarairīyaturnṛpam |
āliliṃgurnṛpaṃ kecinnemurdūrāttathāntike ॥7॥
praviveśa puraṃ rājā sarvaiḥ sākaṃ dhvajālimat|
siktamārgaṃ cārugaṃdhaṃ nānāvāditraśabditam ॥8॥
ūcuḥ parasparaṃ lokāḥ purīyaṃ śobhate'dhunā|
yathā nārī patiṃ prāpya yathā'ndho vāpi sadṛśam ॥9॥
iti śṛṇvannṛyānasthastathā sā cārūhāsinī |
vastrālaṃkāraśobhāḍhyau stūyamānau mudānvitau ॥10॥
ubhau viviśatū ramyaṃ puraṃ nānarddhisaṃkulam |
visṛjya sarvalokaṃ tau vastrālaṃkāra mauktikaiḥ ॥11॥
tāṃbūlaisteṣu yāteṣu bhavanaṃ svamagacchatām |
tataḥ śubhe dine rājā dakṣaprāsādamāgatam ॥12॥
yatkauṃḍinyapure'kāri purā dakṣeṇa dhīmatā|
tatrārcayāmāsa vibhuṃ sarvadā taṃ vināyakam |
upavāsaparo nityaṃ tanmaṃtramavaloḍayan ॥13॥
bhojane śayane yāne vacane śvasanepi ca |
tameva ciṃtayannityamananyamanasā nṛpaḥ ॥14॥
jale sthale nabhomārge svarge deve nare drume |
bhakṣye peye nṛpo'paśyadvināyakamanuttamam ॥15॥
yaṃ yaṃ paśyati taṃ nauti dṛḍhāliṃganatatparaḥ |
nagare taṃ janāḥ sarve piśāca iti menire ॥16॥
tato vināyako'bhyetya dhṛtvā pāṇitale nṛpam |
āha taṃ nṛpa mukto'si kiṃ vāṃchasi vadasva tat ॥17॥
tamuvāca nṛpo nānyaṃ jāne tvaccaraṇāmbujāt |
tato vināyakaḥ prāha putraste cārudarśanaḥ ॥18॥
bhavitā matprasādena guṇī svarṇatanurnṛpa|
vraja svaṃ bhavanaṃ devadvijapūjāparo bhava ॥19॥
sa gatvā bhavanaṃ rājā taccakāra tathaiva ca |
devānāṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca pūjanaṃ tarpaṇaṃ tathā ॥20॥
akarotsarvabhāvena gaṇeśaḥ prīyatāmiti |
alpenaiva tu kālena putrastasyābhavacchubhaḥ ॥21॥
dadau dānānyanekāni putrajanmanimittataḥ |
rukmāṃgadeti nāmāsya cakre dvijavaroditam ॥22॥
vavṛdhe bālako nityaṃ śuklapakṣe yathā śaśī |
cakāra gurusāttaṃ sa śikṣārthaṃ nṛpatiḥ sutam ॥23॥
sa ca śravaṇamātreṇa jagrāha guruṇoditam |
sarvavidyānidhānena dattaṃ yatkapilena ca ॥24॥
so'pi vidyānidhirabhūd gajakarṇa ivāparaḥ |
rūkmāṃgado'ti balavān sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ ॥25॥
tasmai paṭṭābhiṣekaṃ sa cakāra guṇarāśaye|
dadau ca dvijamukhyebhyo dāsoratna dhanāni ca ॥26॥
tato'dhikāṃ mahābhaktiṃ so'pi cakre vināyake |
jajāpaikākṣaraṃ mantraṃ pituḥ prāptaṃ dine dine ॥27॥
ekasmindivase'raṇyaṃ yuvarājā viveśa ha |
mṛgayāṃ vyacaradbhūsti jaghāna navayānmṛgān ॥28॥
atiśrānto dadarśātha muneḥ kasyacidāśramam |
nānāvṛkṣalatājālaṃ tyaktavairamṛgānvitam ॥29॥ 
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe rukmāṃgadābhiṣekavarṇanaṃ nāma saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥27॥

<adhyāya 28 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca ||
tato rukmāṃgado'paśyadṛṣiṃ vācaknaviṃ śubham |
mukundāṃ tasya patnīṃ ca snigdha maṃjula bhāṣiṇīm || 1 ||
atiśrānto narapatirnamaścakre ubhāvapi |
tasminmunau gate snātuṃ yayāce nṛpasattamaḥ ||2||
māturmukunde me dehi śītalaṃ jalamuttamam |
vinā jalaṃ mama prāṇā gamiṣyanti yamakṣayam || 3 ||
ākarṇya tasya vācaṃ sā jagāda madanāturā |
tvādṛśaṃ purūṣaṃ kvāpi mārādapyati sundaram ||4||
na deveṣu na nāgeṣu yakṣagaṃdharva puṃjayoḥ |
paśyāmi cāru sarvāgaṃ mate me hṛdayaṃ tvayi ||5 ||
atyāsaktaṃ tvadadharāmṛtapāne ca dehi tat |
muniruvāca ||
vyalīkaṃ sa tu śrutvaiva śrāntatvād bhṛśaduḥkhita ||6 ||
tilottamottamāṃ tāṃ sa jitendriya tayā jagau |
tyaja prauḍhiṃ na me cittaṃ pāradāryaṃ vigarhite ||7||
vināyaka prabhāveṇa saktaṃ naivātra karhicit |
na jalaṃ pātumicchāmi tvayā dattaṃ suduṣṭayā||8||
ṛṣerāśrama ityevaṃ yāto yāsya ito'śubhe |
taṃ gantumudyataṃ pāṇiṃ dhṛtvā 'bravīvaddhadāturā ||9||
mukundovāca ||
balātkāreṇa yo'nyasya striyaṃ dharṣitumicchati |
sa eva narakaṃ yāti na svayaṃ yātitāmapi || 10 ||
kṛte tretāyuge brahmā strīṇāṃ svātantryamabhyadhāt |
na karoṣi yadā vākyaṃ tadā bhasma bhaviṣyasi || 11 ||
athavā rājyavibhraṣṭaṃ kariṣye vanacāriṇam |
muniruvāca ||
ityuktvā dhāvamānā sā kāmamārgaṇapīḍitā || 12 ||
rabhasā''liṃgayāmāsa cucumbe vadanaṃ haṭhāt |
tato rūkmāṃgadastāṃ tu balāccikṣepa dūrataḥ || 13 ||
mūrchitā patitā bhūmau rambheva marutā hatā |
utthitāṃ tāṃ tadā prāha paradāra viraktadhīḥ || 14||
rukmāṃgado mahābhāge munipatnyavivekini |
parapuṃsi mano yasyāḥ sā vai nirayabhāgbhavet || 15 ||
na me manaścalet kvāpi yadi śuṣyen mahodadhiḥ |
evaṃ nirākṛtā tena taṃ śaśāpa ruṣānvitā || 1 6||
yayā'haṃ kaṣṭamagamaṃ tathā tvaṃ kuṣṭhabhāgbhava |
yataste vajrāt sārabhūtaṃ na ca drutam || 17||
itthaṃ vadatīṃ tāṃ rājā nirbhartsya bahudhā tataḥ |
tvarayā nirgatastasmādāśramād bhṛśaduḥkhitaḥ || 18||
svaṃ dadarśa śarīraṃ sa śvetaṃ baka śarīravat |
kuṣṭharogayutaṃ kāntyā hīnaṃ ca bhṛśa kutsitam || 19||
cintārṇave tadā magna idamāha gajānanam |
kimaparāddhaṃ mayā te'dya kathamatrāgato' pyaham ||20 ||
prāptaḥ kathaṃ nu duṣṭāyā munipatnyā samāgamam |
tvayā nūnaṃ siddhipate duṣṭāḥ saṃvardhitā bhṛśam ||2 1 ||
avatārāśca sādhūnāṃ rakṣaṇāya bibharṣi hi |
iyaṃ na nāśitā duṣṭā svairiṇī rupagarvitā || 22 ||
kathaṃ mama śarīraṃ tat kāṃcanasparddhi suṃdaram |
ivāmavasthāṃ saṃprāptaḥ kena vā duṣṭakarmaṇā ||23 ||
tava bhakti yathā pūrvaṃ sadā kurve yathāvidhi |
tvadanyaṃ naiva śaraṇaṃ yāmi nātha gajānana ||24||
nedaṃ śarīraṃ vaktraṃ vā darśayiṣye janaṃ prati |
prāyopaveśanenedaṃ śoṣayiṣye kalevaram ||25||
niścityaivaṃ narapatirāsa nyagrodhasannidhau |
bhṛtyā itastato dhāvannekṣante sma ca taṃ nṛpam ||2 6||
pravṛttāyāṃ niśāyāṃ ca jagmuḥ svaṃ svaṃ niketanam |
svāmi sevakayorjātā gatiścakrāhvayoriva || 27||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe prāyopaveśanaṃ nāmāṣṭāviṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ||28||

<adhyāya 29 prāraṃbhaḥ>

muniruvāca ||
tasminvaṭe samāsīnaḥ kasmiściddivase nṛpaḥ |
dūrāddadarśa devarṣi nāradaṃ munipuṃgavam || 1 ||
nanāma prārthayāmāsa kṣaṇaṃ viśrāmyatāmiti |
uttatāra nabhomārgānnāradaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ ||2||
pūjayitvā yathāśakti prapaccha munimādarāt |
ahaṃ rukmāṃgado nāma bhīmaputro mahābalaḥ ||3 ||
mṛgayāṃ vyacaranvācaknaverāśramamāgataḥ |
jalayāccā kṛtā tatra tṛṣitena mayā'nagha ||4||
tasya patnī bhṛśaṃ naṣṭā kāmārtā māmacuṃbata |
tasminmunau gate snātuṃ duṣṭabhāvena cetasā ||5||
uvāca māṃ bhajasveti kāmabāṇa prapīḍitā |
jitendriya tayā deva prasādātsā nirākṛtā ||6||
aśapad duḥkhitā'tyantaṃ sā māṃ niṣṭhura cetasā |
kuṣṭhī bhava mahāduṣṭa sakāmāṃ tyajase yataḥ ||7||
śrutvaivaṃ naṣṭavacanaṃ nirgato'haṃ tadāśramāt |
śvetakuṣṭhī tadā jāto vada me niṣkṛtiṃ mune ||8||
viyogānmama bhīmo'pi magnaḥ syād duḥkhasāgare |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasya nāradaḥ prāha viśvavit ||9||
upāyaṃ tasya kuṣṭhasya nāśāya karuṇāyutaḥ |
nārada uvāca ||
āgacchatā mayā mārge dṛṣṭamāścaryamuttamam || 10||
vidarbhe nagaraṃ khyātaṃ kadambamiti saṃjñayā |
tatprāsāde mayā dṛṣṭā mūrti vaināyakī śubhā || 11 ||
ciṃtāmaṇiriti khyātā sarveṣāṃ sarvakāmadā |
tasyāgrato mahākuṃḍaṃ gaṇeśa padapūrvakam || 12 ||
kaścicchūdro mahākuṣṭhī jarājarjarito nṛpa |
tīrthayātrā prasaṃgena kadambapuramāgataḥ || 13 ||
gaṇeśakuṃḍe snātvaiva divyadehamavāpa saḥ |
vināyakasvarūpaistu gaṇairānatitambarāt || 14||
vimānavaramāruhya sa gataḥ sthānamuttamam |
yatra gatvā na śocanti na patanti punaḥ kvacit || 1 5||
dṛṣṭo mayaiva rājendra tatra snānāya sāṃpratam |
snātvā devaṃ samabhyarca cintitārthapradaṃ vibhum || 1 6||
dehi dānāni viprebhya sadyaḥ pūto bhaviṣyasi |
jīrṇāṃ tvacaṃ parityajya surūpī bhujago yathā || 17||
brahmovāca ||
iti vāṇīṃ nāradoktāṃ niśamya nṛpasattama |
na kiṃciduktavān vākyaṃ magna ānaṃdasāgare || 18||
gaṃtuṃ samudyate tasminnārade munipuṃgave |
praṇipatya tato'pṛcchat prapūjya ca muniṃ punaḥ || 1 9||
rukmāṃgada uvāca ||
tasminkṣetre purā kena siddhiḥ prāptā śubhānagha ||20 ||
mūrtiśca sthāpitā kena maṇiratnamayī śubhā |
vaināyakīti me śaṃsa paraṃ kautūhalaṃ mune ||2 1 ||
bhavādṛśānāṃ sādhūnāṃ paropakaraṇe matiḥ |
anyathā bhramaṇe kṛtyaṃ na lokeṣu pradṛśyate ||2 2||
lokeṣu varṣate meghaḥ śeṣeṇa dhriyate dharā |
upakārāya sūryo'pi bhramate harniśaṃ dvija ||2 3 ||
samasya sarvabhūteṣu sarvajñasya tavāgrataḥ |
anabhijñena mūḍhena kiṃ vaktavyaṃ dayānidhe ||24||
pṛcche tathāpi devarṣe saṃśayacchedanāya vai |
nārada uvāca ||
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā bhūpa lokānugrahakāraka || 25 ||
tṛptohaṃ tava vākyena sarvaṃ ca kathayāmi te || 26|| 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe nāradāgamanaṃ nāmaikonatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||2 9||

<adhyāya 30 prāraṃbhaḥ>

nārada uvāca ॥
kadācidamarāvatyāṃ śakraṃ dṛṣṭuṃ gato'smyaham |
sa māṃ saṃpūjya vidhivaduvācāvanato bhṛśam ॥1॥
indra uvāca ॥
kiṃcidāścaryabhūtaṃ me saṃtoṣāya mune vada|
bhramase sarvalokāṃstvaṃ viditaṃ sarvamasti te ॥2॥
nārada uvāca ॥
mṛtyuloke mayā dṛṣṭo gautamasyāśramo mahān |
nānā vṛkṣalatājālairnānākṣigaṇairyutaḥ ॥3॥
ahilyāsahitaṃ tatra gautamaṃ dṛṣṭavānaham |
rūpaṃ vilokya tasyāstu jāto'haṃ kāmavihvalaḥ ॥4॥
yasyā rūpeṇa sāvitrī śacī lakṣmī girīndrajā|
urvaśī menakā rambhā loke khyātā tilottamā ॥5॥
keśābālā yayā'kāri sā'nasūyā'pyarūndhatī|
chāyā saṃjñā raverbhāryā kaśyapasya ca yā'ditiḥ ॥6॥
sadṛśī naiva kā'pisyānnāgapatnīṣu vā kvacit |
na ca me rocate gānaṃ na pūjā na ca bhojanam ॥7॥
brahmacarya ca me svīyaṃ nidrāṃ ca na labhe kvacit |
tvarāvānahamāyāto dṛṣṭuṃ sādhvamarāvatīm ॥8॥
tucchāṃ paśya imāṃ devīṃ vinā tāmamarāvatīm |
nārada uvāca ॥
iti śakraṃ bruvannantarhito'haṃ nṛpate vara ॥9॥
antarhite mayi tadā madvākyaṃ manasā smaran |
jambhabhedī matsaketu viddho mūrchāmavāpa saḥ ॥10॥
paśye kadā munerbhāryāṃ gautamasyetya cintayat |
kadā'dharāmṛtaṃ prāpya mucyeyaṃ madanānalāt ॥11॥
jīvituṃ naiva paśye'nyaṃ tadāśleṣaṃ vinā śubham |
evaṃ niścitya saṃkalpaṃ gautamo'bhūtsajambhahā ॥12॥
citayanneva tāṃ mārge munerāśramamāyayau|
dadarśa tāmahalyāṃ tu snātuṃ yāte sa gautame ॥13॥
abhyaṃtaragataḥ proce priye śayyāṃ śubhāṃ kuru |
sā covāca japaṃ tyaktvā kathamadyāgato gṛham ॥14॥
divaiva suratecchāṃ kiṃ kuruṣe'tivigarhitām |
gautama uvāca ॥
ahaṃ snātuṃ gato yāvattāvaddevā varāpsarāḥ ॥15॥
tatraiva snātumāyātā jātā nagnā'kṣigocarā |
bimbādharā sucārvaṃgī cārupīna payodharā ॥16॥
na lagnaṃ me mano devi jape kāmāstra pīḍitam|
tato'hamāśramaṃ yāto rati dehi priye'dhunā ॥17॥
nocetkāmāgninā dagdhaṃ mṛtaṃ māṃ kva nu paśyasi |
śape tvāṃ pravajiṣye vā nigrahīṣye manobhṛśam ॥18॥
ahalyovāca |
svādhyāyaṃ devapūjāṃ ca tyaktvā kiṃ prārthyate ratiḥ |
nocitaṃ tava brahmarṣe tathā'pyājñāṃ karomyaham ॥19॥
bhartuḥ śuśrūṣaṇādanyo dharmo nāsti striyāḥ kvacit |
nārada uvāca |
svarākṛti svabhāvaiḥ sā jñātvā taṃ svāminaṃ nijam ॥20॥
viveśa śayane rantumahalyā saha vajriṇā|
niḥśaṃkaṃ cumbanāśleṣa nīvīvisraṃsanādibhiḥ ॥21॥
ākṛtyā gautamasyaivaṃ cikrīḍe jambhahā tayā|
divyāngandhānupāghrāya cakitā śaṃkitā bhṛśam ॥22॥
tarkayāmāsa manasi kimayaṃ kūṭarupavān |
kalaṃko'yaṃ mama bhṛśaṃ candrasyeva bhavennu kim ॥23॥
ubhe kule me naṣṭe kiṃ duṣṭasyāsya tu saṃgamāt |
darśayiṣye kathaṃ loke'yaśaḥśyāmaṃ mukhaṃ tvidam ॥24॥
nayiṣyati gatiṃ kāṃ me priyo bhartā tu māṃ muniḥ|
papraccha taṃ śaṭhaṃ kopāt ko'si tvaṃ kūṭarupadhṛk ॥25॥
viśvastā svāmirūpeṇa vada no cecchapāmi te |
ityuktaḥ śāpabhīto'sā āviścakre nijaṃ vapuḥ ॥26॥
divyābharaṇa saṃyuktaṃ kirīṭa kaṭakānvitam |
kuṃḍalādbhuta dīptābhirvilasanmukhapaṃkajam ॥27॥
uvāca sa tatastāṃ tu viddhi māṃ tvaṃ śacīpatim|
lāvaṇyadarśanātte'haṃ vihvalo madanāgniā ॥28॥
na lebhe kutraciccharma tata itthaṃ kṛtaṃ mayā|
ito'pi māṃ bhajasva tvaṃ trailokyeśvaramādarāt ॥29॥
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasya munipatnī ruṣānvitā|
vamantīva mukhājjvālāṃ jagāda tridaśādhipam ॥30॥
asya te vapuṣo mūḍha madbhartari samāgate|
avasthā kā bhavenmanda na jāne'haṃ śatakrato ॥31॥
pātivratyaṃ tvayā bhagnaṃ duṣṭa pāpīyasā mama |
kāmavasthāṃ gamiṣyāmi śāpād gautamavāgbhavāt ॥32॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe'halyādharṣaṇaṃ nāma triṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥30॥

<adhyāya 31 prāraṃbhaḥ>

rūkmāṃgada uvāca |
āgate gautame ko vā vṛttānto'bhūnmahāmune |
vada me sakalaṃ taṃ tu jijñāsā mahatī mama ॥1॥
nārada uvāca |
nityakarma samāpyaiva svāśramaṃ gautamo yayau|
ākāryaṃ nijapatnīṃ tāṃ dehi pādodakaṃ mama॥2॥
kimarthaṃ nāgatā'dya tvaṃ saṃmukhaṃ mama pūrvavat |
nānītamāsanaṃ kasmātkathaṃ cāṭu na bhāṣase ॥3॥
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasya vepamānā lateva sā |
adhomukhī vinirgamya muhurtānmunimāyayau ॥4॥
sāṣṭāṃgaṃ patitā bhūmau tatpādau parimastakā |
vihvalā śāpabhītā sā śanairatha jagau munim ॥5॥
uṣasi tvaṃ prayāto'si snānaṃ kartuṃ nijaṃ vidhim |
tavarūpadharo duṣṭo devendro māmuvāca ha ॥6॥
dṛṣṭā varā'psarobhyo'pi suṃdarī kāminī mayā |
na me mano jape daive vidhau nitye sthiraṃ bhavet |
parāvṛtyāgatastasmādratiṃ me dehi śobhane ॥7॥
tvameveti mayā bhāntyā kṛtaṃ vākyaṃ tathaiva tat |
divyagandhānupāghrāya vikalpo me'bhavatpunaḥ ॥8॥
durātman ko'si no brūhi nocedbhasma bhaviṣyasi |
iti śāpabhayājjātaḥ prakaṭo balasūdanaḥ ॥9॥
tāvadeva bhavadvākyaṃ śrutaṃ tu munisattama |
lajjayā'nāgatā śīghramaparādhaṃ kṣamasva me ॥10॥
svayaṃ nivedane'doṣo doṣo'para nivedane |
mantrāyurgṛharudhraśrī ratayaścauṣadhāni ca ॥11॥
mānāpamāna dānāni prakaṭāni na kārayet |
itthaṃ niśamya sa muniḥ kopavyākulitendriyaḥ ॥12॥
śaśāpa vanināṃ svīyāṃ duḥśīlāṃ tvaṃ śilā bhava |
nājñāsīrme svarupaṃ taṃ svabhāvaṃ ceṣṭitāni ca ॥13॥
pare puṃsi nimagnaṃ te yataśceto'tikāmuke |
yadā dāśarathī rāmo bhamadrājā vane vane ॥14॥
tasyāṃghrisparśanādeva svaṃ rupaṃ pratipatsyase |
nārada uvāca |
tadaiva sā śilā jātā taponidhī vacobalāt ॥15॥
tasyā śāpaṃ samākarṇya cakampe pākaśāsanaḥ |
prakampanasya saṃyogāddhimavat parvato yathā ॥16॥
tarkayāmāsa manasi kathaṃ kāryaṃ mayā'dhunā|
samudramadhye kūpe vā taḍāge kamale'thavā ॥17॥
līno bhūtvā tadā sthāsye tadā jñāsyati māṃ muniḥ |
ato biḍālarūpeṇa vicacāra sa vajrabhṛta ॥18॥
gautamastamacakṣāṇo gṛhe dvāri tathāśrame |
kva gato dānavaripuryo me bhāryāvidūṣakaḥ ॥19॥
dhyānena bubudhe taṃ tu kṣaṇena munisattmaḥ |
na te bhasma kariṣyāmi devendro'si yataḥ khalaḥ ॥20॥
śapāmi tvāṃ śacībhartaḥ sahasrabhagavānbhava |
śrutavānvacanaṃ yāvadroṣeṇa munineritam ॥21॥
tāvaddadarśa svaṃ dehaṃ sahasrabhaga cihnitam |
tato duḥkhārṇave magnaḥ śuśoca balavṛtrahā ॥22॥
indra uvāca |
ahaṃ śikṣāpito vṛddhairnānā dharmānanekaśaḥ |
vṛddhānāṃ vacanaṃ yanme na vicāritamādarāt ॥23॥
sva buddhīrhitakṛtsarvā vināśāya pareritā |
gurorgarīyasī buddhiḥ kṣayadā kāminīmatiḥ ॥24॥
kuto nāradavākyena yāto'haṃ tāmaniṃditām |
devarājo bhavannāsyaṃ lokānāṃ darśaye katham ॥25॥
divyadeho gataḥ kvādya nijabhāryāṃ vade nu kim |
dhiṅgmāṃ ca dhiksmaraṃ yena prāpito garhitāṃ daśām ॥26॥
prāṇibhirbhujyate karma śubhaṃ vā yadi vā'śubham |
tiryag yoniṃ samāsādya kṣapayiṣye'ghamātmanaḥ ॥27॥
nalinī kuḍmale tiṣṭhe hīndra gopakarūpadhṛka | ॥28॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe śakraśāpavarṇanaṃ nāmaikatriṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥31

<adhyāya 32 prāraṃbhaḥ>

nārada uvāca |
śakre tu nalinīṃ yāte tatpuraṃ tvahamāgataḥ |
bṛhaspatipurogāṃstāṃstatrāpaśyaṃ sthitān surān ॥1॥
tebhyo hyakathayaṃ sarvamubhayoḥ śāpakāraṇam |
ahalyāyā mahendrasya saṃyogaṃ ca virūpatām ॥2॥
sahasrabhagatāṃ yātaḥ śakro gautamaśāpataḥ |
ahalyādharṣaṇāddevāstatsaṃgātsā śilā'bhavat ॥3॥
brahmovāca |
śrutvā te nāradoktaṃ tat sarve devāḥ śucānvitāḥ|
atiduḥkhādrudantaste niśvāsocchavāsa vibhramaiḥ ॥4॥
devā ūcuḥ |
yenākāri śataṃ yajñā dānavā yena nirjitāḥ |
trailokyaṃ pālitaṃ yena bhuktamaindraṃ padaṃ śubham ॥5॥
pūjitā bahavo devā brāhmaṇā brahmavittamāḥ |
bhuktā nānāvidhā bhogā anyeṣāmatidurlabhāḥ ॥6॥
kutra sthāsyatyasau devo bhokṣyate svapsyate katham |
kamadya śaraṇaṃ yāmaḥ svakṛte tatkṛte'pi vā ॥7॥
pālayiṣyati kāṃ'smānvai padamaindraṃ śacīṃ tathā |
kathaṃ prasannatāṃ yāyād gautamo munisattamaḥ ॥8॥
svīyabhāryā viyuktaḥsaṃstatkṛtāgaḥ smaran rūṣā |
anyopāyaṃ na paśyāmo gautamasya prasādataḥ ॥9॥
tasmānnārada yāsyāmo gautamaṃ sānvituṃ munim |
evaṃ te niryayurdevā nāradena samanvitāḥ ॥10॥
gautamaṃ te samāsādya baddhāṃjalipuṭā munim |
tuṣṭavurvividhairvākyai stameva śaraṇaṃ gatāḥ ॥11॥
devā ūcuḥ |
tava prabhāvaṃ vaktuṃ no mune śaktirna vidyate |
garimāṇaṃ vadet ko nu merorhimavato'pi ca |
vṛṣṭidhārā rajo bhūmergaṃgāyāḥ sikatā api ॥12॥
kamambudherguṇānviṣṇor gaṇayetko nu mūḍhadhīḥ |
prātarevoptabījānāṃ madhyāhne sasyasaṃpadaḥ ॥13॥
saṃpāditāstvayā pūrvamavitā ṛṣisattamāḥ|
vālakhilyairyajiṃ kṛtvā para indro vinirmitaḥ ॥14॥
brahmādibhiḥ prārthitāste pakṣiṇāṃ tamakalpayan |
payasāṃ nidhirekena prāśitaśculakena yat ॥15॥
aparā sṛṣṭirārabdhā gādhiputreṇa dhīmatā |
stambhito bhuja indrasya cyavanena mahātmanā ॥16॥
tasmātsarvātmanā puṃsāṃ bhavatāṃ sevanaṃ natiḥ |
darśanaṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ pūjā sparśanaṃ pāpanāśanam ॥17॥
upakāreratānāṃ ca dīnānugrahakāriṇām |
indrārthe śaraṇaṃ yātānkṛpāṃ kartuṃ tvamarhasi ॥18॥
gautama uvāca |
bhavatāṃ darśanaṃ carmacakṣuṣāṃ naiva jāyate |
mama puṇyena jātaṃ tat kāmasaṃpādanaṃ nṛṇām ॥19॥
janmāśrama tapodānaṃ deha ātmā vratāni ca |
sārthakānī kṣaṇenaiva jātāni bhavatāṃ khalu ॥20॥
idānīṃ prārthitaṃ kiṃ vastannirupyaṃ mamāgrataḥ |
śakyaṃ cettat kariṣye'haṃ bhavatsmṛtibalena tat ॥21॥
muniruvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā jahṛṣuste divaukasaḥ |
candrodaye yathā harṣaṃ prāpnoti jaladhiḥ sphuṭam ॥22॥
yathā vā bālabhāṣābhiḥ pitarau mudamīyatuḥ |
prārthanāṃ cakrire sarve gautamaṃ taṃ mahāmunim ॥23॥
devā ūcuḥ |
īśvarasyāparādhena kāmo bhasmatvamāgataḥ |
āgaskārī tvayā śakro na ca prāṇairviyojitaḥ ॥24॥
idānīṃ sa yathāsthānaṃ svakīyaṃ prāpnuyānmune |
kṣamitvā tasya cāgāṃsi sarveṣāṃ vacanāddhi naḥ ॥25॥
kṛte tasminprasāde tu sarveṣāṃ vāṃchitaṃ bhavet |
nārada uvāca |
śrutvā devasamūhasya vacanāni sa gautamaḥ ॥26॥
prahasya pratyuvācedaṃ sarvān devagaṇānprati |
gautama uvāca |
nāmāpi tasya na grāhayaṃ patitasya kṛtāgasaḥ ॥27॥
kapaṭasya śaṭhasyāpi duṣṭasyāpyavivekinaḥ |
anutāpavihīnasya niṣkṛtirnaiva vidyate ॥28॥
tathāpi bhavatāṃ vākyātkariṣye tatpriyaṃ surāḥ |
bhavanto'pi yadā ruṣṭāstadā śāpaḥ patenyayi ॥29॥
pūto bhavati janturhi bahubhiryo 'nugṛhyate |
ata ekaṃ vade mantraṃ tasmai taṃ pradiśantu ca| ॥30॥
sarvakartā sarvahartā sarvapātā kṛpānidhiḥ |
vināyako devadevo brahmaviṣṇuśivātmakaḥ ॥31॥
ṣaḍakṣarastasya mantro mahāsiddhi pradāyakaḥ|
upadeśe kṛte tasya divyadeho bhavettu saḥ ॥32॥
bhagāni tasya yāvanti tāvannetro bhaviṣyati |
svarājyaṃ prāpsyate śakra iti satyaṃ vadāmi vaḥ ॥33॥
abhidhāya surānitthaṃ tūṣṇīmāsītsa gautamaḥ|
taṃ ca te pūjayitvā tu namaścakrurmudānvitāḥ ॥34॥
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya punaḥ prāpyānujñāṃ yayuḥ surāḥ |
praśaṃsanto muniṃ tatra yatrāste balavṛtrahā ॥35॥
gautamā jñānasaṃpannāt sātviko'nyo na vartate ॥36॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe mantrakathanaṃ nāma dvātriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥32॥

<adhyāya 33 prāraṃbhaḥ>

nārada uvāca | ūcurdevā vṛtrahaṇaṃ bahiryāhi śatakrato|
vayaṃ hi sahitāstatra nāradena surarṣiṇā ॥1॥
taṃ gatvā gautamamuniṃ prasādya tvāmihāgatāḥ |
upāyastena kathito varo dattastavāpi ca ॥2॥
doṣe jāte svayaṃ santaḥ khyāpayanti janeṣu tam |
tatra pratividhiṃ samyakkurvanti tannirarthakam ॥3॥
ācchādane doṣavṛddhiḥ khyāpane tu laye bhavet |
tasmāttvamapi devendra bahirāgatya taṃ vada ॥4॥
devarṣiṃ prati taṃ brūhi kurūpāyaṃ munīritam |
vaināyakaṃ mahāmantraṃ gṛhāṇa tvaṃ ṣaḍakṣaram ॥5॥
brahmā dadarśa cāṃguṣṭhuṃ vivāhe girijeśayoḥ |
caskanda sa tadā reto lajjito'vāṅmukho gataḥ ॥6॥
jñātvā maheśaḥ kṛtavānnirdoṣaṃ tamupāyataḥ |
iti vāṇīṃ sa śrutvaiva devarṣigaṇanirmitām ॥7॥
ājagāma bahiḥ śakro nalinīkośato nṛpa|
sarveṣāṃ tatra devānāṃ śrutvā vākyāni sādaram ॥8॥
pūyaśoṇitadigdhāṃgo malinaḥ pūtigandhavān |
dṛṣṭvā tathāvidhaṃ devā nemuḥ sarve sureśvarama ॥9॥
ācchādya ghrāṇarandhrāṇi vastrāgrairnṛpasattama |
susnātaṃ punarācāntaṃ tamindraṃ vākpatistadā ॥10॥
ṣaḍakṣaraṃ mahāmantraṃ gaṇeśasyopadiṣṭavān |
upadeśe kṛte tena divyadeho'bhavacca saḥ ॥11॥
sahasranayanaḥ śrīmānbabhau sūryaṃ ivākaraḥ |
tato vādyaninādaiśca jayaśabrdairdivaukasām ॥12॥
gandharvāṇāṃ gānaravairnāditā vidiśo diśaḥ |
mumucuḥ puṣpavarṇāṇi sarve devā mudānvitāḥ ॥23॥
āśiṣo'tha daduḥ sarve munayo nāradādayaḥ |
āliliṃgurmudā devāstuṣṭuvuścāpare ca tam ॥14॥
ke'pyūcustaṃ sumanasaḥ sanāthā hi vayaṃ tvayā |
vinā tvāṃ naiva śobhāmo vinā caṃdraṃ nabho yathā ॥15॥
vinā svapitarau bālā na sukhaṃ yānti sarvathā|
tathā vinā vayaṃ tvāṃ hi labhāmaḥ śarma na kvacit ॥16॥
muniruvāca |
śrutvetthaṃ devavacanaṃ jaharṣa ca śatakratuḥ |
uvāca ca prasannātmā tathyaṃ vākyaṃ surānprati ॥18॥
indra uvāca |
mayā kṛtaṃ karma suduṣkaraṃ yad devarṣivākyena vimohitena |
labdhaṃ phalaṃ duḥsahamadya sarvairuddhārito'haṃ duritaprakopāt ॥18॥
namāmi sarvānamaraprabarhām̐nṛṣīṃśca sarvān suguru prabhāvān |
uddhartumātmānamato'khilā māṃ, saṃtrātumarhāḥ śaraṇaṃ prapannam ॥19॥
kathaṃ prayatno racito bhavadbhiḥ, prasādanāyāsya hi gautamasya |
kathaṃ nu mantraṃ paramaṃ madarthe, sa uktavāṃstat kathayantu sarve ॥20॥
devā ūcuḥ |
muniṃ puraskṛtya guruṃ vayaṃ ca, gatā muniṃ taṃ praṇipatya samyak |
prasādito vāgamṛtairvicitraiḥ , sa yācyamāno'pyavadat svamantram ॥21॥
yasyopadeśena sahasranetro, jāto bhavānsarva sukhāya deva |
prayāhi deva tvamarāvatīṃ svāṃ, praśādhi sarvān vibudhāṃśca lokān ॥22॥
indra uvāca |
nāhaṃ prayāsye svapurīṃ prasādaṃ, vinā gaṇeśasya surarṣivaryāḥ |
vrajantu yūyaṃ kṛtasādhukṛtyā, dhāmāni divyāni mudā ramantaḥ ॥23॥
etāvatā'laṃ prakaṭīkṛto'haṃ, lajjāvilīno bahudurgatiśca |
prasādito yanmunirugratejā, bhavat prasādād bahunetratā me ॥ 24 ॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe trayastriṃśatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥33॥

<adhyāya 34 prāraṃbhaḥ>

nārada uvāca |
kadaṃbavṛkṣasya tale nidhāya, nāsāgradṛṣṭiṃ paramāsane saḥ |
mano nirudhyātha jajāpa mantraṃ, ṣaḍakṣaraṃ jambharipurnarendra ॥1॥
sahasravarṣāṇi gatāni tasya, marutpate māruta bhakṣaṇasya|
valmīka gulmāni śarīradeśe, jātāni vai bhūdharavasthirasya ॥2॥
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān gaṇeśo, yaḥ sarvagaḥ sarvavidura tejāḥ |
svatejasā bahvi śaśīnatejāṃ, syācchādayansarva vilocanāni ॥3॥
caturbhujo ratnakirīṭa mālī, cāvaṃgadaḥ kuṃḍalamaṃḍigaṃḍaḥ |
muktāmayaṃ dāma ca nūpure ca, bibhran mahā kaṭisūtramuccaiḥ ॥4॥
yaḥ puṣkarākṣaḥ pṛthu puṣkaro'pi, bṛhatkaraḥ puṣkaraśālimālaḥ |
āvirbabhūvākhila devamūrtiḥ sindūraśāli purato mathonaḥ ॥5॥
taṃ dṛṣṭvā bhayabhīto'bhūt kimidaṃ kimivāgatam |
kathaṃ ca jīvitaṃ me syādasthiprāṇamayasya ca ॥6॥
vighno'yaṃ kiṃ mahānadya na jāne kena nirmitaḥ |
svedasrāvi śarīraṃ me kampate bodhipatravat ॥7॥
itthaṃ viklavitaṃ tasya bubudhe'khiladṛgvibhuḥ |
vināyako mahendraṃ tamuvāca maṃjulaṃ vacaḥ ॥8॥
vināyaka uvāca |
mā bhayaṃ kuru deveśa māṃ na vetsi kathaṃ sura |
yannirguṇaṃ nirvikāraṃ cidānandaṃ sanātanam ॥9॥
kāraṇātītamavyaktaṃ jagatkāraṇakāraṇam |
yaṃ dhyāyasi sadā devaṃ mantreṇānena niścalaḥ ॥10॥
śrānto'si bahu kālaṃ tvam iti pratyakṣatāṃ gataḥ |
tapasā'nena tuṣṭo'haṃ varaṃ dātumihāgataḥ ॥1॥
brahmāṃḍānāmanantānāmutpatti pralayāvanam |
matta eveti viddhi tvaṃ vṛṇu yadvāṃchase'nagha ॥12॥
nārada uvāca |
śrutvā tasya vaco ramyaṃ bubudhe balabhedanaḥ |
bhagavante mahākāyaṃ devadevaṃ vināyakam ॥13॥
nanāma parayā bhaktyā tata utthāya satvaraḥ |
avīvadacchacīkāntaḥ pratyakṣaṃ brahmarūpiṇam ॥14॥
indra uvāca |
brahmādayo'pi no devā vidustvāṃ sadigīśvarāḥ|
guṇāṃstava mahābāho saṣṭisthityantakāriṇaḥ ॥15॥
padaṃ tu kṛtrimaṃ mahyaṃ śatayajñasamudbhavam |
dattaṃ tatrāpyantarāyā bhavanti bahudhā mama ॥16॥
mayā kathaṃ tu vijñeyo mahimā te gajānana |
yasya te'nugrahaḥ pūrṇo bhaviṣyati maheśvara ॥17॥
sa eva mahimānaṃ te jānīyādvighnakāraṇa |
tasya te guṇarūpāṇi vaktuṃ śaktirbhaviṣyati ॥18॥
nirādhāro'khilādhāro nityajñāno'jaro'maraḥ |
nityānandena saṃpūrṇo māyāvī kṣara eva ca ॥19॥
akṣaraḥ paramātmā ca viśvarūpo'khileśvaraḥ |
ugraistapobhistvāṃ jñātvā nirvṛttā sanakādayaḥ ॥20॥
ṣaḍakṣaraprabhāveṇa dṛṣṭosi parameśvara |
purā'yaṃ brahmaṇādiṣṭo mantro me'nugraheṇa vai ॥21॥
uktavāṃścaiva māṃ brahmā yadā'muṃ vismariṣyasi |
tadaiva bhraśyase sthānāddurdaśāṃ caiva yāsyasi ॥22॥
nato mayā'ti lubdhena durbhāgyaṃvaśagena te|
dharṣitā munipatnī sā tato durgatimāptavān ॥23॥
punaśca dhiṣaṇoktena tena maṃtreṇa dṛṣṭavān |
svarūpaṃ tava deveśa sahasranayano'dhunā ॥24॥
anyamekaṃ varaṃ yāce yatastvaṃ cintitārthadaḥ|
idaṃ kadambanagaraṃ cintāmaṇipuraṃ tviti ॥25॥
anuṣṭhānaphalaṃ prāptaṃ yatte dṛḍhapadāmbujam |
idānīṃ tu varaṃ yāce yaṃ taṃ me dehi vighnapa ॥26॥
tava vismaraṇaṃ deva na bhavenme tathā kuru |
mano me ramatāṃ tava pādāmbuje vibho ॥27॥
adyaprabhṛti loke'smin khyātiṃ yātu gajānana |
cintāmaṇīti tīrthaṃ tu saraśca prathatāmidam ॥28॥
asminsnānena dānena dharmakāmārthamuktayaḥ |
janānāṃ siddhayaḥ santu prasādātte jagadguro ॥29॥
muniruvāca |
ākarṇya vacanaṃ śākraṃ meghagaṃbhāranisvanaḥ|
uvāca ślakṣṇayā vācā vighneśo jagatāṃ patiḥ ॥30॥
vināyaka uvāca |
idaṃ saṃpatsyate sarvaṃ yattvayā prārthitaṃ vibho |
anya eko varaste'stu svapade tvaṃ sthiro bhava ॥31॥
avismṛtaśca satataṃ mama te'stu sureśvara |
yadā ca saṃkaṭaṃ te syāttadā māṃ smara vāsara ॥32॥
āvirbhāvena te kāryaṃ sarvaṃ saṃpādaye'niśam|
idaṃ cintāmaṇipuraṃ khyātaṃ bhuvi bhaviṣyati ॥33॥
kadambapuramityetattīrthaṃ cintāmaṇīti ca |
atra snānena sarveṣāṃ siddhayo'pi kare sthitāḥ ॥34॥
ciṃtitaṃ ca pradāsyāmi ciṃtāmaṇivināyakaḥ|
nārada uvāca |
evaṃ varaṃ tato labdhvā svataḥsiṃdhumānayaddhariḥ ॥35॥
kṛtvābhiṣekaṃ taddvārā pūjayāmāsa taṃ vibhum |
gajānanaṃ mahābhāgaṃ parivārasamanvitam ॥36॥
pūjitaḥ suranāthena tatraivāntardadhe vibhuḥ |
sthāpayāmāsa śakro'pi sphāṭikāṃ mūrtimādarāt ॥37॥
vaināyakīṃ śubhāṃ divyāṃ sarvāvayavasuṃdarām |
kārayāmāsa vipulam prāsādaṃ ratnakāṃcanaiḥ ॥38॥
natvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya śakraḥ svaṃ padamabhyagāt |
tadetadbhuvi vikhyātaṃ ciṃtāmaṇisaro mahat ॥39॥
adyāpi sā śubhajalā gaṃgā śakrasya śāsanāt |
kṛtvā'bhiṣekaṃ tanmūrtau yāti svaṃ dhāma sarvadā ॥40॥
evaṃ te kṣetra mahimā kathito'dbhutadarśanaḥ |
sarvadoṣaharaḥ śrīmān sarvakāmapradaḥ śubha ॥41॥
tatra gatvā mahīpāla snānaṃ kuru yathāvidhi |
sarvadoṣavinirmukto bhaviṣyasi na saṃśayaḥ ॥42॥
brahmovāca |
tato yayau muniḥ śīghramanupṛcchya ca taṃ nṛpam |
āśīrbhirabhinandyaiva sa rukmāṃgadamādarāt | ॥43॥ 
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇa upāṃsanākhaṃḍe ciṃtāmaṇitīrthavarṇanaṃ nāma catustriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥34 ॥

<adhyāya 35 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
devarṣau tu gate tasminnṛpaḥ kimakarottadā |
rukmāṃgado me kathaya kathāmetāṃ manoramām || 1 ||
brahmovāca |
kṛtvopadeśaṃ sumahāntameva gate munau nārada nāmni putra |
rukmāṃgado harṣayuto dadarśa senāṃ svakīyāṃ caturaṃgiṇītām ||2 ||
tayā'pi dṛṣṭo nṛpatirvirūpo yaḥ svarṇakānto ratibhartṛrūpaḥ |
āsīt puretthaṃ kathameṣa jātaḥ, saṃśayya papraccha nṛpaṃ nimittam || 3 ||
senānya ūcuḥ |
girīnvanāni sarito bhrāntvā ca vayamāgataḥ |
kṣuttṛṭparītā rājendra tvaddarśana samutsukāḥ ||4||
pade pade prapaśyantaḥ saṃprāptāste padāmbujam |
imāmavasthāṃ dṛṣṭavā te duḥkhād duḥkhataraṃ gatāḥ ||5 ||
kiṃ nimittamabhūttannaḥ kathayasva nṛpottama |
nṛpa uvāca |
ahamagre samāyātastṛṣitaḥ kṣudhitaśca ha || 6||
apaśyaṃ purataḥ śīghraṃ vācaknavi gṛhāśramam |
tatra gatvā mayā dṛṣṭvā tasya patnī śubhānanā ||7||
nāmnā mukuṃdeti śubhā yācitā sā jalaṃ mayā |
sā tu duṣṭā svairiṇī ca māmuvācāśubhaṃ vacaḥ ||8||
ratiṃ kuru mayā sārdhaṃ no cecchāpaṃ dadāmi te |
nirākṛtā mayā sā tu balācchuddhena cetasā ||9||
tad bhartari gate snātuṃ sā māṃ duṣṭā'śapadruṣā |
tatastu vṛkṣamūle'hamupaviṣṭo hi duḥkhitaḥ || 10 ||
prabhāvāt pūrvapuṇyasya dṛṣṭavān nāradaṃ munim |
tena me kathito'riṣṭanāśako vidhiruttamaḥ || 11 ||
cintāmaṇi kṣetragato gaṇeśatīrtha saṃśritaḥ |
mahimā kathitastena tasya tīrthasya vistarāt || 12 ||
tatra snānaṃ samākhyātaṃ muninā divyacakṣuṣā |
ataḥ snātuṃ gamiṣyāmi svadoṣasyāpanuttaye || 13 ||
yāntu sarve mayā sārdhaṃ tatra snātuṃ yadīcchatha |
snātvā dattvā yathāśakti saṃpūjya ca vināyakam || 14||
pūtāstayoḥ prabhāveṇa yāsyāmaḥ svapuraṃ tataḥ |
ka uvāca |
iti te niścayaṃ buddhvā jagmū rājapuraḥsarāḥ|
dṛṣṭavā tīrthaṃ gaṇeśākhyaṃ divyadeho babhau nṛpaḥ || 15 ||
taptakāṃcanavarṇābho yathāpūrvaṃ munīśvara |
tato rukmāṃgado mene nāradoktamṛtaṃ vacaḥ || 16 ||
tatra snātvā dadau dānānyanekāni nṛpastadā |
rukmāṃgado brāhmaṇebhyo mudā paramayā yutaḥ || 17||
vināyakaṃ pūjayitvā tejorāśiṃ dadarśa saḥ |
vimānamarkapratimaṃ brāhmaṇāḥ sevakāśca te || 1 8|
vināyakagaṇairjuṃṣṭamapsaraḥ kinnarairyutam |
natvā nṛpastān papraccha ke yūyaṃ kuta āgatāḥ || 1 9||
dūtāḥ kasya kimatrāsti kāryaṃ tadbrūta sādaram |
brahmovāca –
śrutvā nṛpativākyāni maṃjulāni vimānagā ||2 0 ||
dūtā vināyakasyocurdhanyosi nṛpasattama |
yena te sarvabhāvena dhyātaściṃtāmaṇiḥ prabhu ||2 1 ||
tīrthayātrā kṛtā samyagdānaṃ datvā yathāvidhi |
cintāmaṇiḥ pūjitaśca kṛtakṛtyo'si sāṃpratam ||2 2||
cintitasya pradānāddhi cintāmaṇirayaṃ smṛtaḥ |
vayaṃ ca kṛtakṛtyāḥ smo darśanāttava suvrata ||23||
mahimānaṃ na jānīmastava bhakternṛpottama |
kaḥ yena vacasā buddhyā jīvasyāpyarpaṇena ca ||24||
ārādhitāstvayā deva sarvabrahmāṃḍanāyaka||
vināyakastasya dūtāstena ca preṣitā nṛpa |||25||
utsukaḥ sa uvācāsmān madbhaktaṃ śīghrayāyinaḥ |
rukmāṃgadaṃ vimānena ānayantu mamāntikam ||26||
iti śrutvā vayaṃ yātā ārohasva nabhogamam |
yāhi śīghrataraṃ deva sahāsmābhirvināyakam ||27||
brahmovāca |
iti śrutvā vacasteṣāmāha rūkmāṃgado nṛpaḥ |
kvāhaṃ mandamatirdūtā kva cākhaṃḍita vigrahaḥ ||28||
aprameye'pratarkyaśca cinmātro vibhuravyayaḥ |
sargasthityapyayānāṃ yaḥ kāraṇaṃ kāraṇātigaḥ ||29||
tasyādaro mayi kathaṃ na jāne tīrthajaṃ phalam |
janmāntaragataṃ ki me phalitaṃ puṇyamuttamam || 30 ||
tena vo darśanaṃ jātamagre'ti phaladaṃ śubham |
yūyaṃ dhanyatarā yeṣāṃ pratyakṣo'harniśaṃ vibhuḥ ||31 ||
ityuktvā pūjayāmāsa natvā caraṇapaṃkajam |
prārthayāmāsa sarvāṃstānpitā mama nṛpottama |
brahmaṇyaḥ satyavādī ca bhīmo bhīmaparākramaḥ ||3 2||
vinā taṃ kathamāyāmi mātaraṃ cāruhāsinīm |
tayā'pyārādhito devo devadevo vināyakaḥ || 33 ||
janmāvadhi na cānyaṃ sā manute devatāṃtaram |
dūta uvāca |
evaṃ cettarhi tīrthe 'sminkuru snānaṃ tayorapi |
śreyastasmai ca tasya ca pitre mātre pradīyatām || 35 ||
tatastāvapi neṣyāmo vimānavaramāsthitau ||3 6||
brahmovāca |
iti tad vacanaṃ śrutvā kṛtvā pratikṛtiṃ kuśaiḥ |
kuśo'si kuśaputro'si brahmaṇā nirmitaḥ purā |||3 7||
tvayi snāte tu sa snāto yasyedaṃ granthibaṃdhanam |
evaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya sarveṣāmānupūrvaśaḥ ||3||
grāmyāṇāṃ sarvalokānāṃ cakre snānavidhiṃ nṛpa |
cintāmaṇi kṣetragataṃ tīrthe gāṇeśa saṃjñake || 39 ||
tato rukmāṃgado rājā sabalo dūtavākyataḥ |
vimānavaramāruhya kauṃḍinyaṃ puramāyayau ||40 ||
vādyaghoṣairbrahmaghoṣairgandharvāpsarasāṃ ravaiḥ |
nāditaṃ gaganaṃ tena vimānena diśo daśa ||4 1 ||
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ pradadau śreyo rukmāṃgado nṛpaḥ |
sarveṣāmeva lokānāṃ snānaśreyo vināyake ||42||
dattamātre tu kauśeya snānaje śreyasi dhruvam |
vināyakājñayā'nyāni vimānāni samāyayuḥ ||43 ||
pratyekaṃ te samāruḍhā ekekaṃ gaganecaram |
evaṃ rukmāṃgado bhīmastanmātā cāruhāsinī ||44||
sarve lokā yayustatra yatra devo vināyakaḥ |
evaṃ tannagaraṃ sarvamābālaśvapacāvadhi ||45||
puṇyād gaṇeśatīrthasya snānajāt svargatiṃ gatam |
ka uvāca|
iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ yadyat pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā mune ||46 ||
cintāmaṇi kṣetragataṃ māhātmyaṃ tīrthasambhavam |
yaḥ śṛṇoti naro bhaktyā so'pi tad gatimāpnuyāt ||47||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanā khaṃḍe kādambapuragatavarṇanaṃ nāma paṃcatriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥35 ॥

<adhyāya 36 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
śrutaṃ gaṇeśatīrthasya māhātmyaṃ kamalāsana |
rukmāṃgadasya caritaṃ kauṇḍinya puravāsinām || 1 ||
tathāpi brūhi me brahman mukuṃdācaritaṃ śubhaṃ |
brahmovāca |
gate rūkmāṃgade sā tu jajvāla madanāgninā ||2||
dāvāgninā yathā grīṣme mahāvanasthalā suta |
mukuṃdā nālabhaccharma vane śītala mārute ||3||
latāpuṣpamaye sthāne caṃdracaṃdanavo'pi ca |
tasyā na rocate hāsyaṃ gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ kathāntaram ||4||
annaṃ jalaṃ vihvalāpāssacittāyā munīśvara |
kṣuttṛṭ śrameṇa tasyāstu kṣaṇaṃ nidrā samāyayau ||5 ||
tāmiṃdro bubudhe kāṃtā prasuptā vijane vane |
kāmāturā vihvalā ca rūkmāṃgada kṛte suta || 6 ||
dhṛtvā raukmāṃgadaṃ rūpaṃ bubhuje kāmukāṃ tu tām |
āliliṃga mudā śakro mukuṃdā jahṛṣe bhṛśam ||7||
sā'pi rukmāṃgadaṃ taṃ tu cucumbe subhṛśaṃ suta |
so'pi tasyāḥ kucau pīnau mamarda dṛḍhamuṣṭinā ||8||
vyaṃśukāṃ vyaṃśuko reme niḥśaṃkaṃ sa tayā saha |
tataḥ sā lajjamāneva svagṛhaṃ pratyapadyata ||9||
iṃdro rūkmāṃgado bhūtastatraivāntardadhe suta |
mene rukmāṃgado bhuktastato garbhaṃ dadhau tu sā || .10 ||
suṣuve navame māsi suvelāyāṃ sutaṃ śubham |
cārusarvānavadyāṃgaṃ rūpeṇa madanātigam || 11 ||
tasya śabdena mahatā dharaṇyā patitasya tu |
sanādaṃ daśadigvṛṃdamabhūtkhaṃ bhūrasātalam || 12 ||
pakṣiṇo babhramuḥ sarve uḍḍīyoḍḍīya sarvataḥ |
vācaknaviḥ samāyātastyaktvā svanityakarma ca || 13 ||
mukuṃdācaritaṃ tena naiva buddhaṃ kadācana|
jātakarmādikaṃ sarvaṃ cakāra bhṛṣaharṣitaḥ || 14||
dadau dānaṃ yathāśakti brāhmaṇebhyo yathārhataḥ |
daśāhe tu vyatīte sa nāmakarmākaronmuniḥ || 15 ||
gṛtsametyanujñāto jyotiḥśāstraparairdvijai |
tatastu paṃcame'bde'sya vratabandhaṃ cakāra ha || 1 6||
vedavratāni catvāri cakāra baṭukasya saḥ |
sakṛnnigadamātreṇa gṛhṇāti brahmatejasā || 57||
vedaśāstranidhirjātaḥ svakarma kuśalo'pi ca |
kadācit sumahūrte tu pitā vācaknaviḥ sutam || 1 8||
gaṇānāṃ tveti ṛṅmantraṃ mahāṃtamupadiṣṭavān |
uvāca ca mahāmantro vaidiko'khila siddhidaḥ || 1 9||
āgamokteṣu mantreṣu sarveṣu śreṣṭha eva ca |
dhyātvā gajānanaṃ devaṃ japainaṃ sthiramānasaḥ ||2 0 ||
parāṃ siddhiṃ samāpyaiva khyātiṃ loke gamiṣyasi |
tato gṛtsamado vipro mantraṃ prāpya piturmukhāt || 21 ||
anuṣṭhānarato bhūtvā japadhyāna paro'bhavat |
evaṃ bahutithe kāle gate tu munipuṃgave |
tasmin magadhadeśe yo rājā magadhasaṃjñitaḥ ||22 ||
cārurūpā mahāmānī dānaśūro'rimardanaḥ |
nānālaṃkāra śobhāḍhyo mahārhāsana saṃśritaḥ ||2 3 ||
sudharmāsanago dhīraḥ puruhūta ivāparaḥ |
caturaṃbalopeto jñānī paṃḍitamānadaḥ ||24||
amātyau dvau jñānanidhī vācaspatyadhikau guṇauḥ|
ambikā nāma bhāryā'sya cārurūpā guṇādhikā ||25||
pativratā mahābhāgā śāpānugrahaṇe kṣamā |
tasya rājñaḥ pituśrāddhe samājagmurmaharṣayaḥ ||2 6||
rājñāhūtā vasiṣṭhātri pramukhāḥ śrutipāragāḥ |
ākārito gṛtsamadastapasvī śucimānasaḥ ||27||
tataḥ śāstra prasaṃgena prauḍhi gṛtsamado'vadat |
tamatri dhigdhigityevamabravīn munisaṃnnidhau || 28||
tapasvīti bhavānmānyo na munistvaṃ yatastava |
janma rūkmāṃgadājjātaṃ rājaputrādvicāraya ||29||
nāsmatsamakṣo pūjārhaṃ ito gaccha svamāśramam |
ityatrivacanaṃ śrutvā krodhadīpta iva jvalan ||3 0 ||
dahanniva trilokīṃ sa bhakṣayanniva tānmunīn |
apera tvapalāyanta siṃhaṃ dṛṣṭvā yathā mṛgāḥ || 31 ||
uvāca tatra sadasi vasiṣṭhādīn munīnprati |
gṛtsamada uvāca |
yarhi rukmāṃgadasyāhaṃ na bhaveyaṃ munīśvarāḥ |
tadā śāpāgninā yuṣmānkuryāṃ bhasmāvaśeṣitān ||3 2||
brahmovāca |
ityuktvā tānmunīn sarvān prayayau mātaraṃ prati |
papraccha tāṃ gṛtsamado vada duṣṭe'tikāmuke ||3 3 ||
mukuṃde vada me tāta no ced bhasma bhaviṣyasi |
ākarṇyevaṃ vacastasya cakampe bhṛśavihvalā ||34||
mukuṃdā mārūteneva kadalī kuḍmalānvitā|
uvāca dīnayā vācā baddhāṃjalipuṭā satī || 35 ||
mukuṃdovāca |
mṛgayāsaktacitto'bhūddhṛṣṭasārtho nṛpottamaḥ |
trailokyasubhago dṛṣṭo mayā rūkmāṃgadaḥ śubhaḥ || 3 6||
anuṣṭhānarate vācaknavau me bhartari priye |
anivāryāḥ striya iti smṛtvā vākyaṃ vidhīritam || 37||
tasmin nṛpe saktamanā jātā'smi sa pitā tava |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasyā maunavānsa yayau muniḥ || 38||
lajjayā'dhomukhaḥ śāpamabhyadhājjananīṃ prati ||
putra uvāca |
duṣṭe mūḍhe pāparate kānane kaṃṭakī bhava ||3 9||
asaṃkhyeya phalā sarve prāṇibhiḥ parivarjitā |
sā'pi śāpaṃ dadau tasmai krodhāviṣṭā sutāya hi ||40||
jananī tvamanādṛtya yataḥ śaptā khalu tvayā |
ataḥ śapāmi tvāṃ putra tvattaḥ putro'ti dāruṇaḥ ||4 1 ||
trailokyabhayado daityo bhaviṣyati mahābalaḥ |
evaṃ śaśapatustau tu mātāputrau parasparam ||42||
ka uvāca |
sā tadaiva śarīraṃ tat tyaktvā'bhūd badarī vane |
varjitā pakṣisaṃghātai jarijairaṃḍajairapi ||8 3 ||
tato'ntarikṣe vāgāsīdindrād gṛtsamado hyabhūt|
sa tu gṛtsamado brahmannanuṣṭhānāya jagmivān ||44||
idaṃ gṛtsamadākhyānaṃ yaḥ śṛṇoti narottamaḥ |
na sa saṃkaṭamāpnoti vāṃchitaṃ labhate'khilam ||45||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe gṛtsamadopākhyānaṃ nāma ṣaṭtriśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||3 6 ||

<adhyāya 37 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca|
bhramanmuni dadarśāgre vanaṃ puṣpakasaṃjñitam|
nānādrumalatākīrṇaṃ puṣpaprākāraśobhitam || 1 ||
śobhitaṃ nirjharajalairnirjarairmunisattamai |
nanāma tān gṛtsamado nyavasacca tadājñayā ||2||
tatra snātvā japaṃ cakre pādāṃguṣṭhāgradhiṣṭitaḥ |
sthireṇa manasā dhyāyandevaṃ vighneśvaraṃ vibhum ||3 ||
nāsāgra nyastadṛṣṭiḥ sannirīkṣanna diśodiśa |
jitendriyo jitaśvāso jitātmā mārutāśanaḥ ||4||
divyavarṣasahasraṃ sa tapastepe sudāruṇam |
unmīlya nayanaṃ paśyadyadā gṛtsamado muniḥ ||5 ||
tadā netrodbhavo vahnīstridaśānparyatāpayat |
śaśaṃkire tadā devāḥ kasyāyaṃ padabhāgbhaveta ||6||
aparaṃ galitaṃ patraṃ bhakṣayannekameva ca |
yatnamāsthāya paramaṃ sthāṇubhūto'ti niścalaḥ ||7||
daśapaṃcasahasrāṇi tepe niścalamānasaḥ |
tato vināyako dṛṣṭvā tapanaṃ tasya durghaṭam ||8||
anugrahāya tasyātha prādurāsīt sudīptimān |
yathā dhenurvatsaravaṃ śrutvā dhāvati satvaram ||9||
tathā vināyako devaḥ śīghraṃ gṛtsamadaṃ yayau |
bhāsayaṃstejasā viśvaṃ sahasraravisannibhaḥ || 1 0||
calatkarṇatālo bṛhaddantilīlo mudā cārūkhelo lasaccandrabhālaḥ |
bṛhatpadmamālo jagatkāryamūlaḥ karakaṃjanālo namatsavimelaḥ || 1 1||
siṃhārūḍho daśabhujo vyālayajñopavītavān |
kuṃkumāgaru kastūrī cārūcaṃdana carcitaḥ ||12||
siddhibuddhi yutaḥ śrīmān koṭisūryādhika dyutiḥ |
anirvācya svarūpo'pi lolayā''sītpuro mune || 1 3||
tattejasā hrataṃ tejā munestasya mahātmanaḥ |
bradhnasya tejasā yadvan nākṣatraṃ caṃdrajaṃ mahaḥ || 14||
nimīlya nayane so'tha cakampe bhṛśavihvalaḥ |
papāta mūrchito bhūmau vismṛta dhyānamaṃgalaḥ || 15||
punaśca manasā dhyāyan gajānanamanāmayam |
manasā tarkayan vighnaṃ nimittaṃ vyākulo muniḥ || 16||
kimetat kṣobhajananaṃ sahasā samupasthitam |
adyaprabhṛti yattaptaṃ tatkathaṃ me vṛthā gatam || 17||
pāhi deveśa sarvātman vighnādasmād bhayānakāt |
tvāmṛte śaraṇaṃ yāmi kamanyaṃ jagadīśvaram || 18||
mahadduḥkhaṃ sadā deva prāptaṃ kena ca hetunā |
nāsmat paṃktau pūjanīya iti yadvahate manaḥ || 19||
brahmovāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā jagāda sa vināyakaḥ |
gaṇeśa uvāca |
anugrahāya saṃprāptaṃ viddhi māṃ gaṇanāyakam | 20
sanakādibhiraprāpyaṃ ciraṃ niyamamāsthitaiḥ |
tyaktvā bhayaṃ brūhi yatte vāṃchitaṃ munisattama ||2 1||
ekāṃguṣṭhena tapasā toṣito'haṃ tvayā'niśam |
brahmovāca |
niśamyetthaṃ vacastasya devadevasya śobhanam ||22||
daṃḍavat praṇanāmainaṃ nijānaṃda pariplutaḥ |
uvāca paramaprīto varadaṃ taṃ vināyakam ||23 ||
gṛtsamada uvāca |
adya me saphalaṃ janma tapaso niyamasya ca |
akhaṃḍānaṃdarūpo yo brahmabhūyo nirākṛtiḥ ||24||
muṃcannaśrūṇi netrābhyāṃ mānadena nanarta ca|
cidānaṃdaghano veda śāstrāṇāmapyagocaraḥ ||25||
sadyo dṛṣṭo mayā sākṣādataḥ kiṃ prārthaye vibho |
tavājñayā tathā'pyekaṃ prārthaye dviradānana ||26||
caturaśīti lakṣāsu yoniṣu śreṣṭhatāsu ca |
manuṣyāṇāṃ mahābhāga varṇāstatra mahattarāḥ ||27||
tatrāpi brāhmaṇāḥ śreṣṭhāstatrāpi jñāninaḥ parāḥ |
jñāniṣvanuṣṭhānaparāsteṣu ca brahmavedinaḥ ||28||
brahmajñānaṃ tathā jñānaṃ dehi me jagadīśvara |
tvayi bhaktiṃ ca sudṛḍhāmavismaraṇameva ca ||2 9||
tava bhakteṣu sarveṣu śreṣṭhatāṃ ca gajānana |
anyaṃ caikaṃ varaṃ yāce taṃ ca me dehi śaṃkara || 30 ||
tava bhaktyaikanilayaṃ trailokyākarṣaṇa kṣamam |
vikhyātaṃ triṣu lokeṣu namasyaṃ suramānuṣaiḥ ||3 1 ||
evaṃ māṃ kuru vighneśa yadi tuṣṭo'khilārthakṛt|
vanaṃ ca puṣpakaṃ nāmnā khyātiṃ yātu sureśvara ||32 ||
asmin sthitvā ca bhaktānāṃ kāmānpūraya nityadā |
idaṃ ca puṣpakapuraṃ caturdikṣu viśeṣataḥ || 33 ||
gaṇeśapuramityevaṃ prathāṃ yātu gajānana |
brahmovāca |
iti tadvacana śrutvā jagāda dviradānanaḥ ||34||
gaṇeśa uvāca |
sādhu sādhu mahābāho prasanne mayi durlabham |
bhaktānāṃ triṣu lokeṣu na kiṃcin munipuṃgava || 3 6||
tvayā yatprārthitaṃ vipra tatte sarvaṃ bhaviṣyati |
vipratvaṃ durlabhataraṃ prasannena mayārpitam || 36 ||
gaṇānāṃ tveti mantrasya vaidikasya yatastvayā | 
japaḥ kṛto munestastvamṛṣirasya bhaviṣyasi ॥ 37||
brahmādiṣu ca deveṣu vasiṣṭhādi muniṣvapi |
khyātiṃ yāsyasi sarvatra paraṃ śraiṣṭhayamupāgataḥ || 38||
sarveṣvārabdha kāryeṣu pūrvaṃ te mama cāparam |
smaraṇaṃ ye kariṣyanti teṣāṃ siddhirbhaviṣyati || 39||
kṛte jñānāddaivatarṣi cchandasāṃ karma niṣphalam |
putraśca balavān sarvaṃ devānāṃ subhayaṃkaraḥ ||40 ||
bhaviṣyati mahākhyātiṃ triṣu lokeṣu yāsyati |
ajeyaḥ sarvadevānāṃ vinā rudraṃ bhaviṣyati ||41||
madbhakto madgataprāṇo manniṣṭho matparāyaṇaḥ |
idaṃ ca nagaraṃ deva yuge puṣpaka saṃjñitam ||42 ||
tretāyāṃ maṇipūraṃ ca bhānakaṃ dvāpare'pi ca |
kalau tu bhadrakaṃ nāma khyātaṃ loke bhaviṣyati ||43||
atra snānena dānena sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt |
brahmovāca |
evaṃ datvā varāṃstasmai tatraivāntardadhe vibhuḥ ||44||
tasminnaṃtarhite tatra sthāpayāmāsa vai muniḥ|
gaṇeśamūrti prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa sundaram ||45 ||
varadeti ca tannāma sthāpayāmāsa śāśvatam |
siddhisthānaṃ ca tatrāsīd gaṇeśasya prasādataḥ ||46||
kāmānpuṣṇāṇi sarveṣāṃ puṣpakaṃ kṣetramityapi |
pūjayāmāsa tāṃ mūrtiṃ bhaktibhāvasamanvitaḥ ||47||
imāṃ kathāṃ yaḥ śṛṇuyān munīndra śrīvighnarājasya varaprakāśām|
sabheta kāmānaripalān gaṇeśa bhaktiṃ dṛḍhāṃ saṃsṛtimocanīṃ ca ॥ 48 ॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe varadākhyānaṃ nāma saptatriṃśattamodhyāyaḥ ||37||

<adhyāya 38 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
tato gṛtsamadasyāsīt kathaṃ vṛttiḥ sureśvara |
tanmamācakṣva yatnena śraddadhānasya padmaja || 1 ||
brahmovāca |
tataḥ sarve munigaṇā mānayāmāsurādarāt |
brahman munivaraśreṣṭhaṃ nemurgṛtsamadaṃ ca tam ||2 ||
varadānād gaṇeśasya vavrustaṃ yajñakarmaṇi |
sarvārambhe gaṇeśasya pūjanādau ca sasmaraḥ || 3 ||
evaṃ vikhyātimagamat sa munirgaṇanāyake |
bhaktiṃ paramikāṃ cakre japanmaṃtraṃ suniścalaḥ ||4||
kadācit sa munirvyāsa cukṣuve balamuttaram |
diśo nabhaśca pṛthivīṃ nādayan girigahvarān ||5 ||
apaśyat purato yāvat tāvadbālaṃ bhayaṃkaram |
raktavarṇaṃ mahānādaṃ japākusumasannibham ||6||
tejorāśiṃ ca muṣṇaṃtaṃ netrālokapathaṃ muhuḥ |
dṛṣṭvā sa tādṛśaṃ bālaṃ cakampe bhayavihvalaḥ ||7||
tarkayāmāsa manasā vighnaḥ ko'yamihāgataḥ ||
na jāne gaṇanāthena datta putro mamādbhutaḥ ||8||
paśyati sma punaḥ so'muṃ cāruvaktraṃ sulocanam |
cāru rūkmāṃgadaṃ cāru mukuṭaṃ cārunūpuram ||9||
cāruṇā kaṭisūtreṇa rājatkaṭitaṭaṃ sutam |
papraccha taṃ muniḥ ko'si kasyāsi kiṃ cikīrṣasi || 10 ||
kva ca te pitarau sthānaṃ vada tejonidhe'rbhaka |
ka uvāca |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasya jagāda bālako munim || 11 ||
bālaka uvāca |
bhūtabhāvibhavajjñānī kiṃ māṃ tvaṃ paripṛcchasi |
tathā'pyājñāvaśo vacmi kṣutāttava janirmama || 12 ||
tvameva janako mātā kṛpāṃ kuru mamoparī |
pālayasva pitarmāṃ tvaṃ dināni katicinmune || 13 ||
trailokyākramaṇe śakto devendraṃ vaśavartinam |
kariṣyāmi na sandeho vīkṣyase paurūṣaṃ mama || 14||
brahmovāca |
ityākarṇya vacastasya bhayaharṣaṃ samanvitaḥ |
uvāca ślakṣṇayā vācā munirgṛtsamado vacaḥ || 15 ||
yadyayaṃ jātamātro'pi śaktastrailokya karṣaṇe |
tasmādasya pradāsyāmi svaṃ mantraṃ svātmajasya hi || 16 ||
yenāsya vāṃchitaṃ devaḥ parituṣṭo vināyakaḥ |
pradāsyati jagannātho mama kīrtirbhaviṣyati || 17||
evaṃ saṃciṃtya manasā tasmai svaṃ mantramādiśat |
gaṇānāṃ tveti taṃ cāha kurvanuṣṭhānamādarāt || 1 8||
japasva vaidikaṃ maṃtraṃ cittaṃ sthāpya gajānane |
saṃtuṣṭaste yadā putra sarvān kāmān pradāsyati || 1 9||
evaṃ prāpta mahāmaṃtro jagāma tapase vanam |
ekāṃguṣṭhenāvatasthe nirāhāro jitendriyaḥ ||2 0 ||
dhyāyan gajānanaṃ devaṃ manasā niścelana saḥ |
japatastasya varṣāṇi sārdhāyutamitāni ca || 21 ||
gatāni tasya mukhato babhūvāgnirdiśo jvalana |
bhayaṃ babhūva devānāṃ daityānāṃ talavāsinām ||22 ||
tatastat tapasā tuṣṭa āvirāsīd gajānanaḥ |
diśo vitimirāḥ kurvaṃśchādayan bhānumaṃḍalam ||2 3 ||
bhrāmayan puṣpakaraṃ cārū suviṣāṇaṃ mudā yutaḥ |
tadbṛṃhita ravaṃ śrutvā bālako vihvalagniva ||24||
unmīlya netre so'paśyaddevaṃ taṃ purataḥ sthitam |
caturbhujaṃ mahākāyaṃ nānābhūṣā vibhūṣitam ||25||
paraśuṃ kamalaṃ mālā modakān bibhrataṃ karaiḥ |
tejasā dharṣitastasya dhairyaṃ kṛtvā nanāma saḥ ||2 6 ||
baddhāṃjalipuṭo bhūtvā prārthayāmāsa taṃ vibhum |
bālaka uvāca |
kiṃ māṃ dharṣayase deva bhaktaṃ tvāṃ śaraṇāgatam ||27||
bhava saumyataro deva dehi me'khilavāṃchitam |
brahmovāca |
ityākarṇya vacastasya saṃjahāra svakaṃ mahaḥ || 28||
uvāca paramaprīto sāvadhāna bhavārbhaka |
yaṃ dhyāyasi divārātro so'haṃ te varado'dhunā ||29||
mamedaṃ paramaṃ rūpaṃ svaprakāśaṃ jaganmayam |
na vidurbrahma rudrādyāḥ kuto vetsyanti mānavāḥ ||3 0 ||
devāśca munayaḥ sarve na ca rājarṣayo'pare |
nāsurāḥ siddhagandharvā na nāgā na ca dānavaḥ || 31 ||
so'haṃ tava tapobaddho varaṃ dātumihāgataḥ |
varayasva varānmatto yānyāṃstvaṃ manasecchasi || 32 ||
sa uvāca tato bālo dhanyo'haṃ tava darśanāt |
pitā dhanyataro me'dya sārthakaṃ janma me tapaḥ ||3 3 ||
stutiṃ kartuṃ na jānāmi bālabhāvātsureśvara |
yatastvaṃ sarvajagatāṃ kartā pātā'pahārakaḥ ||34||
tvad bhāsā bhāsayatyeva raviragniśca candramāḥ |
carācaraṃ cetayase svamāhātmyān mahāmate || 35||
mahimānaṃ mahāntaṃ te keśā api na yaṃ viduḥ |
yadi me varado'si tvaṃ tanme dehi gajānana || 3 6||
trailokyākarṣaṇe śaktiṃ viśiṣṭāṃ dehi me vibho |
devadānavagandharva manuṣyoragarākṣasā ||37||
vaśyā mama sadā santu munikinnara cāraṇāḥ|
manasā cintitaṃ yanme tattat sidhyatu sarvadā ||3 8||
iṃdrādayo lokapālāḥ sevāṃ kurvantu me sadā |
iha bhogānanekānme muktiṃ cānte prayaccha ca || 3 9||
anyaṃ ca te varaṃ yāce purametat tavājñayā |
prathāṃ yātu yataścātra tapamugraṃ tapo mayā ||40 ||
gaṇeśapuramiti ca khyātiṃ yātu janeṣṭadam ||
gaṇeśa uvāca |
trayāṇāmapi lokānāmākramaṃ tvaṃ kariṣyasi ||41 ||
sarvebhyo na bhayaṃ te'sti sarve vaśyāśca te sadā |
āyasaṃ kāṃcanaṃ raupyaṃ mayā dattaṃ puratrayam ||42 ||
abhedyaṃ sarvadevānāṃ kāmagaṃ śaṃkara vinā |
tripureti ca te nāma khyātiṃ loke gamiṣyati ||||43 ||
yadaikena ca bāṇena śivo bhetsyati te puram |
tadaiva yāsyase muktiṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā ||44||
anyatte vāṃchitaṃ sarvaṃ matprasādād bhaviṣyati |
brahmovāca |
dattvetthaṃ sa varāndevastatraivāntardadhe vibhuḥ|
viṣādamagamaddeva viyogāt tripurāsuraḥ ||45||
harṣaṃ ca vipulaṃ lebhe varānprāpya yathepsitān|
trailokyavijayaṃ kartuṃ yatate sma tato balāt ||46||
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe varapradānaṃ nāmāṣṭatriṃśattamo'dhyāya || 38||

<adhyāya 39 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
tataḥ kimakarod brahmastripuro varadarpitaḥ |
tatsarvaṃ kautukaṃ mahayaṃ vaktumarhasyaśeṣataḥ || 1 ||
brahmovāca |
tataḥ kāśmīra pāṣāṇabhavāṃ mūrtiṃ gajānanīma |
sthāpayāmāsa vidhivad brahmaṇairmantrakovidaiḥ||2||
mahāṃtaṃ kāṃcanaṃ divyaṃ maṇimuktā vibhūṣitam |
gaṇeśapuramadhye sa prāsādaṃ kṛtavānśubham || 3 ||
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pūjayāmāsa taṃ vibhum |
namaskārairasaṃkhyātaiḥ stutibhiḥ prārthanairapi ||4||
kṣamāpya devadeveśamanujñopya yayau bahiḥ |
dadau dānānyanekāni brāhmaṇebhyo yathārhataḥ ||5 ||
tatastadabhavatsthānaṃ baṃgāle tripurasya ha |
gaṇeśapuramityevaṃ sarveṣāṃ sarvasiddhidam || 6 ||
tataḥ sa tripuro daityo gajānana varoddhataḥ |
nṛlokānpālayāmāsa devānāmākrame rataḥ ||7||
padātayasturaṃgāśca gajāśca rathinastathā |
daśadigbhyo yayustaṃ tu sevārthaṃ balavattarāḥ ||8||
rājānaḥ sevakā jātā ānukūlyena tasya ha |
pratikūlā gatā mṛtyuṃ yuddhaṃ kartuṃ manīśvarāḥ ॥9॥
evamākramya bhūkhaṃḍa tato'gādamarāvatīm |
tata iṃdro devagaṇai nauṃnāyuddha kare vṛtaḥ ॥10॥
airāvata samārūḍho yayau yuddhāya daṃśitaḥ |
so'pi senāṃ tridhā cakre caturaṃgāṃ mahābalaḥ ॥11॥
bhīmakāyaṃ mahādaityaṃ vajradaṃṣṭraṃ ca dānavam |
dhanuryuddhe gadāyuddhe śastrayuddhe ca kovidam ॥12॥
astrayuddhe mallayuddhe niṣṇātaṃ daityapuṃgavam |
bhīmakāyamavocatsa nṛlokasyādhipo bhava ॥13॥
kālakūṭaṃ vajradaṃṣṭraṃ jagāda tripuro balī |
tribhāgayā'nayā yāhi senayā tvaṃ rasātalam ॥14॥
śeṣamukhyāna sarvanāgān vaśānkuru mamāyajñā|
ahaṃ tribhāgayā śakramākramiṣye'khilānsurān ॥15॥
bhaumakāyo vajradaṃṣṭro yathājñapto pratasthatuḥ |
caturaṃgabalairyuktaḥ svayaṃ nandanamāyayau ॥16॥
divyān vṛkṣān babhaṃjuste vāryamāṇāstu sainikāḥ ||
tatra sthitvā daityarājo dūtān śakrāya prāhiṇot |
atrā nayantu taṃ śakraṃ darśanāya tvarānvitāḥ
vadantu mama vākyaṃ vā mṛtyulokaṃ vrajādhunā ॥18॥
tatra tvāṃ pālayiṣyāmi sāmnā dehyamarāvatīm
yadi yuddhe ca te buddhistadā śīghraṃ prayāhi mām ॥19॥
te gatvā śakramācakhyustripurāsura ceṣṭitam |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ teṣāṃ vajrāhata ivācala ||20||
cakampe cācalaripurvāyuneva yathā taruḥ |
cintayā vyākulībhūtaḥ kimetaditi cintayan ॥21॥
krodhānalena jajbāla saṃraktākhilalocanaḥ |
kurvanbhasmeva lokānāṃ śoṣayanniva vāridhīn ॥22॥
uvāca dūtān gacchantu yāntu yuddhāya satvarāḥ |
svayamairāvatārūḍho jagarja suraśatruhā ॥23॥
tena nādena mahatā kṣobhayanbhuvanatrayam |
śrutvā tadvacanaṃ te tu gatā dūtaḥ yathāgatam ॥24॥
vibudhāścāpi sannaddhā nānāśasrāsipāṇayaḥ |
bhiṇḍipālakarāḥ kecit kecicchaktyṛṣṭipāṇayaḥ ॥25॥
mudgarāsidharāḥ keciddhanurbāṇakarāḥ pare |
gadākheṭakarāḥ kecit kecittu daṃḍapāṇayaḥ ॥26॥
evaṃ devagaṇairyukto vajrabhṛnniryayau bahiḥ |
kṛtasvastyayano viprairnānā vāditra nisvanaiḥ ॥27॥
tripuro dūtavākyena jñātvā yuddhodyamaṃ tu tam |
sannaddhāmakarotsenāṃ prahṛṣṭāṃ caturaṃgiṇīm ||28॥
asaṃkhyātāṃ samādāya hyārūḍho'bhi niryayau |
parasparaṃ dadṛśatuste sene vīrabhūṣaṇe ॥29॥
kolāhalo mahānāsīd bṛṃhitairhreṣitairapi |
kṣveḍitai rathaghoṣaiśca vādyaghoṣairanekadhā ॥30॥
tato huṃkāramātreṇa noditāstripureṇa te |
vīrā yuyudhire devaiḥ sa saṃmardo mahānabhūt ॥31॥
nābhūt svapara bodho'pi jaghnureva parasparam |
evaṃ sutumule janye mṛtāste dānavā bahu ॥32॥
sumanaso'pi patitā daityaśastra prapīḍitāḥ|
babhuste sainikāstatra puṣpitā iva kiṃśukāḥ ॥33॥
aśayyāḥ śayitāḥ kecidaṃghrihīnāstathā'pare|
kramelakā gajādhyakṣā rathāśvāśca padātinaḥ ॥34॥
tataḥ palāyanaparā daityā yātā diśo daśa |
