siṃhaṃ dṛṣṭvaiva sahasā mṛgā jīvanakāṃkṣiṇaḥ ॥35॥
tato nivārya tatsainyaṃ vṛndārakaripuḥ svayam |
krodhānala mahājvālo garjanmegha ivāparaḥ ॥36॥
śatrusānnidhyamagamad bhakṣayanniva rodasī |
svakhaḍgenāhanatīvraṃ hastaṃ vajradharaṃ hareḥ ॥37॥
vajraṃ papāta taddhastāt tadadbhutamivābhavat |
tadā dāyā hanaddaityastenaivairāvataṃ gajam ॥38॥
airāvataḥ prahāreṇa palāyanaparo yayau |
tato jaghāna sa harirmuṣṭinā daityapuṃgavam ॥39॥
sakṣaṇaṃ patito bhūmau tata utthāya vegavān |
jaghāna muṣṭinā śakraṃ dharaṇyāṃ tamapātayat ॥40॥
tata utthāya maghavā daityaṃ prāha ruṣānvitaḥ |
idānīṃ mallayuddhāya sajjībhavāsureśvara ॥41॥
tataḥ sa vismayāviṣṭa uvāca balagarvitaḥ |
kimarthatvaṃ nijaprāṇe nirdayo'si sureśvara ॥42॥
kṛmikīṭapataṃgānāṃ prāṇo'tīva priyo mataḥ |
gaccha deva dharaṇyāṃ te sthānaṃ dattaṃ mayā śubham ॥43॥
brahmovāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā jagāda balavṛtrahā|
yadyahaṃ jīvitācvāṃhi mocayāmi na cedripo ॥44॥
tadā'haṃ dharaṇīṃ yāmi tvadājñā vaśago'dhama |
tvameva hatamūrdhā'dya dharaṇīṃ yāsyase khala ॥45॥
vadatyevaṃ tu devendra dainyendro nijaghāna tam |
muṣṭinā hṛdaye duṣṭastato yuddhamabhūttayoḥ ॥46॥
cāṇūrakṛṣṇayoryadvat paraspara jayaiṣiṇoḥ |
hṛdayaṃ hṛdayenaiva hastaṃ hastena jaghnatuḥ |47॥
jānubhyāṃ jānunī corū tābhyāṃ tau ca nijaghnatuḥ |
mastakaṃ mastakenaiva kūrparaṃ kūrpareṇa tu ॥48॥
pṛṣṭaṃ pṛṣṭena pādābhyāṃ pādau tāvabhijaghnatuḥ |
pādau gṛhītvā daityo'sya bhrāmayitvā muhurmuhuḥ ॥49॥
tatyāja dūrataḥ śakraṃ yathā na jñāyate kvacit |
āruroha svayaṃ taṃ tu caturdantaṃ gajeśvaram ॥50॥
tato devagaṇāḥ sarve himavad girigahvaram |
yayuḥ śakraṃ vicinvanto daityasaṃtrāsatāpitāḥ ॥51॥
kutra vā patito devo drakṣyāmo vā kathaṃ vibhum |
evaṃ saṃciṃtayantaste bhramaṃto dadṛśuśca tam ॥52॥
adhomukhaṃ samāyāntaṃ devendraṃ taṃ tadaiva ha |
praṇemurdevasaṃghāstamāliliṃgastathā'pare ॥53॥
pūjayāmāsurapare bījayāmāsa kaścana |
pādasaṃvāhanaṃ cāsya cakruḥ kecana bhaktitaḥ ॥54॥
tatraiva nyavasan sarve guptarūpāḥ surāstadā |
daityastvairāvatārūḍho yayau tāmamarāvatīm ॥55॥
devasthānāni daityānāmiṃdrāsana gataḥ svayam |
dadau vibhajya pratyekaṃ mānapūrvaṃ suradviṣām  ॥56॥
divya vāditranirghoṣaiḥ śṛṇvan gāṃdharvanisvanān |
kinnaraiḥ sevyamānastu reme cāpsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ ॥ 57 ॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe indraparājayo nāmaikona catvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥39॥

<adhyāya 40 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca |
ākramya devadhiṣṇyāni daityo'gād brahmaṇaḥ padam |
parākramaṃ daityakṛtaṃ śrutvā devamukhātparā ॥1॥
brahmā yayau nābhipadmaṃ viṣṇuḥ kṣīranidhiṃ yayau |
daityasya mānasau putrau pracaṃḍaścaṃḍa eva ca ॥2॥
pracaṃḍaṃ sthāpayāmāsa brahmaloke'dhināyakam |
tataścaṃḍaṃ ca vaikuṃṭhe cakāra svāminaṃ svayam ॥3॥
tataḥ kailāsamagamat taṃ ca daurbhyāmacālayat |
bhayabhītā ca girijā liliṃge śaṃkaraṃ bhṛśam ॥4॥
tataḥ kailāsamagamadyuddhākāṃkṣī mahāsuraḥ |
tena tuṣṭo mahādevo daityasya pauruṣeṇa ca ॥5॥
bahiryayau varaṃ dātuṃ nijabhaktaṃ sukhapradaḥ |
dadarśa tripuraṃ daityaṃ varaṃ vṛṇvityathābravīt ॥6॥
sa vavre yadi tuṣṭo'si dehi kailāsamadya me |
gaccha mandāraśikharaṃ yāvanmama manorathaḥ ॥7॥
śaṃkaro'pi dadau tasmai kailāsaṃ svalpakāline |
svayaṃ jagāma giriśo mandārādriṃ gaṇairvṛtaḥ ॥8॥
kailāsaśikharārūḍho jaharṣa tripurāsuraḥ|
evaṃ devānvaśe kṛtvā punarāyādrasātalam ॥9॥
bhīmakāyo'pi balavān bhūmaṃḍala gato balāt |
vaśe cakāra nṛpatīn ṛṣīn sarvān babandha ca || 10॥
agnikuṃḍāni sarvāṇi devatṛpti karāṇi ca |
āśramāṃśca babhaṃjāśu tīrthāni ca viśeṣataḥ ॥11॥
tāpasāṃstrāsayāmāsa kārāgāra samāśrayāt |
svāhā svadhā vaṣaṭkārān vedasyābhyasanāni ca ॥12॥
sadācārān sadā dveṣṭi sarvagarvasamanvita |
vajradaṃṣṭrotha saptāpi pātālāni vaśe'nayat ॥13॥
śeṣaṃ ca vāsukiṃ caiva takṣakaṃ sarvabhoginaḥ|
nirviṣān sa viṣāṃścaiva cakāra vaśavartilaḥ |
vajradaṃṣṭro ratnajātaṃ bubhuje preṣayacca tam ॥14॥
nāgāṃganābhiśca kutūhalena reme sadā'sau samado'ti harṣāt |
bhogān vimuṃjana vividhāni ratnānyādāya yātastripurāntike saḥ॥15॥
pātālavaśyatāṃ śaṃsan lebhe mānaṃ tatodhikam ॥16॥
vastrāṇi ca mahārhāṇi grāmān dāsānanekaśaḥ |
evaṃ trilokānvaśagān kṛtvā daityo nananda ha ॥17॥
devāḥ sarve guhāvāsāścintayāmāsuranvaham |
kathamasya vadhaḥ kasminkāle vāpi bhaviṣyati ॥18॥
kasmādveti na jānīmo labdho'nena varaṃ kutaḥ |
evaṃ vyākulacitteṣu sureṣu munisattama ॥19॥
āyayau nāradastatra trailokyacara icchayā |
dadarśa dīnāndevāṃstā nuttatāra nabhaḥ pathāt ॥20॥
dṛṣṭvā sarve nāradaṃ tamuttasthu sahasā''darāt |
āliliṃguśca nemuśca pupūjuśca yathākramam ॥21॥
viśrāntaṃ paripapracchustripurasya varādikam |
devā ūcuḥ |
tripureṇa samākrāntaṃ trailokyaṃ sacarācarām ॥22॥
sthāthāni no gṛhītāni keśāstenāpi nirjitāḥ |
śaraṇaṃ kaṃ vrajāmo'dya kathaṃ tasya vadho bhavet ॥23॥
varāśca kena dattā no vadāsya tripurasya bhoḥ |
nārada uvāca |
kathayāmi samāsena daityasya ceṣṭitaṃ mahat ॥24॥
divyavarṣasahasraṃ sa tatāpa paramaṃ tapaḥ |
prasādayāmāsa vibhuṃ gaṇeśaṃ devanāyakam ॥25॥
tenāsmai durdharā dattā varāḥ sarvabhayaṃkarāḥ |
devarṣi pitṛbhūtebhyo yakṣarakṣa piśācataḥ ॥26॥
na nāgebhyo bhayaṃ dattaṃ vinaikaṃ śaṃkaraṃ vibhum |
prasādayantu deveśaṃ dviradānanamādarāt ॥27॥
ārādhayantu sarve'pi vighneśaṃ sarvasiddhidam |
devā ūcuḥ|
kathamārādhanaṃ tasya devadevasya dhīmataḥ ॥28॥
kartavyaṃ muniśārdūla kṛpayā tadvadasva naḥ |
nārada uvāca |
ahamekākṣaraṃ mantraṃ kathayāmyakhilān prati ॥29॥
tena mantreṇa te sarve mayā dattena bhaktitaḥ |
anuṣṭhānaṃ prakurvantu sarve'pi sthiramānasāḥ ॥30॥
yāvat pratyakṣatāmeti devo'sau gaṇanāyakaḥ |
sa eva tadvadhopāyaṃ vadiṣyatyakhilānprati ॥31॥
nānyopāyaṃ prapaśyāmi tasmāt kurvantu me vacaḥ |
brahmovāca |
ityuktvā nāradaḥ sarvānupadiśya manuṃ ca tam ॥32॥
jagāma tatkṣaṇādeva vīṇāgānarato muniḥ |
tataḥ sarve suravarā gaṇeśaṃ dhyānatatparāḥ ॥33॥
ekapādasthitāḥ kecit kecitpadmāsanasthitāḥ |
kecidvīrāsanayutāḥ kecinmīlita locanā|
nirāhārā jitaśvāsā jepurmantraṃ munīṃritam ||34॥
tato bahugate kāle karaṇābdhirgajānanaḥ|
anuṣṭhānaṃ nirīkṣyaiṣāṃ devānāṃ cirakālajama ॥35॥
āvirbabhūva gaṇapasteṣāmagre varapradaḥ |
ullasat svarṇamukuṭaścāru kuṃḍalamaṃḍitaḥ ॥36॥
dantanyastakaro rājat kaṭisūtra varāṃgada |
pāśaṃ sṛṇiṃ ca paraśuṃ kamalaṃ ca bhujairdadhat ॥37॥
raktacandana kastūrī sindūra śaśibhūṣaṇaḥ||
vidyuttejo lasatkāntiḥ koṭisūryasamaprabhaḥ ॥38॥
dṛṣṭvaivaṃ sahasā devaṃ vināyakamanāmayam |
tejasā dharṣitaḥ sarve bhayamāpuśca kecana ॥39॥
praṇemuḥ sahasā keciddevāstaṃ dviradānanam |
pūjayāmāsurapare harṣagadagada bhāṣiṇaḥ ॥40॥
svasaṃkaṣṭa vināśāya tuṣṭuvu ko'pi taṃ vibhuma|
prasādasumukhaṃ devaṃ susukhaṃ saṃkaṭāpaham ॥41॥
devā ūcuḥ|
namo namaste paramārtharūpa namo namaste'khilakāraṇāya |
namo namaste'khila kāraṇāya sarvendriyāṇāmadhivāsine'pi ॥42॥
namo namo bhūtamayāya te'stu namo namo bhūtakṛte sureśa |
namo namaḥ sarvadhiyāṃ prabodha namo namo viśvalayodbhavāya ॥43॥
namo namo viśvabhṛte'khileśa namo namaḥ kāraṇa kāraṇāya |
namo namo vedavidāmadṛśya namo namaḥ sarvavarapradāya ॥44॥
namo namo vāgavicārabhūta namo namo vighnanivāraṇāya |
namo namo'bhakta manorathaghne namo namo bhakta manorathajña ॥45॥
namo namo'bhaktamanoratheśa namo namo viśvavidhānadakṣa |
namo namo daityavināśaheto namo namaḥ saṃkaṭanāśakāya ॥46॥
namo namaḥ kāruṇikottamāya namo namo jñānamayāya te'stu |
namo namo'jñānavināśanāya namo namo bhakta vibhūtidāya ॥47॥
namo namo'bhaktavibhūti hantre nayo namo bhakta vimocanāya |
namo namo'bhakta vibandhanāya namo namaste pravibhakta mūrte ॥48॥
namo namastatva vibodhakāya namo namastatva viduttamāya |
namo namaste'khila karma sākṣiṇe namo namaste guṇanāyakāya ||49
brahmovāca |
evaṃ stutaḥ surairdevo dviradānana īśvaraḥ |
uvāca paramaprīto harṣayat surasattamān ॥50॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
stotreṇa tapasā caiva surāḥ saṃtuṣṭimāgataḥ |
dadāmi sakalābhīṣṭaṃ tad vṛṇudhvaṃ sureśvarāḥ ॥51॥
devā ūcuḥ |
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa tripuraṃ jahi dānavam |
sarveṣāmadhikārānno gṛhītvā yastu tiṣṭhati ॥52॥
tvayaivāsyābhayaṃ dattaṃ sarvāmarasamūhataḥ |
ataḥ sma saṃkaṭaṃ prāptāḥ saṃkaṭānmocayāśu naḥ ॥53॥
tvāmeva śaraṇaṃ prāptā eṣa eva varo hi naḥ |
gaṇeśa uvāca |
vārayiṣye bhayaṃ sarvaṃ tasmādghoratarāddhi vaḥ ॥54॥
bhavatkṛtamidaṃ stotramatiprītikaraṃ mam |
saṃkaṣṭanāśanamiti vikhyātaṃ ca bhaviṣyati ॥55॥
paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ caiva sarvakāmapradaṃ nṛṇām |
trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhedetat saṃkaṣṭaṃ nāpnuyāt kvacit ॥56॥
brahmovāca |
iti dattvā varaṃ teṣāṃ surāṇāṃ jagadīśvaraḥ |
paśyatāṃ munidevānāṃ tatraivāntardadhe vibhuḥ ॥ 57 ॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe stotra nirūpaṇaṃ nāma catvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥40॥

<adhyāya 41 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
varadena gaṇeśena prabhuṇā sarvakāriṇāṃ |
kiṃ kṛtaṃ tanmamācakṣva pṛcchate caturānana ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
tato gajānano viprarūpeṇa tripuraṃ yayau |
mahārhāsana saṃviṣṭaṃ dadarśa dvijasattamaḥ ||2||
sa utthāya namaskṛtya svāsane copaveśayat |
saṃpūjya paripapṛccha kuta āgamyate dvija ॥3॥
kā vidyā kiṃ ca te nāma pṛcchate brūhi me dvija |
prayojanaṃ ca kiṃ te'sti śaktiścet karavāmahe ॥4॥
dvija uvāca |
sāyaṃgṛhā vayaṃ daitya sarvajñāḥ sarvavedinaḥ |
icchāvihārā lokānāṃ bhramāmo hitakāmyayā ||5 ||
kalādharetyeva nāmnā vikhyātā bhuvanatraye |
dṛṣṭukāmā vaibhavaṃ te saṃprāptā bhavanaṃ tava |6||
akhilāḥ saṃpadaste tu dṛṣṭvā tṛptāḥ sma sāṃpratam |
na kailāse na vaikuṃṭhe brahmaloke'pi nedṛśī ||7||
pade śākre na vai saṃpadyādṛśī dṛśyate tava |
daitya uvāca |
nāmamātrāṃ kalādhāraḥ kiṃ vā jānāsi tāṃ dvija ॥8||
saṃpadaḥ sarvalokeṣu yābhya etā praśaṃsasi |
jānāsi ceddarśaya me tāsāṃ madhye mahotkaṭām ॥9॥
dṛṣṭvā dāsye vāṃchitaṃ te priyaṃ prāṇamapi dvija |
na smare parihāse'pi vitathaṃ bhāṣitaṃ mune |10||
kalādhara uvāca |
pareṣāṃ saṃpadaṃ dṛṣṭvā kiṃ syāttava suradviṣa |
vinayātte prasanno'haṃ kalayā te dadāmi vai ॥11॥
kāṃcanaṃ rājataṃ lauhaṃ tripuraṃ śarasaṃsthitam |
rama tatra sthiro daitya cirakālaṃ yathāsukham ||12॥
abhedyaṃ deva gaṃdharvairmānuṣairuragairapi |
kalpitārthapradaṃ tatte kāmagaṃ kāmadaṃ śubham ||13||
yadaivaikena bāṇena kasmiṃścit kālaparyaye |
bhetsyate taddharo daitya tadā nāśamupaiṣyati || 14॥
brahmovāca |
ityuktvā dhanurādāya saṃstabhya śaramantarā|
puratrayaṃ nirmame'sau bhuvanatraya sannibham ॥15॥
vicitrairbhavanai ramyai dīrghikārāma saṃyutaḥ |
nānānakṣi gaṇairjuṣṭaṃ sarvakāmapradaṃ khagam ||16||
māyayā mohito daityastatrastho cahṛṣe bhṛśam |
jagarja ghanavaddaityo lokatrayavikampanaḥ ||17|
na mattaḥ śreṣṭha ityevaṃ garvadarpa samanvitaḥ |
trailokyaṃ kṣobhayāmāsa brāhmaṇaṃ tamathābravīt ॥18॥
yācasva durlabhataraṃ tatte dāsye dvijottama |
ityuktvā sa dvijaḥ prāha taṃ daityaṃ nispṛho'pi san ॥19॥
dvija uvāca |
ahaṃ kailāsamagamaṃ dṛṣṭavān mūrtimuttamam |
śivena pūjitāṃ samyag gāṇeśīṃ cintitārthadām ॥20॥
tāmānaya tvaṃ me dehi śaktiryadyasureśvara |
netādṛśī mayā mūrtistrailokyaṃ caratekṣitā ॥21॥
ato mama manastasyāmāsaktaṃ daityasattama |
tāṃ prāpya kṛtakṛtyo'haṃ bhaviṣyāmyasureśvara ॥22॥
kīrtiṃ te prathayiṣyāmi trailokye sacarācare |
na dātā tripurācchreṣṭho vāṃchitaṃ yaḥ prayacchati ॥23॥
daitya uvāca |
śaṃkara kikaraṃ manye gaṇaye na ca devatāḥ |
ānayitvā pradāsyāmi tāṃ mūrtiṃ dvijapuṃgava ॥24॥
brahmovāca |
ityuktvā pūjayāmāsa taṃ kalādharamādarāt|
dadau tasmai daśa grāmān govastra bhūṣaṇāni ca ॥25॥
muktāphalāni bhūrīṇi mahārhāṇītarāṇi ca |
ratnāni ca pravālāni rāṃkavāstaraṇāni ca ॥26॥
dāsīdāsa śataṃ nānā bhūṣāyuktaṃ dade'suraḥ |
sadaśvān savarūthāṃśca svarṇākṣān rājatān rathān ॥27॥
pragṛhyai tat balāddattaṃ prayayoṃ sa kalādhara |
svāśramaṃ harṣayan patnī sarvānāśramavāsinaḥ ॥28॥
brahmovāca |
evaṃ ca sarvaṃ vṛttāntaṃ nārado'kathayatsurān |
te'pi kālaṃ pratīkṣanto divasānaticakramuḥ ॥29॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe kalādharagamanaṃ nāmaikacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥41 ॥

<adhyāya 42 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
kṛtavān kimu daityaḥ sa gate tāsminkalādhare|
kathamānīya dattā'smai mūrtiścintāmaṇeḥ śubhā ॥1॥
etatkathaya me sarvaṃ vistārya caturānana |
līlā gajānanīḥ śṛṇvan na tṛpyāmi samāsataḥ ॥2॥
brahmovāca
tasmingate yadakarot sa daityo munisattama |
tatsarvaṃ kathayiṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāvahito mune ॥3॥
dūtau saṃpreṣayāmāsa mandarādristhitaṃ śivam |
aśikṣayacchivaṃ gatvā brūtaṃ madvākyamādarāt ॥4॥
mūrtiścintāmaṇeste'sti gṛhe sarvārthadā śubhā|
dehi tāṃ daityarājāya sāmnaiva girijāpate ॥5॥
pātāle svargaloke vā martye vā punaradbhutam |
tatsarvaṃ tena daityaina hyānītaṃ svagṛhe talāt ॥6॥
śīghramānīyatāṃ deva yāvo daityaṃ mahābalam |
na dāsyase yadā sāmnā tadā daityaḥ parākramī ॥7॥
grahīṣyati balāttāṃ tu tato duḥkhamavāpsyasi |
iti daityavacaḥ śrutvā jagmatustau śivaṃ prati ॥8॥
āhatustau mahādevaṃ daityarājena śikṣitam |
itthaṃ dūtavacaḥ śrutvā trinetraḥ krodhamūrchitaḥ ॥9॥
uvāca dūtau dūtau vāmiti kṛtvā kṣame vacaḥ |
kāmasyeva bhavedbhasma no cedvāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥10॥
kiṃ kāryaṃ teta daityena tṛṇībhūtena me vibhoḥ |
āyātu yuddhamevāstu martukāmo mamāntikam ॥11॥
neyaṃ mūrtistena śakyā prāptuṃ janmaśatairapi |
pralayāgniḥ pataṃgena kiṃ śāntimadhigacchati ॥12॥
meroḥ pāṃto mūṣakena śakyaḥ kartuṃ kimojasā |
asaṃkhya jala niṣkāśācchuṣkaḥ syātkiṃ mahodadhiḥ ॥13॥
brahmovāca 
śrutvā tu śāṃkarīṃ vāṇīṃ daityau yātau yathāgatam |
abrūtāṃ svāminaṃ vākyaṃ śaṃbhunā yadudīritam ॥14॥
tadākarṇya prajajvāla daityo vākyārthakovidaḥ |
krodhānalena dīpto'sau trailokyaṃ pradahanniva ॥15॥
ājñāpayacca yuddhāya svasenāṃ caturaṃgiṇīm |
nirgatā sahasā senā mandarācalasaṃmukhā| 16 ||
bhūtalaṃ chādayāmāsa nimaṃryādo yathā'mbudhiḥ |
aneṃkasūrya saṃkāśā vikośaiḥ śastrasaṃcayaiḥ ॥17॥
garjantī ghanavadghoraṃ mṛtyormānasakampinīṃ |
daityastripuramāruhya vimānasadṛśaṃ mahat ॥18॥
manovegaṃ tataḥ paścājjighāṃsuḥ śaṃbhumāyayauṃ |
bṛhanmaṇimayaṃ cāru kavacaṃ kuṃḍale'gade ॥19॥
muktāmālāṃ mudrikāśca kaṭisūtraṃ ca kāṃcanam|
mukuṭaṃ ratnakhacitaṃ mahārghaṃ bibhradullasat ॥20॥
yasya śabdena mahatā cakampe haramānasam |
taddhanuḥ sa niṣaṃgaṃ ca kaurmaṃ kheṭamasiṃ dṛḍham ॥21॥
śuśubhe śaktikā divyā'vahadyāṃ daityapuṃgavaḥ |
gāyamānā nartamānā gaṃdharvāpsarasāṃ gaṇāḥ ॥22॥
bandinaścāraṇāścāpi yayurasyāgrato mudā |
śaṃkaro dūtavākyena śrutvā daityaṃ mamāgatam ॥23॥
asaṃkhya vratasaṃyuktaṃ yuyutsuṃ kālakarṣitam |
śūlapāṇirapi tadā saṃpūjya dviradānanam ॥24॥
praṇamya taṃ parikramya puraskṛtya balaṃ balāt |
āyayau krodhadīptākṣaḥ svasthalād gaṇamaṃḍalam ॥25॥
te vīrā vīraśabdena nādayanto diśo daśa |
abhijagmuḥ praharaṇaiḥ parasparavadhepsavaḥ ॥26॥
abhūtāṃ mīlite seneṃ rajastimirasaṃkule|
svaparajñānarahite jaghnatū raṇamūrdhani ॥27॥
abhavattumulaṃ yuddhaṃ na prājñāyata kiṃcana|
gajāśvarathavīrāṇāṃ hatānāṃ śoṇitokṣite ॥28॥
śānte rajasya yudhyanta vīrā vīraiḥ pṛthak pṛthak|
kecit prāsaḥ ke'pi khaḍgaiḥ kecidbāṇai śilāśitaiḥ ॥29॥
ṛṣṭibhirmuṣṭibhiḥ kecit kecit paraśu tomaraiḥ |
hatānāṃ tatra vīrāṇāṃ hayānāṃ ca padātinām ॥30॥
asṛṅanadī samabhavat keśaśaivala vāhinī |
kheṭakūrmā khaḍgamatsyā śiraḥ kamalabhūṣaṇā ॥31॥
chatrāvartā ghoratarā kabandha vṛkṣavāhinī |
vīra saṃtoṣajananī gṛdhragomāyu harṣakṛt ॥32॥
dṛṣṭvā nadīṃ tu giriśo yayau daityāntikaṃ balī |
daityo'pi tripurārūḍho yayau tasya puraḥ puraḥ ॥33॥
nāyakau saṃgatau dṛṣṭvā dvandvaśo'nye parasparam |
avyākule yuyudhire daiteyāḥ śāmbhavāstadā ॥34॥
nānā praharaṇairdivyaiḥ śastrarastrairdrumairapi |
teṣāṃ yuddhāni nāmāni kathayiṣye samāsataḥ ॥35॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe yuddhavarṇanaṃ nāma dvicatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥ 42||

<adhyāya 43 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca |
ayudhyetāṃ dvandvaśo'tha giriśāsuranāyakau |
ṣaṇmukhena pracaṃḍaśca nandinā caṃḍa eva ca ॥1॥
yuyudhe puṣpadanto'pi bhīmakāyena vīryavān |
bhṛśuṃḍī kālakūṭena viṣavatprāṇahāriṇā ॥2॥
ayudhyetāṃ vīrabhadra vajradaṃṣṭrau mahābalau |
indro'pi yuyudhe tatra daityāmātyena vīryavān ॥3॥
daityaputreṇa balinā jayanto yuddhadurmadaḥ |
kāvyena yuyudhe tatra surācāryo'stravittamaḥ ||4॥
devānāṃ caiva daityānāmevaṃ dvandvānyanekaśaḥ |
varṇituṃ naiva śakyāni mayā varṣaśatairapi ||5||
rathino rathibhiḥ sārdhaṃ gajino gajibhiḥ saha |
aśvārohāḥ svasadṛśaiḥ padātāśca padātibhiḥ ||6॥
yuyudhuḥ saṃyuge ghore nānāvādya ninādite |
heṣitairbṛṃhitaiḥ kṣveḍairneminādaiśca śabdite॥7॥
kecicca mallayuddhena yuyudhurvividhena tu |
jaghnuraṃgāni cāṃgeṣu tyaktvā śastrāṇi saṃyuge ॥8॥
tataḥ pracaṃḍo navabhistāḍayāmāsa ṣaṇmukham |
ākarṇākarṣanisṛtairdṛḍhairbāṇaiḥ śilāśitaiḥ ॥9॥
aprāptāneva tāṃśchittvā śaraiḥ sannataparvabhiḥ |
kātikeyaḥ paṃcabhistaṃ tāḍayāmāsa sāyakaiḥ ॥10॥
saṃmūrchito'patadbhūmau pracaṃḍo bhrāntamānasaḥ |
nandinā'pi hataścaṃḍaḥ paṃcabhi niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ ॥11॥
mūrchāmavāpa sahasā patito dharaṇītale|
bhīmakāyaḥ puṣpadantamavidhyaddaśabhiḥ śaraiḥ ॥12॥
chedayāmāsa samare svaśarairniśitaiḥ sa tān |
tribhirevāhanattaṃ ca so'pi bhūtalamāśritaḥ ॥13॥
kālakūṭaṃ bhṛśuṃḍīstu pātayāmāsa paṃcabhiḥ |
śarairmahābalastatra vīrabhadro ruṣāyutaḥ ॥14॥
vajradaṃṣṭrastato jaghne caturbhirniśitai śaraiḥ |
tānnivārya svayaṃ jaghne vīrabhadraṃ tribhiḥ śarai ॥15॥
tānāpatata evāśu vīrabhadro'cchinat tribhiḥ |
pātayāmāsa vegena vajradaṃṣṭaṃ tribhiḥ śaraiḥ ॥16॥
indro'pi vajrapātena daityāmātyaṃ nyapātayat |
udyamya niśitaṃ khaḍgaṃ daityaputraḥ samāyayau ॥17॥
hantukāmo jayantaṃ taṃ vīreṣu patiteṣu ca |
taṃ nirīkṣya tathā''yāntaṃ khaḍgaṃ ciccheda patriṇā ॥18॥
jayanto'bhyahanacchīghraṃ daityasūnuṃ tribhiḥ śaraiḥ |
tairāhato vamanraktaṃ nyapatanmohasaṃyutaḥ ॥19॥
evaṃ sarveṣu sainyeṣu prabhagneṣu samantataḥ |
dṛṣṭvā palāyanaparāndaityāndevagaṇārditān ॥20॥
adhāvanpṛṣṭataḥ kecitpramayā jayaśālinaḥ |
evaṃ jayatsu deveṣu svasainye vidrute'pi ca ॥21॥
āyayau svayameveśaṃ tripurādhiṣṭito'suraḥ |
śastrayuddhaṃ purā kṛtvā yudhyetāmastrato hyubhau ॥22॥
vāruṇāsraṃ jahau daityo vṛṣṭirghoratarā'bhavat |
nīhārabahule yuddhe na prājñāyata kiṃcana ॥23॥
kvacid vidyutprakāśena svaparajñānakāriṇā |
yuddhamāsīttu tumulaṃ ghorarūpaṃ durāsadam ॥24॥
vilokya sainyaṃ tatsarvaṃ vṛṣṭivātaprapīḍitam |
śilāpātabhayādyāte svasainye tu diśo daśa ॥25॥
vāyavyamastraṃ giriśo mocayāmāsa satvaram |
mahāvātā mahāmeghā viśīrṇāḥ khaṃḍaśo'mbare ॥26॥
vātena ghūrṇitā senā daityānāṃ sarvato diśam |
jagaluḥ pakṣipicchāni vīroṣṇīṣāṇi dūrataḥ ॥27॥
patitāścūrṇitāḥ kecidrathāśva gajapattayaḥ |
unmūlitā latā vṛkṣāśchādayanti sma sainikān ॥28॥
pannagāstreṇa taṃ vāyuṃ tathā daityo nyavārayat |
ākarṇa dhanurākṛṣya tūṇādbāṇaṃ pragṛhaya ca ॥29॥
mantrayannanalāstreṇa śivasainye nyapāyat |
aṃgāravṛṣṭiḥ sahasā patitā sarvadāhinī ॥30॥
pralayaṃ menire sarve jvālā mālābhirarditāḥ |
jvālābhyo dīpyamānābhyo mahābhītikaraḥ pumān ॥31॥
āvirāsīnmahākāyo likhanmūrdhnā nabhastalam |
daṃṣṭrākarālavadano mahārāvaḥ kṣudhāturaḥ ॥32॥
lālayanrasanāṃ ghorāṃ śatayojana vistṛtām |
nāsāpavana vegena bhrāmayankuṃjarānraṇe ॥33॥
bhakṣayāmāsa tāṃ senāmuragāniva pakṣirāṭ |
apīpaladbhargasenā tena puṃsā prapīḍitā ॥34॥
śivapṛṣṭaṃ samāsādya rakṣa rakṣeti cābravīt |
mā bhaiṣṭetyabhayaṃ tasyai dattvā vahni nyavārayat ॥35॥
parjanyāstra prapātena tatkṣaṇād girijāpatiḥ|
bāṇenaikena taṃ ghoraṃ purūṣaṃ sa nyapātayat ॥36॥
tata utthāya puruṣo babhakṣa śivasainikān |
yayuḥ palāyanaparāḥ pramathā bhayavihvalāḥ ॥37॥
skhalanto nipatantaśca niśvasantaścakaṃpire |
śivo'pi niḥsahāyatvāddarīmevānvapadyata ॥38॥
ṣaḍānanādayo vīrā anusasrustameva hi |
jighṛkṣurgirijāṃ daityo vicintyaikākinīṃ girau ॥39॥
vihāya raṇabhūmiṃ tu yayau kailāsameva saḥ |
dūrāddṛṣṭvā tamāyāntaṃ cakampe girijā tadā ॥40॥
uvāca pitaraṃ gatvā daityo māṃ kiṃ nu neṣyati |
tasyāstadvacanaṃ śrutvā nītvā tāṃ durgamāṃgṛhām ॥41॥
avijñātāṃ svetareṇa sthāpayāmāsa nirbhayām |
so'pi daityastato haimaṃ giriṃ tadgrahaṇecchayā ॥42॥
āgato na dadarśātha girijāṃ kvāpi sattama |
bhramandadarśa tatraikāṃ mūrtiṃ citāmaṇeḥ śubhām ॥43॥
sahasrasūryasaṃkāśāṃ nānālaṃkāraśobhinīm |
trailokyasuṃdarāṃ sadyo gṛhītvā svasthalaṃ yayau ॥44॥
nānāvāditra nirghoṣaiḥ stuvadbhirbandibhirvṛtaḥ |
tataḥ pātālamagamat sarvatra vijayī balī ॥45॥
gacchatastasya daityasya haste'pyantarhitā tadā |
mūrtiścitāmaṇeḥ sā tu tadadbhutamivābhavat ॥46॥
tamevāśakunaṃ matvā tatpuraṃ punarāviśat |
atyantaṃ vimanāścintāṃ durutāṃ samupāgataḥ ॥47॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe tricatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥43॥

<adhyāya 44 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
tataḥ kimakarocchambhustripureṇa parājitaḥ |
kathaṃ tamajayaddaityaṃ tripuraṃ jayaśālinam ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
tataḥ śambhuḥ parāṃ cintāmāpede manasā'sakṛta |
svāhā svadhā vihīnaṃ tu bhūtalaṃ paricintayan ॥2॥
kadā svasthānagā devā bhaviṣyanti gatajvarāḥ |
kenopāyena vāsaḥ syād durjayasya parājayaḥ ॥3॥
evaṃ cintāture tasmin nārado munisattamaḥ |
āyayau śaṃkaraṃ dṛṣṭuṃ devān sarvān yadṛcchayā ॥4॥
taṃ dṛṣṭvā jahṛṣe devo martyaḥ prāpya yathā'mṛtam |
pūjayāmāsa vidhivat kṛtāsana parigraham ॥5॥
āliṃgya tamuvācetthaṃ śivaściṃtāturo bhṛśam |
devānāṃ hitamanvicchan daityasya vadhameva ca ॥6॥
śiva uvāca |
daityena sarvadevānāṃ kadanaṃ kṛtamojasā |
tadraṇe bhagnasaṃkalpāḥ sarve devāḥ palāyitāḥ ॥7॥
yātā daśa diśo brahman kaḥ kutrāste na vedamyaham |
mamāpyastrāṇi tenāstraiḥ prabhagnāni sahasraśaḥ ॥8॥
brahmovāca |
śrutvā śivavacaḥ so'mamavādīn munisattama |
manvānaḥ paramāścaryaṃ trailokyeśaparābhavam ॥9॥
nārada uvāca |
sarvajñe sarvavidyeśe sarveśe sarvakartari |
sarvānane sarvahare sarvasyāpi niyantari ॥10॥
śakte kartumakartuṃ vā'nyathākartumapi prabho |
aṇimādi guṇopete ṣaḍaiśvaryavilāsini ॥11॥
kiṃ vaktavyaṃ mayā deva sarvavaktṛvare tvayi |
muninā gānasaktena trilokīṃ bhramatā'niśam ॥12॥
tava vākyānurodhena vicārya prabravīmyaham |
evamuktvā kṣaṇaṃ dhyātvā punaḥ prāha śivaṃ muniḥ ॥13॥
muniruvāca |
yuddhāya gantukāmena nārcito gaṇapastvayā |
ataḥ parābhavaṃ prāpto vahninetraḥ pinākadhṛk ॥14॥
idānīmarcaya purā vighneśaṃ vighnavāraṇam |
prasādya tadvaraṃ labdhvā yāhi yuddhāya sādaram ॥15॥
parājeṣyasi taṃ daityaṃ nātra kāryā vicāraṇā |
brahmovāca|
tenāpi tapasā devo mahatārādhitaḥ purā ॥16॥
tena tasmai varo datto'khila vighnaughahāriṇā |
maheśvaraṃ vinā mṛtyurna kenāpi bhavettava ॥17॥
tasmād giriśa tasyedaṃ kāmagaṃ tu puratrayam |
ekeṣuṇā dārayasva jayopāyo'yamīritaḥ ॥18॥
praharṣamatulaṃ lebhe śrutvā gajānanagiraṃ|
pūrvāktāṃ prāha taṃ munim ॥19॥
śiva uvāca |
satyamuktaṃ tvayā brahmaṃstvadvākyena smṛtaṃ mayā|
upadiṣṭau pūrvameva mantrau tena mune mama ॥20॥
ṣaḍakṣaraikākṣarau tau sarvasaṃkaṭahārakau |
yuddhe vyāsaktacittena na japtau na ca saṃsmṛtau ॥21॥
sarvavighnaharo devo na smṛto hi gajānanaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ kāraṇaṃ kartā pātā hartā vināyakaḥ ॥22॥
muniruvāca |
taṃ toṣaya mahādeva mahādevaṃ gajānanam |
brahmovāca |
visarjya nāradaṃ devo jagāma tapase śivaḥ| ॥23॥
daṃḍakāraṇyadeśe tu padmāsanasthito'japat |
praṇidhāya balātkhāni niyamya dhyānatatparaḥ ॥24॥
so'tapattapa ugraṃ tu śatavarṣāṇi śaṃkaraḥ |
tatastasya mukhāmbhojān nirgatastu pumānparaḥ ॥25॥
paṃcavaktro daśabhujo lalāṭenduḥ śaśiprabhaḥ |
muṃḍamālo sarpabhūṣo mukuṭāṃgadabhūṣaṇaḥ ॥26॥
agnyarka śaśino bhābhistiraskurvandaśāyudhaḥ |
tadbhāsā dharṣito devo'paśyadugraṃ puraḥsthitam ॥27॥
vināyakaṃ paṃcamukhaṃ paṃcāsyamaparaṃ śivam |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā'tarkayaddevaḥ kimahaṃ dvividho'bhavam ॥28॥
kiṃvā mamaiva rūpeṇa tripuro'yamihāgataḥ |
trayastriṃśatkoṭi deveṣvaparaḥ paṃcavaktravān ॥29॥
athavā svapna evāyaṃ dṛṣṭo dīrghataro mayā|
athavā me varaṃ dātumāgato'yaṃ gajānanaḥ ॥30॥
sarvavighnaharaṃ devaṃ yaṃ dhyāyāmi divāniśam |
brahmovāca |
evamākarṇya tadvākyamuvāca dviradānanaḥ ॥31॥
antaryastarkito devaḥ so'yaṃ vighnaharo vibhuḥ |
na me svarūpaṃ jānanti devarṣi caturāranāḥ ॥32॥
na vedāḥ sopaniṣadaḥ kutaḥ ṣaṭśāstra vedinaḥ |
aśeṣa bhuvanasyāhaṃ kartā pātā'pahārakaḥ ॥33॥
brahmādi sthāvaracara triguṇāmahaṃ prabhuḥ |
tapasā'nena tuṣṭo'haṃ varaṃ dātumihāgataḥ ॥34॥
varānvṛṇu mahādeva yāvato matta icchasi ॥35॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe tapovarṇanaṃ nāma catuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥44॥

<adhyāya 45 prāraṃbhaḥ>

muniruvāca |
tataḥ prasanne vighneśe deveśe vighnahāriṇi |
varān ditsati śarvāya kiṃ kiṃ vavre sadāśivaḥ ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
gaṇeśavacanaṃ śrutvā śivo vākyamathābravīt |
smṛtvā gajānanaṃ taṃ tu svasvarūpaṃ varapradam ॥2॥
śiva uvāca |
daśāpi netrāṇi mamādya dhanyānyatho bhujāḥ pūjanatastavādya |
tavānate paṃca śirāṃsi dhanyā nyathaḥ stuteḥ paṃcamukhāni deva ॥3॥
pṛthvī jalaṃ vāyuratho diśaśca, tejaśca kālaḥ kalanātmako'pi |
nabho raso rupamathāni gaṃdhaḥ, sparśaśca śabdo mana indriyāṇi ॥4॥
gaṃdharvayakṣāḥ pitaro manuṣyā, devarṣayo devagaṇāśca sarve |
brahmendrarudrā vasavo'tha sādhyāstvattaḥ prasūtā sacarācaraśca ॥5॥
sṛjasyado viśvamananyabuddhe, rajoguṇātpāsi samastametat |
tamoguṇātsaṃharase guṇeśa, nityo nirīhākhilakarmasākṣī ॥6॥
brahmovāca | tato'hamabruvaṃ devaṃ gaṇeśaṃ taṃ śivājñayā|
nāma cākaravaṃ tasya yattacchṛṇu mahāmate ॥7॥
tvannāma bījaṃ prathamaṃ mātṛkāṇāmoṃkāra rūpaṃ śrutimūlabhūtam |
yato gaṇānāṃ tvamasīha īśo, gaṇeśa ityeva tavāstu nāma ॥8॥
omityathokte gaṇanātha kena, tutoṣa śarvaḥ pradadau varāṃśca |
yaḥ sarvakāryeṣu taveśa kuryāt, smṛti tadantaṃ sa labhedavighnam ॥9॥
vināsmṛtiṃ tena labhecca ko'pi vāṃchārya siddhiṃ kṛmikīṭa ko'pi |
śaivestvadīyairatha vaiṣṇavaiśca śāktaiśca saureratha sarvakārye ॥10॥
śubhāśubhe vaidika laukike vā tvamarcanīyaḥ prathamaṃ prayatnāta |
yanmaṃgalaṃ sarvajaneṣu deva sayakṣa vidyādharapannageṣu ॥11॥
tasyeśvaro maṃgalamūrtitāṃ tvaṃ tato yato maṃgalakṛt svabhakte |
ahaṃ purā'narcayatastaveśa tvayyasmṛte daityavarasya yuddhe ॥12॥
avaṃdanāccāpa parābhavaṃ taṃ tatastavāṃghriṃ śaraṇaṃ prapannaḥ |
kṣamāparādhaṃ mama sarvaśakte jayaṃ prayacchākhilajanya kāle ॥13॥
ye sarvathā tvāṃ na bhajanti deva, jaḍā daridrāḥ prabhavantu te'pi|
bhajaṃti te bhaktiyutā manuṣyāḥ, prāpsyanti siddhiṃ nikhilārthadātrīm ॥14॥
ka uvāca |
itthaṃ niśamyākhilavākyasāraṃ vettā'vadadvākyamidaṃ girīśam |
yadā yadā me smaraṇaṃ vidaddhyāstadā'ntikaṃ te'hamiyāmi meu ॥15॥
mannāmabījena nimaṃtrayaikaṃ bāṇaṃ tu tenaiva puratrayaṃ tat |
nipātayāsman mahasā maheśa, kṛtvā sa daityaṃ khalu bhasmasātvam ॥16॥
tataḥ svanāmnāmavadat sahasraṃ gaṇādhipo'smai praṇatāya samyak |
śivāya bhaktyā parituṣṭacitto, jayapradaṃ kāmakaraṃ janānām ॥17॥
uvāca cainaṃ raṇakāla etat paṭhasva daityānvinihaṃsi śīghram |
trisaṃdhyametasya japānnarāṇāṃ sidhyanti kāmāḥ sakalā abhīṣṭāḥ ॥18॥
śrutvā tu vākyaṃ dviradānanasya, taṃ pūjya samyakśiva ujjaharṣa |
saṃsthāpayāmāsa mahāgaṇeśaṃ prāsādamuccairdṛḍhamāśu cakre ॥19॥
saṃtarpya devān muni siddhasaṃghān dānāni datvā dvijapuṃgavebhyaḥ |
punaśca saṃpūjya namaścakāra devaṃ girīśo varadaṃ gaṇeśaṃ ॥20॥
uvāca sarvān maṇipūriyaṃ tu vikhyātimāgacchatu sarvaloke |
devena sarvaiśca tathoditaistairantardadhuste gaṇanāthamukhyāḥ ॥21॥
antarhite munigaṇe sasure gaṇeśe, śarvopyagāt svanilayaṃ svagaṇairupetaḥ |
gandharva yakṣa nivahairamarāṃganābhirvṛttaṃ nijaṃ paramudā girijāmuvāca ॥22॥
śrutvā tadā'mṛtavacaḥ sakalaṃ girīśāddeveśvarā mumudire munayaḥ sadārāḥ |
yogīśvarāśca nihataṃ tripuraṃ svadhiṣṇyamamaṃsatādhigataṃ giriśa prasādāt ॥23॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe śivasya varadānaṃ nāma paṃcacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥45॥

<adhyāya 46 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
kathaṃ nāmnāṃ sahasraṃ tad gaṇeśa upadiṣṭavān|
śivadaṃ tanmamācakṣva lokānugrahatatpara || 1 ||
brahmovāca |
devaḥ pūrvaṃ purārātiḥ puratrayajayodyame |
anarcanādgaṇeśasya jāto vighnākulaḥ kila ||2||
manasā sa vinirdhārya tatastadvighnakāraṇaṃ |
mahāgaṇapatiṃ bhaktyā samabhyarcya yathāvidhī ||3||
vighnapraśamanopāyamapṛcchattaṃ parājitaḥ |
saṃtuṣṭaḥ pūjayā śaṃbhormaṃhāgaṇapatiḥ svayam ||4||
sarvavighnapraśamanaṃ sarvakāmaphalapradaṃ |
tatastamai svasya nāmnāṃ sahassramidamabravīt ||5||
asya śrīmadgaṇeśadivyasahasranāmāmṛtastotramālāmahāmaṃtrasya śrīmahāgaṇapatirṛṣiḥ |
śrīsvānaṃdeśo brahmaṇaspatirdevatā |
nānāvidhāni cchaṃdāṃsi |
humiti bījaṃ |
tuṃgamitīṃ śaktiḥ |
svāhāśaktiriti kīlakaṃ |
sakalavighnavināśanadvārā śrībrahmaṇaspatiprītyarthaṃ jape viniyoga 1 |
atha nyāsāḥ |
gaṇeśvaro gaṇakrīḍa ityaṃguṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ |
kumāragururīśāneti tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ |
brahmāṃ ḍakuṃbhaścidvyometi madhyamābhyāṃ namaḥ |
raktoraktāṃbaradhara ityanāmikābhyāṃ namaḥ sarvasadgurusaṃsevya iti kaniṣṭhikābhyāṃ namaḥ |
luptavighnaḥ subhavanāmiti karatalakarapṛṣṭhābhyāṃ namaḥ |
atha hṛdayādinyāsāḥ |
chaṃdaścchaṃdodbhava iti hṛdayāya namaḥ |
niṣkalo nirmala iti śirase svāhā |
sṛṣṭisthitilayakrīḍa iti śikhāyai vaṣaṭ |
jñānaṃ vijñānamānaṃdaṃ iti kavacāya hum |
aṣṭāṃgayogaphala bhṛditi netratrayāya vauṣaṭ |
anaṃtaśaktisahitaṃ ityastrāya phaṭ |
iti digbaṃdhaḥ |
atha dhyānam |
raktāṃbhodhisthapotollasadaruṇasarojādhiruḍhaṃ trinetraṃ |
pāśaṃ caivāṃkuśāḍhyaṃ paraśumabhayadaṃ bāhubhirdhārayaṃtaṃ |
śaktyā yuktaṃ gajāsyaṃ puthutarajaṭharaṃ siddhibuddhīsametaṃ |
raktaṃ caṃdrārdhamauliṃ sakalabhayaharaṃ vighnarājaṃ namāmi || 1 ||
śrīsiddhibuddhisahitāya salakṣalābhāya śrosvānaṃdeśāya brahmaṇaspataye sāṃgāya saparivārāya saśaktikāya sāyudhāya savāhanāya sāvaraṇāya namaḥ |
iti nāmamaṃtreṇa mānasaiḥ pañcopacāraiḥ saṃpūjya paṭhet |
| śrīmahāgaṇapatiruvāca |
gaṇeśvaro gaṇakrīḍo gaṇanātho gaṇādhipaḥ |
ekadaṃṣṭro vakratuṃḍo gajavaktro mahodaraḥ || 1 ||
laṃbodaro dhūmravarṇo vikaṭo vighnanāyakaḥ |
sumukho durmukho buddho vighnarājo gajānanaḥ ||2||
bhīmaḥ pramoda āmodaḥ surānaṃdo mahotkaṭaḥ |
heraṃbaḥ śaṃbaraḥ śaṃbhurlaṃbakarṇo mahābalaḥ ||3||
naṃdano laṃpaṭo bhīmameghanādo gaṇaṃjayaḥ |
vināyako virūpākṣo vīraśūravarapradaḥ ||4||
mahāgatirbuddhipriyaḥ kṣipraprasādanaḥ |
rudrapriyo gaṇādhyakṣa umāputro'ghanāśanaḥ ||5||
kumāragururīśānaputro mūṣakavāhanaḥ |
siddhipriyaḥ siddhipatiḥ siddhiḥ siddhivināyakaḥ ||6||
avighnastuṃbaruḥ siṃhavāhano mohinīpriyaḥ |
kaṭaṃkaṭo rājaputraḥ śākalaḥ saṃmito'mitaḥ ||7||
kūṣmāṃḍasāmasaṃbhūtirdurjayo dhurjayo jaya |
bhūpatirbhuvanapatirbhūtānāṃ patiravyayaḥ ||8||
viśvakartā viśvamukho viśvavaṃdyo vinirguṇaḥ |
kaviḥ kavīnāmṛṣabho brahmaṇyo brahmaṇaspatiḥ ||9 ||
jyeṣṭharājo nidhipatirnidhipriyapatipriyaḥ |
hiraṇmayapurāṃtaḥsthaḥ sūryamaṃḍalamadhyagaḥ || 10 ||
karāhatidhvastasiṃdhusalilaḥ pūṣadaṃtabhit |
umāṃkakelikutukī muktidaḥ kulapālanaḥ || 11 ||
kirīṭikuṃḍalihārivanamālimanomayaḥ |
vaimukhyahatadaityaśrīḥ pādāhatyejitakṣitiḥ || 12||
sadyojātaḥ svarṇamuṃjamekhalī durnimittahṛt |
duḥsvapnaduṣṭaśamano guṇī nādapratipṭhitaḥ || 13 ||
surūpaḥ sarvanetrādhivāso vibhrājinūpuraḥ|
pītāṃbaraḥ khaṃḍaradaḥ khaṃḍavaiśākhasaṃsthitaḥ || 14||
citrāṃgaḥ śyāmadaśano bhālacaṃdro havirbhujaḥ |
yogādhipastārakasthapuruṣo gajakarṇakaḥ || 15||
gaṇādhirājo vijayaḥ sthiro gajapatirdhvajī |
devadevaḥ smaraprāṇadīpako vāyukīlakaḥ || 1 6||
vipaścidvaradonnādo nādabhinnamahācalaḥ |
varāhavadano mṛtyuṃjayo vyāghrājināṃbaraḥ || 1 7||
icchāśaktibhavo devatrātā daityavimardanaḥ |
śaṃbhuvaktrodbhavaḥ śaṃbhukopajaḥ śaṃbhuhāsyabhūḥ || 1 8||
śaṃbhutejāḥ śivāśokahārī gaurīsukhāvahaḥ |
umāṃgamalajo gaurītejobhūḥ svardhunībhavaḥ || 1 9||
mattakāyo mahānādo girivarṣmā śubhānanaḥ |
sarvātmā sarvadevātmā brahmamūrddhā kakupśrutiḥ ||2 0 ||
brahmāṃḍakuṃbhaścidvyomabhālaḥ sanyaśiroruhaḥ |
jagajjanmalayonmeṣanimeṣo'gnyarkasomadṛk || 21 ||
girīṃdrakarado dharmādharmoṣṭhaḥ sāmabṛṃhita|
graharkṣadaśano vāṇījihvo vāsavanāsikaḥ ||22 ||
bhrūmadhyasaṃsthitakaro brahmavidyāmadodakaḥ |
kulācalāṃsaḥ somārkaghaṃṭo rudraśirodharaḥ ||23 ||
nadīnadabhujaḥ sarpāṃgulīkastārakānakhaḥ |
vyomanābhiḥ śrīrhṛdayo merupṛṣṭho'rṇavodaraḥ ||24||
kukṣisthayakṣagaṃdharvaṃrakṣaḥkinnaramānuṣaḥ |
pṛthvīkaṭiḥ sṛṣṭiliṃgaḥ śailorurdrasrajānukaḥ ||25 ||
pātālajaṃgho sunipātkālāṃguṣṭhasrayītanuḥ |
jyotirmaṃḍalalāṃgūlo hadayālānaniścalaḥ ||26 ||
hṛtpadmakarṇikāśālī viyatkelisarovaraḥ |
sadbhaktadhyānanigaḍaḥ pūjāvārīnivāritaḥ ||27||
pratāpī kāśyapo yaṃtā gaṇako viṣṭapī balī |
yaśasvī dhārmiko jetā prathamaḥ prathameśvaraḥ ||28||
ciṃtāmaṇidvīpapatiḥ kalpadrumavanālayaḥ |
ratnamaṃḍapamadhyastho ratnasiṃhāsanāśrayaḥ ||2 9 ||
tīvrāśiroddhṛtapado jvālinīmaulilālitaḥ |
naṃdānaṃditapīṭhaśrīrbhogadābhūṣitāsanaḥ || 30 ||
sakāmadāyinīpīṭhaḥ sphuradugrāsanāśrayaḥ |
tejovatīśiroratnaṃ satyānityāvataṃsitaḥ || 31 ||
savighnāśinīpīṭhaḥ sarvaśaktyaṃbujālayaḥ |
lipipadmāsanādhāro vahnidhāmatrayālayaḥ || 32 ||
unnataprapado gūḍhagulphaḥ saṃvṛtapārṣṇikaḥ |
pīnajaṃghaḥ śliṣṭajānuḥ sthūloruḥ pronnamatkaṭiḥ || 33 ||
nimnanābhiḥ sthūlakukṣi pīnavakṣā bṛhadbhujaḥ |
pīnaskaṃdhaḥ kaṃbukaṭho laboṣṭho laṃbanāsikaḥ || 34||
bhagnavāmaradastuṃgāsavyadaṃto mahāhanuḥ |
hrasvanetratrayaḥ śūrpakarṇo nibiḍamastakaḥ ||35||
stabakākārakuṃbhāgro ratnamaulirniraṃkuśaḥ |
sarpahārakaṭīsūtraḥ sarpayajñopavītavān || 36 ||
sarpakoṭīrakaṭakaḥ sarpagraiveyakāṃgadaḥ |
sarpakakṣodarābaṃdhaḥ sarparājottaracchadaḥ || 37||
rakto raktāṃbaradharo raktamālyavibhūṣaṇaḥ |
raktekṣaṇo raktakaro raktatālvoṣṭhapallavaḥ ||3 8||
śvetaḥ śvetāṃbaradharaḥ śvetamālyavibhūṣaṇaḥ |
śvetātapatraruciraḥ śvetacāmaravījitaḥ || 39 ||
sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇaḥ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ |
sarvābharaṇaśobhāḍhyaḥ sarvaśobhāsamanvitaḥ ||40 ||
sarvamaṃgalamāṃgalyaḥ sarvakāraṇakāraṇaṃ |
sarvadaikakaraḥ śārṅgī bījapūrī gadādharaḥ ||4 1 ||
śubhrāṃgo lokasāraṃgaḥ sutaṃtustaṃtuvarddhanaḥ |
kirīṭī kuṃḍalī hārī vanamālī śubhāṃgadaḥ ||42||
ikṣucāpadharaḥ śūlī cakrapāṇiḥ sarojabhṛt |
pāśī dhṛtotpalaḥ śālīmaṃjarībhṛtsvadaṃtabhṛt ||43 ||
kalpavallīdharo viśvābhayadaikakaro vaśī |
akṣamālādharo jñānamudrāvānmudgarāyudhaḥ ||44||
pūrṇapātro kaṃbudharo vidhṛtākṣaḥ sumodakaḥ ||
karasthāmraphalaścūtakalikābhṛtkuṭhāravān ||45 ||
puṣkarathasvarṇaghaṭapūrṇaratnāṃbuvarṣakaḥ |
bhāratīsuṃdarīnātho bināyakaratipriyaḥ ||46||
mahālakṣmīpriyatamaḥ siddhalakṣmīmanorama |
ramārameśapūrvāṃgo dakṣiṇomāmaheśvara ||47||
mahivarāhavāmāṃgo ratikaṃdarpapaścimaḥ |
āmodamodajananaḥ sapramodapra modanaḥ |||48||
samedhitasamṛddhaśrīrṛddhisiddhipravardhanaḥ |
daṃtasaumukhyasumukhakāṃtikaṃdalitāśrayaḥ ||49 ||
madanāvatyāśritāṃghri kṛtasaumukhyadurmukhaḥ |
vighnasaṃpallavodvignasevonnidramadadravaḥ ||5 0 ||
vighnakṛnnimnacaraṇadrāviṇīśaktisaṃvṛtaḥ |
tīvrāprasannavadano jvālinīpālitaikadṛk ||5 1 ||
mohinīṃ mohano bhogadāyinīkīrtimaṃḍitaḥ |
kāminīkāṃtayuktaśrīstejinyadhiṣṭhitaikadṛk ||52||
vasudhārāmadonnādo mahāśaṃkhanidhipriyaḥ |
namadvasumatīmauliprahvaḥ padmanidhiprabhuḥ ||5 3 ||
sarvasadgurūsaṃsevyaḥ śociṣkeśahṛdāśrayaḥ |
īśānamūrddhā deveṃdraśikhaḥ pavananaṃdanaḥ ||54||
pratyugnanayanādityo divyāstrāṇāṃ prayogavit |
airāvatādisarvāśāvāraṇovāraṇāpriyaḥ ||55||
vajrādyastraparīvāragaṇacaṃḍasamāśrayaḥ |
jayājayaparikaro vijayāvijayāvahaḥ ||5 6 ||

kṣayaḥ kuṣṭhaḥ pramehāṃśau bhagaṃdaraviṣūcike |
gulma plīhā na valmīkamatisāraṃ mahaudaraṃ || 183 ||
kāsaṃ śvāsa gudāvartaṃ śūla śophaṃ jalodaraṃ |
śirorogaṃ vamiṃ hikkāṃ gaṃḍamālāmarocakaṃ || 1 84||
vātapittakaphadvaṃdvatridoṣajanitajvaraṃ |
āgaṃtuṃ viṣamaṃ śītamuṣṇaṃ caikāhikādikaṃ || 185 ||
ityādyuktamanuktaṃ vā roga doṣādisaṃbhavaṃ |
sarvaṃ praśamayatyāśu stotrasyāsya sakṛjjapaḥ || 1 86||
prāpyate'sya japātsiddhiḥ strīśūdrapatitairapi |
sahasranāmamaṃtro'yaṃ japtavyastu śubhāptaye || 1 87||
mahāgaṇapate stotraṃ sakāmaḥ prajapannidaṃ |
icchayā sakalān bhogānupabhujyeha pārthivān || 1 88||
manorathaphalairdivyairvyomayānairmanoramaiḥ |
caṃdreṃdrabhāskaropeṃdrabrahmaśarvādisadmasu || 1 89||
kāmarūpaḥ kāmagatiḥ kāmadaḥ kāmadeśvaraḥ |
bhuktvā yathepsitānbhogānabhīṣṭaiḥ saha baṃdhubhiḥ || 190 ||
gaṇeśānucaro bhūtvā mahāgaṇapate priyaḥ |
naṃdīśvarādisānaṃdairnaṃditaḥ sakalairgaṇaiḥ |||| 191 ||
śivābhyāṃ kṛpayā putranirviśeṣeṇa lālitaḥ |
śivabhaktaḥ pūrṇakāmo gaṇeśvaravarātpunaḥ || 192 ||
jātismaro dharmaparaḥ sārvabhaumo'bhijāyate |
niṣkāmastu japannityaṃ bhaktyā vighneśatatparaḥ || 193 ||
yogasiddhiṃ parāṃ prāpya jñānavairāgyasaṃsthiti |
niraṃtaroditānaṃde paramānaṃdasaṃjñite || 1 94||
viśvottīrṇe pare pūrṇe punarāvṛttivarjite ||
līno vaināyake dhāmni ramate nityanirvate || 195 ||
idaṃ prajaptaṃ paṭhitaṃ pāṭhitaṃ śrāvitaṃ śrutaṃ |
vyāhṛtaṃ carcitaṃ dhyātaṃ vimṛṣṭamabhinaṃditaṃ || 196 ||
ihāmutra aviśleṣaṃ sarvaiśvaryapradāyakaṃ |
svacchadacāriṇā'pyeṣa yena saṃdhāryate stavaḥ |
sa rakṣyate śivodbhūtairgaṇairapyaṣṭakoṭibhiḥ|| 1 97||
likhitaṃ pustake stotraṃ maṃtrabhūtaṃ prapūjayet |
tatra sarvottamā lakṣmī sannidhatte niraṃtaraṃ || 1 98||
dānairaśeṣairakhilairvataiśca |
tīrthairaśeṣairakhilairmakhaśca |
na tatphalaṃ viṃdati yadgaṇeśasahasranāmnā smaraṇena sadyaḥ 199
etannāmnāṃ sahasraṃ paṭhati dinamaṇau pratyahaṃ projjihāne |
sāyaṃ madhyaṃdine vā triṣavaṇamathavā saṃtataṃ vā jano yaḥ |
sa syādaiśvaryadhuryaḥ prabhavati vacasāṃ kītimuccaistanoti|
dāridryaṃ haṃti viśvaṃ vaśayati suciraṃ vardhate putrapautraiḥ ||2 0 0||
akiṃcano'pyekacitto niyato niyatāśanaḥ |
prajapaṃścaturo māsān gaṇeśārcanatatparaḥ ||2 01 ||
daridratāṃ samunmūlya saptajanmānugāmapi |
labhate mahatīṃ lakṣmīmityājñā pārameśvarī ||2 0 2||
āyuṣyaṃ vītarogaṃ kulamativimalaṃ saṃpadaḥ śreṇidānāḥ |
kīrtiḥ puṇyāvadātā bhaṇatirabhinavā kāṃtiravyādhibhavyā ||
putrāḥ saṃtaḥ kalatraṃ guṇavadabhimataṃ yadyadanyacca tattanityaṃ yaḥ stotrametat paṭhati gaṇapatestasya haste samastaṃ ||2 03 ||
gaṇaṃjayo gaṇapatiharaiṃbo dharaṇīdharaḥ |
mahāgaṇapatirbuddhipriyaḥ kṣipraprasādanaḥ ||2 04||
amoghasiddhiramitamaṃtraściṃtāmaṇirnidhiḥ |
sumaṃgalo bījamāśāpūrako varadaḥ kaviḥ ||2 05 ||
kāśyapo naṃdano vācāsiddho ḍhuṃḍhirvināyakaḥ |
modakairebhiratraikaviṃśatyā nāmabhiḥ pumān ||2 0 6||
yaḥ stauti madgatamanā madārādhanatatparaḥ |
stuto nāmnāṃ sahasreṇa tenāhaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ||2 07||
namo namaḥ sukharapūjitāṃghraye |
namo namo nirupamamaṃgalātmane ||
namo namo vipulasādhakasiddhaye |
namo namaḥ karikalabhānanāya te ||2 08||
kiṃkiṇīgaṇaracitacaraṇaḥ |
prakaṭitagurumitacārukaraṇaḥ ||
madajalalaharīkalitakapolaḥ |
śamayatu duritaṃ gaṇapatirnṛpa nāmnā ||2 09 ||
iti śrīmadādi śrīmanmahāgāṇeśvare mahāpurāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe īśvaramahāgaṇapatisaṃvāde śrīmadgaṇeśadivyasahasranāmāmṛtastotra kathanaṃnāma saṃpūrṇam ||
śrīmadgaṇeśārpaṇamastu ||
iti śrīmadgaṇeśasahasranāmamālāmaṃtrātmakastotraṃ samāptam ||
śrīsvānaṃdeśārpaṇamastu ||
ṣaṭcatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ॥ 46 ॥

<adhyāya 47 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
nāmnāṃ sahasre saṃprāpte prasanne ca gajānane|
tataḥ kimakarod brahmana harastat kathayasva me ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
gaṇeśavaradānena nāmnāṃ caivopadeśataḥ |
prahaṣṭaḥ śaṃkaro nṛtyaṃ jagarja ca mahāsvanaḥ ॥2॥
āhūya svagaṇāndevān yuddhāvasaramādiśat |
te ca harṣeṇa mahatā yayurdevāḥ śivāntikama ॥3॥
brahmā kubero'tha purandaro'gni, rvāyuśca somo varuṇo'ryamā ca |
gaṃdharva yakṣā grahakinnarāśca, sarve namaskṛtya śivaṃ stuvantaḥ ॥4॥
devā ūcuḥ|
mahādeva jagannātha jagadānandadāyaka |
tvayā hataṃ mahādaityaṃ kadā drakṣyāmahe vayam ॥5॥
sthānabhraṣṭā vayaṃ tena kṛtā viśvavighātinā |
brahmovāca |
evaṃ devavacaḥ śrutvā pinākī niryayau mudā ॥6॥
gaṇeśaṃ manasā dhyāyanyuddhāya kṛtaniścayaḥ |
vegena bhavanaṃ prāpya devagaṃdharvasaṃyutaḥ ॥7॥
tāvacca daityaṃ cāraistu vṛttāntaḥ sannirūpitaḥ |
āgataḥ surasainyena yuddhāya girijāpatiḥ ॥8॥
tasya sainyānyana hṛyanta śastrairastraiśca kaṃcukaiḥ |
sa ca daityo mahānādo yuddhabhūṣaṇa bhūṣitaḥ ॥9॥
vīrānānandayāmāsa vāsaḥ sragbhūṣaṇairdhanaiḥ |
anekayānasaṃstheṣu yoddhṛṣu tripuraṃ svayam ॥10॥
āruroha mahādaityo nādayānvidiśo diśaḥ |
tato yuddhaṃ samabhavatsenayorubhayormahat ॥11॥
śastrairnānāvidhairbāṇairmarmabhedibhirāyasaiḥ |
asṛṅ nadī pravāho'bhūn mārgarodhanakārakaḥ ॥12॥
śastrāhatā babhuryodhāḥ puṣpitāḥ kiṃśukā iva |
kecicca dṛḍhavaireṇa jīvagrāhaṃ mamārayan ॥13॥
kecitkaceṣu dhṛtvaiva śirāṃsi cicchidurbalāt |
dhāvatāṃ rathivīrāṇāmaśvānāṃ kariṇāmapi ॥14॥
aṅghripātabhavo reṇu vyaniśe rodasī kṣaṇāt |
aṃdhakāre ghoratare na prājñāyata kiṃcana ॥15॥
cakrurevaṃ tadā yuddhaṃ vīrā nānāvidhā bhṛśam |
jīvitāśāṃ parityajya martavye kṛtaniścayāḥ ॥16॥
adṛśyanta mṛtā devā gate rajasi vāyunā |
vidrute surasainye ca harṣite ... ॥17॥
āyayau samaraślāghī vajrahastaḥ purandaraḥ |
śatakoṭiṃ nirīkṣyaiva dudruvurdaityadānavāḥ ॥18॥
sa vajrapātairasurāṃścūrṇayāmāsa vajrabhṛt |
tatyajurjīvitaṃ te tu caṇḍavajraprahārataḥ ॥19॥
kecicca bhagnacaraṇāḥ kecidbhagna śirodharāḥ |
kecidvidīrṇajaṭharāśchinnaskandha bhujāḥ pare॥20॥
chinnāśvānāṃ ca vīrāṇāṃ vājināṃ rathināṃ tathā |
keṣāṃcidukho bhinnā jaṃghāsu ca hatāḥ pare ॥21॥
chinnagulphāḥ pare peturmiṣātpeturathāpare |
nihatānāmasṛkpātairjajñire nimnagā bahu ॥22॥
utsāhavardhanāsteṣāṃ vīrāṇāṃ jayakāṃkṣiṇām |
tataḥ śanairgarjamāno dadarśa tripurāsuraḥ ॥23॥
nihataṃ bahudhā sainyaṃ śakraṃ yoddhumathāyayau |
babhrāṇa śakraṃ dṛṣṭvaiva kimarthaṃ martumicchasi ॥24॥
apaihi yuddhājjīvaṃstvaṃ na puṣṭaṃ hanmi vāsava |
kā te śaktirmayā yuddhaṃ kartuṃ tava śacīpate ॥25॥
ajaḥ siṃhena kiṃ yuddhaṃ kariṣyati vadasva me |
ehi yuddhasva śaktiścennoced gaccha yathāsukham ॥26॥
evamukte sthite śakre vavṛṣe sāyakānbahūn |
sajyaṃ kṛtvā dhanurdaityo devasenāmatāḍayat ॥27॥
ekasmānmaṃtritād bāṇādasaṃkhyātā yayuḥ śarāḥ |
mṛdganto devagaṃdharvān rodasī samapūrayan ॥28॥
andhakāraṃ punarabhūd bāṇajālairnirantaram |
tad bāṇairapi te devā vyaṃgāḥ peturdharātale ॥29॥
balahā nyapatat bhūmau prahāreṇāvapīḍitaḥ |
sarveṣu devavaryeṣu mūrchiteṣu maheśvaraḥ ॥30॥
ślāghantaṃ bahugarjantaṃ yudhyantaṃ nābhyarocayat |
manasā'pūjayacchambhustasya daityasya pauruṣam ॥31॥
etasminnaṃtare tatra nārado dṛṣṭumāyayau |
pūjitaḥ prāvadacchambhuṃ śṛṇuṣva nīlalohita ॥32॥
nārada uvāca |
tvayā cintā na kartavyā tripurasya vadhaṃ prati |
vakṣye tasya vadhopāyaṃ taṃ kuruśva maheśvara ॥33॥
tena pūrvaṃ tapo'kāri duṣkaraṃ brahmaṇo'pi hi |
ārādhito gaṇapatiḥ sa tasmai sarvavāṃchitam ॥34॥
yadyattena vṛtaṃ devo dattavānavicāritam |
puratrayaṃ kāmagamamekabāṇāśritaṃ mahat ॥35॥
marudgativihīnaṃ tadabhedyaṃ sarvanirjaraiḥ |
sa eva muktavān guhyaṃ śareṇakena yastava ॥36॥
puratrayaṃ bhetsyate te tasmān mṛtyumavāpsyasi |
evamuktvā gate tasminnārade munisattame ॥37॥
mūrchitānvibudhān śarvaḥ sāvadhānamathā karot |
sasmārebhamukhaṃ vākyaṃ smārito nāradena tu ॥38॥
mahāprayatnamārebhe daityarājavadhāya saḥ |
tejasā sarvanāśāya śakto devo nijecchayā ॥39॥
rathaṃ kurūpaṃ śaśisūryacakraṃ, yantāramindīvarajaṃ cakāra |
dhanurgirīndraṃ śaramacyutaṃ sa dhuryāśvine yāvayujadgirīśaḥ ॥40॥
ācamya devaṃ manasā vicintya, tenopadiṣṭaṃ ca jajāpanāmnām |
sahasramekākṣaramantrayuktamamaṃtrayattena pinākabhugram ॥41॥
amantrayanmahābāṇaṃ viṣṇurupaṃ yadā śivaḥ |
cakampe dhāraṇī śeṣo vanāni girayo'pi ca ॥42॥
babhramuḥ khagaṇāḥ sarve cukūjuśca mahāravān |
śabdenājagavasyāpi bibhrāntāḥ suramānuṣāḥ ॥43॥
amuṃcattaṃ yadā bāṇaṃ tadā dagdhaṃ nabhastalam |
bhūmaṃḍalaṃ sapātālaṃ jvālāmālābhiraṃjasā ॥44॥
dṛṣṭvā papāla daityendraḥ sasainyaḥ purasaṃśrayaḥ |
javenāgāccharaḥ so'pya dahaddaityaṃ puratrayam ॥45॥
daityadehagataṃ tejo bhargaṃ dehe layaṃ yayau |
paśyatāṃ sarvasainyānāṃ daityadānavarakṣasām ॥46॥
tato'ntarikṣe vāgāsīn mukto daityaḥ śivāhataḥ|
tataste tuṣṭuvurdevā munayo'pi trilocanam ॥47॥
jagurgandharvanicayāścāraṇāḥ śrutitatparāḥ |
nanṛtuścāpsaraḥsaṃghāḥ kinnarā vādyavādakāḥ ॥48॥
mumucuḥ puṣpavarṣāṇi nāradādyāḥ surarṣayaḥ |
yayuḥ śivājñayā devāḥ svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ nirādhayaḥ ॥49॥
namaskṛtya maheśānaṃ tripurāsuraghātinam |
munayo'pi nirudvegāḥ svasvānuṣṭhānatatparāḥ ॥50॥
āsaṃstasminhate daitya vedavedāṃgaśālinaḥ |
agnihotrāṇi yajñāṃśca dānāni ca vratāni ca ॥51॥
cakruḥ sarvajanā bhūmau punarutsāhasaṃyutāḥ |
śailādi gaṇapaskandraiḥ sarvaiḥ pāriṣadairapi ॥52॥
kṛtāvanāmastrayakṣyo'pi vibhajya taṃ mahāratham|
jayaśabdaistūryaśabdairdevaduṃdubhiniḥsvanaiḥ ॥53॥
alaṃkṛtaṃ śailarājaṃ kailāsaṃ mudito yayau|
tripurāririti spaṣṭa tato nāmāsya paprathe ॥54॥
evaṃ mahāgaṇapatermantrasāmarthyamidamīritam |
sahasranāmnāmapi ca prabhāvo'yaṃ nirūpitaḥ ॥55॥
na vijñāto madanyena kasyāpi na niveditaḥ |
paṭhanācchravaṇādasya sarvakāmaphalaṃ labhet ॥56॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe śivavijayonāma saptacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥47॥

<adhyāya 48 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
śrutaṃ mayā mahākhyānaṃ tripurasya vadhāśrayam|
tathāpi śrotumicchāmi kva sthitā jagadambikā ॥1॥
kathamāvirbabhūvaiṣā tithau dagdhaḥ kva daityarāṭ |
etatsarvaṃ savistāraṃ kathayasva pitāmaha ॥2॥
brahmovāca |
bāhule paurṇamāsyāṃ sa sāyaṃ dagdho mahāsuraḥ |
divāyuddhaṃ mahāghoraṃ prāgeva varṇitaṃ hi tat ॥3॥
yato'rcitaḥ suraiḥ sarvairjayaśālī surārihā |
atastasyāṃ dīpadānaṃ kurvanti bhuvi mānavāḥ ॥4॥
tasyāṃ snānaṃ ca dānaṃ ca japahomādikaṃ ca yat |
bahulaṃ jāyate yasmāttasmātsā bāhulī smṛtā ॥5॥
tasyāṃ ye tu na kurvanti tripurārimahotsavam |
na labhaṃte jayaṃ kvāpi dagdhapuṇyā bhavanti te ॥6॥
atastasyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ prātararcanti ye śivam |
niśi yattaiḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ vilayaṃ pratipadyate ॥7॥
ājanmataḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ madhyāhnārcanatastathā |
saptajanmārjitaṃ pāpaṃ pradoṣārcanato mune ॥8॥
āvirbhāvaṃ śivāyāstvaṃ kathyamānaṃ mayā śṛṇu |
tasya mṛtyuṃ śivājjñātvā bhayādantarhitā śivā ॥9॥
āvirāsījjaganmātā himācalaguhāmukhāt |
dadarśa parvataṃ ghoraṃ siṃhavyāghramṛgākulam ॥10॥
apaśyantī śivaṃ sā tu virahākulamānasā |
vilalāpa bhṛśaṃ bhītā hā tāta hā śiveti ca ॥11॥
kathaṃ mām na vijānīṣe sarvajñaḥ sansadāśiva |
vana ekākinīṃ ghore kraṃdaṃtīṃ kurarīmiva ॥12॥
kadā me darśanaṃ te syāt kiṃ māṃ vismatavānasi |
śaktā na jīvitaṃ dhartuṃ soḍhuṃ vā virahaṃ hara ॥13॥
tāvattvamapi kutrāsi na me śokaṃ śṛṇoṣi kim |
tvāṃ vinā śaraṇaṃ kaṃ vā yāmi kiṃ vā karomyaham ॥14॥
yojayasva punarmāṃ tvaṃ śivena ca śivena ca |
idānīṃ punarutpannāṃ jānīha jaṭharāttava ॥15॥
varaṃ tameha śīghraṃ me gaveṣaya sadāśivay |
no ceddehaparityāgaṃ bhṛgorasmāt karomyaham ॥16॥
ka uvāca |
evaṃ rudantyāstasyāstu śrutvā tāṃ giramuttamām |
nyavedayaddhimavate kaścidāgatya dhīvaraḥ ॥17॥
dhīvara uvāca |
kācinnitambinī bālā sarvābharaṇabhūṣitā |
tāṭaṃke bibhratī śrutyormaṃḍale iva bhāsyataḥ ॥18॥
nānāratnalasaddīpti mukuṭaṃ mastake bhṛśam |
lalāṭe tu catuṣkoṇaṃ muktāṣoḍaśa śobhitam ॥19॥
puṣpaṃ kāṃcanaratnāḍhyaṃ muktādāmavilambitam |
sīmantāntargataṃ cāru nāsike svarṇamauktikam ॥20॥
aṃgade cāruṇī bāhvorhastayorvalayāni ca |
lasatkāṃcanaratnāḍhayaṃ pratyekamaṃgulīyakam ॥21॥
muktāphalamayīṃ mālāṃ lasatkaṃcuka saṃśritām |
hemaratnamayīṃ kāṃcīṃ kaṭī kṣaumāvṛte śubhām ॥22॥
gulphayoḥ śṛṃkhale haime siṃcaccāru sunūpare|
pādāṃgulīṣvapi pṛthak ttattadbhūṣaṇamuttamam ॥23॥
evaṃ sarvānavadyāṃgīṃ rūdatīṃ vihvalā bhṛśam |
druṣṭā pṛṣṭā na māṃ vakti gṛhṇāti sā tavābhidhām ॥24॥
brahmovāca|
itthaṃ niśamya himavāṃstvaritastāṃ sutāṃ yayo|
sāntvayanhetugarbhābhirvāgbhistāmavadatsudhīḥ ॥25॥
himavānuvāca| kimarthaṃ śocase subhru sṛṣṭisthityantahetuke |
sarvalakṣaṇa saṃpūrṇe sarvaśaktiyute'naghe॥26॥
akartumanyathākartuṃ kartumīśe maheśvari |
avāptasarvakāme ca sarvaprāṇyantaraṃ gate ॥27॥
na viyuktā śivena tvaṃ sarvāntaryāmiṇī śubhe |
athāpi yojayiṣye tvāṃ śivena śivakāriṇā ॥28॥
uttiṣṭheti gṛhītvā tāmāyayau nijamandiram |
nananda pārvatī tatra dṛṣṭvā menāṃ sutānvitām ॥29॥
niśvāsaparamā'pyāsīcchivaṃ draṣṭuṃ samutsukā|
uvāca pitaraṃ natvā sādhūpāyaṃ vadasva me ॥30॥
vrataṃ dānaṃ tapo vāpi duṣkaraṃ śivalabdhaye |
kariṣye tamahaṃ tāta pūrvavattapa uttamam ॥31॥
brahmovāca |
avadajjanakastāṃ tu vicārya manasā sakṛt |
kāryasiddhikaraṃ śīghramupāyaṃ śṛṇu taṃ mune ॥32॥
himavānuvāca |
śṛṇu pārvati vakṣyāmi sādhūpāyaṃ śivāptaye |
upāstiṃ vighnarājasya dharmārthakāmamokṣadām ॥33॥
anuṣṭhitāṃ mahendrādyairdevaiśca nāradādibhiḥ |
prāptāśca siddhayastāstāḥ śakrādi padalakṣaṇāḥ ॥34॥
brahmaṇaḥ sṛṣṭisāmarthyaṃ dattaṃ tena mahātmanā |
viṣṇoścāvanasāmarthyaṃ dattaṃ sarveśveraṇa ha ॥35॥
śivasyāpi ca tenaiva sāmarthyaṃ saṃhṛtau dṛḍham |
śeṣasyāpi ca tenaiva sarvavighnahareṇa ca ॥36॥
dharādharaṇasāmarthyaṃ dattaṃ sarveśvareṇa ha |
yasya svarūpaṃ na vidurbrahmādyā munayo'pi ca ॥37॥
vācāmagocaro yo'sau manaso'thāpyagocaraḥ |
gajānana svarūpeṇa paridṛṣyo'yamīśvaraḥ ॥38॥
atastenaiva rupeṇa sarvāraṃbheṣu pūjyate |
tamupāsasva sarveśaṃ mayā proktena vartmanā ॥39॥ 
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe ambāvirbhāvo nāmāṣṭacatvāriṃśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥48॥

<adhyāya 49 prāraṃbhaḥ>

pārvatyuvāca |
dayānidhe girīndra tvaṃ pitarme vada satvaram |
upāsanāṃ gaṇeśasya sarveśasya jagadguro ॥1॥
yayā śivaṃ samabhyetya śivaṃ prāpsyāmi śāśvatam |
martyaloke'pi lokānāmupakāro bhaviṣyati ॥2॥
himavānuvāca |
tavasnehavaśāddevī rahasyaṃ paramaṃ śubham |
lokānāṃ copakārāya vakṣye śṛṇvekacetasā ॥3॥
pratyūṣakāla utthāya naiṛtīṃ diśamāvrajet |
ācchādya dharaṇīṃ pūrvaṃ tṛṇakāṣṭhadalairapi ॥4॥
urvarāṃ varjayettiṣṭhannutsarpaṃ ca buddhimān |
tyaktvā mūtrapurīṣe tu yathoktaṃ śaucamācaret ॥5॥
dantajihvā viśuddhiṃ ca kṛtvā snātuṃ tato vrajet |
nadīṃ taḍāgaṃ vāpīṃ vā saraḥ kūpamathāpi vā ॥6॥
kṛtvā pūrvaṃ malasnānaṃ mantrasnānaṃ tataścaret |
mṛdā vā candanenāpi tilakaṃ kuṃkumena vā ॥7॥
kuryāddhaute vāsasī vā paridhāya śubhāsane |
upaviśyaikacittastu sarvaṃ nityaṃ samāpya ca ॥8॥
mṛttikāṃ sundarāṃ snigdhāṃ kṣudrapāṣāṇavarjitām |
suviśuddhāmavalmīkāṃ jalasiktāṃ vimardayet ॥9॥
kṛtvā cārutarāṃ mūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya śubhāṃ svayam |
sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇāṃ caturbhujavirājitām ॥10॥
paraśvādi svāyudhāni dadhatīṃ sundarāṃ ghanām |
sthāpya pīṭhe tataḥ pāṇī prakṣālya melayetsudhīḥ ॥11॥
pūjādravyāṇi sarvāṇī jalādīni prayatnataḥ |
aṣṭagaṃdhānakṣatāṃśca raktapuṣpāṇi guggulum ॥12॥
tripaṃcasaptabhiḥ patrairyutān dūrvāṃkurān śubhān |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ nīlā'n śvetānānīya sundarāna ॥13॥
ghṛtadīpaṃ tailadīpaṃ naivedyaṃ vividhaṃ śubham |
modakāpūpa laḍḍūkān pāyasaṃ śarkarāyutam ॥14॥
sūkṣmaṃ ca taṃdulānna ca vyaṃjanāni bahūni ca |
sacandrapūgacūrṇāḍhyaṃ khādyakhādirasaṃyutam ॥15॥
elālavaṃgasaṃmiśraṃ tāmbūlaṃ kesarānvitam |
jambvāmrapanasādīni drākṣārambhā balāni ca ॥16॥
tattadṛtubhava nīśe nārikelāni cānayet |
bahuprakāramārtikyaṃ kāṃcanīṃ dakṣiṇāṃ tathā ॥17॥
evaṃ saṃbhṛtasaṃbhāra ekāntasthalamāsthitaḥ |
cailājine kuśamaye āsane ca kṛtāsanaḥ ॥18॥
bhūtaśuddhiṃ prakurvīta prāṇānāṃ sthāpanaṃ tathā |
digbaṃdha pūrvaṃ devāṃśca gaṇeśādīnnametpurā ॥19॥
antarbahirmātṛkāśca nyasedāgama mārgataḥ |
sannidhāmādikā mudrā darśayedguru mārgataḥ |
mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā ṣaḍaṃganyāsameva ca ॥20॥
pūjādravyāṇi saṃśodhya tato dhyāyed gajānanam |
ekadantaṃ śūrpakarṇaṃ gajavaktraṃ caturbhujam ॥21॥
pāśāṃkuśadharaṃ devaṃ modakān bibhrataṃ karaiḥ |
raktapuṣpamayīṃ mālāṃ kaṃṭhe haste parāṃ śubhām ॥22॥
bhaktānāṃ varadaṃ siddhi buddhi buddhibhyāṃ sevitaṃ sadā |
siddhibuddhipradaṃ nṛṇām dharmārthakāmamokṣadam |
brahmarudraharīndrādyaiḥ saṃstutaṃ paramarṣabhiḥ ॥23॥
āgaccha jagadādhāra surāsuravarārcita |
anāthanātha sarvajña gīrvāṇa paripūjita ॥24॥
svarṇasiṃhāsane divyaṃ nānāratnasamanvitam |
samarpitaṃ mayā deva tatra tvaṃ samupāviśa ॥25॥
devadeveśa sarveśa sarvatīrthāhṛtaṃ jalam|
pādyaṃ gṛhāṇa gaṇapa gandhapuṣpākṣatairyutam ॥26॥
pravālamuktāphala putra ratna tāṃbūla jāṃbūnadamaṣṭagaṃdham |
puṣpākṣatāyuktamamoghaśakte, dattaṃ mayā'rghyaṃ saphalī kuruṣva ॥27॥
gaṃgādisarvatīrthebhyaḥ prārthitaṃ toyamuttamama |
karpūrailā lavaṃgādi vāsitaṃ svīkuru prabho ॥28॥
campakāśoka bakula mālatī mogarādibhiḥ |
vāsitaṃ snigdhatā hetu tailaṃ cāru pragṛhyatām ॥29॥
kāmadhenu samudbhūtaṃ sarveṣāṃ jīvanaṃ param|
pāvanaṃ yajñahetuste payaḥ snānārthamarpitam ॥30॥
dhenudugdhasamudbhūtaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvajanaḥ priyam |
mayā''nītaṃ dadhivaraṃ snānārthaṃ pratigṛhyatāmū ॥31॥
navanīta samutpannaṃ sarvasaṃtoṣakāraṇam |
yajñāṃgaṃ devatāhāro ghṛtaṃ snātuṃ samarpitam ॥32॥
puṣpasāraghasaṃbhūtaṃ sarvatejovivardhanam |
sarvapuṣṭikaraṃ deva madhusnānārthamarpitam ॥33॥
ikṣusārasamudbhūtāṃ śarkarāṃ sumanoharām |
malāpahāriṇīṃ snātuṃ gṛhāṇa tvaṃ mayā'rpitām ॥34॥
sarvamādhuryatāhetuḥ svāduḥ sarvapriyaṃkaraḥ |
puṣṭikṛt snātumānīt ikṣusārabhavo guḍaḥ ॥35॥
kāṃsye kāṃsyena pihito dadhimadhvājyapūrita; |
madhuparko mayā''nītaḥ pūjārthaṃ pratigṛhyatām ॥36॥
sarvatīrthāhṛtaṃ toyaṃ mayā prārthanayā vibho |
suvāsitaṃ gṛhāṇedaṃ samyaksnātuṃ sureśvara ॥37॥
raktavastrayugaṃ deva lokalajjānivāraṇam |
anarghyamatisūkṣmaṃ ca gṛhāṇedaṃ mayārpitam ॥38॥
rājataṃ brahmasūtraṃ ca kāṃcanaṃ ratnasaṃyuktam|
bhaktyopapāditaṃ deva gṛhāṇa parameśvara ॥39॥
aneka ratnayuktāni bhūṣaṇāni bahūni ca |
tattadaṃge kāṃcanāni yojayāmi tavājñayā ॥40॥
aṣṭagandhā samāyuktaṃ raktacandanamuttamam |
dvādaśāṃgeṣu te deva lepayāmi kṛpāṃ kuru ॥41॥
raktacandanasaṃmiśrāstaṃdulāṃstilakopari |
śobhāya saṃpradāsyāmi gṛhāṇa jagadīśvara ॥42॥
pāṭalaṃ karṇikāraṃ ca baṃdhūkaṃ raktapaṃkajam |
mogaraṃ mālatīpuṣpaṃ gṛhyatāṃ parameśvara ॥43॥
nānā paṃkaja puṣpaiśca grathitāṃ pallavairapi |
bilvapatrayutāṃ mālāṃ gṛhāṇa sumanoharām ॥44॥
daśāṃgaṃ guggulaṃ dhūpaṃ sarvaṃ sauṃgaṃdhya kārakam |
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ tvaṃ gṛhāṇa mayā'rpitam ॥45॥
sarvajña sarvalokeṣa tamonāśanamuttamam |
gṛhāṇa maṃgalaṃ dīpa devadeva namo'stu te ॥46॥
nānā pakvānnasaṃyuktaṃ pāyasaṃ śarkarānvitam |
nānā vyaṃjanaśobhāḍhayaṃ śālyodanamanuttamam ॥47॥
dadhidugdhaghṛtairyuktaṃ lavaṃgailā samanvitam |
marīcicūrṇasahitaṃ kvathikā vaṭakānvitam ॥48॥
rājikādhānyasaṃyuktaṃ methīpiṣṭaṃ satakrakam |
hiṃgujīrakakūṣmāṃḍa marīci māṣa piṣṭakaiḥ ॥49॥
saṃpāditaiḥ supakvaiśca bharjitairvaṭakairyutam |
modakāpūpa laḍḍūka śaṣkulī maṃḍakādibhiḥ ॥50॥
parpaṭairapi saṃyuktaṃ naivedyamamṛtānvitam|
haridrā hiṃgulavaṇasahitaṃ sūpamuttamam ॥51॥
sasāmudraṃ gṛhāṇedaṃ bhojanaṃ kuru sādaram |
sutṛptikārakaṃ toyaṃ sugandhaṃ ca pibecchayā ॥52॥
tvayi tṛpte jagattṛptaṃ nityatṛpte mahātvani |
uttarāpośanārthaṃ te dadmi toyaṃ suvāsitam ॥53॥
mukhapāṇiviśuddhyarthaṃ punastoyaṃ dadāmi te |
dāḍimaṃ madhuraṃ nimbu jambvāmrapanasādikam ॥54॥
drākṣāraṃbhā phalaṃ pakvaṃ karkandhūḥ khārjuraṃ phalam |
nārikelaṃ ca nāriṃgamāṃjiraṃ jambiraṃ tathā ॥55॥
urvārukaṃ ca deveśa phalānyetāni gṛhyatām |
mukhapāṇiviśuddhyarthaṃ punastoyaṃ dadāmi te ॥56॥
gṛhāṇa candanaṃ cāru karāṃgodvartanaṃ śubham |
nānā parimala dravyairnirmitaṃ cūrṇamuttamam ॥57॥
sugandhi nāmakaṃ puṇya gandhi cāru pragṛhyatām |
cāru śālurasaṃbhūtaṃ vaṃśasārasamudbhavam ||58॥
sīmantabhūṣaṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ lākṣāraṃjitamastu te |
sacandrapūga cūrṇāḍhyaṃ khādyakhādirasaṃyutam ॥59॥
elālavaṃgasaṃmiśraṃ tāmbūlaṃ kesarānvitam |
nyūnātirikta pūjāyāḥ saṃpūrṇaphalahetave ॥60॥
dakṣiṇāṃ kāṃcanīṃ deva sthāpayāmi tavāgrataḥ |
sitapītaistathā raktairjalajaiḥ kusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ ॥61॥
grathitāṃ sundarāṃ mālāṃ gṛhāṇa parameśvara |
haritāḥ śvetavarṇā vā paṃcatripatrasaṃyutāḥ ॥62॥
dūrvāṃkurā mayā dattā ekaviṃśatisaṃmitāḥ |
ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyākāḥ kuryāddeva pradakṣiṇāḥ ॥63॥
pade pade te deveśa naśyantu pātakāni me |
auduṃbare rājate vā kāṃsye kāṃcanasaṃbhave ॥64॥
pāge prakalpitān dīpāngṛhāṇa cakṣurarpakān |
paṃcārārti paṃcadīpairdīpitāṃ parameśvara ॥65॥
cārucandranibhaṃ dīpa gṛhāṇa vīcivāraṇam |
yathā'sya nekṣyate bhasma tathā pāpaṃ vināśaya ॥66॥
stotrairnānāvidhaiḥ sūktaiḥ sahasranāmabhistataḥ |
upaviśya stuvītainaṃ kṛtvā sthirataraṃ manaḥ ॥67॥
dīnādhīśa dayānidhe suragaṇaiḥ saṃsevyamāna dvija|
brahmeśāna mahendra śeṣa girijā gaṃdharva siddhaiḥ stuta |
sarvāriṣṭa nivāraṇaikanipuṇaḥ trailokyānāthaṃ prabho|
bhaktiṃ me saphalāṃ kuruṣva sakalāṃ kṣāntvā'parādhānmama ॥68॥
iti mūrtiṃ samabhyarcya daṃḍavatpraṇipatya ca |
japenmantraṃ tato devi sarvasiddhipradāyakam ||69॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe gaṇeśa pārthivapūjā nirūpaṇaṃ nāmaikonapaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥49॥

<adhyāya 50 prāraṃbhaḥ>

pārvatyuvāca |
ahaṃ mantre na jānāmi girīndra me vada svayam |
anugrahaṃ gaṇeśasya yāmi yāmi śivaṃ śivam ॥1॥
himāvānuvāca |
mantrā nānāvidhā devi nānāsiddhipradāyakāḥ |
asaṃkhyātā gaṇeśasya kāṃścitteṣu vadāmi te ॥2॥
kṛtvā padmāsanaṃ samyaṅniyamya khāni sarvaśaḥ |
kṛtanyāsavidhiḥ paścājjapaṃ kuryānnijecchayā ॥3॥
ekākṣarasyamantrasya lakṣaṃ lakṣārdhameva ca |
ṣaḍakṣarasya mantrasya niyataṃ cāyutaṃ tathā ॥4॥
tathā paṃcākṣarasyāpi daśāṣṭākṣarayorapi |
aṣṭāviṃśadvarṇamantro japtavyo'yuta saṃkhyayā ॥5॥
evaṃ nānāvidhānmaṃtrāṃjjapanti sveṣṭasiddhaye |
tvaṃ tu pārvati me vākyaṃ śṛṇuṣvaikāgramānasā ॥6॥
ekākṣaraṃ ṣaḍarṇaṃ ca gṛhāṇa mantramuttamama |
nabhaḥ śuklacaturthyāṃ tvamārambhaṃ kuru suvrate ॥7॥
anuṣṭhānaṃ māsamātraṃ kuru kāryaṃ bhaviṣyati |
śivaṃ prāpsyasi suvyaktamanyadyaccāpi vāṃchitam ॥8॥
pārthivī pūjitā mūrtiḥ striyā vā puruṣeṇa vā |
ekā dadāti sā kāmyaṃ dhanaputrapaśūnapi ॥9॥
asādhyaṃ sādhayenmartyo mūrtidvayaprapūjanāt |
trimūrti pūjanādrājyaṃ ratnāni sarvasampadaḥ ॥10॥
caturmūrtiḥ pūjayedyo dharmārthaṃ kāma mokṣabhāk |
sārvabhaumo bhavedrājā paṃcamūrti prapūjanāt ॥11॥
ṣaṇmūrtipūjayā sṛṣṭi sthiti pralayakṛd bhavet |
saptāṣṭa nava mūrtīnāṃ pūjayā sarvavidbhavet ॥12॥
bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyaṃ ca vetti devaprasādataḥ |
trayastriṃśatkoṭi devā vahnīndra śivaviṣṇavaḥ ॥13॥
sanakādyā munigaṇāḥ sevante daśapūjanāt |
ekādaśārcanādeva daśarudrādhipo bhavet ॥14॥
dvādaśāditya rājyaṃ ca labhecca dvādaśārcanāt |
atisaṃkaṭavelāsu kuryād vṛddhyā prapūjanam ॥15॥
aṣṭottaraśataṃ yāvattāvattattadavāpnuyāt |
mahāmuktimavāpnoti lakṣapūjanato'nvaham ॥16॥
kārāgṛhānmuktikāmaḥ kārayenmūrtipaṃcakam |
triḥsapta rātrānmucyeta gaṇeśānugrahānmune ॥17॥
saptamūrtīḥ prakurvīta pratyahaṃ paṃcavatsaram |
mahāpāpātpramucyeta gaṇeśe bhaktimānnaraḥ ॥18॥
ājanma maraṇādekāṃ pūjayetpārthivī naraḥ |
gaṇeśaḥ sa tu vijñeyo darśanādvighnanāśanaḥ ॥19॥
tameva sarvakāmeṣa pūjayenna gaṇādhipama |
tasyaiva pūjayā prīto na devaḥsyāttathā svayama ॥20॥
sarvarogaprapīḍāsu mūrtīnāṃ trayamuttamam |
navāhaṃ pūjayedyastu sarvapīḍāṃ vyapohati ॥21॥
sauvarṇī rājatī tāmrī rītikāṃsyasamudbhavā |
mauktikī ca pravālī ca sarvametatprayacchati ॥22॥
evaṃ kṛte vrate devi sarvānkāmānavāpsyasi |
yāvadbhādrapade māsi caturthī parilabhyate ॥23॥
tasyāṃ mahotsavaḥ kāryo yathāvibhavamādarāt |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ tatkathāvādyagāyanaiḥ ॥24॥
prabhāte vimale snātvā pūrvavatpūjayedvibhum |
gaṇeśaṃ varadaṃ devaṃ tato homaṃ samārabhet ॥25॥
kuṃḍe sāṃgaṃ sthaṃḍile vā hūyājjapa daśāṃśataḥ |
pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ kuryād balidānapuraḥsaram ॥26॥
ācāryaṃ pūjayet paścād gobhvāsodhanādibhiḥ |
brāhmaṇāṃ stoṣayet paścāddhomaśeṣaṃ samāpayet ॥27॥
tarpayettaddaśāṃśena taddaśāṃśena bhojayet |
brāhmaṇānvedaviduṣaḥ sapatnīkāṃśca kāṃścana ॥28॥
tebhyo bhūṣaṇavāsāṃsi dadyācchaktyā ca dakṣiṇām |
dadyāt strīṇāmalaṃkārān yoṣidbhyaśca sakuṃcukān ॥29॥
tuṣṭaye gaṇanāthasya siddhibuddhiyutasya ha |
vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kurvīta yathāvibhavamācaret ॥30॥
tataḥ suhṛjjanayutaḥ svayaṃ bhuṃjīta sādaram |
aparasmindine mūrtiṃ nṛyāne sthāpayenmudā ॥31॥
chatradhvajapatākābhiścāmarairupaśobhitām |
kiśorairdaṇḍayuddhena yudhyadbhiśca puraḥsaram ॥32॥
mahājalāśayaṃ gatvā visṛjya ninayejjale|
vādyagītadhvaniyuto nijamandiramāvrajet ॥33॥
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe himavadgirijā saṃvāde caturthīvratakathanaṃ nāma paṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥50॥

<adhyāya 51 prāraṃbhaḥ>

pārvatyuvāca |
tava vākyāmṛtenāhaṃ pitaḥ prītā'smi sāṃpratam|
parantu saṃśayo me'sti taṃ nudasva himālaya | ॥1॥
kena kena kṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ vratametanmahīdhara |
ākhyānaṃ kena vā kasmai kāṃ kāṃ siddhimito'gamat |
etatsavistaraṃ brūhi saṃśayacchedanāya me |
yaśca pṛcchati yo vakti gajānana kathā śubhāḥ ॥3॥
śṛṇotyanyo'pi yo martyastrayaste puṇyabhāginaḥ |
jīvitaṃ saphalaṃ teṣāṃ janma jñānaṃ ca karma ca ॥4॥
brahmovāca |
evaṃ tayā kṛtapraśno himavānabhyabhāṣata |
varadasya gaṇeśasya nānājanakṛtaṃ vratam ॥5॥
himavānuvāca |
śṛṇu pārvati vakṣyāmi saṃvādaṃ te purātanam |
setihāsaṃ vratasyāsya sarvasiddhikarasya ha ॥6॥
kailāse girivarye tu sukhāsīnaṃ jagadguruma |
krīḍamānaṃ mudā devairgandharvaiḥ paramarṣibhiḥ ॥7॥
natvā stutvā mahātejāḥ paripapraccha ṣaṇmukhaḥ ॥8॥
skanda uvāca |
devadeva jagannātha bhaktānāmabhayaṃkara |
nānākhyānāni divyāni śrutāni tvatprasādataḥ ॥9॥
tṛptiṃ nāta na gacchāmi pāyaṃ pāyaṃ sudhāmiva |
adhunā brūhi me deva vrataṃ sarvārthasiddhidam ॥10॥
anuṣṭhānena yasyātha karasthā siddhayo nṛṇām |
jāyante sādhakānāṃ tu varadasya prasādataḥ ॥11॥
śiva uvāca |
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā skanda sarveṣāṃ hitakārakam |
kathayāmi tava prītyā mahāsiddhipradaṃ bhuvi ॥12॥
vināyakapriyaṃ tāta vratānāmuttamaṃ vratam |
sarveṣāṃ puruṣārthānāṃ sādhakaṃ kṛttikātmaja ॥13॥
vinā yajñaistathā dānairjapahomādibhirvinā |
sarvasiddhikaraṃ skanda putrapautrapravardhanam ॥14॥
rājānaṃ rājaputraṃ vā tanmantriṇamathāpi vā|
vaśamānayati kṣipraṃ vratametanma'hattamam ॥15॥
mahāpāpopapāpaiśca bahujanmasusaṃcitaiḥ |
vratasyāsya prabhāveṇa taiḥ pumānmucyate kṣaṇāt ॥16॥
bhājanaṃ sarvasiddhīnāṃ jāyate mānavo bhuvi |
prītidaṃ tu gaṇeśasya nānena sadṛśaṃ kṣitau ॥17॥
skanda uvāca |
tāta kasminbhavenmāsi vratametanmahottamam|
nidhānaṃ kīdṛśaṃ cāsya kena vā''caritaṃ purā ॥18॥
sarvametanmamācakṣva yadyasti karuṇā mayi |
śiva uvāca |
nabhaḥ śuklacaturthyāṃ tu snātvā gurugṛhaṃ vrajet |
praṇamya taṃ guruṃ paścātpūjayitvā yathāvidhi ॥19॥
pādyācamanavastrādyairbhūṣaṇaiśca mahattaraiḥ |
toṣayitvā tu taṃ samyagājñayā prārabhed vratam ॥20॥
sarvasiddhikaraṃ tāta vrataṃ gāṇeśvaraṃ guro |
tvameva śrīgaṇeśo'si diśa kāmapradaṃ prabho ॥21॥
upadiṣṭe vrate tena sārdhaṃ gaṃgātaṭaṃ vrajet |
taḍāgaṃ vā devakhātaṃ tatra snāyādyathāvidhi ॥22॥
śvetasarṣapayuktena tilakalkena ṣaṇmukha |
snātvā dhātrīyuteneha kṛtvā nityaṃ gṛhaṃ vrajet ॥23॥
upaviśyāsane śuddhe pūjayitvā gaṇādhipam |
gurūpadiṣṭamārgeṇa vrataṃ paścātsamārabhet ॥24॥
mṛdāmūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya caturthyāṃ śrāvaṇasya tu |
pratyahaṃ pūjayetkṛtvā yāvad bhādracaturthikā ॥25॥
brahmacaryavrate sthitvā kartavyaṃ vratamuttamam |
upavāsaikabhaktābhyāṃ naktenāyācitena vā ॥26॥
caturthakāle bhuṃjīta haviṣyānnaṃ samāhitaḥ |
akṣaraṃ madhuraṃ bhuṃjan bhaktimānācared vratam ॥27॥
japan gāṇeśvarīṃ vidyāṃ ṣaḍānana ṣaḍakṣarīm |
dvicaturakṣarāṃ vāpi tathaivaikākṣarāmapi ॥28॥
daśākṣarāmatha skanda dvādaśārṇāmathāpi vā|
niyutaṃ vā'yutaṃ vāpi pratyahaṃ japa iṣyate ॥29॥
tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā daśāṃśaṃ homamācaret |
dhyāyan gajānanaṃ devamahorātramatandritaḥ ॥30॥
prāpte proṣṭhapade māsi caturthyāṃ tu gajānanam|
sauvarṇa palamānena tadardhāṃrdhena vā punaḥ ॥31॥
mayūravāhanaṃ kuryādathavā ''khugataṃ śubham |
kṛtvā maṃḍapikāṃ tatra dhānyarāśiṃ prakalpayet ॥32॥
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ tāmraṃ kalaśaṃ sthāpayettataḥ |
tasyopari nyasetpātraṃ rukmarājatatāmrakam ॥33॥
vastrayugmena saṃveṣṭya sapātraṃ kalaśaṃ tataḥ |
paṃcapallavasaṃyuktaṃ paṃcaratnasamanvitam ॥34॥
pīṭhapūjāṃ purā kṛtvā sthāpayettama taṃ vibhum|
mūlamaṃtraiśca vedoktaiḥ pūrvoktairapi ṣaṇmukha ॥35॥
dhyātvā gajānanaṃ devamāvāhya parayā mudā |
āsanaṃ ca tathā pādyaṃ dadyādācamanīyakam ॥36॥
arghyaṃ ratnayutaṃ skanda snānaṃ paṃcāmṛtaiḥ śubham |
suvāsitābhiradbhiśca snāpayet parameśvaram |
raktavastrayugaṃ dadyādupavītaṃ tathottamam ॥37॥
nānāvidhairābharaṇairbhūṣayet parameśvaram |
gaṃdhākṣatairdhūpadīpairnaivedyairvividhairapi ॥38॥
vaṭakāpūpalaḍḍūkaśālyannapāyasādibhiḥ|
paṃcāmṛtairvyaṃjanaiśca bhojayet parameśvaram ॥39॥
udvartana kare dadyātphalaṃ tāmbūlameva ca |
kāṃcanī dakṣiṇāṃ dadyācchatraṃ vyaṃjanacāmare ॥40॥
nīrājanaṃ maṃtrapuṣpaṃ datvā stotrāṇyudīrayet |
sahasranāmabhiḥ stutvā brāhmaṇānpūjayettataḥ ॥41॥
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kṛtvā gītanṛtyādi maṃgalaiḥ |
prabhāte vimale snātvā nityaṃ kṛtvā yathāvidhi ॥42॥
pūjayetpūrvavaddevaṃ tato homaṃ samācaret |
dravyairnānāvidhairhutvā kuryādācāryapūjanam ॥43॥
gobhūtilahiraṇyādyaṃ gurave tannivedayet |
anyebhyo brāhmaṇebhyaśca pradadyādbhūridakṣiṇām ॥44॥
brāhmaṇān bhojayet paścācchatamaṣṭottaraṃ tathā |
adhikānyadi śaktiścedathavā'pyekaviṃśatim ॥45॥
dīnāṃdhakṛpaṇebhyaśca dadyādannaṃ sapāyasam |
punaśca dakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt pragṛhayāśiṣamuttamām ॥46॥
suhṛdbandhuyuto maunī svayaṃ bhuṃjīta sādaram ॥47॥
śiva uvāca |
evaṃ te kathitaṃ skanda varadasya vrataṃ śubham |
bhuktimuktipradaṃ nṛṇāṃ sarvakāmaphalapradam ॥48॥
purātanamitihāsamasminnarthe vadāmi te|
mahādharmaparo rājā purā'bhūtkardamābhidhaḥ ॥49॥
pālayāmāsa vasudhāṃ sāgarāntāṃ svatejasā|
guṇairyasya hṛtā devā nityaṃ yatsadasi sthitāḥ ॥50॥
kadāciddaivayogena bhṛgustasya gṛhaṃ yayau |
tata utthāya rājā sa bahumānapuraḥsaram ॥51॥
upaviśyāsane śreṣṭhe pūjito guruvattataḥ |
bhuktvā sthitaṃ munivaraṃ rājā vacanamabravīt ॥52॥
rājovāca |
bhagavan sarvatatvajña kiṃcit pṛcchāmi tadvada |
pūrvajanmani kaścāhaṃ kiṃ mayā sukṛtaṃ kṛtam ॥53॥
yenedṛśaṃ mayā rājyaṃ prāptaṃ nihatakaṇṭakam |
prāptaṃ naiva nṛpairanyairna ca prāpsyanti cāpare ॥54॥
devānāmapi pūjyo'haṃ gaṃdharvoragarakṣasām |
kuberasaṃpadātulyā sampadaṃ paśya me mune ॥55॥
trilokyāṃ ratnabhūtaṃ yattadānītaṃ svatejasā |
yaṃ yaṃ padārthamicchāmi taṃ taṃ paśyāmi veśmani ॥56॥
kenetthaṃ karmaṇā prāptaṃ tadvadasva mama prabho |
kāmaye tatpunaḥ kartuṃ puṇyaṃ puṇyavatāṃ vara ॥57॥
bhṛguruvāca |
kathayāmi nṛpaśreṣṭha praṇidhāna balādaham |
tvamāsīḥ pūrvajanuṣi kṣatriyo durbalaḥ śuciḥ ॥58॥
kuṭuṃbabharaṇārthaṃ tvaṃ nānā karmāṇyathā karoḥ |
kriyamāṇaṃ ca tatkarma na cāsīt phaladaṃ tava ॥59॥
atyaṃta niṣṭhurairvākyairdārāpatyaiśca pīḍitaḥ |
apraṣṭvā putradārāṃstvaṃ gatavān gahanaṃ vanam ॥60॥
bibhramandikṣu sarvāsu dṛṣṭavānasi saubharim |
siddhāsane samāsīnaṃ sevitaṃ munipuṃgavaiḥ ॥61॥
kathayantaṃ mahāvidyāṃ śiṣyebhyo duḥkhanāśinīm |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ saubhariṃ divyamanyāṃścarṣigaṇānnṛpa ॥62॥
daṇḍavat patito'si tvaṃ bhuvi taiścābhinanditaḥ |
upaviṣṭaḥ svāsane ca muninā tena darśite ॥63॥
pṛṣṭavānasi taṃ divyaṃ labdhvā'vasaramādṛtaḥ |
kṣatriya uvāca |
svāmin saṃsāraduḥkhena bahudhā kleśito mune ॥64॥
dārāpatya suhṛdbhiśca vāgbāṇairbhṛśatāḍitaḥ |
na vairāgyaṃ mano yāti niṣṭhureṣu suhṛtsu ca ॥65॥
śītoṣṇakṣutpipāsābhiḥ pīḍito'pi bhṛśaṃ mune |
upāyaṃ vada me kiṃcid duḥkhasāgaratārakam ॥66॥
śiva uvāca |
iti śrutvā vacastasya saubhariḥ kṛpayā yutaḥ|
upāyaṃ cintayāmāsa nṛpaduḥkhavināśanam ॥67॥
tatastaṃ kṣatriyaṃ prāha sarvapāpavimocanam |
ṛṣiruvāca |
vrataṃ yatkathayiṣyāmi kuru niścitamānasaḥ ॥68॥
yasyānuṣṭhānamātreṇa sarva duḥkha layo bhavet |
brāhmaṇaiḥ kṣatriyarvaiśyaistathā brahmarṣibhiḥ kṛtam ॥69॥
muktāste sarvaduḥkhebhyaḥ prāptāḥ siddhimanuttamām |
varadasya gaṇeśasya dharmārthakāmamokṣakṛt ॥70॥
kṣatriya uvāca |
ko'sau gaṇeśaḥ kiṃśīlaḥ kiṃ rupaḥ kiṃ svabhāvavān |
kiṃ karmā kathamutpannastatsarvaṃ kathayasva me|
yadi me śravaṇāyālaṃ yadyanugrāhyatā mayi ॥71॥
ṛṣiruvāca |
yad brahma nityaṃ virajaṃ viśokaṃ jñānasvarupaṃ paramārthabhūtam|
anādi madhyāntamanantapāraṃ, gaṇādhipaṃ taṃ pravadanti santaḥ ॥72॥
yasmādoṃkārasaṃbhūtiryato vedā yato jagat |
yena sarvamidaṃ vyāptaṃ taṃ viddhi gaṇanāyakam ॥73॥
jagatsarjanakāmena brahmaṇā yasya tuṣṭaye |
abdānāṃ tu śataṃ pūrṇaṃ tapastaptaṃ suduṣkaram ॥74॥
tato hṛṣṭamanā vedhāḥ prasannaṃ tamapūjayat |
nānāvidhopacāraiśca divyaratnaiḥ phalaistathā ॥75॥
siddhibuddhī ubhe kanye manasā kalpite dadau|
tuṣṭo devo dadau tasmai vidyāmekākṣarīṃ vibhuḥ ॥76॥
tato labdhavaro brahmā cakāra sakalaṃ jagat |
ṣaḍakṣareṇa mantreṇa viṣṇunā toṣitaḥ purā ॥77॥
kṛtvā mūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya pūrvoktavidhinā vratam |
avyaṃgaṃ varṣamekaṃ tu niyamān kṛtavān hariḥ ॥78॥
varaṃ labdhvā gaṇeśāttu pālayāmāsa vai jagat |
evaṃvidhāṃ vijānīhi gaṇeśaṃ bhuvi saṃstutam ॥79॥
viśvarūpamanādiṃ ca sarvakāraṇakāraṇam |
tamārādhaya yatnena sarvaduḥkhādviyuṃkṣyase ॥80॥
kṣatriya uvāca |
kasminkāle prakartavyametad vratamanuttamam |
vidhinā kena me brūhi sāṃprataṃ munisattama |
kariṣye vacanātte'haṃ sarvaduḥkhapraśāntaye ॥81॥
muniruvāca |
śrāvaṇe bahule pakṣe caturthyāmārabhed vratam |
kurvīta parayā bhaktyā yāvadbhādracaturthikā |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pārthivaṃ gaṇanāyakam |
pratyahaṃ pūjayedbhaktyā brāhmaṇānbhojayettataḥ ॥82॥
tvametatkuru bhūpāla sarvānkāmānavāpsyasi |
iti śrutvā vrataṃ tasmātkṛtavānasi suvrata ॥83॥
samāpte vrate tatra saubharyāśramamaṃḍale |
tāvattasya gṛhaṃ divyaṃ gaṇeśasya prasādataḥ ॥84॥
divyanārīnarayutaṃ dāsīdāsasamanvitam |
vedaghoṣasamāyuktaṃ jātaṃ godhanasaṃkulam ॥85॥
divyavastrasamāyuktā nānālaṃkārasaṃyutā |
apatyaistādṛśairyuktā vismitā''sīt pratīkṣatī ॥86॥
āyāsyati kadā bhartetyevaṃ cintāparā'bhavat |
tāvattvaṃ munimāmantrya gato'si nijamālayam ॥87॥
divyaṃ taccharaṇaṃ hitvā svagṛhaṃ parimārgayan |
tāvaddhāryā preṣitāstvāṃ ninyustadbhavanaṃ narāḥ ॥88॥
tatastvayā'pi vijñātaḥ prabhāvo varadasya saḥ|
tadvratasya prabhāveṇa rājyabhāgiha janmani ॥89॥
śiva uvāca |
iti śrutvā bhṛgorvākyaṃ kardamo harṣanirbharaḥ |
cakāra sakalaṃ rājā bhṛguṇā yatsamīritam ॥90॥
vratasyāsya prabhāveṇa jñānavairāgyavānnṛpa|
bhuktvā bhogānyathākāmaṃ sthāpya putrānnije pade ॥91॥
gato gāṇeśvaraṃ dhāma yato nāvartate punaḥ |
sarvārthasādhakaṃ skanda vratānāṃ vratamuttamam ॥92॥
naitādṛśaṃ vrataṃ dānaṃ śrutamanyatkadācana |
sarvārthasādhakaṃ skanda vrataṃ kuru yadīcchasi ॥93॥
devadānava gandharvairṛṣibhirmanujaiḥ kṛtam |
nalena indumatyā ca rājñā candrāṃgadena ca ॥94॥
saṃprāptāḥ sarvakāmāṃste gatā gāṇeśvaraṃ padam |
giriruvāca |
iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ setihāsaṃ vrataṃ mahat |
kuru tvaṃ manasā dhyātvā varadaṃ gaṇanāyakam ॥95॥
vratametanmahābhāge śaṃkaraṃ prāpsyase tataḥ |
snehena kathitaṃ te'dya prakāśaṃ kuru mā vratam ॥96॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe himavatpārvatīsaṃvādo nāmaikapaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥51॥

<adhyāya 52 prāraṃbhaḥ>

pārvatyuvāca |
nalenedaṃ kṛtaṃ kasmāt ko nalo tvada me pitaḥ |
mano viśrāntimāyāti śṛṇvantyā me kathānakam ॥1॥
himavānuvāca |
niṣadheṣu mahārājo nalo nāmābhavatpurā |
brahmaṇyo vedavicchūro dānī mānī dhanī muniḥ ॥2॥
rathī khaḍgī śarī cāpi niṣaṃgī kavacī balī |
kṛtāstro devapūjyaśca trilokī gamanaḥ śuciḥ ॥3॥
śeṣo'pi mūkatāṃ yāti varṇituṃ yadguṇānapi |
na saṃkhyā vidyate'śvānāṃ gajānāṃ rathināṃ tathā ॥4॥
dhānuṣkāṇāṃ śastrabhṛtāṃ agniśastrabhṛtāmapi |
cakampuryad bhayāddevā indrādyāḥ sadigīśvarāḥ ॥5॥
damayantīti bhāryā''sīdyasya sauṃdaryamandiram |
damayitvā'khilānbrahmā sāraṃ pragṛhya ca ॥6॥
damayantīti vikhyātā yatasteneti nirmitā |
damayantyai trilokasthā nārīṇāṃ cārutāmadāt ॥7॥
nānālaṃkārasaṃyuktā nānāmaṇivibhūṣitā |
muktāhāralasatkaṃṭhā sadguṇāḍhyā suśobhanā ॥8॥
yasyāmātyo mahānāsīt padmahastaḥ parākramī |
buddhyā bṛhaspatisamo naye cāṃgirasā samaḥ ॥9॥
tuṃgatve merusadṛśo gāṃbhīrye codadheḥ samaḥ |
kadācidupaviṣṭo'sau nalo rājā mahāmanāḥ ॥10॥
niṣasāda sabhāgehe nṛpamaṃḍalamadhyagaḥ |
nṛtyantyapsarasastasya purataścārudarśanāḥ ॥11॥
stuvanti bandinastaṃ ca brahmarṣigaṇasaṃyutam |
etasminneva kāle tu gautamau nṛpatiṃ yayau ॥12॥
utthāya sādaraṃ rājñā svāsane viniveśitaḥ |
pūjitaḥ parayā bhaktyā tataḥ papraccha taṃ nalaḥ ॥13॥
nala uvāca |
svāminnanugṛhītaste darśanena mahāmune |
janma rājyaṃ pitā mātā kulaṃ jīvitamadya me ॥14॥
saphalaṃ svāgame hetuṃ vada tūrṇaṃ mahāmune |
gautama uvāca |
mamāsīnmahatī vāṃchā dṛṣṭuṃ te vaibhavaṃ nṛpa ॥15॥
stuvaṃti devāḥ svargasthā brahmendra hari śūlinaḥ |
dhanyo'si martyalokastho martyairdevaiśca śasyase ॥16॥
nityatṛpto'pyahaṃ tṛptaḥ pūjāṃ dṛṣṭvā ca vaibhavam |
idānīmanujānīhi gamiṣye svāśramaṃ prati ॥17॥
nala uvāca |
vedavedāṃgavid brahman sarvaśāstrapravartaka |
dayānidhe kṣaṇaṃ sthitvā chindhi me saṃśayaṃ mune ॥18॥
muniruvāca |
sādhu pṛṣṭaṃ mahārāja sthito'haṃ snehabhāvataḥ |
nāgā nṛpāśca devāśca yatte nājñā vilaṃghinaḥ ॥19॥
nṛpa uvāca |
mamaiva vismayo brahman nirīkṣya vaibhavaṃ svakam |
itthaṃ mamābhavatkena puṇyena tapasā'pi vā ॥20॥
vada tattvena kaścāhamabhavaṃ pūrvajanmani |
muniruvāca |
gauṃḍadeśātpare deśe pure pippalasaṃjñike ॥21॥
tvamāsīḥ kṣatriyaḥ pūrvaṃ daridro jñānavān śuciḥ|
jāyāpatyaiḥ suhṛdbhiśca vākśaraistāḍito bhṛśam ॥22॥
rātrāvapṛṣṭvā sarvāṃstvaṃ nirvedād gahanaṃ vanam |
gatavānvṛkṣavallībhiḥ siṃhavyāghragajairmṛgaiḥ ॥23॥
sevitaṃ śītalajalai sarobhirjalajādibhiḥ |
āśramo'tha tvayā'darśi bhramatā bhramatā vane ॥25॥
taponidheḥ kauśikasya vedaghoṣanināditaḥ |
tatra gatvā muniṃ taṃ tvaṃ praṇato bhaktibhāvataḥ ॥25॥
utthāpitaḥ kauśikena dīnānātha dayāvatā |
duḥkhitaṃ tvāṃ viditvā sa āśīrvādamavīvadat ॥26॥
gajānano me deveśo bhavitā te śubhaṃkaraḥ |
śrutvā tadāśiṣaṃ saumyāṃ prāpto'si paramāṃ mudam ॥27॥
pṛṣṭavānasi taṃ vipramupāyaṃ sarvakāmadaṃ |
dāridryanāśakaṃ rājan bhuktimuktipradaṃ śivam ॥28॥
gaṇeśārādhanaṃ tubhyaṃ kathayāmāsa kauśikaḥ |
kauśika uvāca |
vrataṃ kuru gaṇeśasya māsamātraṃ narādhipa ॥29॥
kuru mūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya mṛnmayīṃ cārudarśanām |
pūrvoktavidhinā pūjāṃ kuru śṛṇu kathānakam ॥30॥
dine dine māsamātraṃ tataḥ siddhimavāpsyasi |
muniruvāca |
iti śrutvā kauśikaṃ taṃ papraccha bhūmipaḥ puḥna ॥31॥
gajānanaṃ na jānāmi tatsvarupaṃ vadasva me|
jñātvā taṃ devadeveśaṃ kariṣye vratamuttamam ॥32॥
iti tena kṛte praśne jagāda munisattamaḥ|
anirvācyasvarūpasya parabrahmasvarupiṇaḥ ॥33॥
vaikārikasvarūpāṇi gajānanakṛtāni tam ॥34॥
kauśika uvāca |
yaḥ kartā sarvalokānāṃ pitā mātā jagadguruḥ |
brahmendra śiva viṣṇūnāṃ dhyeyo yaḥ sa gajānanaḥ ॥35॥
muniruvāca |
iti śrutvā tu tadvākyaṃ praṇipatya munīśvaram |
anujñātastatastena gato'si nijamaṃdiram ॥36॥
ārabhya śrāvaṇe śuddhe caturthyāṃ vratamuttamam |
akaronmṛnmayīṃ mūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya yathoditām ॥37॥
sthāne ca vacane mauṃne gamane śayane bhuji |
dhyāyangajānanaṃ devaṃ prāyāḥ siddhimanuttamām ॥38॥
nānā gajarathāśvādyairgodhanairdhanasaṃcitaiḥ |
dāsīdāsayutaḥ śrīmānjāto vrataprabhāvataḥ ॥39॥
dattamānasi dānāni sarvāṇi devatuṣṭaye |
gaṇeśasya mahāmaulyaṃ prāsādaṃ kṛtavānmudā ॥40॥
bhuktvā bhogānyathākāmaṃ kālena nidhanaṃ gataḥ |
jāto'si niṣadhe deśe nṛpastvaṃ nalanāmakaḥ ॥41॥
atastvayyacalā lakṣmī trilokī janavandite |
idānīmanujānīhi yatpṛṣṭaṃ tannirūpitam ||42॥
himavānuvāca |
evaṃ gate gautame tu vrataṃ cakre nalo nṛpaḥ |
tadvākyājjātaviśvāso mūrtiṃ kṛtvā suśobhanām ॥43॥
śrutvā kathāṃ gaṇeśasya pratyahaṃ pūjya bhaktitaḥ |
alabhatsarvakāmānsa vratasyāsya prabhāvataḥ ॥44॥
iti te kathitaṃ kanye nalenetthaṃ kṛtaṃ vratam |
upadiṣṭaṃ gautamena pūrvajanmakṛtaṃ vratam ॥45॥
yasya prabhāvaḥ saṃpūrṇo vaktuṃ kenāpi neṣyate ॥46॥
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe nalavratanirūpaṇaṃ nāma dvipaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥52॥

<adhyāya 53 prāraṃbhaḥ>

himavānuvāca |
idānīṃ kathayiṣyāmi rājñā candrāṃgadena ca|
sapatnyā ca kṛtaṃ hyetadindumatyā śubhānane ॥1॥
mālave viṣaye khyātaṃ nagaraṃ karṇanāmakam |
tatra candrāṃgado rājā babhūvātiparākramī ॥2॥
aṇimādi guṇopetaḥ sarvaśāstrārthatatvavit |
yajvā dānī mahājñānī vedavedāṃgapāragaḥ ॥3॥
atikrāntā sabhā yasya sudharmāmatimānuṣīm |
muṣṇāti cakṣuṣastejo ravikānta marīcibhiḥ ॥4॥
atisvacchāni vāsāṃsi ratnastaṃbhāśrayātsute |
nīlalohitapītatvāccitratāṃ yānti kānicit ॥5॥
atisādhvī mahābhāgā patiśuśrūṣaṇe ratā|
dharmaśīlā vrataparā śvaśrūśvaśurasevinī ॥6॥
gṛhakṛtyeṣu cāvyagrā devatātithipūjikā |
nāmnā cendumati khyātā sarvāvayavasundarā |
dharmaśīlo'pi nṛpatiramātyaiḥ prārthito bhṛśam |
jīvahiṃsāṃ mahāghorāṃ pāpārddhiṃ parivarjaya ॥7॥
kadāciddaivayogena mṛgayāṃ krīḍituṃ yayau |
tarakṣururuvārāha mṛgapakṣiyutaṃ vanam ॥8॥
nīlakaṃcuka saṃvīto nīloṣṇīṣottaracchadaḥ |
baddhagodhāṃgulitrāṇaḥ śastrikākhaḍgakheṭavān ॥9॥
javenāśvasamāruḍho śaracāpakaro balī|
tādṛśairvīranikarairamātyaiḥ sevakairvṛtaḥ ॥10॥
nighnan mṛgān varāhāṃśca preṣayannagaraṃ prati |
bhramamāṇo vane rājā dṛṣṭo rākṣasapuṃgavaiḥ ॥11॥
cakampe rākṣasān dṛṣṭavā śītajvarayuto yathā |
darīmukhān gartanetrān vyāditā syānnabhaspṛśaḥ ॥12॥
dṛṣṭvā tān vidrutāḥ sarve vīrāḥ sevijanā āpi |
kecidyamakṣayaṃ yātā mūrchitāḥ ke'pi bhūgatāḥ ॥13॥
tatraikā rākṣasī krūrā dṛṣṭvā kāmātisuṃdaram |
taṃ nṛpaṃ parirabhyaiva cucuṃbe kāmamohitā ॥14॥
tasyāmātyānbhage kṛtvā bhakṣayāmāsa sevakān |
etasminnaṃtare rājā palāyanaparo yayau ॥15॥
kāsāramadhye magno'bhūnnādrākṣīdrākṣasī ca tam |
sa dhṛto nāgakanyābhirnītaḥ pātālamandiram ॥16॥
bhūṣito nāgakanyābhirvastrālaṃkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ |
papracchurnāgakanyāstaṃ kuta āgamanaṃ tava ॥17॥
kosi kasyāsi kiṃ tāta vada satyaṃ narottama |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā tā jagāda narādhipaḥ॥18॥
ahaṃ candrāṃgado nāma hemāṃgadasuto balī|
mālave viṣaye karṇanagare vasatirmama ॥19॥
rākṣasībhayavitrastaḥ praviṣṭaḥ salilaṃ mahat |
bhavatībhirihānīto yatpṛṣṭaṃ tanniveditam ॥20॥
bhakṣitāḥ sarvalokā me mṛgayābhiratasya ha|
sarojalaprasādena jīvaṃstiṣṭhāmi sāṃpratam ॥21॥
iti tā vacanaṃ śrutvā punarucurnṛpaṃ tadā ॥22॥
tā ūcuḥ |
bhavāsmākaṃ patiriha sarvaṃ setsyati te priyam|
asmākaṃ nāgakanyānāṃ bhogaścātīva durlabhaḥ ॥23॥
tāsāmiti vacaḥ śrutvā provāca nṛpasattamaḥ |
ekapatnīvrataṃ me'sti mātarastatkathaṃ tyaje ॥24॥
somavaṃśaprasūtānāṃ rājñāṃ dharmānbravīmi vaḥ |
paradravyaṃ paradrohaṃ paradārāṃstathaiva ca ॥25॥
paranindāṃ ca necchanti nṛpāḥ saumyāśca sādhavaḥ |
adhītiryajanaṃ dānaṃ śaraṇāgatapālanam ॥26॥
niṣiddhācaraṇaṃ naiva vidhyarthaṃ pratipālanam |
ete dharmā trivarṇānāṃ yājanāditrayaṃ dvije ॥27॥
adhikaṃ sarvavarṇānāmātithyaṃ paramaṃ matam |
iti śrutvā nṛpavaco'khilā nāgakumārikāḥ ॥28॥
praśepustaṃ viyuktastvaṃ bahudhā svastriyā bhava |
babandhurnigaḍaistaṃ tā duḥkhitāḥ kāmavihvalāḥ ॥29॥
rākṣasī tu jalaṃ prāśya tadarthe tatsarogatam |
bhakṣayitvā jalacarānnā tṛpyatparamaṃ tadā ॥30॥
imaṃ vṛttāntamaśṛṇodrājñī rājīvalocanā |
paryaṃkasthā svadūtātsā rākṣasīto'vaśeṣitāt ॥31॥
bhagnaṃ niśamya nṛpatiṃ duḥkhitā patitā bhuvi |
mūrchāmavāpa mahatīṃ sakhībhiḥ parivrījitā ॥32॥
siktā śītalatoyena rudantībhirmuharmuhuḥ|
upaviṣṭā prarudati ghnaṃti vakṣaḥ śiro mukham ॥33॥
śuśoca paramālāpairbhartaḥ kānteti jalpatī |
kvagataḥ prāṇanātho me hitvā māṃ priyavādinīm ॥34॥
sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇā praṇatāṃ priyakāriṇīm |
bhartṛkāryaratāṃ nityaṃ nityaṃ cātithipūjikām ॥35॥
kutra bhokṣyati me nātho nidrāṃ vā kutra yāsyati |
kāṃcanaṃ śayanaṃ hitvā parārghyāstaraṇairvṛtam ॥36॥
kathaṃ snāsyati kānto me hitvā tailaṃ sugaṃdhikama |
lokānāṃ pālanaṃ ko'dya vinā tena kariṣyati ॥37॥
prajānāṃ sarvalokānāṃ bālānāṃ raṃjanaṃ ca kaḥ |
astaṃ yāto guṇanidhiḥ pratāpa nidhiradya kim ॥38॥
diśaḥ śūnyāḥ prapaśyāmi vinā tena mahātmanā |
kvāhaṃ sukhaṃ prapaśyāmi kva sukhaṃ so'pi yāsyati ॥39॥
ihaloke pare vāpi na sukhaṃ kulayoṣitām |
hīnānāṃ svāmināṃ deva yadetatsvāminā saha ॥40॥
śaraṇāgata guptiṃ ca dīnānāṃ kaḥ kariṣyati |
evaṃ bahuvidhaṃ dīnā rudatī bhṛśavihvalā ॥41॥
tatyāja troṭayitvā sā bhūṣaṇāni ca dūrataḥ |
kaṃkaṇāni ca sarvāṇi babhaṃja ca mumūrcha ca ॥42॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe candrāṃgadopākhyāne tripaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥53॥

<adhyāya 54 prāraṃbhaḥ>

pārvatyuvāca |
tasyāṃ tu mūrchitāyāṃ tu kimakurvan janāstadā |
tanme vistarato brūhi hṛdayāmandanaṃ pitaḥ ॥1॥
himavānuvāca |
tadā sarve nāgarikā nānāvākyaviśāradāḥ|
pramṛjya svāni cāśrūṇi samādathyurnṛpastriyam ॥2॥
janā ūcuḥ |
mātaruttiṣṭha mā śokaṃ kuru putre nanaḥ kṛthāḥ |
pretaṃ dahati śokāśru tasmādbhartṛhitaṃ kuru ॥3॥
martyeṣu na hi dṛṣṭo'sti cirajīvī śubhānane |
yathā jīrṇaṃ parityajya vāso'nyad gṛhyate janaiḥ ॥4॥
varṣmāpi dehinā tadvat tyaktvā'nyadgṛhyate śubham |
atyāścaryamidaṃ bhadre svayaṃ mṛtyumukhe sthitaḥ ॥5॥
śocate mṛtamaparaṃ svayaṃ maraṇadharmavān |
svadehena viyogaṃ ca na vetti bhāvinaṃ janaḥ ॥6॥
madīyaṃ manyate sarvadaivakālavaśe sthitam |
brahmādisthāvarāntaṃ yadviśvaṃ sābdhi carācaram ॥7॥
jñātvā taṃ naśvaraṃ śokaṃ tyaktvottiṣṭha suputriṇi |
dharmavān puṇyaśīlaste bhartā muktiṃgato bhavet ॥8॥
jīvanyadi bhavetsvarge tadā yāsyati karhicit |
atipuṇyān mānavo'pi yāti svargagataḥ punaḥ |
athavā paripṛcchāmo muniṃ kaṃcidihāgatam ॥9॥
atītānāgatavidaṃ kathayiṣyati so'khilam |
anantaraṃ hi yatkāryaṃ tatsarvaṃ vidadhāmahe ॥10॥
himavānuvāca |
evaṃ prabodhitā lokaiḥ kṣaṇamindumatītmadā |
āśvastā sarvavākyena pramṛjyāśrūṇi vāsasā ॥11॥
visarjayāmāsa tadā lokānsarvān samāgatān |
tyaktvā saubhāgyacihnāni prapede kṛśatāṃ bhṛśam ॥12॥
rudatī śocatī tasthau śvasatī mūrchitā muhuḥ |
tato dvādaśavarṣānte nārado divyadarśanaḥ ॥13॥
ājagāma gṛhaṃ tasyā yadṛcchā vicaranmuniḥ |
dṛṣṭvā taṃ vilalāpāśu vadantī bhartuceṣṭitam ॥14॥
duḥkhaṃ cākathayattasmai jātaṃ dvādaśavārṣikam |
niśamya ruditaṃ tasyā harṣayanmunirabravīt ॥15॥
nārada uvāca |
kvāpi tiṣṭhati te bhartā na taṃ śocitumarhasi |
śiracchādaya nīlena vāsasā karṇabhūṣaṇe ॥16॥
karṇayoḥ kuru bhāle tvaṃ bibhratī kuṃkumaṃ śubham |
valayāni ca karayoḥ kaṃṭhe maṃgalasūtrakam ॥17॥
himavānuvāca |
sarvajñasya munervākyādviśvastā satyavādinaḥ |
tatkālaṃ sarvamānāyya harṣitā sā tathā'karot ॥18॥
ākāryaṃ brāhmaṇān sarvān saṃpūjya nāradaṃ purā|
saṃpūjya sakalāṃstāṃstu dadau dānānyanekaśaḥ ॥19॥
vādayāmāsa vādyāni śarkarāṃ ca gṛhegṛhe |
preṣayāmāsa ca tadā harṣādindumatī śubhā| ॥20॥
vastratāmbūla dānena dadāvājñāṃ janasya sā |
praṇipatya punardevaṃ muniṃ nāradamādarāt ॥21॥
svabhartṛprāptaye'pṛcchadupāyaṃ rājakanyakā |
indumatyuvāca |
kutra tiṣṭhati me bhartā kathaṃ tiṣṭhati vā mune ॥22॥
kenopāyena vedajña darśanaṃ tasya me bhavet |
anugrahaṃ kuru mune tamupāyaṃ vadasva me |
vrataṃ dānaṃ tapo vāpi duḥsādhyamapi yadbhavet ॥ 23॥
nārada uvāca |
vrataṃ tu paramaṃ te'haṃ kathayāmi samāsataḥ |
nabhaḥśuklacaturthyāṃ tadārabhetparayā mudā ॥24॥
nadyāṃ taḍāge vāpyāṃ vā dantadhāvanapūrvakam|
snānaṃ prabhātasamaye kuru saṃkalpapūrvakam ॥25॥
śuklavāsā gṛhaṃ gatvā mṛnmayīṃ mūrtimuttamām |
kuryāccaturbhujāṃ samyag gaṇeśasya manoramām ॥26॥
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pūjayet sthiracetasā |
ekānnamekabhuktaṃ vā svayaṃ kuryātprayatnataḥ |
upoṣaṇaṃ vā kurvīta yāvadbhādracaturthikā ॥27॥
gītavāditranṛtyādyairbrāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ |
mahotsavaṃ prakurvīta vibhave sati śobhane ॥28॥
evaṃ vrataṃ kuru śubhe bhartrāsaha sameṣyasi |
pātāle nāgakanyā bho ruddhena jīvitā śubhe ॥29॥
satyaṃ bravīmi te satye nānyathā mama bhāṣitam |
himavānuvāca |
ityuktā tena muninā vrataṃ prārabhadādarāt ॥30॥
