gate munau nabhomāsi dinaiḥ katibhirāgate |
dviradānanamūrtiṃ sā cakāra pārthivīṃ śubhām ॥31॥
pūrvoktavidhinā pūjāṃ cakāra sumanoharām |
divyagandhairdivyavastrairdivyaiśca kusumairapi ॥32॥
divyairnānāvidhaiścaiva naivedyaiḥ phalakāṃcanaiḥ |
dīpaiḥ puṣpāṃjalībhiśca prakramairnatibhiḥ stavaiḥ ॥33॥
tannāmasmaraṇairdhyānaistoṣayāmāsa taṃ vibhum |
gītavāditranṛtyādyairbrāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanaiḥ ॥34॥
palamātraṃ payo bhuktvā cakāra vratamuttamam |
nabhaḥśuklacaturthyādi ciranaṣṭaṃ priyāptaye ॥36॥
nāradasya munervākyādyāvadbhādracaturthikā ॥36॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe indumatīnāradasaṃvāde catuḥpaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥54॥

<adhyāya 55 prāraṃbhaḥ>

himavānuvāca |
samāpte tu vrate tasyā gaṇeśasya prasādataḥ |
pātāle nāgakanyānāmanyathā'bhūnmatistadā ॥1॥
mocayāmāsa rājānaṃ pupūjuśca yathāvidhi |
vastrairābharaṇaiścaiva nānāratnamahādhanaiḥ ॥2॥
aśvaṃ manojavaṃ dattvā visasarjurnṛpaṃ tadā |
kāsārād bahirāgatya baddhvā cāśvaṃ mahādrume ॥3॥
snāti yāvannāgarikairdṛṣṭaḥ katipayairnṛpaḥ |
aparai pratipatyainaṃ papracchuḥ ko bhavāniti ॥4॥
kutratyaḥ kuta āyātaḥ kiṃ nāmā vada naḥ prabho|
iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā papraccha nṛpasattamaḥ ॥5॥
indumatyāḥ kumārasya kuśalaṃ khedasaṃyutaḥ |
tato bubudhire te tamāliliṃgurmudā janāḥ ॥6॥
ūcuśca nṛpa te bhāryā snātvedānīṃ gatā gṛham |
upavāsavratavatī kṛśā dhamanisaṃyutā ॥7॥
kumāre jīvamāsthāya jīvamātrāvaśeṣitā |
kecicca nagaraṃ gatvā śubhāṃ vārtāmaghoṣayan ॥8॥
atyāptajanavākyena śrutvā sā nṛpamāgatam |
ānandābdhau nimagnā'bhūd brahmajñānīva yogavit ॥9॥
tato'mātyānpuraskṛtya sainyānya preṣayacca tam |
citradhvajapatākābhiraśobhayata sā puram ॥10॥
vīthīrāsiṃcayāmāsa sabhāḥ saṃpādya yatnataḥ |
svātmānaṃ bhūṣayāmāsa vāso'laṃkārabhūṣaṇaiḥ ॥11॥
gobhūhiraṇyadānādyaistoṣayitvā dvijāna bahūn |
ārārtikyaṃ kare dattvā pativat strījanasya sā ॥12॥
gītavāditraghoṣeṇa purātsara upāgamat |
amātyāstu puro gatvā praṇamya nṛpapuṃgavam ॥13॥
āliliṃgurmudā sarve praṇemuśca yathākramam |
upaviṣṭe tu nṛpatāvupaviṣṭāstadājñayā ॥14॥
sarvānkuśalapraśnādyaistoṣayitvā yathākramam |
mānayitvā tathā lokāndatvā tāmbūlavāsasī ॥15॥
svayaṃ jagāma śibiramindumatyā nṛpottamaḥ |
kārayāmāsa sa vidhiṃ dvādaśābdadṛśairdvijaiḥ ॥16॥
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā gaṇeśārcanapūrvakam |
saṃpūjya śaṃkaraṃ samyag dvijāṃśca dakṣiṇādibhiḥ ॥17॥
babhaṃja śrīphalaṃ tyaktvā tatastatpurato yayau|
dadarśa tatrendumatīmindoḥ śeṣāṃ kalāmiva ॥18॥
nīrājayitvā rājānaṃ yuvatībhiḥ sabhartṛbhiḥ |
lājapuṣpamayīṃ vṛṣṭiṃ tābhiḥsā'kāra yattadā ॥19॥
pramṛjya nayane samyagānandāśru pariplute |
harṣaśokānvitau tau tu parasparamathocatuḥ ॥20॥
parasparaviyogādhiṃ kathayāmāsatuḥ śucā |
anivāryaprakṛtibhiḥ sāntvitau bahuloktibhiḥ ॥21॥
ānayitvā tu rājānaṃ samārohya mahāgaje |
nānālaṃkārasaṃyukte patākā chatra saṃyute ॥22॥
pādacāraiḥ parivṛte caturghaṃṭāvirājite |
śataṃ yaṣṭidharāstasya vārayanto yayuḥ puraḥ ॥23॥
agni śastra dharāścāśva rathārohā anekaśaḥ |
savya dakṣiṇato rājño gajalakṣaṃ yayurlaghu ॥24॥
naṭā nṛtyāṃganā vādyavādakā bandinaḥ puraḥ |
tataḥ paścād gajānīkāḥ puraṃ viviśurādarāt ॥25॥
sainyena rajasā vyāpte dinanāthe hataprabhe |
alaṃkṛte pure tasminna prājñāyata kiṃcana ॥26॥
tataḥ parasparaṃ natvā jagmuḥ svaṃ svaṃ niveśanam |
mukhyā rājagṛhaṃ nītāḥ punā rājñā prapūjitāḥ ॥27॥
vastra tāmbūla dānena tenājñātā gṛhaṃ yayuḥ |
rājā'tha brāhmaṇānbhojya bubhuje jñātibhiḥ saha ॥28॥
tato rātrau suṣupatuḥ paryaṃke cārunirmite |
parārdhyāstaraṇopete paricchadupadhānvite ॥29॥
ūcatuḥ sva sva duḥkhaṃ tau śocantau ca punaḥ punaḥ |
purodhasā sānvitau tau śayāte sma sukhaṃ tataḥ ॥30॥
śrutvā vināyakavrata mahimānaṃ nṛpottamaḥ |
patntyā'bhūtaṃ sakalaṃ svayaṃ kartuṃ mano dadhe ॥31॥
āgate śrāvaṇe māsi rājā candrāṃgadastadā |
mahotsavena kṛtavān vratametacchubhānane ॥32॥
ṛṣiruvāca |
evaṃ pitṛvacaḥ śrutvā pārvatī bhṛśaharṣitaḥ |
nabhomāsaṃ samāsādya cakāra vratamādarāt ॥33॥
yathoktavidhinā mūrtiṃ kṛtvā pūjāṃ ca yatnataḥ |
bhakṣayantī payomātraṃ dhyāyantī dviradānanam ॥34॥
tataścaṃcalatā yātaṃ śaṃkarasyāpi mānasam |
āsasādāśramaṃ tasyāḥ pārvatyāḥ śūlabhṛt svayam ॥35||
gaṇeśasya caturthyāṃ tu saṃpūrṇe tadvrate śubhe|
paśyati sma haraṃ devī vṛṣārūḍhaṃ nijāśrame ॥36॥
utthāya praṇanāmāsya pādāmbujayugaṃ mudā |
pūjayāmāsa vidhivacchaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram ॥37॥
uvāca ca mahādevaṃ pārvatī premavihvalā | devyuvāca |
kathaṃ māṃ tyajya gatavānkiṃ māṃ vismṛtavānasi ॥38॥
tvadviyogānnimeṣo'pi kalpakalpo'bhavadvibho |
gaṇeśasya vratamidaṃ pitrādiṣṭaṃ kṛtaṃ mayā ॥39॥
varadasya prasādena saṃprāptaṃ darśanaṃ tava ॥40॥
brahmovāca |
etasminneva kāle tu himavānupajagmivān |
satyāḥ karaṃ kare tasya nyavedayata sādaram ॥41॥
tato devāḥ sagaṃdharvā dviradānanamādarāt |
pūjayāmāsuratha tau śivau śiva karau satām ॥42॥
devaduṃdubhayo neduḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiḥ papāta ha|
sarve gajānanaṃ natvā stutvā ca vividhaiḥ stavaiḥ ॥43॥
jayaśabdaiḥ śaṃkaro'pi tuṣṭāva dviradānanam |
ardhāṃge pārvatīṃ kṛtvā vṛṣamāruhaya satvaraḥ ॥44॥
yayau kailāsaśikharaṃ sarve svaṃ svaṃ padaṃ yayuḥ ॥45॥
brahmovāca |
iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ vyāsa pṛṣṭaṃ tvayā tu yat |
māhātmyaṃ gaṇanāthasya vratasya ca mahāmune ॥46॥
idānīṃ punaranyacca kathayāmi kathānakam |
yacchrutvā sarvapāpebhyo muktaḥ kāmām̐llabhennara ॥47॥ 
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe śivapārvatīsaṃyogonāma paṃcapaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥55॥

<adhyāya 56 prāraṃbhaḥ>

bhṛguruvāca |
evaṃ te kathitaṃ rājan māhātmyaṃ sakalaṃ mayā |
punaśca śṛṇu vyāsena yacchrutaṃ brahmaṇo mukhāt || 1 ||
somakānta uvāca |
kathaṃ śrutaṃ brahmamukhād vyāsenāmitabuddhinā |
na tṛptimadhigacchāmi tanme vada mahāmune ||2||
bhṛguruvāca |
evaṃ śrutvā kathāṃ vyāso'pṛcchad brahmāṇamādarāt |
sa cāpi kathayāmāsa sādaraṃ pṛcchate'nagha || 3 ||
vyāsa uvāca |
punaḥ kathaya me brahmana gaṇanātha kathāṃ parām |
tṛṣṇā me vardhate bhūyaḥ śrotuṃ vighneśa satkathām ||4||
brahmovāca |
aparāṃ śṛṇu me vyāsa kathāṃ kautukasaṃyutām |
śūrasena prabhṛtibhiranubhūtāṃ gajānanīm ||5 ||
madhyadeśebhavadrājā śūraseno mahābalī |
sahasrākhya pure ramye vedavedāṃga pāragaḥ ||6||
dhanavān rupavān dātā hotā pātā janasya ha |
śaktitrayayuto mānī ṣāḍguṇya pariniṣṭhitaḥ ||7||
upāyānāṃ caturṇāṃ ca cālane caturo'rihā |
caturvidha balopeto dvijadeveṣu bhaktimān ||8||
pṛthivīmaṃḍalaṃ yasya vaśavartyabhavatsadā |
nagaraṃ śakranagarādviśiṣṭaṃ bhāti bhūtale ||9||
yasya patnī puṇyaśīlā nāmnā'bhūt puṇyaśālinī |
yasyā rupeṇa sadṛśī nāsīt trailokyamaṃḍale || 1 0||
pātivratya guṇairyasyā lajjāṃ prāptā'pyaruṃdhatī |
asūyā tyāgayogenāpyanasūyā'bhavallaghuḥ || 11 ||
sa kadācinnṛpo'mātyaiḥ saṃvṛto vīrasattamaiḥ |
upaviṣṭo nṛpasabhāṃ dadṛśe gaganecaram || 1 2||
vimānaṃ vahnisadṛśaṃ netratejoharaṃ param |
gāna śravaṇa saktāste rājñā saha sabhāsadaḥ || 13 ||
vihvalāste kiṃ kimiti jñātuṃ dūtānacodayan |
te dūtā prāgamandraṣṭuṃ vimānaṃ sūryasannibham || 14||
kasyacidvaiśyaputrasya rājadūtasya kuṇḍina. |
patitaṃ dṛṣṭipātena tadvimānaṃ mahītale || 15||
tato dūtāḥ samāgamya śaśaṃsuḥ nṛpatiṃ tadā |
tadvimānaṃ mahārāja puṇyakṛddeva saṃyutam || 1 6||
daidīpyamānaṃ patitaṃ duṣṭadṛṣṭyā mahītale |
tato'ti harṣito rājā prakṛtidvayasaṃyutaḥ || 17||
aśvāruḍho yayau tatra vimānaṃ draṣṭumatsukaḥ |
manyamāno mahābhāgyaṃ svajanaiḥ parivāritaḥ || 18||
anekavādya nirghoṣaiḥ vimānasyāntikaṃ yayau |
dadṛśustatra śakraṃ vai namuruttīrya yānataḥ || 19||
nānālaṃkāra saṃyuktaṃ sarvadevagaṇairyutam |
baddhāṃjalipuṭo rājā provāca balasūdanam ||2 0 ||
dhanyeyamadya dharaṇī dhanyaṃ janma ca saṃpadaḥ |
pūrvajāḥ sarvacakṣūṃṣi dhanyānyadya śacīpate || 21 ||
yaddarśanaṃ mṛtyuloke bhavatāṃ sānugasya me |
yasya te vaśagā lokā brahmeśānādayaḥ surāḥ ||2 2 ||
śatāśvamedhato dṛśyo nānyathā jāyate kvacit |
na jāne kena puṇyena so'dya dṛṣṭo'khilairjanai ||2 3||
idaṃ ca te vimānaṃ yat kathaṃ nipatitaṃ bhuvi |
etanme saṃśayaṃ deva chettumarhasi sāṃpratam ||24||
yatavānkutra ca bhavāngantā vā śaṃsa me prabho |
śakra uvāca |
rājan śṛṇu mahāścaryaṃ kathitaṃ nāradena me ||25 ||
tadahaṃ kathayiṣye tvāṃ śṛṇuṣvaikamanā nṛpa |
nārada uvāca |
mṛtyulokaṃ gataḥ śakra bhruśuṃḍerāśramaṃ prati |
gaṇeśasya svarūpeṇa tiṣṭhato japato'niśam || 26||
tad dhyānāttatsvarūpasya dṛṣṭaṃ kautukamadbhutam |
pūjitastena muninā gajānana svarupiṇā || 27||
na vā taṃ munimāpṛcchya darśanārthe tavāgata |
bhūmau sārūpyatā naiva dṛṣṭā'nyatra śatakrato ||28||
śakra uvāca |
ahaṃ ca nāradaṃ pūjya visṛjya ca kṣaṇena tam |
atyutsukatayā yāto muniṃ dṛṣṭuṃ tathāvidham ||29 ||
vimānavaramāsthāya manomaruta vegavat |
muniṃ dṛṣṭavā ca saṃpūjya dviradānanarūpiṇam || 30 ||
bhruśuṃḍiṃ praṇipatyātha tasya pūjāṃ pragṛhya ca |
calitaḥ saparīvāro gantukāmo'marāvatīm || 31 ||
yāvatte nagarābhyāśe vimānamidamāgatam |
tāvad dūtasya te dṛṣṭyā kuṣṭhinaḥ pāpakāriṇaḥ || 32 ||
patitaṃ bhūmibhāge'sminsarvaṃ te kathitaṃ nṛpa |
śūrasena uvāca |
tapasā kena vā śakra gajānana svarupatā || 33 ||
prāptā bhruśuṃḍinā tena tadvadasva mama prabho |
na hi me tṛptirastīha śuṇvatastatkathāmṛtam ||34||
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe ṣaṭpacāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥ 56 ॥

<adhyāya 57 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śakra uvāca |
hanta te kathayiṣyāmi kathāmetāṃ purātanīm |
yathā tenāpi sādṛśyaṃ gaṇanāthasya bhaktitaḥ || 1 ||
daṃḍakāraṇya deśeṣu nagare naṃdurābhidhe |
ekaḥ kaivartako duṣṭo nāmnā nāmaiva viśrutaḥ ||2 ||
ābālyāccaurya nirato yauvane jārakarmakṛt|
pratyakṣaṃ dṛṣṭivigamādbharate ca śapatyapi || 3 ||
śapathānkurute mithyā paramhadbheda kārakān |
tato niḥsārito grāmād dyūtamadyarato yataḥ ||4||
lokairbahirdūratara giri kandara kānane |
sthitaḥ kāntāyuto mārge jaghāna pathikānbahūn ||5||
evaṃ bahudhano jāto nānālaṃkaraṇai striyam |
bhūṣayāmāsa sasutāṃ toṣayāmāsa māyayā ||6 ||
śastra khaḍgaṃ ca kheṭaṃ ca pāśāndhanuranuttamam |
daṃḍaṃ cobhayato lohaṃ vahanpūrṇe maheṣudhī|
vṛkṣāgre koṭare vāpi sthito mārayate bahūn ||7||
nānāvidhāni vastūni vāsāṃsyābharaṇāni ca |
gṛhe saṃcayate nītvā vikrīṇāti purāntare ||8 ||
yatheṣṭaṃ bhajate nityaṃ viṣayānmandire nije |
evaṃ pāpasamācāro hantyaraṇye paśūnapi ||9||
ekadā kasyacit pṛṣṭhe dhāvito yojanāvadhi |
sa tu tasmādgato dūraṃ skhalitvā patito bhuvi || 10 ||
tata utthāya kaṣṭena śanai śanairyayau khalaḥ |
apaśyatpathi gacchansa tīrthaṃ gāṇeśvaraṃ śubhaṃ || 11 ||
akaronmajjanaṃ tatra śramameva vyapohitum |
tato'gacchansvaviṣayaṃ dadarśa pathi mudgalam || 1 2||
japantaṃ gaṇanāthasya mantraṃ nāmasamanvitam |
khaḍgamudyamya niṣkośaṃ mudgalasyāntikaṃ yayau || 13 ||
kaivartako nāma nāmā hantuṃ kṛtamatistadā |
jagalustasya śastrāṇi khaḍgo'pi dṛḍhamuṣṭitaḥ || 14||
anyathā cābhavad buddhistasya duṣṭasya tatkṣaṇāt |
dviradānana bhaktasya mudgalasya prabhāvataḥ || 15||
jahāsa munimukhyo'sau dṛṣṭvā taṃ tu tathāvidham |
papraccha saṃyataḥ kasmācchastrāṇi galitāni te || 1 6||
baddhānyapi ca sarvāṇi patitāni kuto vada |
indra uvāca |
gaṇeśatīrthasnānena munerālokanena ca |
jñānavairāgya yukto'sāvabravīn mudgaruṃ vacaḥ || 1 7||
kaivartaka uvāca |
āścaryaṃ paramaṃ manye kuṃḍasnānena me matiḥ |
viparītābhavad brahman viśeṣāddarśanena te || 1 8||
bālyāt prabhṛti me duṣṭā buddhiḥ pāparatā'bhavat |
asaṃkhyātāni pāpāni kṛtānyadyāvadhi prabho || 1 9||
idānīmeva nirviṇṇā matirme tvatprasādataḥ |
nādāsye punaretāni śastrāṇi patitāni me ||2 0 ||
kurū me'nugrahaṃ pūrṇamuddharasva bhavārṇavāt |
sādhavo hyanugṛhṇanti dīnān duṣkṛtino'pi ca || 21 ||
na sādhu saṃgamaḥ kvāpi vṛthā dṛṣṭo mahāmune |
yathā śevadhi samparko na dhātuṣu vṛthā bhavet ||2 2 |
| śakra uvāca |
evamukto mudgalo'sau taṃ jagāda kṛpānvitaḥ |
śaraṇāgata saṃtyāge ramarandoṣaṃ viśeṣataḥ ||2 3 |
| mudgala uvāca |
vidhiprayukte dānādau nādhikāro'sti kutracit |
tathāpi nāmajapaṃ kathayāmi prasādata ||24||
gajānanasya paramaṃ sarvasiddhikaraṃ nṛṇām |
tataḥ praṇāmamakarot kaivarto mudgalaṃ munima ||25 ||
gaṇeśāya nama iti nāmamaṃtramupādiśat ||2 6 ||
sva yaṣṭiṃ ropayāmāsa purastasya dharātale |
uvāca cainaṃ saṃprītyā yāvadāgamanaṃ mama ||27||
tāvajjapasva nāmedaṃ yāvadyaṣṭiśca sāṃkurā |
ekāsana gato vāyubhakṣa ekāgramānasaḥ ||28||
sāyaṃ prātarjalaṃ dehi yaṣṭimūle nirantaram ||29||
śakra uvāca |
upadiṣṭo mudgalena nāmā kaivartakastadā |
nirāśo jīvite tasthau tasminnantarhite munau || 30 ||
ekāsana gato'raṇye jajāpa nāmamaṃtrakam |
purodhāya muneryaṣṭi vṛkṣacchāyā samāśritaḥ || 31 ||
nirāhāro nirīhaśca jitendriya gaṇo vaśī |
evaṃ sahasravarṣānte sā yaṣṭiḥ sāṃkurā'bhavat || 3 2||
tataḥ pratīkṣāmakaronmunerāgamanasya yaḥ |
valmīkaveṣṭita tanurlatājāla samanvitaḥ ||3 3 ||
tato muni mudgalo'pi daivācca deśamāyayau |
sasmāra ca tadā yaṣṭiṃ taṃ ca kaivartakaṃ yadā ||34||
dadarśa bhramamāṇaḥ sa sāṃkaśaṃ yaṣṭimuttamām |
valmīkākrānta dehaṃ ca taṃ ca kaivartakaṃ muniḥ || 35||
duścaraṃ munibhiḥ sarvairāsthitaṃ tapa uttamam |
lakṣitaṃ netramātreṇa yatatā muninā tadā ||3 6||
utsādya tasya dehasthaṃ valmīkaṃ munisattamaḥ |
maṃtritena jalenāsya siṣecāṃgaṃ ca sarvaśaḥ ||37||
sadaiva divyadehaṃ taṃ jagāda munisattamaḥ |
prāptavantaṃ gaṇeśasya sārūpyaṃ karayo gataḥ ||3 8||
dvikaraṃ prajapaṃtaṃ ca nāma vaināyakaṃ śubham |
unmīlīte tadā netre muninā bodhitastu saḥ ||3 9 ||
tasya netrodbhave vahnirvidyudvad gaganaṃ gataḥ |
trilokīmudyato dagdhuṃ muninā vārito yataḥ ||40 ||
so'pi taṃ munimānamya svaguruṃ karūṇāyutam |
āliliṃga mudā yuktastanayaḥ pitaraṃ yathā ||4 1 ||
upadiṣye mudgalena nāma kaivartakastadā |
valmīkāt punarutpannaṃ mānayāmāsa taṃ sutam ||42 ||
cakāra nāma karmāsya mudgalo munirādarāt |
bhruśuṃḍīti tato nṛpa ||43||
ekākṣaraṃ ca mantraṃ ca tasmai prāhātha mudgala ||44||
varānasmai dadau paścādbhava tvaṃ munisattamaḥ |
indrādi devagaṃdharvaiḥ siddhairarcyatamo bhava ||45||
yathā gajānano devo dhyāto dṛṣṭo'ghanāśanaḥ |
tathāhi tvamapi mune bhrūśuṃḍiḥ prathito bhava || 46 ||
yasya te darśanaṃ syātsa kṛtakṛtyaṃ bhavennara |
āyuśca kalpalakṣaṃ te bhavitā mam vākyataḥ ||47||
evaṃ bahuvidhā tasmai varānyacchati mudgale |
yayurindrādayo draṣṭuṃ munayo nāradādayaḥ ||48||
ūcustaṃ praṇipatyaiva bhrūśuṃḍe tava darśanāta |
sārthakaṃ janma no vidyā pitarau ca tapo yaśaḥ ||49||
tvameva gaṇanātho'si pūjanīyo'si no mune |
sa tu saṃpūjyavānsarvān praṇamya ca visṛjya ca ||5 0 ||
punaḥ padmāsana gato jajāpaikākṣaraṃ manum |
puro mūrtiṃ pratiṣṭhāpya gaṇeśasya manoharām ||5 1 ||
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirapūpujaddinedine |
āśramaśca babhau vāpī sarovṛkṣa latādibhi ||5 2 ||
tyaktavaire siṃhamṛgai nakulairbhujagottamaiḥ |
tato varṣaśatasyānte prasanno'bhūd gajānanaḥ ||53 ||
uvāca matsvarūpastvaṃ kimarthaṃ tapase tapaḥ |
kṛtakṛtyo'si sāyujyamāyuṣo'nte ca lapsyase ||54||
idaṃ kṣetra suvikhyātaṃ nāmaleti bhaviṣyati |
anuṣṭhāna kṛtāmatra nānāsiddhipradaṃ nṛṇām ||55 ||
manmūrti darśanādatra na punarjanmabhāgbhavet |
aputracca labhet putrān vidyārthī jñānamāpnuyāt ||5 6|
śakra uvāca |
iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ śūrasena nṛpottama |
yatvayā paripṛṣṭaṃ me kimanyacchrotumicchasi ||57||||57|| 
iti śrī gaṇeśa purāṇe upāsanākhaṃḍe bhrūśuṃḍyupākhyānaṃ nāma saptapaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ ||57||

<adhyāya 58 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca |
evaṃ śrutvā śūraseno marutvad vākyamuttamam |
punaḥ papraccha taṃ vyāsa pratiśrutvā kathāmṛtam ॥1॥
śūrasena uvāca |
kenopāyena deveśaṃ yānaṃ te gaganaṃ vrajet |
tamupāyaṃ kuru vibho vada vā kiṃ karomi te ॥2॥
brahmovāca |
evaṃ punaḥ kṛtapraśnaṃ nṛpaṃ prāha surārihā |
śṛṇvatāṃ sarvalokānāṃ smayamāna idaṃ vacaḥ ॥3॥
indra uvāca |
kaścitsyādiha saṃkaṣṭacaturthī vratakārakaḥ |
vipro vā kṣatriyo vāpi nagare te nṛpottama ॥4॥
tasyābdakṛtapuṇyena samyag dattena bhūbhuja |
idānīṃ pracale desannānyathā'ghutapūrūṣai ॥5॥
nṛpa uvāca |
kathaṃ vrataṃ tu saṃkaṣṭacaturthyā vada me mune |
kiṃ puṇyaṃ kiṃ phalaṃ tasya ko vidhiḥ kasya pūjanam ॥6॥
purā kena kṛtaṃ cātra siddhiryasmiñ kṛte'bhavat |
etatsarvaṃ śunāśīra kṛpayā vada vistarāt ॥7॥
śakra uvāca |
atrāpyudāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam |
kṛtavīryasya saṃvādaṃ nāradena mahātmanā ॥8॥
babhūva balavānrājā kṛtavīryo mahīnale |
satyaśīlo vadānyaśca yajvā mānī mahārathaḥ ॥9॥
jitendriyo mitāhāro devadvijaniṣevakaḥ |
yasyāśvagajayodhānāṃ rathināṃ sarvadhanvinām ॥10॥
na saṃkhyā vidyate rājan sahyādriviṣayāsinām |
mandire yasya sauvarṇāḥ paryaṃkā bhājanāni ca ॥11॥
nāsīdauduṃbaraṃ pātraṃ pākārthamapi sarvadā |
yasya dvādaśa sāhasrā brāhmaṇāḥ paṃktibhojinaḥ ॥12॥
sugandhā nāma yasyāsītpatnī dharmaparāyaṇā |
pativratā patiprāṇā trailokye'timanoramā॥13॥
nānālaṃkāra subhagā dvijadevātithipriyā |
evaṃ tau daṃpatī rājannaputrau saṃbabhūvatuḥ ॥14॥
putrārthaṃ sarvadānāni vratāni ca tapāṃsi ca |
cakraturniyamānanyān yajñāṃśca bahudakṣiṇān ॥15॥
nānātīrthāni kṣetrāṇi jagmatuḥ putralipsayā |
tathāpi nābhavat putro janmāntara kṛtenasoḥ ॥16॥
ekadā duḥkhito rājā samāhūya sa mantriṇaḥ |
rājyaṃ mudrāṃ ca kośaṃ ca janāṃjanapadāstathā ॥17॥
tebhyo nivedya sarvaṃ tau jagmaturvanamuttamama |
valkalājinasaṃvītau dampatī tapaāsthitau ॥18॥
jitendriyau jitāhārau jīrṇaparṇānilāśinau |
cakṣurmātreṇa lakṣyau tau dṛṣṭvā'yaṃ nārado muniḥ ॥19॥
janakaṃ kṛtavīryasya pitṛlokasthamabravīt |
aputratvāt prāyagato vidyate tanayastava ॥20॥
kṛtavīryo mṛtyuloke śvaḥparaśvo mariṣyati |
yadi tasya bhavetputro nākalokapradarśakaḥ ॥21॥
kṛtavīryastadā jīvenmṛto vā svargamāpnuyāt |
ityuktvā nārado yātaḥ paśyati smādbhutaṃ bhuvi ॥22॥
bhrūśuṃḍeḥ pitarau putrau tasya patnīṃ sakanyakām |
agnijvālākule ghore kumbhīpāke hyadhomukhān ॥23॥
yāmyadūtaistāḍyamānān krandanto vividhān ravān |
teṣāmākranditaṃ śrutvā nāradaḥ karuṇānidhiḥ ॥24॥
āgatya kathayāmāsa teṣāṃ duḥkhaṃ bhruśuṃḍaye |
nārada uvāca |
śakrādayo devagaṇā munayaḥ kapilādayaḥ ॥25॥
yasya te darśanaṃ kartuṃ yānti herambarūpiṇaḥ |
tatkathaṃ pitaraupatnī putrāḥputryaśca kiṃkarāḥ ॥26॥
yamaloke kumbhipāke pacyante tava doṣataḥ |
tvaṃ cāpi jñānasaṃpannaḥ kathaṃ tannāvabudhyase ॥27॥
pūrvajānāṃ tvamuddhāre yatnaṃ kuru mahāmune |
śakra uvāca |
bhrūśuṃḍirati saṃtapto vākyaṃ śrutvā munīritam |
duḥkhitaḥ pitṛduḥkhena bhṛśaṃ jajvāla vahnivat |
upāyaṃ cintayāmāsa teṣāmuddhārakārakam |
dadau śreyaṃ sa saṃkaṣṭacaturthīṃ vratajaṃ tadā ॥29॥
vilokya praṇidhānena bhruśuṃḍī sa parokṣavit |
dhyātvā gajānanaṃ devaṃ kare dhṛtvā śubhaṃ payaḥ ॥30॥
uvāca pitṝnuddiśya yācandevaṃ gajānanam |
bhruśuṃḍiruvāca|
yadi bhaktyā kṛtaṃ me'sti gaṇanātha vrataṃ tava ॥31॥
tadā tasya prabhāvena śīghramuddhara pūrvajān |
evamuktvā tu tattoyaṃ gajānanakare'kṣipat ॥32॥
kṣiptamātre tadā toye gajānanaprasādataḥ |
vimānāni samāruhya sarve te devarūpiṇaḥ ॥33॥
sevyamānā apsarobhiḥ stūyamānāśca cāraṇaiḥ |
gaṃdharvairgīyamānāste gaṇeśasthānamāpnuvan ॥34॥
anye'pi kuṃbhipāke ca narā duṣkṛtino'bhavan |
te'pi sarve vimānasthāḥ padaṃ gāṇeśvaraṃ yayuḥ | ॥35॥
evaṃ vratasya mahimā mayā te parikīrtitaḥ |
yadekadinapuṇyena sarve sadgatiṃ gataḥ| ॥36॥
janmāvadhi kṛtaṃ yena saṃkaṣṭaṃ vratamādarāt |
śeṣo'pi tasya puṇyasya saṃkhyā kartuṃ na vai kṣamaḥ ॥37॥
atastasya prabhāvena vimānaṃ pracalenmama ॥38॥ 
iti śrī gaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe saṃkaṣṭacaturthīvratakathanaṃ nāmāṣṭapaṃcāśattamo'dhyāyaḥ॥58 ॥

<adhyāya 59 prāraṃbhaḥ>

rājovāca |
kuṃbhīpākādviniḥsṛtya teṣu yāteṣu vai divam|
kamupāyaṃ sa kṛtavān kṛtavīryapitā tadā ॥1॥
śakra uvāca |
brahmalokaṃ jagāmāśu dadarśa kamalāsanam|
duḥkhito nāradācchrutvā vaṃśavicchedamātmanaḥ ॥2॥
praṇamya tamapṛcchatsa vaṃśaviccheda kāraṇam |
mama putro'tidharmātmā vadānyo yajñakārakaḥ ॥3॥
devatātithibhaktaśca mānya mānayitā bhṛśam |
nānāvidhaprayatnaṃ sa putrārthaṃ kṛtavān vidhe ॥4॥
tathāpi nābhavatkasmāttasya putraḥ sureśvara |
rājyaṃ prakṛtisātkṛtvā vāyubhakṣaḥ sthito vane ॥5॥
asthimātrāvaśeṣo'sāvadya śvo vā mariṣyati |
janmāṃtarīyaṃ tasyaino yena gacchellayaṃ prabho ॥6॥
tamupāyaṃ dayāṃ kṛtvā vada me kamalāsana |
taṃ prāpayiṣye tatrāhaṃ svavaṃśasya vivṛddhaye ॥7॥
evaṃ sulalitāṃ vāṇīṃ śrutvocāca tathā vidhiḥ |
śrūyatāṃ tava putrasya pūrvajanma mayeritam ॥8॥
sāmanāmnā bhavetpūrvaṃ tatraiva nagare'ntyajaḥ |
duṣkarmā'titarāṃ yasya darśanaṃ puṇyanāśakṛt ॥9॥
ekadā tena vicaśya lobhena pathi ghātitāḥ |
dvādaśa brāhmaṇāḥ śāntāstyaktāstena guhāṃtare ॥10॥
sarvaṃ tadīyamādāya niśi svagṛhamāgataḥ |
māghakṛṣṇacaturthyāṃ sa udaye śaśino nṛpa ॥11॥
gaṇeśeti gaṇeśeti putramāhūya satvaram |
alabdhānnajalastena sahaiva bubhuje mudā ॥12॥
kālena tava putro'sau paṃcatvamagamanniśi |
candrodaye caturthyāṃ tu kṛṣṇāyāmeva bhūbhuja ॥13॥
ajñānakṛta saṃkaṣṭacaturthīvrata saṃbhavāt |
puṇyādvināyakaṃ dhāma jagāma sukhadaṃ tu saḥ ॥14॥
vimānavaramārūḍho vīkṣyamāṇopsaro gaṇaiḥ |
vaimānikaiḥ stūyamāno divyapuṣpairathārcitaḥ ॥15॥
tenaiva puṇyaśeṣeṇa kṛtavīryābhidho nṛpaḥ |
tavaputra samāpede putratvamadhunā bhuvi ॥16॥
tadaghaughakṣayāttasya putra utpatsyate'nagha |
iti brahmavacaḥ śrutvā cakampe sa nṛpottama ॥17॥
papracchu punarevāsāvupāyaṃ pāpanāśanam |
kṛtavīrya pitovāca |
brahmahatyā kṛtaṃ tasya pāpaṃ naśyetkathaṃ vidhe ॥18॥
tadvadasva dayāsiṃdho yadyapi syātsuduṣkaram |
brahmovāca |
sa cedvrataṃ tu saṃkaṣṭacaturthīsaṃjñakaṃ tava ॥19॥
sutaḥ kariṣyate samyaktadā pāpātpramokṣyate |
rājovāca |
kathaṃ tatkriyate brahmankasminmāse vrataṃ śubhe ॥20॥
tatsarvaṃ vada me svāminyena pāpaṃ layaṃ vrajet |
brahmovāca |
caturthī bhaumavāre tu māghe kṛṣṇe bhavedyadi ॥21॥
śubhe muhūrte candre ca kuryātprāraṃbhamasya ha |
dantadhāvanapūrvāṇi snānāni caikaviṃśatim | ॥22॥
kurvīta nityakarmāṇi japenmantraṃ tataḥ param |
nirāhāro bhavenmauni paranindāvivarjitaḥ |
duṣṭakarma ca tāṃbūlaṃ varjayenniyamena ca |
varjayejjalapāṇaṃ ca paradrohaṃ ca paiśunam ॥24॥
tilāmalakakalkena dinānte snānamācaret |
ekākṣaraṃ śaḍarṇaṃ vā japedvā vaidikaṃ mamum ॥25॥
gaṇeśaprītaye tasya nāmamantraṃ yathāvidhi |
dhyāyet sthireṇa manasā devadevaṃ gajānanam ॥26॥
muhūrtamātreṇa tataḥ pūjayedgaṇanāyakam |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ naivedyairvividhairapi ॥27॥
modakāpūpa śaṣkulī laḍḍukairvaṭakairapi |
pāyasairvividhairannairvyaṃjanairlehyacoṣyakaiḥ ॥28॥
phalairnānāvidhaiḥ pūga tāṃbūlairdakṣiṇādibhiḥ |
ekaviṃśatidūrvābhirdīpaiśca kusumairapi ॥29॥
candrodaye'rghyadānena tithaye mantrapūrvakam |
gajānanāya paścāttu candrāya ca tataḥ param ॥30॥
nivedya pūjanaṃ natvā kṣamāpya ca tataḥ punaḥ |
brāhmaṇānbhojayedbhaktyā śaktyā vā caikaviṃśatim ॥31॥
daśa dvādaśa vā'śakto dakṣiṇābhiḥ sutoṣayet |
kathāṃ śrutvā tataḥ samyak svayaṃ bhuṃjīta vāgyataḥ ॥32॥
gītavāditraghoṣeṇa śeṣāṃ rātriṃ tato nayet |
evaṃ vrataṃ caikavarṣaṃ kṛtaṃ cedyatnato nṛpa ॥33॥
sarvapāpa kṣayāttasya bhavitā putra uttamaḥ |
anyaṃ vā cintayetkāmaṃ yaṃ yaṃ taṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ ॥34॥
sarvasaṃkaṭanāśaḥ syāt paracakrād bhayaṃ na hi |
śamīmūle japaṃstiṣṭhannupavāsa parāyaṇaḥ ॥35॥
ācandrodayaparyantaṃ vratametatsamācaret |
andho mūko jaḍaḥ paṃgustadīpsitamavāpnuyāt ॥36॥
dārān putrān dhanaṃ rājyaṃ labhate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
śravaṇādiṣu māseṣu ghṛtalaḍḍavādikaṃ pṛthak ॥37॥
bhakṣeyadvarṣaparyantaṃ tasya siddhiranuttamā |
śrāvaṇe sapta laḍḍūkā nabhasye dadhibhakṣaṇam ॥38
āśvine copavāsaṃ ca kārtike dugdhapānakam |
mārgaśīrṣe nirāhāraṃ pauṣe gomūtrapānakam ॥39॥
tilāṃ ca bhakṣayenmāghe phālgune ghṛtaśarkarām |
caitramāse paṃcagavyaṃ vaiśākhe śatapatrikām ॥40॥
ghṛtasya bhojanaṃ jyeṣṭhe āṣāḍhe madhubhakṣaṇam |
kṛtavīryapitovāca |
aṃgārakacaturthyāṃ tu viśeṣo'bhihitaḥ kutaḥ ॥41॥
vada tvaṃ kṛpayā brahman praśrayāvanatāya me |
śṛṇvato na ca me tṛptirgajānana kathāṃ śubhām ॥42॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe caturthīvratakathanaṃ nāmaikonaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥59॥

<adhyāya 60 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca |
aṃgārakacaturthyāstu mahimānaṃ mahīpate |
śṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā kathayāmi samāsataḥ ॥1॥
avantī nagare rājan bhāradvājo mahāmuniḥ |
vedavedāṃgavitprājñaḥ sarvaśāstraviśāradaḥ ॥2॥
agnihotrarato nityaṃ śiṣyādhyayanatatparaḥ |
nadītīragatastiṣṭhannanuṣṭhānarato muniḥ ॥3॥
ekasminneva samaye dṛṣṭā tenāpsarovarā |
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā cakame bhoktuṃ bhāradvājo mahāmuniḥ ॥4॥
akasmāt kāminīṃ dṛṣṭvā kāmāsakto'bhavanmuniḥ |
kāmabāṇābhibhūtaḥ sa nipapāta mahītale ॥5॥
ativihvalagātrasya tasya reta pracaskhale |
praviṣṭaṃ tasya tadretaḥ pṛthivīṃ bilamadhyataḥ ॥6॥
tata ekaḥ kumāro'bhūjjapākusuma sannibhaḥ |
taṃ dharitrī snehavaśāt pālayāmāsa sādaram ॥7॥
januḥ svaṃ tena dhanyaṃ sā manute pitarau kulam |
tataḥ sa saptavarṣastāṃ papraccha jananīṃ nijām ॥8॥
mayi lohitimā kasmānmānuṣaṃ dehamāsthite |
kaśca me janako mātastanmamācakṣva sāṃpratam ॥9॥
dharaṇyuvāca |
bhāradvājamunerretaḥ skhalitaṃ mayi saṃgatam |
tato jāto'si re putra vardhito'si mayā śubham ॥10॥
sa uvāca |
tarhi taṃ me muniṃ mātadarśayasva taponidhim |
brahmovāca |
tamādāya tadā devī bhāradvājaṃ jagāma kuḥ| ॥11॥
uvāca praṇipatyainaṃ tvadvīryaprasavaṃ sutam |
vardhitaṃ taṃ purodhārya svīkuruṣva mune'dhunā ॥12॥
tadājñayā yayau dhātrī svadhāma ruciraṃ tadā |
bhāradvājaḥ sutaṃ labdhvā mumude cāliliṃga tam ॥13॥
āghrāya śira utsaṃge sthāpayāmāsa taṃ mudā |
sumuhūrte śubhe lagne cakāropanayaṃ muniḥ ॥14॥
vedaśāstrāṇyupādiśya gaṇeśasya manuṃ śubham |
uvāca kurvanuṣṭhānaṃ gaṇeśaprītaye ciram ॥15॥
saṃtuṣṭo dāsyate kāmānsarvāṃstava manogatān |
tataḥ sa narmadātāre padmāsanagato muniḥ ॥16॥
saṃniyamyendriyāṇyāśu dhyāyanheraṃbamantarā |
jajāpa paramaṃ mantraṃ vāyubhakṣo bhṛśaṃ kṛśaḥ ॥17॥
evaṃ varṣasahasraṃ sa tapastepe sudāruṇam |
māghakṛṣṇacaturthyāṃtamudaye śaśino'male ॥18॥
darśayāmāsa rūpaṃ svaṃ gaṇanātho'tha digbhujam |
divyāṃbaraṃ bhālacandraṃ nānāyudhalasatkaram ॥19॥
cāruśuṃḍaṃ lasaddantaṃ śūpakarṇaṃ sakuṃḍalam |
sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ nānālaṃkāramaṃḍitam ॥20॥
dadarśa rūpaṃ devasya sa bālaḥ purataḥ sthitam |
utthāya praṇipatyainaṃ tuṣṭāva jagadīśvaram ॥21॥
bhauma uvāca |
namaste vighnanāśāya namaste vighnakāriṇe |
surāsurāṇāmīśāya sarvaśaktyupabarhiṇe ॥22॥
nirāmayāya nityāya nirguṇāya guṇacchide |
namo brahmavidāṃ śreṣṭha sthitisaṃhārakāriṇe ॥23॥
namaste jagadādhāra namastrailokya pālaka |
brahmādaye brahmavide brahmaṇe brahmarūpiṇe ॥24॥
lakṣyālakṣya svarūpāya durlakṣaṇabhide namaḥ |
namaḥ śrīgaṇanāthāya pareśāya namo namaḥ |
iti stutaḥ prasannātmā paramātmā gajānanaḥ ॥25॥
uvāca ślakṣṇayā vācā bālakaṃ saṃpraharṣayan |
gajānana uvāca |
tavogratapasā tuṣṭo bhaktyā stutyā'nayā'pi ca ॥26॥
bālabhāve'pi dhairyācca dadāmi vāṃchitān varān |
evamukto bhūmiputro vaca ūce gajānanam ॥27॥
bhauma uvāca |
dhanyā dṛṣṭirjananamapi me darśanācca sureśa |
dhanyaṃ jñānaṃ kulamapi vibho bhūḥ saśailā'dya dhanyā |
dhanyaṃ caitatsakalamapi tapo yena dṛṣṭo'khileśo |
dhanyā vāṇī vasatirapi yayā saṃstuto mūḍhabhāvāt ॥28॥
yadi tuṣṭo'si deveśa svarge bhavatu me sthitiḥ |
amṛtaṃ pātumicchāmi devaiḥ saha gajānana ॥29॥
kalyāṇakāri me nāma khyātimetu jagattraye |
darśanaṃ me caturthyāṃ te jātaṃ puṇyapradaṃ vibho ॥30॥
ataḥ sā puṇyadā nityaṃ sarvasaṃkaṣṭahāriṇī |
kāmadā vratakartṝṇāṃ tvatprasādāt sadā'stu ca ॥31॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
amṛtaṃ pāsyase samyag devaiḥ saha dharāsuta |
maṃgaleti ca nāmnā tvaṃ loke khyātiṃ gamiṣyasi ॥32॥
aṃgāraketi raktatvādvasumatyā yataḥ sutaḥ |
aṃgārakacaturthīṃ ye kariṣyanti narā bhuvi ॥33॥
teṣāmabdasamaṃ puṇyaṃ saṃkaṣṭīvratasaṃbhavam |
nirvighnatā sarvakārye bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ ॥34॥
avantīnagare rājā bhaviṣyasi parantapa |
vratānāmuttamaṃ yasmātkṛtaṃ te vratamuttamam ॥35॥
yasya saṃkīrtanānmartyaḥ sarvān kāmānavāpnuyāt |
brahmovāca |
iti datvā varo devaḥ pidadhe dviradānanaḥ | ॥36॥
tatastu maṃgale devaṃ sthāpayāmāsa bhaktitaḥ |
śuṃḍāmukhaṃ daśabhujaṃ sarvāvayavasuṃdaram ॥37॥
prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa gajānanamudāvaham |
saṃjñā maṃgalamūrtīti devadevasya so'karot || 38॥
tato'bhavat kāmadātṛ kṣetraṃ sarvajanasya tat |
anuṣṭhānāt pūjanācca darśanāt sarvamokṣadam ॥ 39 ॥
tato vināyako devo vimānavaramuttamam |
preṣayāmāsa svagaṇairbhaumamānetumantikam ॥ 40 ॥
te gatvā tena dehena taṃ bhaumamānayan balāt |
gaṇeśasyāntikaṃ rājaṃstadbhutamivābhavat ॥41॥
tato bhaumo'bhavat khyātaḥ trailokye sacarācare |
yato bhaumena saṃkaṣṭacaturthī bhaumasaṃyutā ॥42॥
kṛtā prāptaṃ ca svargaṃ cāmṛtapānaṃ suraiḥ saha |
ataścāṃgārakayutā caturthī prathitā bhuvi ॥43॥
cintiārthapradānena cintāmaṇiriti prathām |
prayāto maṃgalamūrtiḥ sarvānugrahakārakaḥ ॥44॥
pārineṃrāttu nagarāt paścime prathito'bhavat |
cintāmaṇiriti khyītaḥ sarvavighna nivāraṇaḥ ॥45॥
adhunā siddhagaṃdharvai pūjyate sa vidhūdaye |
dadāti vāṃchitānarthān putrapautrādi saṃpada ॥46॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe' aṃgāraka caturthī vratopākhyāne ṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥60॥

<adhyāya 61 prāraṃbhaḥ>

brahmovāca |
ekadā'haṃ gato rājankailāsaṃ giriśālayam |
upaviṣṭaḥ sabhāmadhye'paśyannāradamāgatam ॥1॥
tenāpūrvaṃ phalaṃ caikaṃ śaṃkarāya niveditam |
yayāce gaṇapadastu kumāro'pi ca śambhave ॥2॥
kasmai deyaṃ phalamidamapṛcchacchaṃkaropi mām|
ahaṃ provāca balāya kumārāyeti taṃ tadā ॥3॥
dattaṃ śivena tasmai taccukrodha ca gajānanaḥ |
tato brahmā svabhavanaṃ gatvā sraṣṭumiyeṣa saḥ ॥4॥
tato vighnakaro vighnaṃ cakāra paramādbhutaṃ |
ugra rūpaṃ samāsthāya bhīṣayāmāsa māṃ tadā ॥5॥
mayi bhrānte'tha sa vidhurdṛṣṭvā krūrataraṃ tu tam |
ahasatparaṃ candra svagaṇai rdviradānamam ॥6॥
tataḥ paramakruddho'sāvaśapattaṃ vibhuṃ tadā |
adarśanīya trailokye madvanyātvaṃ bhaviṣyasi ॥7॥
kadācitkena dṛṣṭaḥ sa mahāpātakavānbhavet |
evaṃ śaptvā gato devo nijadhāma gaṇairvṛtaḥ ॥8॥
candrastu malino dīno līnaścintāmaye hṛde |
aṇimādi guṇopete jagatkāraṇakāraṇe ॥9॥
kathamācaritaṃ dauṣṭyamajñānād bālavanmayā |
adarśanīyaḥ sarveṣāṃ vivarṇo malino'bhavam ॥10॥
kathaṃ surūpo vandyaśca kalābhiḥ suratoṣakaḥ |
etasminnaṃtare devaiḥ śrutaḥ śāpo mahānvidhoḥ ॥11॥
tatreyurvahni śakrādyā yatrāste'sau gajānanaḥ |
vijijñapuḥ suroghāste vighnakāraṇakāriṇe ॥12॥
devā ūcuḥ |
tvaṃ devadeva jagatāmasi vandanīya, stvaṃ pāsi haṃsi vidadhāsi yatheṣṭamīśa |
tvaṃ nirguṇo'pi guṇakṛd guṇināṃ tryadhīśa, tvāmeva deva śaraṇaṃ vayamadya yātāḥ ॥13॥
pāhīśa no'khilajagattava śāpabhītaṃ, candre'parādhini kathaṃ patitaṃ sukaṣṭam |
tasmād vayaṃ ca jagadīśa cagaccacandraḥ, śarmāpnuyāma bhuvaneśa tathāvidhehi ॥14॥
kaṣṭe nipatitaṃ viśvamadṛśye śaśini prabho|
anugrahaṃ ca candre'smiṃstrailokye kartumarhasi ॥15॥
nājñāsīdyasya te rūpaṃ mahimānaṃ śrutitrayam |
sa kasya stavanīyaḥ syām̐ ttathāpi stūyave'khilaiḥ ॥16॥
kṛtakṛtyā vayaṃ te'dya darśanādbhāṣaṇādapi |
śaraṇaṃ te prapannāḥ sma prapannārti harāvyaḥya ॥17॥
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā jagāda dviradānanaḥ |
suprasannaḥ stavaisteṣāṃ dharmakāmārthamokṣadaḥ ॥18॥
vikaṭa uvāca |
prasanno'haṃ surāḥ stutyā vṛṇudhvaṃ vāṃchitaṃ mahat |
asādhyamapi vo dadyāṃ triṣu lokeṣu yadbhavet ॥19॥
devā ūcuḥ |
sudhākare'nugrahaṃ te sarvaṃ īhāmahe vayam |
anugṛhīte tasmiṃstu sarveṣāṃ no graho bhavetaṃ ॥20॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
ekābdaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhārdhamathāpi vā|
adarśanīyo'stu śaśī brūtānyaṃ vā varaṃ surāḥ ॥21॥
tataste daṃḍavatsarve praṇemurdviradānanam |
uvāca praṇatāṃstāṃstu vikaṭo bhāvapūrvakam ॥22॥
apramāṇaṃ kathamaho vacanaṃ kartumudyataḥ |
śaraṇaṃ ca prapannānāṃ na tyāgaṃ kartumutsahe ॥23॥
meruścalet patet sūryo vahniḥ śīto bhavedyadi |
maryādāmutsṛjet siṃdhurvaco me nānṛtaṃ bhavet ॥24॥
athāpi gūḍhato vākyaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ surasattamaḥ |
bhādraśuklacaturthyāṃ yo jñānato'jñānato'pi vā ॥25॥
abhiśāpī bhaveccandradarśanād bhṛśaduḥkhabhāk |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā devā mumudire'khilāḥ ॥26॥
omiti praṇipatyocuḥ puṣpavṛṣṭiṃ ca cikṣipuḥ |
tadanujñāṃ gṛhītveyurdevāścandramasaṃ prati ॥27॥
tamūcustvaṃ mūḍhataro yo'hasad dviradānanam |
trailokyaṃ saṃkaṭe kṣiptaṃ tvayā śreṣṭhāparādhinā ॥28॥
trailokyanāyake deve jagat traya vidhāyini |
avyaye nirguṇe nitye parabrahma svarūpiṇi ॥29॥
gajānane'khilagurāvaparādhaṃ yato'caraḥ |
evaṃ niyamitaṃ tena sarvalokahitepsunā ॥30॥
asmābhiḥ paramakleśāt prasanno'sau kṛto vibhuḥ |
bhādrapadacaturthyāṃ tvaṃ na draṣṭavyaḥ kadācana ॥31॥
tvaṃ cāpi śaraṇaṃ yāhi devadevaṃ gajānanam|
tatprasādācchuddhatanuḥ parāṃ khyātimavāpsyasi ॥32॥
iti candro vacaḥ śrutvā devānāṃ hitamātmanaḥ |
jagāma śaraṇaṃ devaṃ śaraṇāgatavatsalam ॥33॥
gajānanaṃ sureśānaṃ maheśānādivanditam |
jajāpa paramaṃ mantramekākṣaramaghāpaham ॥34॥
vidhurindopadiṣṭaṃ sa parameṇa samādhinā|
daśa dvādaśa varṣāṇi tapastepe sudāruṇam ॥35॥
gaṃgāyā dakṣiṇe tīre sarvasiddhipradāyini |
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān puro yāto gajānanaḥ ॥36॥
raktamālyāṃbaradharo raktacaṃdanacarcitaḥ |
caturbhujo mahākāyaḥ sindūrāruṇavigrahaḥ ॥37॥
prabhayā bhāsayaṃllokān koṭisūryādhika prabhāḥ |
tejaḥ samūhaṃ dṛṣṭvā taṃ pracakaṃpe bhṛśaṃ śaśī ॥38॥
paramaṃ dhairyamāsthāya kṛtāṃjalipuraḥ puraḥ |
tarkayāmāsa manasā prāyo'yaṃ dviradānanaḥ ||39॥
varaṃ dātumihāyāta iti matvā nanāma tam |
tuṣṭāva parayā bhaktyā devadevaṃ gajānanam ॥40॥
candra uvāca |
namāmi devaṃ dviradānanaṃ taṃ, yaḥ sarvavighnaṃ harate janānām |
dharmārthakāmāṃstanute'khilāmāṃ, tasmai namo vighnavināśanāya ॥41॥
kṛpānidhe brahmamayāya deva, viśvātmane viśvavidhāna dakṣa |
viśvasya bījāya jaganmayāya, trailokyasaṃhārakṛte namaste ॥42॥
trayīmayāyākhilabuddhidātre, buddhipradīpāya surādhipāya |
nityāya satyāya ca nityabuddhe, nityaṃ nirīhāya namo'stu nityam ॥43॥
ajñānadoṣeṇa kṛto'parādhastaṃ kṣantumarho'si dayākarastvam |
tavāpi doṣaḥ śaraṇāgatasya, tyāge mahātman kuru me'nukaṃpām ॥44॥
brahmovāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā suprasanno gajānanaḥ |
tasmai varāndadau devaḥ stutyā natyā sutoṣitaḥ ॥45॥
yathāpūrvaṃ sthitaṃ rūpaṃ tathā tatte bhaviṣyati |
bhādraśuklacaturthyāṃ tvāṃ yo naraḥ saṃprapaśyati ॥46॥
tasyābhiśāpo nūnaṃ syātpāpaṃ hāniśca mūrkhatā |
tasyāmadarśanīyo'si yaduktaṃ me suraiḥ saha ॥47॥
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturthyāṃ tu vrataṃ yatkriyate naraiḥ |
tavodayehaṃ pūjyastvaṃ pūjanīyaḥ prayatnataḥ ॥48॥
darśanīyaḥ prayatnena viparīte vrataṃ vṛthā|
lalāṭe kalayā tiṣṭha mama prītikaraḥ śaśin ॥49॥
pratimāsaṃ dvitīyāyāṃ namasyaśca bhaviṣyasi |
evaṃ labdhavaraścandro yathāpūrvo'bhavattadā ॥50॥
sthāpayāmāsa varada mūrti devarṣibhiḥ saha |
bhālacandreti nāmāsya cakruste munayaḥ surāḥ ॥51॥
prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa kāṃcanaṃ ratnasaṃyutam |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pūjayāyāsa sādaram ॥52॥
pūjayitvā surāḥ sarvai munayaśca varāndaduḥ |
siddhikṣetramidaṃ loke vikhyātaṃ tu bhaviṣyati ॥53॥
anuṣṭhānavatāmatra sarvasiddhikaraṃ bhavet |
tataste deva munayo natvā taṃ dviradānanam ॥54॥
yayuḥ prasannamanasaḥ svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ mudā yutāḥ |
yāteṣu devamuniṣu devo'pi pidhade vapuḥ ॥55॥
antarhite bhālacandre candrau hṛṣṭamanā iva |
svaṃ dhāma pratipede vai svaṃ dhāma prayayau tadā ॥56॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe candraśāpānugrahavarṇanaṃ nāmaikaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥61॥

<adhyāya 62 prāraṃbhaḥ>

kṛtavīrya pitovāca |
caturthyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe tu bhakticandrodaye yataḥ |
kriyate tannimittaṃ yatpṛṣṭaṃ tanme nirūpitam ॥1॥
durvāṃkurārpaṇasyāpi śrotumicchāmi kāraṇam |
kimarthaṃ gaṇanāthasya priyā durvāṃkurā vada ॥2॥
brahmovāca |
hanta te kathayiṣyāmi durvāṃkurasamarpaṇe |
yatphalaṃ gaṇānāthasya śrūyantāṃ taṃ nṛpottama ॥3॥
jāmbeti nagarī khyātā deśe dakṣiṇato'bhavat||
kṣatriyaḥ sulabho'syāṃ tu guṇī dānī dhanī balī 4|॥
vivekyāsin mānyamānī śamī damaparāyaṇaḥ |
sarva śāstrārthatatvajñaḥ sarva vedārtha tatvavit ॥5॥
vikaṭe bhaktimānnityaṃ stutistotra parāyaṇaḥ |
tasya bhāryā sumudrā'sinnāmnā parama viśrutā ॥6॥
atyanta sundarī sādhvī svarūpāddhik kṛtāpsarāḥ |
deva viprātithiparā patyuścittānuvatinī ॥7॥
pativratānāṃ sarvāsāṃ mānyā śreṣṭhatamā nṛpa |
ekasmin divase tau tu dampatī snānanirmalau ॥8॥
upaviṣṭau purāṇārthaṃ parasparamanonugau |
yāvattāvat samāyāto brāhmaṇo madhusūdanaḥ |
bhikṣābhilāṣī satataṃ parameśvaracintakaḥ ॥9॥
kucailaśca daridratvāt sāmbaro'pi digaṃbaraḥ |
sulabhastaṃ tu dṛṣṭvaiva praṇanāma mudāyutaḥ ॥10॥
jahāsa cainaṃ sahasā mohāviṣṭo dvijottamam |
sa uvāca tataḥ śāpaṃ pratikṣubdho mahāmuniḥ ॥11॥
krodhasaṃraktanayanastrailokyaṃ pradahanniva |
halakarṣaṇa ko nityaṃ nityaṃ duḥkhasamanvitaḥ ॥12॥
vṛṣo bhava kubuddhe tvaṃ yo'hasadvivṛtai rdvijaiḥ |
bhartuḥ śāpaṃ pariśrutya sumudrā krodhamūrchitā ॥13॥
aśapattaṃ dvijaṃ ruṣṭā sarpiṇīva padāhatā |
yatastvayā'vivekena śāpo dattaḥ patermama ॥14॥
atastvaṃ cakrivānbhūtvā viṣṭāśī bhava kuddhina |
so'pi tāmaśapatkrūraṃ śrutvā śāpaṃ tadīritam ॥15॥
strītvāttvamaśapo yasmāttvaṃ cāṃḍālī bhaviṣyasi |
daridrā doṣabahulā viṇmūtrādā'śubhaṃkarī ॥16॥
evaṃ parasparaṃ śaptvā tyaktvā dehān sudurlabhān |
sulabho vṛṣabho jāto halakarṣaṇavānbhavat ॥17||
na viśrāṃtiḥ kṣaṇaṃ tasya vipraśāpāttadā'bhavat |
dvijo'pi rāsabheryonau jāto'sau madhusūdanaḥ ॥18॥
jātā sumudrā cāṇḍālī duṣṭā prāṇivihiṃsikā |
daridrā paiśācavatī viṇmūtrāśanatatparā ॥19॥
ati śuṣkaśarīrā sā danturā vikaṭānanā |
kadācidaṭamānā sā dakṣiṇe nagarasya tu ॥20॥
prāsādaṃ gaṇanāthasya dadarśa paramādbhutam |
nānāvṛkṣa latā jālairnānā pakṣigaṇairyutam ॥21॥
yatra yogīśvarā kecidanuṣṭhānaratāḥ sadā |
upāsakā gaṇeśasya niyamasthā vasanti hi ॥22॥
kecitkāmārthinaḥ kecit putra mokṣadhanārthinaḥ |
kadācidbhādramāsasya caturthyāṃ tu gṛhe gṛhe ॥23॥
tasminpure saṃpravṛtto gaṇanātha mahotsavaḥ |
ativṛṣṭiḥ pravṛttā ca mahāpralaya sūcikā ॥24॥
cāṃḍālī vṛṣṭibhītā sā yāti yadyanniśanam |
tatastato'khilajanaṃ nirastā tāḍanādibhiḥ ॥25॥
tato devālayaṃ yātā pāṇāvagniṃ pragṛhya sā|
tatrāpyatāḍayan kecidyogibhiste nirākṛtāḥ ॥26॥
prajvālya sā tṛṇairagnimuṣṇānyaṃgāṇi kurvatī |
akasmād vāyunā prāgādeko dūrvāṃkurastadā ॥27॥
patito gaṇanāthasya mastake daivayogataḥ |
sa rāsabhaḥ śīta mīto yāto devālayaṃ tadā ॥28॥
tadā vṛṣo lāṃgalāttu mukto daivātsamāyayau |
prāsādaṃ gaṇanāthasya bhāvino'rthasya gauravāt ॥29॥
ubhāvapi tṛṇaṃ tasyāścāṃḍālyāstau babhakṣatuḥ |
parisupte jane tatra tayoryuddhamavartata ॥30॥
lattābhyāṃ caiva śṛṃgābhyāṃ gajānanasamīpataḥ |
tayorāsyānnipatitau śuṃḍāyāṃ ca pade tadā ॥31॥
dūrvāṃkuro gaṇeśasya tutoṣa ca gajānana |
tataḥ sā yaṣṭimādāya devāntikamupāgamat ॥32॥
ahannatīṃ khara vṛṣau svayaṃ pūjāṃ babhakṣa ca |
khuraśabdaṃ tayoḥ śrutvā bubudhe nidrito janaḥ ॥33॥
bahiścakāra daṃḍena muṣṭikūrparaghātataḥ |
palāyanaparā sā'pi tāḍitā śarkarādibhiḥ॥34॥
cāṃḍālyā rāsabhasyāpi sparśaśaṃkākulairjanaiḥ|
snāpitastīrthatoyena mantritena gajānanaḥ|
pūjitaḥ parayā bhaktyā nānādravyairanekaśaḥ॥35॥
vṛṣa rāsabha cāṃḍālīratiduṣṭā janāḥ punaḥ|
tāḍayāmāsuravyagrā laguḍairjānubhistalaiḥ |
devadvāre ca pihite gatisteṣāṃ na vidyate ॥36॥
bhramatāṃ kraṃdatāṃ teṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ dāruṇaiḥ svaraiḥ|
manastu devadevasya vikaṭasya tadā hratam ॥37॥
eteṣāṃ tu prasaṃgena punaḥ pūjā mamābhavat |
etaiśca pūjitaścāhamekadūrvāṃkurādibhiḥ ॥38॥
pradakṣiṇā bahutarā kṛtā duṣṭeśu satsvapi |
bhādraśuklacaturthyāṃ me dūrvāmekāṃ samarpayet ॥39॥
sa me mānyaśca pūjyaśca yaśca kuryātpradakṣiṇām |
tasmādetāṃ vimānena svadhāma prāpayāmyaham ॥40॥
evaṃ vimānamapraiṣīt svagaṇairupalakṣitam |
svarūpadhāribhirdevo gandharvāpsarasāṃ gaṇaiḥ ॥41॥
yutaṃ vāditranicayaiḥ puṣpaiḥ parimalairapi |
divyabhogasamāyuktaṃ patākādhvajamaṃḍitam 42॥
tānādāya gaṇāste tu gajānanasvarūpiṇaḥ |
tasmin vimāne nikṣipya divyadehānmudāyutān ॥43॥
gajānanājñayā tasya dhāma saṃprāpayaṃ javāt |
paśyatāṃ sarvalokānāmāścaryamabhavadhṛdiḥ ॥44॥
eteṣāṃ pūrvapuṇyena gatireṣeti cābruvan |
tato yogīśvarāḥ kecittyaktvā dhyānaṃ gatā gaṇān ॥45॥
papracchu kathameteṣāṃ brūta puṇyā gatirūrdhvagā |
ati pātakināmeṣāṃ puṇyaleśo na vidyate ॥46॥
gatiḥ sudurlabhā kasmāt prāptā no gadatānaghāḥ |
tadevāśu cariṣyāma styaktvā'nuṣṭhānamātmanaḥ ॥47॥
asaṃkhyāto gataḥ kālo na devo dṛkpathaṃ gataḥ |
viraktānāṃ vāyubhujāmanuṣṭhānavatāṃ sadā ॥48॥
dhāmaprāptiḥ kadā naḥ syād gaṇeśasya vadantu naḥ ॥49॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe dūrvopākhyānaṃ nāma dviṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥62॥

<adhyāya 63 prāraṃbhaḥ>

gaṇā uvāca |
śṛṇudhvaṃ yoginaḥ sarve sthirīkṛtya manaścalam|
yanna śeṣo bhaved vaktuṃ samarthaścaturānanaḥ ॥1॥
tad vaktuṃ kaḥ samarthaḥ syādyathāśakti vadāma ca|
kurvanti stavanaṃ yasya sarvadā'jaśivādayaḥ ॥2॥
tasya ko gaṇanāthasya mahimānaṃ vadet sphuṭam |
itthaṃ tadīyāṃ līlāṃ vā tathāpi śṛṇutānaghāḥ ॥3॥
durvāṃkurāṇāṃ mahimā na jñāto munibhiḥ suraiḥ |
yajñairdānaistapobhiśca vratairhomai rna vāpyate ॥4॥
atrāpyudāharantīmamitihāsaṃ purātanam |
vāsavasya ca saṃvādaṃ nāradasya mahātmanaḥ ॥5॥
ekadā nārado'gacchadvāsavaṃ draṣṭumutsukaḥ |
pūjitā parayā bhaktyā kṛtāsana parigraham ॥6॥
muniṃ papraccha balahā dūrvāmāhātmyamādarāt |
indra uvāca |
kimarthaṃ devadevasya gaṇeśasya mahātmanaḥ |
viśeṣataḥ priyā brahman mahādūrvāṃkurā mune ॥7॥
muniruvāca |
kathayāmi yathā jñātaṃ dūrvāmāhātmyamuttamam|
sthāvare nagare pūrvaṃ kauṃḍinyo'bhūnmahāmuniḥ ॥8॥
upāsako gaṇeśasya tapobalasamanvitaḥ |
ramaṇīyatarastasya grāma dakṣiṇa bhāgataḥ ॥9॥
āśramaḥ sumahānāsīllatāvṛkṣa samanvitaḥ |
sarāṃsi phullapadmāni yatrāsan sumahāṃti ca ॥10॥
bhramarairupajuṣṭāni haṃsakāraṃḍakairapi |
cakravākairbakaiścaiva kacchapairjala kukkuṭaiḥ ॥11॥
sa tu dhyānaratastatra prārabhattapa utkaṭam |
puraḥ sthāpya mahāmūrti gaṇeśasya caturbhujām ॥12॥
suprasannāṃ suvaradāṃ durvāyuktāṃ supūjitām |
jajāpa paramaṃ mantraṃ ṣaḍarṇaṃ devatoṣakam ॥13॥
papraccha saṃśayāviṣṭā patnī nāmnā''śrayā'sya tam |
āśrayovāca |
svāmin gajānane deve dūrvāṃbhāraṃ dinedine ॥14॥
samarpayasi kasmāttvaṃ tṛṇaiḥ ko'pi na tuṣyati |
asti cet puṇyametena tanme tvaṃ kṛpayā vada ॥15॥
kauṃḍinya uvāca |
śṛṇu priye pravakṣyāmi dūrvāmāhātmyamuttamama |
dharmasya nagare pūrvamāsīdutsava uttamaḥ ॥16॥
sarve devā sagandharvā āhūtāścāpsaro gaṇāḥ |
siddhacāraṇa nāgāśca munayo yakṣa rākṣasāḥ ॥17॥
tilottamāyā nṛtyantyāḥ prāvāro nyapatat bhuvi |
dadarśa tasyāḥ sa yamaḥ kucau cārū bṛhattamau ॥18॥
abhavatkāmasaṃtapto'viśrānto nirapatrapaḥ |
iyeṣāliṃgituṃ tasyāścumbituṃ ca tadānanam ॥19॥
sadaso nirgatastasmāllajjayā'dhomukho yamaḥ |
gacchatastasya retaśca skhalitaṃ patitaṃ bhuvi ॥20॥
jvālāmālyabhavattasmātsa purūṣo vikṛtānanaḥ |
kurvandaṃṣṭrākhaṃ krūraṃ trāsayan bhuvanatrayam ॥21॥
dadāha pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ jaṭābhirgaganaṃ spṛśat |
cakampe tasya śabdena trilokī mānasaṃ bhṛśam ॥22॥
tadaiva viṣṇūmagamaste tu sarve sabhāsadaḥ |
stutiṃ nānāvidhāṃ kṛtvā nānāstotrairyathāmati ॥23॥
prārthayāmāsuravyagrāḥ sarvalokahitāya tam |
sa taiḥ sarvaiḥ samagamad gajānanamanāmayam ॥24॥
tasya nāśaṃ tato jñātvā tuṣṭuvuḥ sarva eva tam |
devāmunayaścocuḥ|
namo vighnasvarupāya namaste vighnahāriṇe ॥25॥
namaste sarvarūpāya sarvasākṣinnamo'stu te |
namo devāya mahate namaste jagadādaye ॥26॥
namaḥ kṛpānidhe tubhyaṃ jagatpālana hetave |
namaste pūrṇatamase sarvasaṃhārakāriṇe ॥27॥
namaste bhaktavarada sarvadātre namonamaḥ |
namaste'nanyaśaraṇa sarvakāmaprapūraka ॥28॥
namaste vedaviduṣe namaste vedakāriṇe |
kamanyaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāmaḥ ko nu naḥ syād bhayāpaha ॥29॥
akāla eva pralayaḥ kathaṃ labdho janairayam |
hā gajānana deveśa hā hā vighnaharāvyaya ॥30॥
sarveṣāṃ maraṇe prāpte kathamasmānupekṣase |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā karūṇābdhirgajānanaḥ ॥31॥
āvirāsītpurasteṣāṃ śiśurūpo'ribhītihā |
bibhrat kamalanayane śatacandranibhānanam ॥32॥
koṭisūryaprabhājālaḥ koṭikandarpajidvapuḥ |
kuṃdakuḍmalaśobhāṃji ddaśano'dharabiṃbajit ॥33॥
unnaso bhṛkuṭī cārunayanaḥ kaṃbukaṃṭhayuk |
viśālavakṣā jānuspṛg bhujadvayayuto balī ॥34॥
gaṃbhīra nābhi vilasadudarotilasatkaṭiḥ |
raṃbhāśobhā parispardhi gurū ruścāsajānuyuk ॥35॥
sucārū jaṃghā gulphaśrī vilasat pādapadmakaḥ |
nānālaṃkāra śobhāḍhyo mahārghavasanāvṛtaḥ ॥36॥
evaṃ devaṃ nirīkṣyaiva nagarasya puro bhuvi |
uttasthurdevamunayo jayaśabdapuraḥsaram ॥37॥
praṇemurdaṃḍavad bhūmau śakraṃ devagaṇā yathā |
devā ṛṣaya ūcuḥ |
ko bhavān kuta āyātaḥ kiṃ kāryaṃ vada no vibho ॥38॥
vayamevaṃ vijānīmo brahmaiva bālarūpadhṛka |
analāsura saṃtrāsāt tyaktvā karmāṇi saṃsthitān ॥39॥
āvirbhūtaṃ tu nastrātuṃ duṣṭasaṃhārakārakam |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā śiśurūpī gajānanaḥ ॥40॥
babhāṣe hāsyavadanaḥ sarvān devamunīn prati |
bāla uvāca |
bhavanto jñānasaṃpannā yaduktaṃ satyameva tat ॥41॥
ahaṃ tasya vadhāyaiva duṣṭasya parapīḍinaḥ |
nijecchayā bālarūpī vegenāgāṃ surarṣayaḥ ॥42॥
upāyaṃ vacmi vastasya vadhe taṃ kurutānaghāḥ |
sarvairbhavadbhistaṃ dṛṣṭvā nodanīyo balādaham ॥43॥
draṣṭavyaṃ kautukaṃ tasya mama caiva mahattaram |
evaṃ śrutvā kṛpāvākyaṃ sarve te harṣanirbharā ॥44॥
ucuḥ parasparaṃ sarve na jānīmo'sya pauruṣam |
īśvaro bālarūpeṇa kartṛmasya vadhaṃ nu kim ॥45॥
avatīrṇo bhavet trātuṃ pīḍitaṃ bhuvanatrayam |
itthamuktvā tu te sarve praṇemuḥ sādaraṃ ca tam ॥46॥
etasminneva kāle tu kālānalasvarūpadhṛk|
dahan daśa diśo bhakṣannaralokaṃ samāyayau ॥47॥
kolāhalo mahānāsīllokānāṃ krandatāṃ tadā |
dṛṣṭvaiva sarve munayaḥ palāyanaparā yayuḥ ॥48॥
taṃ ca te munayaḥ procuḥ śīghraṃ kuru palāyanam |
no ceddhiṃsiṣyate tvādya sumahānanalo dhruvam ॥49॥
timiṃgilo yathā mīnānuragān garūḍo yathā|
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā paramātmā gajānanaḥ ॥50॥
bālarūpadharo'tiṣṭhat parvato himavāniva|
surarṣayo yayurdūrāta tyaktvā tatraiva bālakam ॥51॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe durvāmāhātmye triṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥63॥

<adhyāya 64 prāraṃbhaḥ>

āśrayovāca |
devarṣiṣu prayāteṣu bāle cācalavatsthite |
kimāsīt kautukaṃ tatra bāla kālānalodbhavam ॥1॥
tatsarvaṃ vistarānmahyaṃ kathayāśu mahāmune |
nārada uvāca |
evaṃ tayā kṛtaḥ praśnaḥ kauṇḍinyo munisattamaḥ ॥2॥
yadabravīcchacībhartastattvaṃ śṛṇu mayoditam |
kauṃḍinya uvāca |
acale calavadvāle sthite tasmingajānane ॥3॥
kālānala ivākṣobhya āyayau so'nalāsuraḥ |
tasminkṣaṇe'calā vā'pi cacālācalasaṃyutā ॥4॥
namodadhvāna sadṛśa ghanagarjitaniḥsvanaiḥ |
nipeturvṛkṣaśākhābhyaḥ pakṣivṛndāni bhūtale ॥5॥
nirvārirvāridhirjāto vṛkṣā unmūlitāstadā |
prakampanena mahatā na prājñāyata kiṃcana ॥6॥
tasminneva kṣaṇe devo bālarūpī gajānanaḥ |
vadhārā nalarūpaṃ taṃ daityaṃ māyābalena hi ॥7॥
prāśatsarveṣu paśyatsu jaladhiṃ kumbhajo yathā|
tataḥ so'cintayaddevo yadyayaṃ jaṭhare gataḥ ॥8॥
dahet tribhuvanaṃ kukṣau dṛṣṭamāścaryamutkaṭam |
tataḥ śakro dadauṃ candraṃ tasya vahneḥ praśāntaye ॥9॥
bhālacandreti taṃ devāstuṣṭuvur munayo'pi ca |
tathāpi na ca śānto'bhūdanalaḥ kaṃṭhamūrdhvagaḥ ॥10॥
tato brahmā dadau siddhi buddhī mānasa kanyake |
rambhoru padmanayane keśaśaṃ vailasaṃyute ॥11॥
candravaktre'mṛtagirau kūpanābhī sarid balī|
mṛṇālamadhye pravāla haste śaṃtyasya kāraṇe ॥12॥
uvāce me samāliṃgya tava śānto'nalo bhaveta |
tayorāliṃgane śāntaḥ kiṃcideva hutāśanaḥ ॥13॥
dadau sukomalaṃ tasmai kamalaṃ kamalāpatiḥ |
padmapāṇiriti procustaṃ sarve suramānuṣāḥ ॥14॥
aśānte'gnau tu varuṇaḥ siṣeca śītalairjalaiḥ |
sahasraphaṇinaṃ nāgaṃ giriśo'smai dadāvatha ॥15॥
tena baddhodaro yasmād vyālabaddhodaro'bhavat |
tathāpi śaityaṃ nāpede kaṃṭho'syānalasaṃyutaḥ ॥16॥
aṣṭāśīti sahasrāṇi munayastaṃ prapedire |
amṛtā iva dūrvāste pratyekaṃ sekaviṃśatim ॥17॥
āropayan mastake'sya tataḥ śānto'nalo'bhavat|
tutoṣa paramātmā'sau durvāṃkurabharācitaḥ ॥18॥
evaṃ jñātvā tu te sarve pupūjastaṃ gajānanam |
durvāṃkurairanekaistai rcaharṣāṃsau gajānanaḥ ॥19॥
uvāca ca munīn devān matpūjā bhaktinirmitā|
mahatī svalpikā vāpi vṛthā durvāṃkuraivinā ॥20॥
vinā dūrvāṃkuraiḥ pūjā phalaṃ kenāpi nāpyate |
tasmāduṣasi mad bhaktairekāvā'pyekaviṃśatiḥ ॥21॥
bhaktyā samarpitā dūrvā dadāti yatphalaṃ mahat |
na tatkratuśatairdānairvratānuṣṭhāna saṃcayaiḥ ॥22॥
tapobhirugrairniyamaiḥ koṭijanmārjitairapi |
prāpyate munayo devā yad dūrvābhiravāpyate ॥23॥
kauṃḍinya uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā devā dūrvāṃkuraiḥ punaḥ |
ānarcuḥ paramātmānaṃ devadevaṃ gajānanam ॥24॥
jagarjānandayukto'sau nādayanrodasī bhṛśam |
hṛṣṭānāṃ sarvadevānāṃ munīnāṃ ca nṛṇāmapi ॥25॥
anekaśo varān datvā pidadhe bālarūpadhṛk |
kālānalapraśamana iti taṃ te samūcire ॥26॥
prāsāde nirmite sarve sthāpya mūrtiṃ gajānanīm |
vighnaharo'yamityasyābhidhāṃ cakrurmudā surāḥ ॥27॥
atra snānaṃ tathā dānaṃ tapo'nuṣṭhānameva ca |
anantaṃ jāyate vighnaṃ harasyāsya prasādataḥ ॥28॥
jayaḥ prāpto yatastena puraṃ ca vijayāṃbhidham |
sarveṣāṃ ca hatā vighnā vighnaharteti so'bhavat ॥29॥
kauṃḍinya uvāca |
iti te kathitaṃ sarvaṃ dūrvāmāhātmyamuttamama |
śravaṇāt paṭhanāccāsya sarvapāpakṣayo bhaveta ॥30॥
purātanamitihāsaṃ śṛṇu me gadataḥ priye ॥31॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe dūrvāmāhātmyavarṇanaṃ nāma catuḥṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥64॥

<adhyāya 65 prāraṃbhaḥ>

kauṇḍinya uvāca |
kasmiṃścitsamaye devi sukhāsīnaṃ gajānanam |
nārado munirabhyāgād dṛṣṭuṃ taṃ bahuvāsaraiḥ ॥1॥
sāṣṭāṃgapraṇipatyainaṃ prāha naḥ sārthakaṃ januḥ |
yatpuṇyaṃ nicayairjātaṃ darśanaṃ te gajānana ॥2॥
ityuktvā svāṃjaliṃ baddhvā tasthau tatpurato muniḥ |
dhṛtvā kareṇa tatpāṇimupaveśa yadāsane ॥3॥
gajānano mahābhāgo mahābhāgaṃ mahāmunim |
nārado bhagavāṃstena saṃtuṣṭo munipuṃgavaḥ 4
uvāca taṃ gaṇādhīśamāścaryaṃ hṛdi me'sti yat |
tanniveditumāyāto natvā tvāṃ punarāvraje ॥5॥
gajānana uvāca |
kimāścaryaṃ tvayā dṛṣṭaṃ hṛdi kiṃ te'bhivartate |
vada sarvaṃ viśeṣeṇa tato vraja nijāśramam ॥6॥
nārada uvāca |
maithile viṣaye deva janako rājasattamaḥ |
atimānī vadānyaśca vedavedāṃgapāragaḥ ॥7॥
annadānarato nityaṃ brāhmaṇān pūjayatyasau |
nānālaṃkāravāsobhirdakṣiṇābhiranekaśaḥ ॥8॥
dīnāndhakṛpaṇebhyaśca bahu dravyaṃ dadātyasau|
yācakairyācyate yadyattattattena pradīyate ॥9॥
tathāpi na vyayaṃ yāti dravyaṃ tasya mahātmanaḥ |
gajānanasya saṃtuṣṭyā dravyaṃ tad vardhate nu kim ॥10॥
ityāścaryaṃ mahaddṛṣṭuṃ prayātastad gṛhānaham |
brahmajñānābhimānena upahāsaṃ mamākarot ॥11॥
ahaṃ ca tamuvācetthaṃ dhanyo'si nṛpasattama |
cintitaṃ te'pi bhaktyā'yaṃ prayacchati gajānanaḥ ॥12॥
sa tu garvāduvācetthamahamīśo jagat traye|
ahaṃ dātā ca bhoktā ca pātā dāpayitā tathā ॥13॥
matsvarūpaṃ vinā nānyad vidyate bhuvanatraye |
kartā ca kāraṇaṃ cāhaṃ karaṇaṃ munisattama ॥14॥
nārada uvāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā krodhenāhaṃ jagāda tam |
īśvarājjagataḥ kartā nānyaḥ kaścana vidyate ॥15॥
tvaṃ tu dharmamimaṃ rājan dambhenaiva karoṣi kim |
darśayiṣye sākṣyamasya svalpakālena te'nagha ॥16॥
ityuktvā tamahaṃ yātastvadantikamibhānana |
kauṃḍinya uvāca |
ākarṇyetthaṃ munervākyaṃ pūjayāmāsa taṃ vibhuḥ ॥17॥
arghyādibhiralaṃkārairdivyaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sacaṃdanaiḥ |
munirājñāṃ pragṛhyaiva vaikuṃṭhe viṣṇumabhyagāt ॥18॥
gajānano'pi mithilāṃ rājabhaktiṃ parīkṣitum |
kutsitaṃ veṣamādāya sarvajño'pi samāyayau ॥19॥
anekakṣatasaṃyuktaṃ sravadraktamamaṃgalam |
makṣikānicayākrāṃtaṃ radahīnamivāturam ॥20॥
gacchaṃtaṃ tādṛśaṃ dṛṣṭvā narā nāsānirodhanam |
kurvanti vāsasā kecit ṣṭhīvanaṃ ca yathātathā ॥21॥
skhalan mūrchan patan gacchan arbhakāvali saṃyuta||
nṛpadvāraṃ samāgamya dvārapālānuvāca saḥ ॥22॥
rājñai nivedyatāṃ dūtā atithiṃ māṃ samāpatam |
brāhmaṇaṃ kṣudhitaṃ vṛddhamicchābhojanakāṃkṣiṇam ॥23॥
te tadvākyaṃ tathācaraṇyurgatvā taṃ janakaṃ nṛpam |
ānīyatāmiti prāha dūtā draṣṭuṃ tu kautukam ॥24॥
te dūtā prāpayāmāsuḥ kucailamalinaṃ nṛpam |
dadarśa dūrājjanako kampantaṃ makṣikāvṛtam ॥25॥
asṛk sravantaṃ vṛddhaṃ taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ śramavāriṇam |
tarkayāmāsa janaka īśvaro rūpadhṛṅnu kim ॥26॥
chalituṃ māṃ samāyāto yadi puṇyaṃ bhavenmama |
samādhāsye mano hyasya bhaviṣyaṃ nānyathā bhavet ॥27॥
ityevaṃ ciṃtayatyeva janake nṛpasattame |
praveśito dvārapālairbrāhmaṇaḥ paryadṛśyata ॥28॥
brāhmaṇa uvāca |
candrāṃśudhavalāṃ kīrtiṃ śrutvā te'haṃ samāgataḥ |
dehi me bhojanaṃ rājan kṣudhitasya cirād bhṛśam ॥29॥
mama tṛptirbhavedyāya tāvadannaṃ pradīyatām |
tavakratuśataṃ puṇyaṃ bhaviṣyati nareśvara ॥30॥
kauṃḍinya uvāca |
iti vācaṃ niśamyāsau gṛhamadhye nināya tam|
saṃpūjya vidhivaccainaṃ svādvannamupaveśayat ॥33॥
taddattaṃ puratastasya babhakṣa tatkṣaṇena saḥ |
asaṃkhyāteṣu pātreṣu paṃktuṃ kṣiptaḥ sutaṃdulāḥ ॥34॥
dīyate puratastasya yaḥ siddhaścodano'bhavat |
sa bhakṣayati sarvaṃ taṃ tata ūce jano nṛpam ॥35॥
rākṣaso'yaṃ bhavetprāyaḥ kimarthaṃ dīyate bahu |
rākṣasebhyaḥ pradānena na kiṃcit puṇyamāpyate ॥36॥
kecidūcustribhuvane bhakṣite'pyasya no bhavet |
tṛptiḥ paramikā rājan dhānyamasmai pradīyatām ॥37॥
tato dhānyāni sarvāṃṇi gṛhe bhūmau sthitāni ca |
ānīya cikṣipustasya puragrāmagatāni ca ॥38॥
puṃso'sya dvijarūpasya sarvabhakṣasya cātitheḥ |
na tṛptimagamatso'tha bhakṣiteṣu ca teṣu ca ॥39॥
tato dūtā nṛpaṃ procurdhānyaṃ kvāpi na labhyate|
iti dūtavacaḥ śrutvā janake'dhomukhe sthite ॥40॥
svastītyuktvāgamadvipro na tṛpto'sau gṛhaṃ gṛhaṃ|
dīyatāmanna mityāha te janāstaṃ jagustadā ॥41॥
sarveṣāṃ gṛhagaṃ dhānyaṃ sarvaṃ rājñā samāhṛtam |
jagdhaṃ tvayā'khilaṃ brahman gamyatāṃ yatra te ruciḥ ॥42॥
dvija uvāca |
kīrtirasya śrutā lokān na dātā janakātparaḥ |
tṛptikāmaḥ samāyāto hyatapto'haṃ kathaṃ vraje ॥43॥
tūṣṇīṃ bhūteṣu lokeṣu babhraman sa dadarśa ha |
virocanā triśirasormandiraṃ dvijayorvaram ॥44॥
tanmadhyaṃ prāviśatso hi gṛhasvāmīva sattayā |
sarvopaskārarahitaṃ dhātupātravivarjitam ॥45॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe paṃcaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥65॥

<adhyāya 66 prāraṃbhaḥ>

kauṃḍinya uvāca |
dharāmātrāsano tau tu nabhaḥprāvārasaṃyutaiḥ|
digambaro sarvadhātu saṃsparśavarjitāvubhau ॥1॥
ayācitabhujau nityaṃ jalenaivākhilāḥ kriyāḥ |
dvijarūpadharo'paśyat kurvāṇau satvaśuddhaye||2॥
gṛhaṃ ca mākṣikāpuṃjairmaśakairabhito vṛtam |
mūrtiṃ ca gaṇanāthasya pūjitāṃ puṣpapallavaiḥ ॥3॥
ananyabhaktyā tābhyāṃ ca tatparābhyāṃ dadarśa saḥ |
tāvūce śrūyatāṃ vākyaṃ yanmayā procyate'nayoḥ ॥4॥
mithiladhipateḥ kīrtī śrutvāhaṃ kṣudhito bhṛśam |
tṛptikāmaḥ samāyāto na sa tṛptiṃ samākarot ॥5॥
karmaṇā dāṃbhikenaiva satvaṃ na parirakṣyate |
mama tṛptikaraṃ kiṃcid gṛhe cedasti dīyatām ॥6॥
dampatī ūcatuḥ |
cakravartī nṛpo yo'sau tena tṛptirna te kṛtā |
āvābhyāṃ tu daridrābhyāṃ ki deyaṃ tṛptikārakam ॥7॥
nadī nada jalairabdhirasaṃkhyairyo na pūryate |
bindumātreṇa payasā sa kathaṃ pūryate vada ॥8॥
dvija uvāca |
bhaktyā dattaṃ svalpamapi bahutṛptikaraṃ mama |
abhaktyā vā'tha dambhena bahu dattaṃ vṛthā bhavet ॥9॥
tāvūcatuḥ |
āvayorna gṛhe kicicchapathaste dvijottama |
pūjāyai gaṇanāthasya prātardūrvāṃkurā hṛtaḥ ॥10॥
pūjito gaṇanāthastaistata eko'vaśiṣyate |
dvija uvāca |
bhaktyā dattaḥ sa eko'pi tṛptaye syāt pradīyatām ॥11॥
kauṃḍinya uvāca |
virocanā dadau tasmai śrutvā vākyaṃ tadīritam |
ekaṃ dūrvāṃkuraṃ bhaktyā tena tṛpto'bhavaddvijaḥ ॥12॥
śālyannaṃ pāyasānnaṃ ca nānā pakvānnameva ca |
vyaṃjanāni ca sarvāṇi lehyacoṣyānyanekadhā ॥13॥
virocanā dadau kalpya tasmin dūrvāṃkure ca tam |
gṛhītvā brāhmaṇastaṃ tu babhakṣa parayā mudā ॥14॥
tasmin dūrvāṃkure bhaktyā datte te nātha bhaktitaḥ |
praśaśāma dvijasyāsya tatkṣaṇājjaṭharānalaḥ ॥5॥
tṛptiśca paramā tena prāptā tat kṣaṇamātrataḥ |
āliliṃga triśirasaṃ tṛpto harṣād dvijastadā ॥16॥
tatyāja kutsitaṃ rūpaṃ prakaṭo'bhūd gajānanaḥ |
caturbhujo'ravindākṣaḥ śuṃḍādaṃḍa virājitaḥ ॥17॥
kamalaṃ paraśuṃ mālāṃ dantaṃ karatalairdadhat |
mahārha mukuṭo rājatyarṇakuṃḍala maṃḍitaḥ ॥18॥
divyavastra parīdhāno divyagandhānulepanaḥ |
uvāca tau prasannātmā dampatī sa gajānanaḥ ॥19॥
vṛṇītaṃ taṃ varaṃ śīghraṃ manasā yaṃ yamicchathaḥ |
tāvūcatuḥ |
janmanī yatra nau syātāṃ tatra te dṛḍhabhaktitā ॥20॥
muktirvā dīyatāṃ deva dustarād bhavasāgarāt |
na cāvāṃ cittayoranyarapekṣitamibhānana ॥21॥
kauṃḍiṇya uvāca |
iti tadvākyamākarṇya tathetyuktvā gajānanaḥ |
punarāliṃgya pidadhe bhaktaṃ triśirasaṃ mudā ॥22॥
etasmātkāraṇād dūrvābhāro'smai dīyate mayā |
asaṃkhya bhakṣaṇādyo na tṛptiṃ devaḥ samāyayau ॥23॥
dūrvāṃkureṇa caikena sa tṛptiṃ paramāṃ yayau|
iti te kathitaḥ samyagāśraye mahimā śubhaḥ|
dūrvāsamarpaṇabhavaḥ śravaṇāt sarvakāmadaḥ ॥24॥
itihāsamimaṃ bhaktyā śṛṇute śrāvayecca yaḥ|
sa putra dhanakāmāḍhyaḥ paratreha ca modate ॥25॥
gajānane labhed bhaktiṃ niṣkāmo muktimāpnuyāt |
gaṇā ūcuḥ |
śrutvāpītthamitīhāsamāśrayā saṃśayaṃ punaḥ ॥26॥
prapede hṛdi taṃ jñātvā kauṃḍinyaḥ punarabravīt |
āśraye śṛṇu me vākyaṃ saṃśayasyāpanuttaye ॥27॥
yadbravīmi hṛdisthasya mayā jñātasya te'naghe|
ekaṃ dūrvāṃkuraṃ gṛhaya gaccha śīghraṃ biḍaujasam ॥28॥
vadāśīrvacanaṃ pūrvaṃ paścādyācasva kāṃcanam |
dūrvāṃkureṇa tulitaṃ gṛhītvā sadihānaya ॥29॥
na nyūnaṃ nāghikaṃ grāhayaṃ tasya bhārācchubhānane ॥30॥
gaṇā ūcuḥ |
iti vāṇīṃ tasya śrutvā gṛhītvaikaṃ tadāśrayā |
dūrvāṃkuraṃ yayau śakraṃ bharturvākyaparāyaṇā ॥31॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe ṣaṭṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥66॥

<adhyāya 67 prāraṃbhaḥ>

gaṇā ūcuḥ |
ājñaptā tena muninā svābhipretārthasiddhaye |
eka dūrvāṃkuraṃ gṛhya śakrasannidhimāyayau ॥1॥
tamuvācāśrayā śakra dehi me kāṃcanaṃ śubham |
yācituṃ tvāṃ samāyātā bhartṛvākyāt sureśvara ॥2॥
indra uvāca |
kimarthaṃ tvamihāyātā yadyājñā preṣitā bhavet |
mayā saṃpreṣitaṃ syāce jātarūpaṃ svaśaktitaḥ ॥3॥
āśrayovāca |
dūrvāṃkurasya tulayā yad bhavet kāṃcanaṃ sura|
tad gṛhīṣye śacīrbharta nyūnaṃ na ca vādhikam ॥4 ॥
śakra uvāca |
dūtaināṃ naya śīghraṃ tvaṃ kuberabhavanaṃ prati |
sa dāsyati suvarṇaṃ ca dūrvāṃkuramitaṃ śubham ॥5॥
gaṇā ūcuḥ |
ājñayā devarājyasya devadūtastayā saha |
prāyāt kuberabhavanaṃ śakrasya vacanāttadā ॥6॥
asyai dūrvāṃkuramitaṃ jātarūpaṃ pradīyatām |
indreṇa preṣitā sādhvī munipatnī mayā saha |
prāpitā bhavanaṃ te'dya yāmi deva namo'stu te ॥7॥
kubera uvāca |
atyāścaryamahaṃ manye muniḥ śakrastathā''śrayā|
mohāviṣṭā na jānanti dūrvāṃkuramitaṃ kiyat ॥8॥
kāṃcanaṃ tena kiṃ vā syād bahulaṃ kiṃ na yācitam |
gaṇā ūcuḥ |
evameva dadau tasyai bahulaṃ kāṃcana saha ॥9॥
na jagrāha bhayād bharturnyūnādhika viśaṃkayā |
svarṇakāra tulāyāṃ taṃ dūrvāṃkuramadhārayat ॥10॥
nābhavattulayā tasya paryāptaṃ tattu hāṭakam |
vaṇik tulā samānītā tatrāpi nābhavat samam ॥11॥
tailakāratulāyāṃ tu dūrvāṃkura samaṃ na ca |
dhaṭobaddhastatastatra hāṭakaṃ dattamekataḥ ॥12॥
dūrvāṃkuro'paratrāpi yātipatramadhastadā |
anyadanyaddadhau tatra kuberaḥ kāṃcanaṃ bahu ॥13॥
taccāpi nābhavattena samaṃ dūrvāṃkureṇa ca |
sarva kośagataṃ dravyaṃ dattaṃ tena girīndravat ॥14॥
tathāpi nābhavatsāmyaṃ tena durvāṃkureṇa tat |
patnīmāhūya tāṃ prāha kuberaḥ kautukānvitaḥ ॥15॥
kuru madvākyataḥ subhru dhaṭārohaṇamagrataḥ |
na samaṃ cet samārokṣye nijasattvarirakṣayā ॥16॥
pativratā''jñayā tasya dhaṭamāruruhe tadā|
na samā sā'pi tenāsīttataḥ sarvāṃ purī dadau ॥17||
dhaṭamadhye kubero'sau na cordhvaṃ jāyateṃ'kuraḥ |
śrutvā dūtamukhādindro gajārūḍhaḥ samāyayau ॥18॥
svakīyadravyasahito dhaṭamāruruhe svayam |
dūrvāṃkuro na cordhvaṃ sa tathāpi samajāyata ॥19॥
adhomukho gataściṃtāṃ kimetaditi cintayan |
viṣṇuṃ haraṃ ca sasmāra tatrārohaṇakāmyayā ॥20॥
tatrāgatau sanagarau dhaṭamāruhatāṃ tadā |
tathāpi nordhvamagamattadā dūrvāṃkuraṃ sphuṭam ॥21॥
tataste tata utteruḥ śivaviṣṇudhaneśvarāḥ |
varuṇendrāgni marutaḥ kauṃḍinyamabhito yayuḥ ॥22॥
devā devarṣayaścaiva siddhavidyādharoragāḥ |
dinānte samanuprāpte svaṃ nīḍamiva pakṣiṇaḥ ॥23॥
namaskṛtya muniṃ sarve procurudvignacetasaḥ |
sarva ūcuḥ |
vṛjinaṃ vilayaṃ yātaṃ darśanāttava bho mune ॥24॥
pūrvapuṇyabhavādagre kalyāṇaṃ no bhaviṣyati |
tava patnyāhṛtaṃ sattvaṃ sarveṣāmadya naḥ sphuṭam ॥25॥
mahimānaṃ na jānīmo dūrvāṃkurasamudbhavam |
eka dūrvāṃkura tulāṃ trailokyamapi nālabhat ॥26॥
gajānana śirasthasya tvayā bhaktyā'rpitasya ca |
janīyān mahimānaṃ kaḥ samyag dūrvāṃkurasya ha|
yajñadāna vrata tapaḥ svādhyāyo'pi na tatsamaḥ ॥27॥
gajānanaikabhaktasya japatastapato'niśam |
tavāpi mahimānaṃ ko jānīyātsarvadehinaḥ ॥28॥
evamuktvā muniṃ sarve pūrvaṃ pūjya gajānanam |
sarve sabhārya pupūjustuṣṭuvurnanṛturjaguḥ ॥29॥
na brahmā na hariḥ śivendra maruto, nāgnirvivasvān yamaḥ |
śeṣo śeṣa kalānidhiśca varuṇo, no candramā nāśvino |
no vācāmadhipo na caiva garuḍo, no yakṣarāṇ nāṃgirā |
māhātmyaṃ pariveda deva nigamaiḥ rajñātarūpasya te ॥30॥
evaṃ saṃtoṣya sarve te devadevaṃ gajānanam |
muniṃ ca samanujñāpya yayuḥ svaṃ svaṃ niketanam ॥31॥
āśrayā'pi tato jñātvā dūrvāmahātmyamuttamam |
viśvasta bhartṛvākye sā dūrvābhiḥ pratyapūjayat ॥32॥
vighneśvaraṃ sarvadevaṃ sarvairdurvābhirarcitam |
praṇanāma ca kauṃḍinyaṃ bhartāraṃ satyavādinam ॥33॥
uvāca suprasannā sā svātmānaṃ nindatī bhṛśam |
āśrayovāca |
mādṛśī naiva dṛṣṭā'sti yāte vākye saṃśayā ॥34॥
viśeṣaviduṣāṃ svāmini viśeṣaviduṣā tvayā |
samyak kṛtaṃ mama vibho sarvabhūta dayāvatā ॥35॥
tatkṣamasvāparādhaṃ me tvāmahaṃ śaraṇāgatā |
tataḥ prātaḥ samutthāya dūrvā ādāya satvarau||36॥
snātvā devaṃ samabhyarcya dūrvārpaṇamakurvatām |
ananyabhaktyā jñātvā tau dūrvāmāhātmyamuttamam ॥37॥
sāyaṃprātardevadevaṃ pūjayantau nirantaram |
tyaktvā yajñavrataṃ dānaṃ jñātvā devo gajānanaḥ ॥38॥
kṛpayā parayāviṣṭaḥ svadhāma pratyapādayat |
gaṇā ūcuḥ |
agādhaṃ varṇitaṃ dūrvāmāhātmyamidamuttamam ॥39॥
aśeṣa varṇane śeṣo neśo neśo harīśvarau|
trailokyaṃ tulayā yasyāḥ patreṇaivā'bhavatsamam ॥40॥
dūrveti smaraṇāt pāpaṃ trividhaṃ vilayaṃ vrajet |
tat smṛto smaryate devo yataḥ so'pi gajānanaḥ ॥41॥
iti ciṃtāmaṇi kṣetramahimā varṇitaḥ sphuṭam |
śravaṇāt kīrtanāt dhyānād bhuktimuktiphalapradaḥ ॥42॥
etasmāt kāraṇādyānaṃ trayāṇāṃ preṣitaṃ śubham |
rāsabhasya mukhād dūrvā gatā deve vṛṣasya ca ॥43॥
cāṃḍālyā śītanāśāya tvānītā tṛṇabhārataḥ |
vāyunā prabalenāpi gatā dūrvāṃ gajānane ॥44॥
yatastasya priyā dūrvā saṃtuṣṭo'sau vināyakaḥ |
niṣpāpatvāt trayāṇāṃ sa sānnidhyaṃ dattavānnijam ॥45॥
gandhamātreṇa dūrvāyāḥ saṃtuṣṭo jāyate vibhuḥ |
prasaṃgena tu bhaktyā vā kiṃ punarmastakārpaṇāt ॥46॥
brahmovāca |
iti dūtamukhādrājñā sa śruto mahimā tadā |
dūrvāyā munibhiḥ sarvairna dṛṣṭo na ca saṃśrutaḥ ॥47॥
snātvā dūrvāṃkurān gṛhya pupūjuste vināyakam |
sevakāścāpi dūrvābhirānarcuḥ śrīgajānanam ||48॥
āsansarve divyadehāstejasā sūryavarcasaḥ |
śṛṇvanto devavādyānāṃ nānārāvān samantataḥ ॥49॥
vimānavaramārūḍhā divyavastrānulepinaḥ |
yātā vaināyakaṃ dhāma kecittadrūpadhāriṇaḥ ॥50॥
narā nāgarikāḥ kecidāgatāstaṃ mahotsavam |
draṣṭuṃ dūrvābhirānarcurekaviṃśatibhiḥ pṛthaka ॥51॥
bhuktvā bhogāṃśca te sarve gāṇeśasthānamāgaman |
vimānaṃ cāpi calitamūrdhvaṃ tat puṇyapuṃjataḥ ॥52॥
tasmād gaṇeśa bhaktena kāryaṃ dūrvābhirarcanam |
na karoti naro yastu pramādāttābhirarjanam ॥53॥
sa cāṃḍālastu vijñeyo narakān prāpnuyāda bahūn |
na tanmukhaṃ nirīkṣeta kadācidapi mānava ॥54॥
yastu dūrvābhirarcettaṃ devadevaṃ gajānanam |
tasya darśanato'nyo'pi pāpī śuddhimavāpnuyāt ॥55॥
alābhe bahu dūrvāṇāmekayaivābhi pūjayet |
tenāpi koṭiguṇitā kṛtā pūjā na saṃśayaḥ ॥56॥
brahmovāca |
iti nānāvidho rājan mahimā kathitastava|
setihāsastu dūrvāṇāṃ śravaṇātpāpanāśanaḥ ॥57॥
nākhyeyo duṣṭabuddhestu priye putre nivedayet |
indra uvāca |
iti brahmamukhācchutvā paramākhyānamuttamama ॥58॥
nananda paramaprīto nanāma kamalāsanama |
tadājñayā yayau sthānaṃ svakīyaṃ vismayānvitaḥ ॥59॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe dūrvāmāhātmye saptaṣaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥67॥

<adhyāya 68 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śūrasena uvāca |
anyāṃ kathaya śakra tvaṃ gaṇanātha kathāṃ punaḥ |
kṛtavīryasya janakaḥ kiṃ cakāra tataḥ param ॥1॥
indra uvāca |
śrutvā'khyānaṃ tu saṃkaṣṭacaturthyāṃ rājasattamaḥ |
dūrvākhyānaṃ tu saṃśrutvā cintāviṣṭo nṛpo'bhavat ॥2॥
aputrasya gatirnāsti putro me bhavitā katham |
svapne dadarśa pitaraṃ kārtavīryastathā vidhiḥ ॥3॥
sagadgadābhyāṃ kaṃṭhābhyāṃ nocatustau parasparam |
āliliṃgaturanyonyaṃ premavihvala cetasā ॥4॥
tataḥ putraṃ kare dhṛtvā paryaṃke'sthāpayat pitā |
uvāca ca tvayā putra putrārthaṃ bahudhā śramaḥ ॥5॥
kriyate cāhamapyekamupāyaṃ vacmi te'nagha |
mṛtyulokādāgatena kathitaṃ nāradena me ॥6॥
tadaivāhaṃ gataḥ putra brahmaṇaḥ sadanaṃ prati ॥7॥
papracchāhaṃ namaskṛtya brahmāṇaṃ sarvavedinam|
kathaṃ me bhavitā putraḥ santatiḥ kamalāsana ॥8॥
kathitaṃ tena ṣaṃkaṣṭacaturthī vratamuttamam |
ka uvāca |
asmin vrate kṛte pāpa kṣaye jāte nṛpottama ॥9॥
saṃtatistava putrasya bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ |
pitovāca |
tadā tathaiva likhitaṃ brahmaṇā kathitaṃ yathā ॥10॥
gṛhāṇa pustakaṃ caitadyathāvat kuru ca vratam |
yāvadabdaṃ samāpyeta tāvat siddhi vināyakaḥ ॥11॥
prasanno bhavitā devaḥ sarvasaṃkaṣṭahārakaḥ |
tasmin prasanne putraste bhavitā nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥12॥
ityuktvā'ntarhitastasya putrasya janako nṛpa |
jajāgāra tato rājā kṛtavīryastu vai balī ॥13॥
dadarśa pustakaṃ haste svapnārthān parisasmare ॥14॥
mumocāśrūṇi netrābhyāṃ śokānandapariplutaḥ |
piturviyogācchokārtaḥ pustakāpteśca harṣitaḥ ॥15॥
tāvattasyā gatāmātyāḥ parivāryābruvannṛpam |
amātyā ūcuḥ |
jahi pramādaṃ rājaṃstvaṃ sāvadhānamanā bhava ॥16॥
tyaja śokaṃ vadāsmānkiṃ kāraṇaṃ śokakārakam |
asmānapi samāviṣṭastvacchokācchoka utkaṭaḥ ॥17॥
indra uvāca |
śrutvā vākyamamātyānāṃ kārtavīryo jagāda tān |
mayā svapne pitā dṛṣṭastena me vihvalaṃ manaḥ ॥18॥
pustakaṃ caiva saṃkaṣṭa caturthīvratabodhakam |
dattaṃ mama kare tena pidadhe tatkṣaṇācca saḥ ॥19॥
tadviyogena śocāmi gatārthaṃ nirdhano yathā |
uktaṃ ca tena me vākyaṃ putrārthaṃ kurvidaṃ vratam ॥20॥
prabuddhohaṃ yadāmātyā haste dṛṣṭaṃ ca pustakam |
āścaryaṃ harṣaśokaiścāpyaśru muṃcāmi nānyathā ॥21॥
amātyā ūcuḥ |
yaḥ pitā sarvalokānāṃ manuṣyoragarakṣasām |
sa eva pitṛrūpeṇa parituṣṭo gajānana ॥22॥
upāyamavadatte sa santatyai rājasattama |
anyathā pustakaṃ kva tyaṃ svapnārthaḥ kva pramābhaveta ॥23॥
vinā prasādaṃ svapno'pi viparyayamudāhṛtaḥ |
indra uvāca |
ityamātyavacaḥ śrutvā sāvadhānamanā nṛpaḥ ॥24॥
āhūya paripapraccha paṃḍitān suhṛdo'pi ca |
vadantu pustakasyārthaṃ prasādāttasya bho dvijāḥ ॥25॥
ta ūcuḥ pustakaṃ dṛṣṭvā tasyārthaṃ sarvasaṃsadi |
dvijā ūcuḥ |
brahmaṇaḥ kṛtavīryasya saṃvādo'tra mahānnṛpa ॥26॥
caturthī sarva saṃkaṣṭa nāśinyatra nirūpitā|
candrodaye gaṇeśasya pūjā proktā savistarā ॥27॥
aṃgārakacaturthyāstu mahimā bahuśo'pi ca |
tithideva vidhūnāṃ ca arghyadānaṃ samaṃtrakam ॥28॥
ekaviṃśati viprāṇāṃ bhojanaṃ caiva pūjanam |
nānā dānāni tebhyaśca deyānīti nirūpitam ॥29॥
dūrvāsamarpaṇaphalaṃ śvetadūrvā phalaṃ pṛthak |
etadvrataṃ mahābhāga bhāgyāt prāptaṃ tvayā'nagha ॥30॥
na dṛṣṭaṃ na śrutaṃ loke
bhaviṣyatyupakārakam |
śravaṇāt smaraṇāccāpi saṃkaṣṭaharaṇaṃ nṛṇām ॥31॥
indra uvāca |
śrutvā tatpaṃḍitamukhādrājā sarvajanaśca ha |
āścaryānandasaṃyuktaḥ pupūje sa dvijottamān ॥32॥
vastrālaṃkāraratnāni dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ dadau bahu |
atrimāhūya rājā'pi guruṃ nijakulasya ca ॥33॥
sumuhūrte tato'gṛhṇāt paripūjya yathāvidhi |
devaṃ vināyakaṃ taṃ ca vidyātekākṣarāṃ śubhām ॥34॥
jajāpa mantraṃ tamananyabhaktyā, dhyāyan gaṇeśaṃ vijitendriyaḥ sa|
vrataṃ ca cakre gaṇanāthatuṣṭyai, sutāptaye saṃkaṭanāśanaṃ tat ॥35॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe vratanirūpaṇaṃ nāmāṣṭaṣṭitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥68॥

<adhyāya 69 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śūrasena uvāca |
brahmaṇā kathamādiṣṭaṃ kṛtavīryāṃsa siddhidam |
tanmamācakṣva saṃkaṣṭa caturthī vratamuttamam ॥1॥
indra uvāca |
satyaloke sukhāsīnaṃ sarvajñaṃ caturānanam |
kṛtavīryapitā gatvā papraccha praṇato nṛpaḥ ॥2॥
kṛtavīrya uvāca |
devadeva jagaddhātaḥ praṇatārtinivāraṇa |
vartate hadaye yanme pṛcchāmi tvāṃ vadasva tat ॥3॥
āpatsu vartamānānāṃ nṛṇāṃ vyākulacetasām |
cintayā vyagramanasāṃ viyoge suhṛdāṃ tathā ॥4॥
durlabhaṃ prāptaye nṛṇāṃ kāryasiddhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet |
arthasiddhiḥ kathaṃ nityaṃ putrasaubhāgyasampadā ॥5॥
sarvasaṃkaṣṭanāśārthaṃ kāryaṃ ki mānavaiḥ prabho|
brahmovāca |
śṛṇu rājan pravakṣyāmi sarvasiddhipradaṃ vratam ॥6॥
yasyānuṣṭhānamātreṇa cintitaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ |
oṣadhībhiḥ śuklatilairdivā snāyāt prasannadhīḥ ॥7॥
saṃkalpaṃ kārayet samyag dhyātvā devaṃ gajānanam |
gaṇeśaṃ pūjayed bhaktyā mantrairāgamasaṃbhavaiḥ ॥8॥
kṛṣṇapakṣe caturthyāṃ ca niśi candrodaye'pi ca |
nṛpa uvāca |
kathaṃ saṃpūjayed brahman devadevaṃ gajānanam ॥9॥
vistareṇa mama brūhi praṇayāt paripṛcchate |
brahmovāca |
nityakarma samāpyātha niśāyāmudaye vidhāḥ ॥10॥
śucau deśe gomayena lipte maṃḍapikānvite |
tatra pīṭhaṃ gaṇeśasya pūjayet kuṃkumākṣataiḥ ॥11॥
sthāpayet kalaśaṃ tatra paṃcaratnasamanvitam |
tasyopari nyasetpātraṃ sauvarṇakalaśānvitam ॥12॥
tadabhāve tu raupyaṃ vā tāmraṃ vaiṇavameva ca |
tasyopari nyasedvastraṃ kṣaumaṃ vā śaktisaṃyutaḥ ॥13॥
tasyopari likhedyantramāgamoktavidhānataḥ |
tatra mūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya sauvarṇī lakṣaṇānvitām ॥14॥
ekadaṃtaṃ mahākāyaṃ taptakāṃcanasannibham |
laṃbodara viśālākṣaṃ jvalat pāvakalocanam ॥15॥
ākhupṛṣṭhasamārūḍhaṃ cāmarairvījitaṃ gaṇaiḥ |
śeṣayajñopavītaṃ ca cintayettaṃ gajānanam ॥16॥
dhyānam |
āgaccha devadeveśa saṃkaṭānmāṃ nivāraya |
yāvadvrataṃ samāpyeta tāvattvaṃ sannidhau bhava ॥17॥
sahasraśīrṣetyāvāhanam |
gaṇādhīśa namaste'stu sarvasiddhipradāyaka |
āsanaṃ gṛhayatāṃ deva saṃkaṭānmāṃ nivāraya ॥18॥
puruṣa eve … āsanam |
umāputra namastestu namaste modakapriya |
pādyaṃ gṛhāṇa deveśa saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya ॥19॥
etāvānasya … pādyam |
laṃbodara namaste'stu ratnayuktaṃ phalānvitam |
ayaṃ gṛhāṇa deveśa saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
tripādūrdhvetyarghyam ॥20॥
gaṃgādi sarvatīrthebhyaḥ āhṛtaṃ toyamuttamam |
gṛhāṇācamanīyārthaṃ saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
tasmādvirāla… ācamanīyam ॥21॥
payo dadhi ghṛtaṃ caiva śarkarāmadhusaṃyutam |
paṃcāmṛtaṃ gṛhāṇedaṃ saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya ॥22॥
mantraiḥ paṃcāmṛtam |
narmadā caṃdrabhāgā ca gaṃgāsaṃgamajairjalaiḥ |
snāpito'si mayā bhaktyā saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
yatpuruṣeṇeti snānam ॥23॥
ibhavaktra namastubhyaṃ gṛhāṇa parameśvara |
vastrayugyaṃ gaṇādhyakṣa saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
taṃ yajñamiti vastram ॥24॥
vināyaka namastubhyaṃ namaḥ paraśudhāriṇe |
upavīta gṛhāṇedaṃ saṃkaṭānme nivāraya |
tasmā … yajñopavītam ॥25॥
īśaputra namastubhyaṃ namo mūṣakavāhana |
candanaṃ gṛhayatāṃ deva saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
tasmādyajñāditi caṃdanam ॥26॥
ghṛtakuṃkuma saṃyuktāstandulāḥ sumanoharāḥ |
akṣatāste namastubhyaṃ saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
akṣataḥ ॥27॥
caṃpakaṃ mallikādūrvāṃ puṣpajātiranekaśaḥ |
gṛhāṇa tvaṃ gaṇādhyakṣa saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
tasmādaśveti puṣpam ॥28॥
lambodara mahākāya dhūmraketo suvāsitam |
dhūpaṃ gṛhāṇa deveśa saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
yatpuruṣamiti dhūpaḥ ॥29॥
vighnāṃdhakārasaṃhārakāraka tridaśādhipaḥ |
dīpaṃ gṛhāṇa deveśa saṃkaṭaṃ me nivāraya |
brāhmaṇo'syeti dīpaḥ ॥30॥
modakāpūpalaḍḍūka pāyasaṃ śarkarānvitam |
pakvānnaṃ saghṛtaṃ deva naivedyaṃ pratigṛhayatām |
candramā manasa iti naivedyam ॥31॥
nārikela phalaṃ drākṣāṃ rasālaṃ dāḍimaṃ śubham |
phalaṃ gṛhāṇa deveśa saṃkaṭānme nivāraya |
nābhyā āsīditi phalam ॥32॥
kramukailā lavaṃgādi nāgavallīdalāni ca |
tāṃbūlaṃ gṛhyatāṃ deva saṃkaṭaṃ me vināśaya |
iti tāṃbūlam ॥33॥
sarvaprītikaraṃ deva hiraṇyaṃ sarvasiddhaye |
dakṣiṇārthaṃ gṛhāṇedaṃ saṃkaṭaṃ me vināśaya |
saptāsyāsanniti dakṣiṇām ॥34॥
tato durvāṃkurān gṛhya viśatyekaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ |
ebhirnāmapaderdevamarcayetsusamāhitaḥ ॥35॥
gaṇādhipatya namaḥ |1| umāputrāya namaḥ |2| aghanāśanāya namaḥ |3| ekadantāya namaḥ |4| ibhavaktrāya namaḥ |5| mūṣakavāhanāya nama |6| vināyakāya namaḥ |7| īśaputrāya namaḥ |8| sarvasiddhipradāya namaḥ |9| laṃbodarāya namaḥ |10| vakratuṃḍāya namaḥ |11| modakapriyāya namaḥ |12| vighnavidhvaṃsakartre namaḥ |13| viśvavaṃdyāya namaḥ |14| amareśāya namaḥ |15| gajakarṇāya namaḥ |16| nāgayajñopavītine namaḥ |17| bhālacandrāya namaḥ |18| paraśudhāriṇe namaḥ |19| vighnādhipāya namaḥ |20| vidyāpradāya namaḥ |21| karpūrānalasaṃyuktaṃ śeṣāghaughavināśanam |
nīrājanaṃ gṛhāṇeśa saṃkaṭānmāṃ vimocaya |
nīrājanam ॥36॥
campakāśoka bakulaḥ pārijātabhavaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
puṣpāṃjaliṃ gṛhāṇemāṃ saṃkaṭānmāṃ vimocaya |
yajñeneti puṣpāṃjaliḥ ॥37॥
tvameva viśvaṃ sṛjasībhavaktra tvameva viśvaṃ paripāsi deva |
tvameva viśvaṃ harase'khileśa tvameva viśvātmaka āvibhāsi ॥38॥
iti stutiḥ |
namāmi devaṃ gaṇanāthamīśaṃ vighneśvaraṃ vighnavināśadakṣam|
bhaktārtihaṃ bhaktivimokṣadakṣaṃ, vidyāpradaṃ vedanidhānamādyam ॥39॥
namaskārāḥ |
evaṃ stuvīta vidhivata praṇameta punaḥ punaḥ |
pradakṣiṇāṃ prakurvīta yathā śaktyaikaviṃśatim ॥40॥
ye tvāmasaṃpūjya gaṇeśa nūnaṃ vāṃchaṃti mūḍhā vihitārthasiddhim |
ta eva naṣṭā niyataṃ hi loke jñāto mayā te sakala prabhāvaḥ ॥41॥
prārthanā |
ācāryastvaṃ dvijādhyakṣa sarvasiddhipradāyaka |
vāyan gṛhyatāṃ brahman saṃkaṭānmāṃ nivāraya ॥42॥
phalapuṣpākṣatairyuktaṃ jalaṃ te dakṣiṇānvitam |
viśeṣārghyaṃ mayā dattaṃ saṃkaṭānmāṃ nivāraya ॥43॥
iti ṣoḍaśopacārairmantreṇānena pūjayet |
oṃ namo heraṃba madamodita mama saṃkaṭaṃ nivāraya svāhā |
indrādilokapālāṃśca samantāt pūjayet sudhīḥ ॥44॥
pakvamudgatilairyuktān modakān ghṛtapācitān |
bhakṣyānyanyāni tāmbūlaṃ yathāśakti prakalpayet ॥45॥
tato dūrvāṃkurāngṛhya viṃśatyekaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ |
ebhirnāmapadairdevamarcayet susamāhitaḥ ॥46॥
gaṇādhipa namastubhyamumāputrābhayaprada |
ekadantebhavaktreti tathā mūṣakavāhana ॥47॥
vināyakeśa putreti sarvasiddhipradāyaka |
lambodara namastubhyaṃ vakratuṃḍāghanāśana ॥48॥
vighnavidhvaṃsakartreti viśvavandyāmareśvara |
gajavaktra namastubhyaṃ nāgayajñopavītine ॥49॥
bhālacandra namastubhyaṃ namaḥ paraśudhāriṇe |
vighnādhipa namastubhyaṃ sarvavidyāpradāyaka ॥50॥
evaṃ saṃpūjayeddevaṃ dūrvābhiśca pṛthak pṛthak |
yaduddiśya kṛtaṃ samyag yathāśaktiprapūjanam ॥51॥
tena tuṣṭo bhavāśu tvaṃ hṛtsthānkāmānprapūraya |
vighnānnāśaya me sarvān duṣṭāṃśca samupasthitān ॥52॥
tvatprasādena sarvāṇi kāryāṇīha karomyaham |
śatrūṇāṃ buddhināśaṃ ca mitrāṇāmudayaṃ kuru ॥53॥
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ praṇipatya punaḥ punaḥ |
tato homaṃ prakrurvīta śatabhraṣṭottaraṃ vratī ॥54॥
modakairvāṇakaṃ kāryaṃ vratasaṃpūrṇahetave |
laḍḍūkairvaṭakādyairvā trisaptaphalasaṃyutam ॥55॥
raktavastreṇa saṃchādya svācāryāya nivedayeta |
gaṇādhipa namastubhyaṃ sarvasaṃkalpasiddhida ॥56॥
vāyanasya pradānena saṃkaṭānmāṃ nivāraya |
iti vāyanamaṃtraḥ |
kathāṃ śrutvā tataḥ puṇyāṃ dadyādarghyaṃ samāhitaḥ ॥57॥
tithīnāmuttame devi gaṇeśapriya vallabhe |
saṃkaṭaṃ hara me devi gṛhāṇārghyaṃ namo'stu te ॥58॥
iti tithyarghaḥ |
laṃbodara namastubhyaṃ satataṃ modakapriya |
saṃkaṭaṃ hara me deva gṛhāṇārghyaṃ namo'stu te ॥59॥
iti devārghaḥ candrāya saptavāraṃ tu maṃtreṇānena pārthiva |
kṣārodārṇava saṃbhūta atrigotra samudbhava |
gṛhāṇārghyaṃ mayā dattaṃ rohiṇyā sahitaḥ śaśin |
caṃdrārghamaṃtra ॥60॥
tataḥ kṣamāpayeddevaṃ tato viprāṃśca bhojayet |
svayaṃ bhuṃjīta taccheṣaṃ brāhmaṇabhyo yadarpitam ॥61॥
saptagrāsān maunayukto yathāśaktyā yathāsukham |
itthaṃ kuryāttu māseṣu caturṣvapi vidhānataḥ ॥62॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe ekonasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥69॥

<adhyāya 70 prāraṃbhaḥ>

rājovāca |
vrataṃ purā kṛtaṃ kena bhuvi kena prakāśitam |
kiṃ puṇyaṃ ki phalaṃ cāsya karuṇādvada me prabho ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
purā skande gate caiva pārvatyaiva kṛtaṃ vratam |
caturṣvapi ca māseṣu śivavākyena pārthiva ॥2॥
paṃcame māsi dṛṣṭastu kārtikeyo hyaparṇayā |
agastyena kṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ samudraṃ pātumicchatā ॥3॥
triṣu māseṣu vighneśa prasādāt pītavānamum |
ṣaṇmāsāvadhi rājendra damayantyā purā kṛtam ॥4॥
nalamanveṣayantyā tu tato dṛṣṭo nalastayā |
pradyumnasya sutaṃ rājaṃścitralekhā'nayatpurā ॥5॥
kva gataḥ kena nīto vā iti śocantamākulama |
pradyumnaṃ putraśokārtaṃ rukmiṇī pratyabhāṣata ॥6॥
śṛṇu putra pravakṣyāmi yadvrataṃ svasya mandire |
śambareṇa purā''nīte bālake tvayi ṣaḍdine ॥7॥
tvadviyogajaduḥkhena hṛdayaṃ meṃ vyadūyata |
kadā drakṣyāmyahaṃ putramapūrvamatisuṃdaram ॥8॥
anyastrīṇāṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā mama cetasi jāyate |
mamāpi putro bhavitā sāvanena pramāṇataḥ ॥9॥
iti cintākulāyā me gatānyabdāni bhūriśaḥ |
tato me daivayogāttu lomaśo munirāgataḥ ॥10॥
tenopadiṣṭaṃ saṃkaṣṭacaturthīvratamuttamam |
sarvacintāharaṃ putra caturvāraṃ kṛtaṃ mayā ॥11॥
tatprasādāt tvamāyāto hatvā śambaramāhave |
tvamapyetat kuru vrataṃ tato jñāsyasi taṃ sutama ॥12॥
brahmovāca |
pradyumnena kṛtaṃ pūrvaṃ gaṇanātha sutoṣakam |
śruto bāṇāsurapure niruddho nāradāt purā ॥13॥
uddhavasyājñayā kṛṣṇaḥ kṛtavān vratamuttamam |
bhītaḥ īśvarasaṃgrāmādekavāraṃ yathāvidhi ॥14॥
gatvā sa śoṇitapuraṃ jitvā bāṇāsuraṃ mṛdhe |
ānītaḥ uṣayā sārddhamaniruddhaḥ kṣaṇānnṛpaḥ ॥15॥
mayā'pi sṛṣṭikāmena kṛtametat nṛpottama |
kṛtā nānāvidhā sṛṣṭirvratasyāsya prabhāvataḥ ॥16॥
anyairdevāsuranaraiḥ kṛtaṃ vighnopaśāṃtaye |
ṛṣibhirdānavairyakṣaiḥ kinnaroragarākṣasaiḥ ॥17॥
āpadyapi ca kaṣṭāyāṃ kuryāttacchāṃtaye vratam |
nānena sadṛśaṃ loke sarvasiddhikaraṃ vratam ॥18॥
tapodānaṃ japastīrthaṃ mantro vidyā na ca kvacit |
imāṃ śrutvā kathāṃ rājan svayaṃ bhuṃjīta vāgyataḥ ॥19॥
jānumadhye karaṃ kṛtvā gaṇeśaṃ hṛdi cintayet |
dvijātīnāṃ ca yaccheṣaṃ bhoktavyaṃ saha bāṃdhavaiḥ ॥20॥
kāryaṃ bahvalpamāsaistu siddhyate nātra saṃśayaḥ |
bahunātra kimuktena nānyatasyācchīghrasiddhidam ॥21॥
nopadeśyamabhaktāya nāstikāya śaṭhāya ca|
deyaṃ putrāya śiṣyāya bhaktiyuktāya sādhave ॥22॥
mama priyo'si rājendra dharmiṣṭaḥ kṣatriyarṣabhaḥ |
kārya kartā'si lokānāmupadiṣṭamatho vratam ॥23॥
tasmāt sarvavrateṣvetat kartavyaṃ prathamaṃ tvayā |
siddhiṃ yāsyaṃti kāryāṇi nānyathā bhāṣitaṃ mayā ॥24॥
yadā yadā paśyati kāryamudyataṃ, nārī naro vā vidadhātu sadvratam |
siddhyanti kāryāṇi manīṣitāni, kiṃ durlabhaṃ vighnahare prasanne ॥25॥
sūta uvāca |
itthaṃ sa śrutvā vidhivannṛpottamo, vrataṃ cakārākhila duḥkhaśāntaye|
vrataprabhāvājjitavānsa vairiṇo bubhoja rājyaṃ sa sutairakaṃṭakama ॥26॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe caturthīvratopākhyānaṃ kṛtavīryaṃ nāma saptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥70॥

<adhyāya 71 prāraṃbhaḥ>

rājovāca |
udyāpanaṃ kathaṃ kāryaṃ vratasyāsya mahāmate |
tanme vistarato brūhi lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
prathame māsi kartavyaṃ paṃcame saptame'thavā |
udyāpanaṃ manuṣyendra vratasaṃpūrṇa hetave ॥2॥
pūrvoktena vidhānena pūjayed bhaktimānnaraḥ |
puṣpamaṃḍapikā kṛtvā nānāvastravicitratām ॥3॥
kṛtvā tu sarvatobhadraṃ nānāraṃgavicitratam |
pūjayettama deveśaṃ kalaśopari pūrvavat ॥4॥
candanena sugandhena puṣpairnānāvidhairapi |
nārikela phalenaiva dadyādarghyaṃ samāhitaḥ ॥5॥
tithīnāmuttame devi gaṇeśapriya vallabhe|
saṃkaṭaṃ hara me devi gṛhāṇārghyaṃ namo'stu te ॥6॥
laṃbodara namastubhyaṃ satatamodakapriya |
saṃkaṣṭaṃ hara me deva gṛhāṇārghyaṃ namo'stu te ॥7॥
kṣīrodārṇavasaṃbhūta atrigotra samudbhava |
gṛhāṇārghyaṃ mayā dattaṃ rohiṇyā sahitaḥ śaśin ॥8॥
bhojyaṃ bhakṣyaṃ ca lehyaṃ ca peyaṃ coṣyaṃ nivedayet |
phalairanyaiśca bahubhistoṣayed gaṇanāyakam ॥9॥
ācāryaṃ varayettatra ṛtvijaścaikaviṃśatim |
gaṇānāṃtveti mantreṇa ayutaṃ homamācaret ॥10॥
athavā mūlamantreṇa sahasraṃ vā tadardhakam |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ cāpi balidānaṃ tataścaret ॥11॥
pūrṇāhutiṃ hunetpaścād vasordhārā ca pātayet |
homaśeṣaṃ samāpyaivaṃ brāhmaṇān bhojayettataḥ ॥12॥
vastrayugmaṃ ca kalaśān dakṣiṇāsanasaṃyutān|
tebhyo dadyādyathāśakti vittaśāṭhyaṃ na kārayet ॥13॥
ācāryaṃ pūjayet paścād vastrālaṃkaraṇādibhiḥ |
tasmai bhuktavate dadyād vāyanaṃ phalasaṃyutam ॥14॥
śūrpa pāyasa saṃpūrṇa raktavastreṇa veṣṭitam |
sauvarṇaṃ taṃ gaṇādhīśaṃ tasmai dadyāta sadakṣiṇam ॥15॥
tilānāmāḍhakaṃ dadyād vratasaṃpūrṇa hetave |
tato gāṃ kapilāṃ dadyāt savatsāṃ savibhūṣaṇām ॥16॥
tataḥ kṣamāpayed viprān vighneśaḥ prīyatāmiti |
vratasyodyāpanaṃ kṛtvā hayamedhaphalaṃ labhet ॥17॥
pitovāca |
evaṃ me brahmaṇādiṣṭaṃ vrataṃ lokopakārataḥ |
tadetat kathitaṃ te'dya kuru putrārthamādarāt ॥18॥
indra uvāca |
yathāyathā samādiṣṭaṃ pitrā tena mahāvratam |
tathā tathā kṛtaṃ tena kārtavīryeṇa dhīmatā ॥19॥
vyākhyāya kathitaṃ samyak paṃḍitairvratamuttamam |
siddhibuddhiyutāṃ mūrtiṃ sthāpya kāṃcananirmitām ॥20॥
mahāmaṃḍapikāmadhye brāhmaṇaiḥ paṭhyate kvacit |
kvacit purāṇaṃ pāṃḍityaṃ kvacit gāyana nartane ॥21॥
nānāvidhānāṃ vādyānāṃ dhvanayo nirgatā divam |
vivadaṃte janāḥ kvāpi madhyasthāḥ kecanābhavan ॥22॥
sa tu rājā mahādhīmān jajāpa paramaṃ manum |
japtvā hutvā pūjayitvā bhojayitvā dvijān bahūn ॥23॥
gā dadāvayutaṃ tebhyo'laṃkṛtebhyo hyalaṃkṛtaḥ |
dīnāndhakṛpaṇebhyo'nnaṃ dadau dānānyanekaśaḥ ॥24॥
saṃtuṣṭānāṃ sa sarveṣāṃ putrāśīrjagṛhe nṛpaḥ |
teṣāṃ dvijānāṃ tapatāṃ sarvadā satyavādinām ॥25॥
āśirbhiḥ svalpakālena sasatvā'bhūnnṛpāṃganā|
suṣuve śubhavelāyāṃ putraṃ lakṣaṇasaṃyutam ॥26॥
dadau dānānyanekāni putrajanmapraharṣitaḥ |
vratabaṃdha vivāhaṃ ca kāle tasyākaronnṛpaḥ ॥27॥
jñānavijñānasaṃpannaṃ putraṃ rājye'bhiṣicya ca |
satputro bhogasaṃpanno jagāmānte padaṃ vibhoḥ ॥28॥
ṛtvigbhyaḥ paṃḍitaiḥ sārdhaṃ prekṣakaiḥ sakalairapi |
tasya puṇyena te sarve gāṇeśaṃ dhāma cāgaman ॥29॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe ekasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥71॥

<adhyāya 72 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śūrasena uvāca |
vrate jāte kathamabhūttayoḥ putraḥ śatakrato |
tatsarvaṃ me vada vibho vistarāt paripṛcchataḥ ॥1॥
inda uvāca |
kiṃ kiṃ na jāyate rājan parituṣṭe gajānane|
prasādāttasya sā rājñī garbha dadhre nṛpasya yat ॥2॥
prasūtā navame māsi maṃgalaṃ putramaikṣata |
dviskandhaṃ cāruvadanaṃ bāhuhasta vivarjitam ॥3॥
sunāsaṃ padmanayanamūrū jānu vivarjitam |
jaṃghā pādavihīnaṃ taṃ dṛṣṭvā mātā rudoda ha ॥4॥
sovāca |
durbhagāyāḥ kathamabhūdīdṛśo bālako mama|
karapādavihīno'yaṃ kathaṃ datto gajānana ॥5॥
āsaṃ śubhā'haṃ vandhyaiva kimīdṛk putravatyaham |
kathaṃ na jāto nāśo me pūrvajanmakṛtainasaḥ ॥6॥
kathaṃ prasādaḥ phalitastavāpi dviradānana |
kathaṃ brāhmaṇavākyāni niṣphalānīdṛśāni me ॥7॥
pāṇibhyāṃ nighnatī vakṣo lalāṭaṃ ca muhurmuhuḥ |
tasyāṃ rudatyāṃ sarvāṃstā rurudustatra yā sthitāḥ ॥8॥
tāsāṃ kolāhalaṃ śrutvā nṛpo'pi tatra cāgamat |
tasyāpi ruditaṃ śrutvā pradhānā api cāgaman ॥9॥
teṣāṃ ca ruditaṃ śrutvā nāgarā rurudustadā ॥10॥
rājovāca |
keyaṃ dīnānukaṃpitvaṃ tava deva gajānana |
kathaṃ dayā kṛtā te'dya mahyamevaṃ pradarśitā ॥11॥
kathaṃ smaraṇamātreṇa pāpāni harase'nagha |
japaḥ stapaḥ smṛtirdānapūjanaṃ dvijatarpaṇam ॥12॥
anuṣṭhānaṃ tathā homaḥ sarvaṃ vyarthaṃ gataṃ mama |
tasmādyaivaṃ hi balavat prayatnastu nirarthakaḥ ॥13॥
na jñāyate karmagatiḥ kadā kiṃ vā bhaviṣyati |
mūṣaka prāpyate yadvat parvatasya vidāraṇāt ॥14॥
tathā me'jāni putreyamājanma kṛtayatnataḥ |
tata ūcuḥ prakṛtayo nṛpaṃ śokākulaṃ tadā ॥15॥
pradhānā ūcuḥ |
alaṃ śoke na bhūpāla kathaṃ bhāvyanyathā bhavet |
rāmaḥ kiṃ na mṛgaṃ veda tatpṛṣṭaṃ gata eva saḥ ॥16॥
dharmarājo na kiṃ veda niṣidhyaṃ dyūtakarma tat |
tathāpi sa gato rantuṃ sarvaṃ hitvā vanaṃ gataḥ ॥17॥
cārutā na bhavedasya bhraśamākrandite sati |
