adṛṣṭaṃ ced bhavet samyagagre cārurayaṃ bhavet ॥18॥
puṣpaṃ phalaṃ vā kālena yathā yāti mahīruhāma |
tathā kālena bhavitā samyakca pṛthivīpatiḥ ॥19॥
indra uvāca |
ityamātyavacaḥ śrutvā sāvadhāno'bhavannṛpaḥ |
uvāca nṛpapatnīṃ tāmuttiṣṭhottiṣṭha mā śucaḥ ॥20॥
svayaṃ ca sumanā rājā samāhūya dvijottamān |
gaṇeśa pūjanaṃ cakre svastivācanameva ca ॥21॥
kṛtyā'bhyudayika śrāddhaṃ dadau dānānyanekaśaḥ |
mālyālaṃkāra vāsāṃsi gāvo ratnānyanekaśaḥ ॥22॥
suhṛtsaṃbandhi bhṛtyebhyo vastrāṇi vividhāni saḥ |
nānā vāditra jīvebhyo dadau rājā yathārhataḥ ॥23॥
bandicāraṇa dīnāndhakṛpaṇebhyaśca sarvaśaḥ |
gṛhe gṛhe ca tāṃbūla śarkarā dāpayacca saḥ ॥24॥
kārtavīryeti nāmāsya cakāraikādaśe'hani |
bhojayāmāsa nagaraṃ mahotsāha puraḥsaram ॥25॥
tato dvādaśa varṣāṇi vyatītāni sutasya ha |
āyayau svecchayā tasya dattātreyastadā gṛham ॥26॥
pādayoḥ svaśiraḥ sthāpya kṛtavīryo nanāma tam |
āliliṃga munistaṃ sa utthāya nṛpasattamam ॥27॥
upaviśyāsane ramye pūjayāmāsa sādaram |
viṣṭaraṃ pādyamarghyaṃ ca gāṃ ca vastropavītakam ॥28॥
dadau dhūpaṃ ca dīpaṃ ca naivedyaṃ vividhaṃ phalam |
udvartanaṃ ca tāṃbūlaṃ ratnakāṃcana dakṣiṇām ॥29॥
suprasannaṃ sukhāsīnaṃ pādasaṃvāhanādibhiḥ |
uvāca rājā samyaktaṃ dattātreyaṃ mahāmunim ॥30॥
janmajanmāntarīyaṃ me puṇyaṃ phalitamadya vai |
yaccarmacakṣuṣāṃ jātaṃ sākṣātte darśanaṃ mune ॥31॥
agre śubhataraṃ bhāvi prasādāttava suvrata |
yato bhavādṛśāṃ naiva darśanaṃ pāpakarmaṇām ॥32॥
kṛtavīryavacaḥ śrutvā dattātreyastamabravīt |
apūrvaṃ tanayaṃ te'dya draṣṭukāmo'hamāgataḥ ॥33॥
tata ānandayukto'sau nṛpaḥ prāha muniṃ punaḥ |
nṛpa uvāca |
anuṣṭhānaṃ tapo dānaṃ vṛthā mama gataṃ vratam ॥34॥
jagadīśena datto'yaṃ putro hṛcchalya eva saḥ|
anenā darśanīyena gataścādarśanīyatām ॥35॥
indra uvāca |
ānīya bālakaṃ tasmai munaye'darśayannṛpaḥ |
ācchādayannetrakaṃje tatsutaṃ vīkṣya sanmuniḥ ॥36॥
jñātvā dhyānena tatkarmaṃ munistaṃ punarabravīt |
ayameva suto rājan kartā sarvān vijitya ha ॥37॥
nācānto'si vijṛmbhya tvaṃ saṃkaṣṭī vrata jāgare |
nindito vratarājastu sarvasaṃkaṣṭanāśanaḥ ॥38॥
aṃgahīnaḥ suto jāta upāyāt sāṃgatāmiyāt |
rājovāca |
ṛtamuktaṃ tvayā svāmin kṛpāṃ kṛtvā vadasva me ॥39॥
yenopāyena sāṃgo me tvatprasādāt suto bhavet |
indra uvāca |
muniḥ sa kṛpayāviṣṭastatsutāya tadā'bravīt ॥40॥
mantramekākṣaraṃ sāṃgaṃ tata ūce punaśca tam |
ārādhanaṃ gaṇeśasya mantreṇānena bhaktiḥ ॥41॥
upavāsaikabhaktānāṃ niyamān kurū suvrata |
dvādaśābdaṃ tatastubhyaṃ darśanaṃ sa ca dāsyāti | 42||
taddṛṣṭipātamātreṇa divyadeho bhaviṣyasi |
ityuktvā nṛpamāmantrya pidadhe munipuṃgavaḥ ॥43॥
gate munau tataḥ putraḥ pādahīno mahāmanaḥ |
jagāda kṛtavīryaṃ sa gahanaṃ prāpayasya mām ॥44॥
gajānana prasādārthamanuṣṭhānaṃ karomyaham |
ruditvaḥ pitarau tasya śrutvā vāṇīṃ tadīritām ॥45॥
apreṣayadvanaṃ cāru narayānena taṃ pitā |
bhṛtyāḥ parṇakuṭīmadhye saṃsthāpya nagaraṃ yayuḥ ॥46॥
tasthau tatra svayaṃ samyak tapase kṛtaniścayaḥ ॥47॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe dvisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥72॥

<adhyāya 73 prāraṃbhaḥ>

indra uvāca |
gurūpadiṣṭaṃ mantraṃ jajāpa niyamena saḥ |
dhyāyan gajānanaṃ devaṃ niṣṭhāmāsthāya naiṣṭhikīm ॥1॥
vāyubhakṣo nirāhāro loṣṭa pāṣāṇa sannibhaḥ |
evaṃ dvādaśa varṣāṇi vyatītāni tapasyataḥ ॥2॥
hastapādavihīnasya bālakasya mahātmanaḥ |
nirvāṇaṃ tasya taddṛṣṭavā dvādaśābdaṃ gajānanaḥ ॥3॥
taḍāgamadhyāduttasthau pravālamayamūrtigaḥ |
uvāca saṃmukho bhūtvā niṣṭhā bhakti sutoṣitaḥ ॥4॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
nirjane'smin vane yasmātsiṃhavyāghraniṣevite |
nikuṃja bahule tiṣṭhadyato dvādaśavārṣikam ॥5॥
ataste'haṃ varaṃ dāsye śṛṇu yatte manogatam |
śrutvā vāṇīṃ taduditā māsthito dehabhāvanām ॥6॥
kṛtavīryasuto natvā provāca dviradānanam |
śṛṇvatsu sarvamuniṣu vimānastheṣu bhūriśaḥ ॥7॥
putra uvāca |
tvatpādayugale deva bhaktiravyabhicāriṇī |
astu me satataṃ nānyadutsāho me prayāci ma ॥8॥
tathāpi yāce deveśa pitroḥ saṃtoṣahetave |
śarīre cārutāṃ dehi sarvasaṃtoṣakāriṇīm ॥9॥
indra uvāca |
śrutvā vākyaṃ tadīyaṃ tu māyāvī sa gajānanaḥ |
aṇimānaṃ samāsthāya viveśodaramādarāt ॥10॥
tasmin praviṣṭe putro'sau divyadeho'bhavannṛpa |
sahasrabhujavānjātaḥ kṛtavīryātmajastadā ॥11॥
ṛjupāda dvayayutaḥ sthitaḥ parvatasannibhaḥ |
vavarṣuḥ puṣpavarṣāṇi devā devarṣayo'pi ca ॥12॥
tuṣṭuvustaṃ ca devaṃ ca gītavāditranisvanaiḥ |
jagarja kārtavīryo'sau sahasrabhujamaṃḍitaḥ ॥13॥
tasya śabdaṃ samākarṇya ghanagarjitasannibham |
samavartī bhṛśa trastaḥ pareṣāṃ gaṇanā kutaḥ ॥14॥
pṛthivyāṃ sarvarājānaḥ samakapaṃta tadbhayāt |
ayaṃ paṃcaśataṃ bāṇān saṃgrāme mocayediti ॥15॥
brahmādayastato yānāduttīryātha tamabruvan |
smaraṇāttava devānāṃ prāptiḥ syād gatavastunaḥ ॥16॥
hṛdisthaṃ sarvalokānāmāmayaṃ tvaṃ vineṣyasi |
yataḥ sākṣādviṣṇurūpaḥ sahasrārjuna saṃjñayā ॥17॥
triṣu lokeṣu vikhyāto yāvat kalpaṃ bhaviṣyasi |
sādhutrāṇe ca nirataḥ śaraṇāgata pālakaḥ ॥18॥
vijetā sarva śatrūṇāṃ pṛthivī maṃḍalādhipaḥ |
iti nānā varān datvā pidadhurdevatā gaṇaḥ ॥19॥
hastyaśva narayānāni chatracāmara dīpikāḥ |
rathānupāyanānyanyānyājahrūrakhilā nṛpāḥ ॥20॥
tataḥ saṃsthāpayāmāsa mahāprāsādamadhyagām |
prāvālīṃ gaṇanāthasya mūrtiṃ dvijapuraḥsaraḥ ॥21॥
pravālagaṇapaśceti tasya nāma dadhurdvijāḥ |
dadau grāmān brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pūjāyai sthāpitāśca ye ॥22॥
pravālakṣetramiti tat paprathe bhuvi siddhidam |
tatra śeṣo dharāṃ dhartumanuṣṭhānaparo'bhavat ॥23॥
prāpa tasmād gaṇeśāttu varān bahutarānapi |
dharādharaṇasāmarthyaṃ sarvajñātvaṃ tathaiva ca ॥24॥
mūrdhnā sahasraṃ śraiṣṭhyaṃ ca navanāgakuleṣvapi |
ati saṃtuṣṭa manasā tena yat sthāpitaṃ purā ॥25॥
dharaṇīdhara ityevaṃ dvitīyaṃ nāma paprathe |
smaratāṃ śṛṇvatāṃ cāpi sarvakāmaphalapradam ॥26॥
saṃpūjya sarvān dvijapuṃgavāṃstān, rājñaśca sarvāṃnanupṛcchya caitān |
sahasrabāhurnagaraṃ svakīyaṃ, draṣṭuṃ svamātāpitarāvathāgāt ॥27॥
jaharṣatustau taṃ dṛṣṭvā nagaraṃ ca tathāvidham |
dadaturdvijamukhyebhyo nānādānāni sarvaśaḥ ॥28॥
indra uvāca |
abhyadhāpi tu saṃkaṣṭa caturthī mahimā'dabhutaḥ |
kṛtavīrya sutāccāyaṃ mṛtyulokaṃ gataḥ śubhaḥ ॥29॥
anubhūtaḥ suragaṇaiścandrasenādibhirnṛpaiḥ |
atipuṇyatamaścāyaṃ smaraṇādapi siddhidaḥ ॥30॥
etatprasādāt saṃprāptā rāvaṇenākhilājñatā |
etat prasādāt saṃprāptaṃ purā rājyaṃ ca pāṃḍavai ॥31॥
etadvratasya yatpuṇyaṃ haste me dīyate yadi |
tadaiva tadvimānaṃ tu pracaledamarāvatīm ॥32||
ka uvāca |
iti śakramukhāttasya vratasya mahimā śrutaḥ |
śūrasenena bhūpena svānandābdhau nimajyatām ॥33॥
vandite pādakamale śakrasya pratigarjitā |
tamuvāca tataḥ prītaḥ śūraseno nṛpottamaḥ ॥34॥
śrutvā kathāṃ divyarūpāṃ caturthīvratasaṃbhavām |
sa uvāca |
phalitaṃ pūrvapuṇyaṃ me yenāśrāvīdṛśaṃ vratam ॥35॥
na hayetasmāt puṇyataraṃ triṣu lokeṣu kiṃcana |
ityuktvā śakramārebhe vrataṃ kartuṃ svayaṃ tataḥ ॥36॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe caturthī vratamāhātmyaṃ nāmatrisaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥73॥

<adhyāya 74 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
brahman kathaṃ kṛtaṃ tena śūrasenena bhūbhujā|
setihāsaṃ śakramukhācchrutvā sādaramuttamam ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
tataḥ svadūtān prāhedaṃ gacchantu nagaraṃ prati |
sa āneyastu saṃkaṣṭacaturthī vratakārakaḥ ॥2॥
te dūtāstvarayā yātāḥ papracchuste gṛhaṃ gṛhaṃ |
dadṛśurbhramamāṇāste vimānaṃ suṃdaraṃ śubham ॥3॥
yā'bhavad duṣṭa cāṃḍālī galat kuṣṭhāsravanmukhā |
makṣikākṛmibhāreṇa durgandhena samāvṛtā ॥4॥
śuṣkodarī dīrghakeśā śuṣkadantākṣi nāsikā |
atyantamalinā dīrghaśrotrarandhrā ghanasvanā ॥5॥
netuṃ tāmīdṛśīṃ dūtairānītāṃ gaṇapasya hi |
atyāścaryamidamiti rājadūtāḥ praṇamya tām ॥6॥
ūcuste devadūtāstān gaṇanāthasya kiṃkarān |
atyaṃtaṃ kutsitā jātyā hīneyaṃ svargagā katham ॥7॥
keyamāsītpurā dūtāḥ kathamīdṛg babhūva ca |
kena puṇyena yuṣmābhiḥ svargaṃ tu nīyate katham ॥8॥
etadvadantu sarvaṃ no yadi vaktuṃ kṣamaṃ bhavet |
devadūtā ūcuḥ |
āsīd baṃgāla viṣaye sāraṃgadhara nāmataḥ ॥9॥
kṣatriyastasya kanyeyaṃ sundarā nāma sundarī |
pikakaṃṭhā śaśimukhī ratilāvaṇyajitvarā ॥10॥
yaddāsyakaraṇe nārhāḥ prasiddhā aṣṭanāyikāḥ |
yogināmapi yā cetaḥ kaṭākṣena vimohayet ॥11॥
yuvāno mumucuḥ śukraṃ kecittadrūpavīkṣaṇāt |
jāramārgeṣu niratā sā'bhavadviśvamohinī ॥12॥
mahārhavastrālaṃkārā nānāviṣayabhoginī |
baṃgāla nagare khyātā veśyevāsīd gatatrayā ॥13॥
bhartāraṃ citranāmānaṃ vaṃcayatyeva sarvadā |
nyāyādasaṃkhya dravyasya pitrā saṃpāditaṃ purā ॥14॥
kadācicchayane taṃ sā hitvā suṃdaraveṣabhṛt |
niśīthe calitā tena haste'dhāri ruṣāvaśāt ॥5॥
uvāca bhartsayaṃstāṃ ca citranāmā patistadā |
dhik tvāṃ pāpasamācāre yā jāre niratā'niśam ॥16॥
tadvākyamithyamākarṇya śāntakopā'bhavattadā |
abhakṣyabhakṣaṇenāsau mattā balavatī bhṛśam ॥17॥
andhe tamasi saṃgṛhya śastrikāṃ dakṣahastataḥ |
tayā tadudaraṃ bhitvā citranāmapatestadā ॥18॥
gatā taṃ puruṣaṃ rantuṃ yo jāro manasi sthitaḥ |
yāvatsā ramate tatra tāvatparyantavāsinā ॥19॥
jāgratā caritaṃ tasyā jñātvā rājñe niveditam |
taddūtāstamasi sthitvā tāvatsā gṛhamāgatā ॥20॥
dadhustāṃ rājadūtāste ninyū rājasamīpataḥ |
nijaghnustāṃ bahirnītvā dūtā rājājñayā tadā ॥21॥
sā nītā narake ghore yamadūtaistadāyajñā |
adhomukhī sthitā tatra saṃdaṣṭā kṛmibhirbhṛśam ॥22॥
atyaṃtaṃ bubhuje duḥkhaṃ smarantī pūrvaceṣṭikam |
kalpānte mṛtyuloke'bhūccāṃḍālī durbhagā bhṛśam ॥23॥
ekadā madirāṃ pītvā mattā suptā divaiva sā |
niśāyāṃ prathame yāme prabuddhā kṣudhitā bhṛśam ॥24॥
tadaiva bhikṣituṃ yātā gṛhaṃ sā vratakāriṇaḥ |
tenānnamasyai dattaṃ yattayā bhuktaṃ vidhūdaye ॥ 25॥
gaṇeśeti ca jalpantyā svecchayā daivayogataḥ |
tadaiva gaṇanāthena vimānaṃ preṣitaṃ śubham ॥26॥
brahmovāca |
devadūtavacaḥ śrutvā punarūcurnamasya te |
rājadūtā ūcuḥ |
idamatyadbhutaṃ dṛṣṭamasmābhiḥ kāryakāribhiḥ ॥27॥
rājñā yadupadiṣṭaṃ no vākyametannibodhata |
śakro gṛtsamadaṃ dṛṣṭuṃ vimānavaramāsthitaḥ ॥28॥
bhruśuṃḍimāyayau devairdṛṣṭvā natvā prapūjya tam |
tadanujñāṃ ca pūjāṃ ca gṛhītvā svāṃ purīṃ yayau ॥29॥
gacchatastasya yānaṃ tacchūrasenapure'patat |
kuṣṭhino vaiśyaputrasya dṛṣṭipātena tatkṣaṇāt ॥30॥
śūraseno gatastatra namaskṛtya prapūjya tam |
vimāna patane'pṛcchadupāyaṃ gamane'pi ca ॥31॥
indreṇoktaṃ tu saṃkaṣṭacaturthī vrata saṃbhavāt |
puṇyāt prasthāsyate yānaṃ tadarthe yatnamācara ॥32॥
vayaṃ tadājñayā dūtāstaṃ gaveṣitumāgataḥ |
anayā cedvratamidaṃ devadūtāḥ kṛtaṃ yadi ॥33॥
tarhīyaṃ nīyatāṃ śūrasenaṃ bhūmipatiṃ prati |
śreyo dāsyati cāṃḍālī saṃkaṣṭīvratasaṃbhavam ॥34॥
punardviguṇa puṇyeyaṃ vimānaṃ prati yāsyati |
vimānamapi śakrasya svapurīmāgamiṣyati ॥35॥
yuṣmākamasyā asmākaṃ kāryaṃ rājñaḥ śacīpateḥ |
evaṃ kṛte tu bhavitā kriyatāṃ yadi rocate ॥36॥
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā'bhidadhu rdevasevakāḥ |
ājñāno no gaṇeśasyāstīmāṃ dātuṃ parasya hi ॥37॥
ityuktvā tāṃ samutthāya vimāne nidadhuryadā |
tadaiva sā divyakāntirdivyavastrāṃgabhūṣaṇā ॥38॥
devadūtaistu sā nītā divyavāditraniḥsvanaiḥ |
gajānanasamīpe sā rājadūtā yathāgatam ॥39॥
yayuste śūrasenaṃ taṃ vṛttāntaṃ samanubruvan |
bruvatsu teṣu tadyānaṃ cāṃḍālyā saha saṃvrajet ॥40॥
dadṛśuste dīpyamānaṃ bhāsayat pradiśo diśaḥ |
tasyā dṛṣṭirnipatitā tasmin yāne śacīpateḥ ॥41॥
vimānaṃ vāyusaṃsparśādvimānamūrdhvamāyayau |
paśyatsu sarvalokeṣu vismiteṣu surarṣiṣu ॥42॥
svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ yayuḥ sarve śakre yāte'marāvatīm |
sā'pi vaināyakaṃ dhāma saṃprāptā divyadehabhāk ॥43॥
pāpaṃ hitvā tu saṃkaṣṭacaturthīvrata puṇyataḥ |
idaṃ yaḥ śṛṇuyāt samyak śrāvayedvā prayatnataḥ ॥44॥
prāpnuyāt sakalān kāmān saṃkaṣṭanāśanaṃ naraḥ ॥45॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe catuḥsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥74॥

<adhyāya 75 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
śrutvā dṛṣṭvā śūraseno mahimānaṃ vratodbhavam |
svayaṃ kartuṃmanāḥ prāha vasiṣṭhaṃ munipuṃgavaṃ ॥1॥
rājovāca |
muhūrtaṃ vada saṃkaṣṭacaturthīvratahetave |
vrataṃ kartumihecchāmi sadyaḥ pratyayakārakam ॥2॥
vasiṣṭha uvāca |
māghe kṛṣṇe bhaumavāre kuruṣva vratamuttamam |
sarvasiddhikaraṃ sarvakāmadaṃ rājasattama ॥3॥
brahmovāca |
rājā saṃbhṛtasaṃbhāraḥ sapatnīko'ti bhaktimān |
acireṇaiva saṃprāpte vasiṣṭhoktadine śubhe ॥4॥
prārīpsamānaḥ saṃkaṣṭacaturthīvratamuttamam |
prāptaḥsnānaṃ vidhāyātha nityakarma samāpya ca ॥5॥
gaṇeśaṃ pūjayitvaiva svastivācya dvijarṣabhān |
pūjayitvā vaśiṣṭhaṃ sa tato'nujñāṃ samāpya ca ॥6॥
gaṇeśe mana ādhāya tannāmajapatatparaḥ |
ekāṃguṣṭhasthitastāvadyāvadastamiyādraviḥ ॥7॥
punaḥ snānaṃ cakārāśu sāyaṃsaṃdhyāmupāsya ca |
prārabhatpūjanaṃ samyagvasiṣṭhena samanvitaḥ ॥8॥
mahāmaṃḍapikāṃ kṛtvā kadalīstaṃbhamaṃḍitām |
nānālaṃkāravastrāḍhyāṃ chatracāmaraśobhitām ॥9॥
ādarśapaktirucirāṃ puppamālāvibhūṣitām |
nānāmaṇiprabhāyuktāṃ dīpamālā virājitām ॥10॥
tanmadhye svarṇakalaśe sthāpayitvā gajānanīm |
sauvarṇīṃ pratimāṃ cāru sarvāvayavasuṃdarām ॥11॥
nānālaṃkārarucirāṃ nānāratnavibhūṣitām |
paṭhatsu dvijamukhyeṣu gāyatsu gāyakeṣu ca ॥12॥
nadatsu sarvatūryeṣu nṛtyatsu nartakeṣu ca |
vaidikaiścaiva paurāṇaistāṃ mūrtiṃ samapūjayat ॥13॥
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ paṃcāmṛta puraḥsaram |
modakāpūpalaḍḍūka pāyasānnaṃ saśarkaram ॥14॥
nānāvyaṃjanaśobhāḍhyaṃ paṃcāmṛta samanvitam |
naivedyaṃ purataḥ sthāpya jalaṃ ca cārugaṃdhayuk ॥15॥
samarpayāmāsa nṛpo gajānanasutuṣṭaye |
phalāni pūga tāṃbūlaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ ratnakāṃcanīm ॥16॥
dūrvāṃ nirājanaṃ mantrapuppāṃjalīnanekadhā |
tithaye gaṇanāthāya candrāyārghyaṃ dadāvatha ॥17॥
brāhmaṇānpūjayāmāsa sādaraṃ cāpyabūbhujat |
ayutaṃ gā dadau tebhyo vastrālaṃkāradakṣiṇāḥ ॥18॥
tataḥ suhṛdbaṃdhuyutaḥ svayaṃ ca bubhuje nṛpaḥ |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ cakre gītavādyakathādibhiḥ ॥19॥
prabhāte vimale snātvā punaḥ saṃpūjya pūrvavat |
mūtiṃ tāṃ gaṇanāthasya vasiṣṭhāya sadakṣiṇām ॥20॥
sopaskarāṃ dadau rājā tatastuṣṭo gajānanaḥ |
vimānaṃ preṣayāmāsa sarvasaṃpad virājitam ||21||
taddūtaiḥ sthāpitastatra gajānana svarūpavān |
paśyatāṃ sarvalokānāṃ mana ānandayan muhuḥ ॥22॥
calito devalokāya rājā puṇyaprabhāvataḥ |
tamūcurdevadūtāste gajānanasvarūpiṇaḥ ॥23॥
nānālaṃkāraśobhāḍhyā mukuṭākrāṃtamastakāḥ |
vināyakena tuṣṭena vimānaṃ preṣitaṃ tava ||24॥
tvaddarśanautsukyadhṛtā tato vayamupāgatāḥ |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā mumocāśrūṇi bhūmipaḥ॥25॥
jagāda gadgadgirā romāṃcāṃcitavigrahaḥ |
tānuvāca tato rājā śūrasenaḥ smayanniva ॥26॥
avyaktasyāprameyasya nitasya jagadīśituḥ |
vāṅmano gocarasyāpi na heturmama darśane ॥27॥
na samarthāśca yaṃ vedāḥ śāstrāṇi ca nirūpitama |
yaṃ ca smaranti satataṃ keśādyā devatāgaṇāḥ ॥28॥
tenāhaṃ cet smṛto dūtāstarhi bhāgyaṃ mahanmama |
ityākarṇya tu tadvākyaṃ dūtā ūcuḥ punarnṛpam ॥29॥
na jānīmo vayaṃ rājan bhaktānāṃ mahimā katham |
nirguṇaṃ yannirākāraṃ yena sākāratāmiyāt॥30॥
rājovāca |
ekā me mahatī vāṃchā tuṣṭe deve bhavatsu ca |
tuṣṭeṣu nagarīṃ hitvā kathaṃ yāyāṃ gajānanam ॥31॥
na bhakṣitaṃ vinā'pyatairmayā hālāhalaṃ kvacit |
sa kathaṃ paramānandaṃ bhokṣyaṃ etairvinā'naghāḥ ॥32॥
punarūcuśca taṃ dūtāstvadicchāṃ paripūryatām |
na ced gajānanaḥ krodhāttāḍayiṣyati no'khilān ॥33॥
brahmovāca |
tataḥ sarvajanaṃ netuṃ vimāne nidadhuḥ kṣaṇāt |
caturā karajā brahmannikṣiptāḥ sarvajantavaḥ ॥34॥
sarve divyāmbarā divyavastrālaṃkāraśobhinaḥ |
ūcuḥ parasparaṃ sarve kimetadadbhutaṃ mahat ॥35॥
asmābhirna kṛtaṃ puṇyaṃ vimānaṃ kathamīdṛśam |
apare cābruvaṃstatra rājapuṇyabalādidam ॥36॥
yathā nidhibalāddhātumātrasya hāṭakaṃ bhavet |
atipāpe yathā sādhuvacasā siddhimāpnuyāt ॥37॥
tathaiva rājapuṇyena vayaṃ sarve samuddhṛtāḥ |
tato vaināyakā dūtā gatimūrdhvāmakalpayan ॥38॥
jaḍībhūtaṃ vimānaṃ tannodatiṣṭhan mahītalāt|
tataḥ saṃśayitaṃ sarvaṃ kathametaddivaṃ vrajet ॥39॥
ūcuḥ parasparaṃ lokā nirdaivasya nidhiḥ kutaḥ |
ārurukṣeta kathaṃ bhikṣāpātraṃ śikyaṃ mahattaram ॥40॥
dadṛśuḥ sarvataḥ kecit kuṣṭhī dṛṣṭipathaṃ gataḥ |
uvāca kaścidrājānaṃ kuṣṭhinaṃ tvamimaṃ tyaja ॥41॥
āsminnadho gate yānamūrdhvametad bhaviṣyati |
śūrasena uvācaitān dūtāṃstaṃ tyaktumicchataḥ | 42॥
ahaṃ pāpasamācārastyājyo neyaḥ ime'khilāḥ |
athavā kathyatāmasya pūrvapāpaṃ ca janma ca ॥43॥
mat snehāt sarvavetṛtvādupāyaṃ kṛpayā'pi ca |
dūtā ūcuḥ |
na kāryā nṛpate cintā vadiṣyāmo vayaṃ tava ॥44॥
janmapāpamupāyaṃ ca kuṣṭhino duṣṭa karma ca ॥45॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe paṃcasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥75॥

<adhyāya 76 prāraṃbhaḥ>

dūtā ūcuḥ |
gauṃḍe tu viṣaye gauḍanagare'bhūtpurā dvijaḥ |
tapasvī jñānavān prājño devabrāhmaṇa pūjakaḥ |
tasya putro'yamajani jananī cāsya śākinī ॥1॥
tasya patnī tu sāvitrī sāvitrīva pativratā |
ekaputrasnehavaśādalaṃkārairalaṃkṛtaḥ ॥2॥
atyaṃtasuṃdarataro ratibharteva śobhate |
tanmātā pitarau tasya viyogaṃ necchataḥ kṣaṇam ॥3॥
sa tu yauvanamāpanno nijabhāryāṃ vihāya tām |
paradārarato nityaṃ paraniṃdāparāyaṇaḥ ॥4॥
abhavatpāpaniṣṭhaśca pitṛvākyāvamardanaḥ |
ekadā nagare tasmin veśyā nara vimohinī ॥5॥
āgatā tadgatamanā yaccakre'sau śṛṇuṣva tat |
svālaṃkārān peṭikāyāṃ niṣkāsyā sthāpayadbalāt ॥6॥
mātāpitroḥ samakṣaṃ ca paścāccauryaṃ punaśca tān |
veśyāyai pratipādyaitāṃścikrīḍa subhṛśaṃ tayā ॥7॥
hitvendriyārthān sakalān sugaṃdhadravyasaṃyutaḥ |
tadekaniṣṭhaḥ saṃjāto yogī brahmaparo yathā ॥8॥
kāmāgnivihvalaścāsīt kṣībo madyabalādyathā |
pitā tu khedasaṃpannaḥ kṣutṛṅabhyāṃ parivarjitaḥ ॥9॥
vilokayāmāsa sutaṃ nagare pratimandiram |
alabdhadarśanastasya śvāsocchvāsaparāyaṇaḥ ॥10॥
niśīthe svapriyāṃ prāha budhaḥ putro gataḥ kva nu|
vinā tena vṛthā gehaṃ vinā dīpaṃ yathā niśi ॥11॥
vinā jalaṃ vṛthā vāpī vināpatyaṃ yathā'balā |
kadā nu darśanaṃ tasya bhavitā prāṇarakṣakam ॥12॥
śākinyuvāca |
kṣudhātṛṣā pariśrāntā cintāśoka samanvitā |
tiṣṭhāmi nātha no jāne kva gataḥ priyabālakaḥ ॥13॥
yadi me darśanaṃ tasya syājjīvāmi na cānyathā |
punaścacāla dūrvaḥ sa yaṣṭimādāya muṣṭinā ॥14॥
yaṃ yaṃ paśyati mārge'smiṃstaṃ taṃ pṛcchati cātmajam |
yadā śrāntaḥ kṣudhākrāntastadā bhrāntaśca so'bhavat ॥15॥
anivṛddho mahābhīmo nāmnā bhīmo'ntyavarṇajaḥ |
sa tena pṛṣṭastanayaṃ tenāpyuktaṃ paraṃ sphuṭam ॥16॥
bhīma uvāca |
veśyāgṛhe te tanayo budho'sau, budhaḥ sukhaṃ kroḍati kāmasaktaḥ |
kasyātmajo kā jananī pitā vā, vṛthaiva khedaṃ kuruṣe dvijeṃdra ॥17॥
dūrva uvāca |
kathaṃ mama suto jāto veśyāyāṃ nirato budhaḥ |
tasyā gṛhaṃ jagāmāśu dṛṣṭavāṃstatra taṃ sutam ॥18॥
unmattaṃ madirāsaktaṃ nayanaṃ madavihvalam |
pitā dadhāra taṃ haste ruṣāśikṣāpayatsutam ॥19॥
tvattaḥ kaṃṭakavṛkṣo'yaṃ pāṣāṇo vā mato'dhikaḥ |
kathaṃ na tyajase prāṇān ki syāttairjīvitaṃ khala ॥20॥
brahmovāca | pitṛvākyaṃ samākarṇya roṣāviṣṭo budhaḥ sutaḥ |
talaprahāraṃ taṃḍe'sya dattavān pitureva saḥ ॥21॥
uktavān kṛmikīṭādī sādhāraṇamukhe katham |
kṛtavānasi me vighnaṃ krīḍākāle narādhama ॥22॥
akasmātkākasya śakṛta patitaṃ mayi vā katham |
punarlattāprahāreṇa prāṇāṃstasya gṛhītavān ॥23॥
pitrā prāṇeśu tyakteṣu hareśvareti so'vadat |
nananda sa sutaḥ pāde baddhvā taṃ dūrato'kṣipat ॥24॥
madhu pītvā punā reme veśyayā sa yathecchakam |
prātarjagāma svagṛhaṃ dadarśa jananī ca tam ॥25॥
āliliṃga sutaṃ harṣāt snehasnuta payodharā |
babhāṇa kva gatāścāsi kva sthitaḥ kiṃ kṛtaṃ tvayā ॥26॥
vada me vistarādvatsa pitā te duḥkhito bhṛśam |
ahaṃ cāpi nirāhārā nirjalā niśi jāgratī ॥27॥
pratīkṣamāṇā vatsa tvāṃ pitā'nveṣṭuṃ gataścirāt |
gaccha tvaṃ pitaraṃ draṣṭumityuvāca punaḥpunaḥ ॥28॥
tayājñaptamiti krodhācchuṣkakāṣṭhena tāṃ sutaḥ |
tāḍayāmāsa śirasi patitā sā dharātale ॥29॥
niścetanāṃ parijñāya baddhvā pāśe'kṣipad bahiḥ |
svayaṃ veśyāgṛhaṃ gatvā cikrīḍa bhṛśaharṣitaḥ ॥30॥
dadahuste janāḥ santo daṃpatī svasvakāṣṭhataḥ |
na śāsti rājā daṃḍyaṃ taṃ brāhmaṇatvād dvijādhamam ॥31॥
punargṛhāgataṃ taṃ tu patnīvākyaṃ śanairjagau |
sāvitryuvāca |
prāṇanātha vadāmyekaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva mahāmate ॥32॥
atikhyāte'tivimale varṇe janma bhavādṛśām |
ācāraścānyathā duṣṭastyaktavyaḥ sa vivekataḥ ॥33॥
ihaloke tu tatkāryaṃ yena saukhyaṃ pare bhaveta|
pūrve vayasi tatkāryaṃ yena śaṃ vārdhake bhavet ॥34॥
aṣṭamāseṣu tatkuryādyena varṣāsu śaṃ bhavet |
divase karma tatkāryaṃ yena rātrau sukhaṃ bhavet ॥35॥
pituḥ pritatvādrājā'pi brāhmaṇatvāt kṣamatyasau |
sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇāṃ sundarāmanusāriṇīm ॥36॥
tyakvā mām dharmapatnīṃ tvaṃ veśyāyāṃ nirataḥ katham |
loko nindati sarvatra bhayāttvāṃ na vadatyasau ॥37॥
sa māṃ janaḥ pratibrūte patiste kīdṛśaḥ śubhe |
lajjayā'dhomukhī prāṇāṃstyaktumīhe tadaiva ca ॥38॥
yatheṣṭaṃ cenmayā sārdhaṃ ramate'harniśaṃ pate |
na ko'pi kiṃcid brūyāttvāṃ doṣo vā na bhavenmahān ॥39॥
hitaṃ cetkuru madvākyaṃ tyaktvā tāṃ svavivekataḥ |
ajñānādvā pramādādvā kṛtaṃ karma tyajedbudhaḥ ॥40॥
idaṃ kṛtaṃ mamaiva syādiṣṭaṃ naiva kṛtaṃ yadi |
aniṣṭaṃ sarvathā me syāttanna kāryaṃ viniścayāt ॥41॥
anyathā kurvataḥ puṃsa ihaloko na vā paraḥ |
iti striyā vākyabāṇairbudho viddho hi marmasu ॥42॥
roṣāviṣṭo mahākrūro jagāda prajvalanniva |
budha uvāca |
nirlajje niṣṭhure prauḍhe duṣṭe ruṣṭe mayi sphuṭam ॥43॥
gamiṣyāsi gatiṃ tāṃ tvaṃ yathā tau pitarau gatau |
sāvitryuvāca |
yena pitrorna maryādā śikṣitrośca svataṃtrayoḥ ॥44॥
rakṣitā tad viparītāṃ sa māṃ trātā kathaṃ bhavet |
bhartṛhastānmṛtiḥ śreyaḥ paratreha ca sātūstriyāḥ ॥45॥
brahmovāca |
evaṃ bruvantyāṃ patnyāṃ sa dhammillaṃ sahasā'grahīt |
kāṣṭhairloṣṭairmuṣṭibhiśca pāṣāṇaistāmatāḍayat ॥46॥
sā rāmaṃ bhartṛrūpeṇa sasmāra pūrvapuṇyataḥ |
prāṇāṃstatyāja sahasā bhinnā marmasu tena ha ॥47॥
svargaṃ gatvā divyadehā bubhuje paramaṃ sukham |
ākṛṣya caraṇe tāṃ sa cikṣepa dūrato niśi ॥48॥
niraṃkuśo harṣayuto reme veśyā samanvitaḥ |
tvadarthaṃ nāśitā sarve budhastāmiti so'bravīt ॥49॥
tato bahugate kāle budho'gātkālabhergṛham |
tasmin snātuṃ gate duṣṭo bhāryāṃ tasyāgrahītkace ॥50॥
atyaṃtaṃ suṃdaratarāmāliliṃga cucumba ca |
niḥśaṃkaṃ śayane nītvā mamarda kucamaṃḍalīm ॥51॥
reme tayā yatheṣṭaṃ sa tataḥ sā taṃ śaśāpa ha|
sulabhovāca |
kathaṃ nāma kṛtaṃ duṣṭaṃ pitṛbhyāṃ sulabheti me ॥52॥
ahaṃ te sulabhā jātā duṣṭabuddhe narādhama |
snātuṃ gate bhartari me kālabhau munipuṃgave ॥53॥
balātkāro mayi kṛto duṣṭaḥ kuṣṭhī bhaviṣyasi |
janmāntare te nāmāpi na gṛhṇīyājjanaḥ kvacit ॥54॥
brahmovāca |
tato bhīto yayau veśyāgṛhameva budhaḥ punaḥ |
reme tayā surāṃ pītvā na kiṃcidapi cintayan ॥55॥
evaṃ duṣṭāni karmāṇi vaktavyāni kathaṃ mayā|
puṇyakṣayo bhavettasya paradoṣaṃ ya īrayet ॥56॥
sa kālānnidhanaṃ prāpto dūtairnīto yamālayam |
yamenoktaṃ kimānīto nīyatāṃ narakeṣyayam ॥57॥
yamavākyaṃ samākarṇya dūtā ninyustadaiva tam|
cikṣipurnarake tāvadyāvadābhūtasaṃplavam ॥58॥
bhuktabhogaḥ sa vaiśyasya gṛhe janma samāptavān |
ṛṣipatnyāḥ sa śāpena kuṣṭhī jāto bhṛśaṃ tadā ॥59॥
pitṛhā mātṛhā strīhā madyapī gurūtalpagaḥ |
tasya saṃsparśanādeva sacailaṃ snānamācaret ॥60॥
nāmāpyetasya na grāhyaṃ mahādoṣakaraṃ yataḥ |
etādṛśo'yaṃ duṣṭātmā yadā tyaktaṃ itastadā ॥61॥
vimānaṃ yāsyate cordhvaṃ nūnaṃ nāstyatra saṃśayaḥ ॥62॥
brahmovāca |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ teṣāṃ dūtānāṃ bhṛśakaṃpitaḥ ॥63॥
śūrasena uvācaitānna mayā'jñāyi duṣkṛtam |
punarutthāya rājā'sau praṇanāma bhṛśāturaḥ ॥64॥
prāha tāndevadūtānsa kṛpāṃ kṛtvā vadantu me|
upāyamasya duṣṭasya sarvadoṣāpanuttaye ॥65॥
dūtā ūcuḥ |
uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha nṛpate brūmo doṣāpanuttaye |
upāyaṃ rājaśārdūla śṛṇuśvaikamanā bhava ॥66॥
gaṇeśasya bhavennāma yat khyātaṃ caturakṣaram |
japa tat karṇarandhre'sya sarvadoṣakṣayo bhavet ॥67॥
udaye dinanāthasya yathā sarvatamaḥ kṣayaḥ |
nānyopāye'dhikāro'sya yato nāmaiva japyatām ॥68॥
brahmovāca |
śūraseno dūtavākyājjayapūrvaṃ jajāpa ha |
trivāraṃ vaiśyakarṇe tu nāma yaccaturakṣaram ॥69॥
gajānaneti śrutvaiva divyadeho'bhavacca saḥ |
bhāsayaṃstejasā sarvamaruṇo bhuvanaṃ yathā ॥70॥
sarvapāpakṣaye jāte nāmaśravaṇamātrataḥ |
dīpayan sarvadigdeśān vimānaṃ cādhyarohata ॥71॥
tadvimānaṃ kṣaṇād dūtaiḥ sarvalokasamanvitam |
nītaṃ vaināyakaṃ dhāma vighnarājyasya śāsanāt ॥72॥
iti sarvaṃ samākhyātaṃ yadyatpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā'nagha ॥73॥
mahāpuṇyaṃ ca saṃkaṣṭacaturthīvratamuttamam |
dharmyaṃ yaśasyamāyuṣyaṃ śravaṇāt sarvasiddhidam ॥74॥
sarvapīḍāpraśamanaṃ sarvaṃ vighna vināśanam |
dūrvānāma prabhāvaśca kimanyacchrotumicchasi॥75|| 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe ṣaṭsaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥76॥

<adhyāya 77 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
anyenākāri kenedaṃ vada tatkautukaṃ mahat |
brahmovāca |
jāmadagnyena rāmeṇa vratametat purā kṛtam ॥1॥
so'pi kīrti jayaṃ jñānamāyurdīrghaṃ ca labdhavān |
vyāsa uvāca |
rāmaḥ kathaṃ samutpannaḥ kasmāt kasyāṃ pitāmaha ॥2॥
etatsarvaṃ suvistāryaṃ brūhi tvaṃ mama pṛcchataḥ |
brahmovāca |
śvetadvīpe'ti vikhyāto jamadagnirmahāmuniḥ ॥3॥
manasā sṛṣṭisaṃhārānnigrahānugrahānapi |
yaḥ karoti trikālajñastato devāścakampire ॥4॥
yasyāsīt patnyapi tathā reṇukā nāma nāmataḥ |
yasyā lāvaṇyaguṃjāpi patnyāḥ kāmasya nābhavat ॥5॥
ratirityeva lokeṣu vikhyātā'bhūttatastu sā |
yayā saṃmohyate sarvaṃ kṣamastadvarṇane tu kaḥ ॥6॥
yannetraśobhā saṃprāptyai cakorā jalavāsinaḥ |
hariṇāśca tapaścesa rvane chandatṛṇāśinaḥ ॥7॥
yadāsya śobhā saṃprāptyai sevate candra īśvaram|
anādinidhanā devī mūlaprakṛtirīśvarī ॥8॥
syāmajani rāmo'sau viṣṇuryogeśvaro yathā |
jamadagnermahābhāgāt sākṣādīśvararūpiṇaḥ ॥9॥
atisundara gātro'sau sākṣānmanmatha manmathaḥ |
mātāpitṛvacaḥ kartā vikhyātabalapauruṣaḥ ॥10॥
devadvijaguruprājña gobodhipūjane rataḥ |
vedavedāṃgaśāstrāṇāṃ smṛtīnāmapi pāṭhakaḥ ॥11॥
vākye bṛhaspatisamaḥ kṣamayā pṛthivisamaḥ |
gāṃbhīrye'bdhisamo yastu pitrorvākyaparāyaṇaḥ ॥12॥
pitrorājñāṃ gṛhītvā sa nimeṣāraṇyamāyayau |
anekavidyābhyasane matiṃ kṛtvā parāṃ sthirām ॥13॥
tasmingate tadā rājā kārtavīryo mahābalaḥ |
yasya pratāpādabhavadvaśyaṃ bhūmaṃḍalaṃ sadā ॥14॥
yasmiṃllakṣmī sthirā viṣṇoraṃśabhūte'khilārthade |
ājau paṃcaśataṃ bāṇān sṛjate ripughātakān ॥15॥
indrādivṛndasevyo'sau vikhyātabalapauruṣaḥ |
asaṃkhyātā gajā yasya rathavājipadātigaḥ ॥16॥
yasya nirgamane sainyāścchādayanti mahītalam |
varṣākāle yathā meghā dhārābhirvyomamaṃḍalam ॥17॥
yasya kaṃbusvanaṃ śrutvā dviṣo yāṃti diśo daśa |
yathā siṃhasvaṃ śrutvā mattamātaṃgakoṭayaḥ ॥18॥
yadā tālī paṃcaśataṃ svecchayā kurute nṛpaḥ |
tadā sanādaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ kampate munisattama ॥19॥
asau yadṛcchayā yattaścaturaṃgabalānvitaḥ |
nīlavastradharo nīla chatrastādṛśasainikaḥ ॥20॥
vanāni sarito durgān parvatāṃśca viloḍayan |
nānāmṛgagaṇānvīkṣya vinighnannāpi kāṃścana ॥21॥
tataḥ sahayādriśikhare'paśyadāśramamuttamam |
yathā kailāsaśikhare giriśālayamuttamam||22॥
apṛcchat sevakān rājā kasyedamuttamaṃ sthalam ॥23॥
bhṛtya uvāca |
jamadagniriti khyātaḥ sākṣātsūrya ivāparaḥ |
munirāste mahābhāga śāpānugrahakārakaḥ ||24॥
yasya darśanataḥ pāpakoṭayo yānti saṃkṣayam |
icchā cettama gantavyaṃ darśanaṃ te bhaviṣyati ॥25॥
tavaprasādāt sarveṣāmupakāro bhaviṣyati |
brahmovāca |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā gamanāyodyato nṛpaḥ ॥26॥
nyaṣedhayat sarvabalaṃ śreṣṭhānādāya tat kṣaṇāt |
yayau taṃ muniśārdūlaṃ jamadagniṃ taponidhim ॥27॥
dadarśa taṃ kuśāsīnaṃ jvalantamiva pāvakam |
praṇanāma sa sāṣṭāṃgaṃ te sarve sahayāyinaḥ ॥28॥
nānāviśālaśālāsu kuṃjavṛkṣāvalīṣu ca|
niṣeduḥ sainikāste tu rājā tasthau muneḥ paraḥ ॥29॥
āsane muninādiṣṭe caturbhiḥ parivāritaḥ |
niṣasāda tadā rājā kṛtavīryasuto balī ॥30॥
apūjayat tānsarvān pādyārghyaviṣṭarādibhiḥ |
sa muniḥ pradadau gāśca śiṣyaiḥ sarvānapūjayat ॥31॥
nānāsarovarajale nirmale cāruśītale |
amajjanta tadā sarve sainikā mṛgayārditāḥ ॥32॥
śṛṇvanvedaninādāṃśca śāstraśabdāṃśca puṣkalān |
abhito vādaśabdāṃśca tacchiṣyaiḥ samudīritān ॥33॥
abravīnmunimukhyaṃ taṃ rājā rājīvalocanaḥ|
adya me pitarau dhanyau janma jñānaṃ tathā tapaḥ ॥34॥
puṇyavṛkṣo'dya phalito yajjātaṃ tava darśanam |
adya me saṃpado dhanyāḥ kula dhanyaṃ yaśo'pi me ॥35॥
parabrahmeti yat proktaṃ tadeva tvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |
ātithyaṃ tava dṛṣṭvaivaṃ parituṣṭaṃ mano mama ॥36॥
iti śrutvā tu tadbrāhmīṃ smayanniva prasannadhīḥ |
ko'si kasyāsi kiṃ nāma kiṃ kāryaṃ tava vartate ॥37॥
iti taṃ paripapraccha jānannapi munistadā |
rājovāca |
rājñāṃ svakāryaṃ kartṛṇāṃ bhavādṛśa nirīkṣaṇāt ॥38॥
nānyat prayojanamurū śreyasā'jani tatsphuṭam |
kṛtavīryasuto'haṃ ca kārtavīrya iti śrutaḥ ॥39॥
anujñāto gamiṣyāmi tato'haṃ nagaraṃ prati |
muniruvāca |
mayā'śrāvi mahākīrtistata rājanyasattama ॥40॥
āsītvaddarśanākāṃkṣā jātā puṇyairna satphalā |
deha ātmā tapo jñānamāśramo mama sāṃpratam ॥41॥
abhūttvadāgamādrājan sārthakāḥ sarvasampadaḥ |
akṛtvā bhojanaṃ kiṃcitkathaṃ tvaṃ gaṃtumarhasi | 42||
mama ślāghā bhavelloke nyūnaṃ ki tava vidyate |
kiṃcid bhuktvā vraja vibho sanāthaṃ kuru māṃ prabho ॥43॥
rājovāca |
satyaṃ bhojanakālo'sti bhoktavyaṃ ca tavājñayā|
annābhāve śrotriyāṇāṃ jalaṃ yācca pibedapi ॥44॥
tathā'pyetān vihāyāhaṃ sainikān gaṇanātigān |
jalaṃ pātuṃ na śaknomi kathaṃ bhoktuṃ samutsahe ॥45॥
jāne'haṃ manasā brahmanna śaktiḥ sarvabhojane|
bhavaddarśanamātreṇa kṛtakṛtyo vraje'dhunā ॥46॥
muniruvāca |
cintāṃ mā kuru rājarṣe bhojayiṣye sasainikam |
caturvidhena cānnena kimasādhyaṃ tapasvinām ॥47॥
gṛhe te vāhinī yā syāttāmapyākāraya prabho|
kṣaṇaṃ viśrāmyatāṃ tīre nadyāścārutare śubhe ॥48॥
yāvatsiddhaṃ bhavedannaṃ paścāddrakṣyasi kautukam |
ka uvāca |
antaḥ sa vismayāviṣṭo jagāma kṛtavīryajaḥ ॥49॥
vākyamākarṇya tu munernadītīraṃ suśobhanam |
muniḥ patnīṃ samāhūya vṛttāṃtaṃ taṃ jagāda tām ॥50॥
āhūya kāmadhenuṃ tau pupūjatuḥ kṣaṇena tām |
prārthayāmāsaturubhau lajjāṃ nau rakṣa dhenuke ॥51॥
asaṃkhyātabalo rājā bhojanāya nimaṃtritaḥ |
yathāruci bhavettasya sa sainyasya bhujiḥ śubhe ॥52॥
tathā kuru kṣaṇena tvaṃ no cet satyaṃ layaṃ vrajeta |
akīrtiśca bhavelloke yathecchasi tathā kuru ॥53॥
evaṃ saṃprārthitā tābhyāṃ kāmadhenurmahāpuram |
nirmāya svaprabhāvena nānāmaṃdirasuṃdaram ॥54॥
nānāratnamayaṃ stambhaṃ śobhinānā sabhāgṛham |
nānāpuṣpalatācārū vāṭikārāmamaṃḍitam ॥55॥
nānādhvajapatākāḍhyaṃ nānāvāditraniḥsvanam |
aṣṭāpadānāṃ bhāṃḍānāṃ nānā paṃktivirājitam ॥56॥
caturvidhānnasaṃpannaṃ nānābhojanapaṃktimat |
mahātoraṇaśobhāḍhyaṃ parikhāvalayāṃkitam ॥57॥
anekadāsadāsībhirvilasaccāru vedikam |
nivārayanti lokāśca yatra tatra sthitā narāḥ ॥58॥
puro gaṃtuṃ na cājñā'sti jamadagnimuneriti |
pariviṣṭeṣu pātreṣu saṃkhyātīteṣu sarvataḥ ॥59॥
dīpaprabhā bhāsureṣu nānāvyaṃjanaśāliṣu |
sūpa pāyasa pakvānna paṃcāmṛtayuteṣu ca ॥60॥
sakhādya lehyacoṣyādi peya paṃktivirājiṣu |
sāmudrapakva nimbābhrāmṛta bilvādiśāliṣu ॥61॥
siddheṣu pātreṣu munistadā'sāvākārayacchiṣyagaṇaṃ tadānīm |
śrīkāmadhenoḥ paripūrṇa toṣāda , bhoktuṃ sasainyaṃ kṛtavīryaputram ॥62॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe saptasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥77॥

<adhyāya 78 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
tato muniḥ śiṣyagaṇamuvāca tvarayā vraja |
ākārayituṃ nṛpatiṃ nadītīra nivāsinam ॥1॥
te śiṣyāstaṃ nṛpaṃ gatvā gṛhya tat praṇatiṃ purā|
datvā''śīrvacanaṃ tasmai munerājñāṃ tathābruvan ॥2॥
cala rājannavahito bhojanāya sasainikaḥ |
pātrāṇi pariviṣṭāni saṃkhyātītānī ṣaḍrasaiḥ ॥3॥
ka uvāca |
tata utthāya rājā'sau samāhūya sasainikān |
susnātaḥ sarvasainyena kārtavīryo nṛpo yayau ॥4॥
muniveśma tato'paśyanna dṛṣṭaṃ na ca vai śrutam |
etādṛśaṃ trilokeṣu gṛhatoraṇa māpasā ॥5॥
sa niṣiddho vetradharairmunisaṃketa kāmukaiḥ |
te niṣiddhāḥ śiṣyagaṇaistato'gād bhojanālayam ॥6॥
sthitvā madhye paśyati sma rājā'sau saṃpadaṃ muneḥ |
amanyat tadā svānte nedṛśī śrīrharau hare ॥7॥
pālake'pālake sraṣṭaryapi brahmaṇi nedṛśī|
tato muniḥ puro bhūtvā pratipātre nyaveśayat ॥8॥
rājānumatimājñāya paṃktau paṃktau sasainikān |
dāpayāmāsa pātrāṇi ye bahirbhojinaśca tān ॥9॥
upaviṣṭeṣu sarveṣu rājānamupaveśayat |
bubhujuste tadā vādye vādite sarva eva hi ॥10॥
adṛṣṭapūrvāṇyannāni svādumūlaphalāni ca |
parasparaṃ ca papracchuḥ kimidaṃ kimidaṃ tviti ॥11॥
āścaryaṃ menire sarve yāmenetthaṃ kathaṃ kṛtam |
yatheṣṭaṃ bhuṃjate sarve tṛptāścānnāni tatyajuḥ ॥12॥
te prakṣālanapātrāṇi pratipātre nyavedayan |
muniśiṣyāṃjalaṃ cāpi śalākā api cāravaḥ ॥13॥
gajāśvavṛṣavṛndebhyaḥ śiṣyāḥ śeṣānnamarpayana |
tato'nyasmin gṛhe sarve vistiīrṇe kṛtasaṃstare ॥14॥
ikṣudrākṣāmrapanasa dāḍimāni babhakṣire |
elālavaṃga karpūra cūrṇakhādira pūgakān ॥15॥
jagṛhurmunidattāṃste nāgavallīdalairyutān |
tato dadāvalaṃkārān vāsāṃsi ca mudā muniḥ ॥16॥
tebhyo yathārha sarvebhyo rājñe'narghyaitarāṇi ca |
muniruvāca |
kiṃ mayā bhojanaṃ deyaṃ muninā vanyavṛttinā॥17॥
avāptasarvakāmāya rājñe te viṣṇurūpiṇe |
yaśo'kāri triloke me tvayā madvākyakāriṇā ॥18॥
jamadagnergṛhe rājñā sasainyena bhujiḥ kṛtā |
atipuṇyavato loke sādhuśabdaḥ prayujyate ॥19॥
mahān kṣudrasya vākyaṃ cet kurūte sopi sādhutām |
prāpnoti sarvalokeṣu kīrtiṃ sphītāṃ samutkaṭām ॥20॥
madvākyakaraṇe te'pi dūre'riṣṭaṃ gataṃ mahat |
brahmovāca |
iti vāṇīṃ samākarṇya jamadagnisamīritām ॥21॥
papraccha rājā taṃ tatra vismayāviṣṭamānasaḥ |
rājovāca |
nādarśi prathamaṃ kiṃcididaṃ kiṃ māyayā kṛtam ॥22॥
tapaḥ prabhāvādvā'kāri satyaṃ me vada suvrata |
muniruvāca |
anṛtaṃ noktapūrvaṃ me parihāse'pi vā nṛpa ॥23॥
ataḥ satyaṃ bravīmi tvāṃ kāmadhenvā'khilaṃ kṛtam |
brahmovāca |
kumatistasya bhavati yasya daivamadakṣiṇam ॥24॥
aniṣṭaṃ balavadbhāvi duṣṭagrahakṛtaṃ mune|
bhuktvā tṛpto'pi rājā'sau samyak sabalavāhanaḥ ॥25॥
ādāne kāmadhenoḥ sa matiṃ cakre'bravīdidam |
rājovāca |
munīnāṃ śāṃtacittānāṃ kaṃdamūlaphalāśinām ॥26॥
manasā sṛṣṭisaṃhāra kartṛṇāmaspṛhāvatāma |
jitandriyāṇāṃ vijñānāṃ nekṣe dhenvā prayojanam ॥27॥
vanyamāruta bhakṣyāṇāṃ mokṣasādhanakāriṇām |
vedābhyasanaśīlānāṃ sampadā kiṃ prayojanam ॥28॥
śāstrapāṭhanaśīlāṃnā dharmaśāstrārtha vedinām |
yogābhyāsaratānāṃ ca kāmadhenoḥ prayojanam ॥29॥
kāryā sa mahate syāttu mahadvastu mahadgatam |
araṇyavāsine tubhyaṃ mahadratnaṃ ca nocitam ॥30॥
tasmāddeyā kāmadhenuṃ mama brahman mudā tvayā |
mayi sthitā tavaivāsti cetasi tviti dhāryatām ॥31॥
maryādā rakṣa tāṃ brahmannuktamevaṃ purastava |
no cedrājñāṃ kimagrāhayaṃ lokeṣu balaśālinām ॥32॥
svarāṣṭre pararāṣṭraṃ vā caturaṃgabalaiḥ saha |
brahmovāca |
śrutvaivaṃ vacanaṃ tasya kārtavīryasya durmateḥ ॥33॥
pratijajvāla roṣeṇa jamadagnirmahāmuniḥ|
sarpiṣā bahulenaiva jātavedā yathā dvijaḥ ॥34॥
uvācāraktanayanaḥ śikṣayanniva bhūmipam |
muniruvāca |
sādhuḥ śuddhī mahān rājā prārthitāṃ bhojanāya hi ॥35॥
kauṭilyaṃ tava na jñātaṃ bakasyeva hṛdi sthitam |
yathā vā kokilā kāka bālaṃ puṣṇāti māyayā ॥36॥
ante tat kākabhāvena bhakṣyābhakṣyaratiṃ vrajet |
ahamevābhavaṃ bhrānto ya imāṃ rājamitratām ॥37॥
na dṛṣṭāṃ na śrutāṃ loke nānubhūtāṃ ca kenacit ॥38॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe kārtavīryopākhyānaṃ nāmāṣṭasaptatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥78॥

<adhyāya 79 prāraṃbhaḥ>

muniruvāca |
upakārastvayā'rambhi sādhuvṛttānusāriṇā |
ātmaghātaṃ puraskṛtvā kāmadhenuṃ tvamicchasi ॥1॥
bhrānto'si nṛpa satyaṃ tvamaprāpyaṃ yo'bhivāṃchasi |
trailokyanāśajaṃ pāpaṃ tava mūrdhni patiṣyati ॥2॥
brahmovāca |
iti vākśaraviddho'sau pralayānalasannibhaḥ |
rājā babhūva roṣeṇa mukhādanalamudvaman ॥3॥
uvāca paramakruddho jamadagniṃ muniṃ tadā|
rājovāca |
na mayā duṣṭavacanaṃ kasyāpi śrūyate śaṭha ॥4॥
kiṃ karomi dvijo'si tvamiti kṣāntaṃ vacaḥ kaṭu |
ka uvāca |
tata utthāya rājā'sau dūtānājñāpya satvaram ॥5॥
muktvā kīlāt kāmadhenuṃ śīghraṃ yāntu mamāntikam |
āveṣṭayaṃstadā dūtāḥ kāmadhenuṃ tadājñayā ॥6॥
phūtkāreṇaiva tasyāste tyaktvā prāṇāndivaṃ yayuḥ |
anyān krodhānalenāsau rājadūtān dadāha ca ॥7॥
śvāsamārutavegena vyomni vīrāḥ pare'bhraman |
ācchādya maṃḍalabhānorna prājñāyata kiṃcana ॥8॥
diśaḥ satimirā jātā vyomāpi na ca bhāsate |
dharākampaḥ samabhavad vṛkṣāḥ petuḥ prakampitāḥ ॥9॥
sainikāśca tato bhītā yānti sarve diśo daśa |
kenacittāḍitā sā gauḥ kaśāghātena dūrataḥ ॥10॥
uḍḍīyoḍḍīya sā dhenuradhāvat sainyameva tat |
gajavṛndaṃ yathā siṃho garutmānuragāniva ॥11॥
hāhākāro mahānāsīdvīrāṇāṃ tatra dhāvatām |
tānuvāca na bhīḥ kāryā kārtavīryo mahābalī ॥12॥
mayā dhmāte mudā śaṃkhe bhītā yāsyati mandiram |
kāmadhenuḥ kiyaccāsti paśyantu kautukaṃ mama ॥13॥
tato dadhmau mahāśaṃkhaṃ trailokyaṃ pūrayan ravaiḥ |
na bibhyat kāmadhenustāṃ tāḍayāmāsurojasā॥14॥
yaṣṭibhirlohanicayaiḥ sarve te rājasevakāḥ |
yatra yatra prahāro'syāḥ śarīre jāyate tataḥ ॥15॥
sannaddhāḥ sarva śastrāḍhyā nānāvīrāḥ viniḥsṛtāḥ |
śakāśca barbarā āsaṃstasyā keśasamudbhavāḥ ॥16॥
paṭaccarāḥ pādadeśā devaṃ sarvai prajajñire |
nānāyavana jātīyā nānāvīrāstathā'pare ॥17॥
vājivāraṇasaṃghāśca rathino balavattarāḥ |
te caiva yuyudhustatra kārtavīryasya sainikaiḥ ॥18॥
nyapatan kārtavīryasya sainikāstaiḥ prahāritāḥ |
apare militāstatra niśi vṛkṣe pataṃgavat ॥19॥
parasparaghātahatā nipetuḥ śataśo narāḥ |
bhaṅktvā śastraiśca śastrāṇi mallayuddhaṃ pracakrire ॥20॥
evaṃ sutumule janye śastreṣu nipatatsu ca |
svīyo vā parakīyo vā na prājñāyata kiṃcana ॥21॥
rajasācchādite sūrye jaghnurevaṃ parasparam |
kolāhalo mahānāsīd hanyatāṃ hanyatāmiti ॥22॥
heṣitairbṛṃhitaiḥ kṣveḍairnibiḍo rathanemibhiḥ |
mṛdaṃga tāla veṇūnāṃ bherīṇāṃ ninadairapi ॥23॥
evaṃ gavyaiḥ kārtavīryairathyaśvagajapattibhiḥ |
abhavat sumahad yuddhaṃ bhūtarākṣasabhītidam ॥24॥
pakṣijambūkasukhadaṃ vīrapatnī bhayapradam |
keṣāñcijjānunī bhagne keṣāṃcicca śirāṃsi ca ॥25॥
khaḍgakheṭakabhallāṃnāṃ śarāṇāṃ dhanuṣāmapi |
bhagnānāṃ na ca saṃkhyā'sti vīrāṇāṃ rathināpi ॥26॥
prapetuḥ kārtavīryasya śeṣā ye sainikāstadā|
pṛṣṭhalagnā yayurgavyā hasanto na ca jaghnire ॥27॥
ninindurmuninā kiṃ vo'niṣṭaṃ kṛtamitīha te |
pūrvadoṣaprasaṃgena rājño jātā sudurmatiḥ ॥28॥
evaṃ prabhagne sainye sa uttasthau kṛtavīryajaḥ |
hastayorjagṛhe paṃca śataṃ bāṇān dhanūṃṣi ca ॥29॥
dattvā bhūmau vāmajānuṃ dhanūṃṣyākṛṣya vegavān |
cikṣepa śarajālaṃ sa gavye sainye mahābhuja ॥30॥
phalahīnamabhūttasya śarajālaṃ nṛpasya tat |
anīteścaritaṃ tadvad vandhyāyāḥ surataṃ yathā ॥31॥
punaḥpunarjahau bāṇāṃstāvato rājasattama |
kāmadhenorna caiko'pi vīro bhagno'bhavattadā ॥32॥
śarajāle vṛthā yāte saṃtapto'bhūnnṛpastadā |
kva gataṃ mama sāmarthyamiti cintāturo'bhavat ॥33॥
vyākule na prahartavyamiti sarvaṃ yayurdivam |
kāmadhenurmudā yuktā kiṃ yuddhaṃ laghunā saha ॥34॥
gatāyāṃ kāmadhenau sa kārtavīryo muniṃ yayau|
roṣeṇovāca te brahman kāpaṭyaṃ viditaṃ mayā ॥35॥
na sa vipro'bhi mantavyo yasya syātkapaṭaṃ hṛdi |
ityekaṃ bāṇamādāya vivyādha dvijapuṃgavam ॥36॥
lagne hṛdi mahābāṇe prāṇatyāgaṃ cakāra saḥ|
reṇukā taṃ nṛpaṃ prāha brahmahatyā vṛthā kṛtā|॥37॥
sa ūce raktanayanaḥ krodhāviṣṭo nṛpastu tām |
tūṣṇīṃ tiṣṭha na ced hanmi tvāmapīha munipriye ॥38॥
ekaviṃśati bāṇaistāṃ duṣṭo rājā'hanadruṣā |
sasmāra manasā sā'pi jamadagni muniṃ tadā ॥39॥
paścāduvāca nṛpatiṃ duṣṭa cāṃḍāla kiṃ kṛtam |
aparādhaṃ vinā kasmādāvāṃ nihatavānasi ॥40॥
tavāpi bhavitā nāśo mūjānāṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
śrutvetthaṃ vacanaṃ tasyāstato yāto nṛpastadā ॥41॥
alpāvaśiṣṭa sainyena cintākrānto yayau purīm |
vinindan hṛdi cātmānaṃ śocaṃśca mṛtasainikān ॥42॥
nirutsāho nirudyogo viveśa nijamandiram ॥43॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe kārtavīryopākhyāna ekonāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥79॥

<adhyāya 80 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
reṇukā tu gate tasmin śuśoca bhṛśavihvalā |
kva gatā mama putrāste saṃhāre'sminnupasthite ॥1॥
mṛte bhartari kiṃ kuryāṃ bāṇajālasamāvṛtā |
kva gato'sau mahākrodhī rāmo me priyabālakaḥ ॥2॥
tasmin dṛṣṭe mama prāṇā gamiṣyanti surālayam |
smṛtamātrastayā rāmo yayau mātustadā'ntikam ॥3॥
bāṇajālacitāṃ tāṃ ca pitaraṃ mṛtamaikṣata |
kārtavīryeṇa duṣṭena dṛḍhabāṇahataṃ hṛdi ॥4॥
papāta mūrchayā bhūmau vātabhagna iva drumaḥ |
pitaraṃ mātaraṃ caiva ruroda bhṛśaduḥkhitaḥ ॥5॥
rāma uvāca |
adyāndhakāraḥ sarvatra adya śūnyā diśo daśa |
merurvinā yathā pṛthvī śakrahīnā'marāvatī ॥6॥
nāśramaḥ śobhate tadvat pitrā hīno'yamadya me |
trilokī gaṃgayā hīnā tadvadāśrama maṃḍalī ॥7॥
na śobhate reṇukayā hīnā'pyāśrama maṃḍalī|
gataṃ bhayaṃ tu devānāṃ munayo'dya nirīśvarāḥ |
yadāsīttapasā cāsya kimayaṃ vā gṛhīṣyati ॥8॥
evaṃ bahuvidhākāraṃ cakra ākraṃdanaṃ tu saḥ |
aceṣṭata bhṛśaṃ rāmo matsyo nīraṃ vinā yathāṃ ॥9॥
tataḥ punarmāṃtaraṃ sa rudanneva samāyayau |
niṣkāśayāmāsa śarānaṃke kṛtvā śirodharām ॥10॥
cakranda ca punā rāmo mātṛduḥkhena duḥkhitaḥ |
trailokyaṃ bhasmasāt kartuṃ yā kṣamā jananī mama ॥11॥
sā kathaṃ patitā bhūmau duṣṭabāṇa prapīḍatā |
na vismarasi māṃ nityaṃ kṣaṇaṃ krīḍāgataṃ purā ॥12॥
tyaktvā māṃ kathamadya tvaṃ kutra vā gantumudyatā|
dadāsi me payo bhūri vastrāṇyannāni śobhane ॥13॥
phalamūlāni cārūṇi yāsi tyaktvā kathaṃ nu sā |
mātāpitṛbhyāṃ hīnasya dhiṅme jīvitamadya me ॥14॥
brahmovāca |
iti putravacaḥ śrutvā reṇukā bhṛśaduḥkhitā|
pramṛjyāśrūṇi putrasya jagāda bhṛśavihvalā ॥15॥
ahaṃ te nikaṭe sthāsye na śokaṃ kartumarhasi |
śṛṇu vārtāṃ purā vṛttāṃ rājā'sau kṛtavīryajaḥ ॥16॥
madhyāhne saha sainyena prāptaḥ āśramamaṃḍalam|
pitrā te mānitaḥ samyag bhojito balasaṃyutaḥ ॥17॥
kāmadhenoḥ prasādena bhuktvā'yācata dhenukām |
tūṣṇīṃbhūte munau kopāt kāmadhenuṃ mumoca saḥ ॥18॥
spṛṣṭamātrā ca sā dhenuḥ sasarja balamutkaṭam |
caturaṃga tato yuddhamabhavadrājasainikaiḥ ॥19॥
bhagnāste'pyapalansarve rājā yuddhamathākarot |
sa ca paṃcaśataṃ bāṇān vāraṃvāramathāsṛjat ॥20॥
so'pi bhagno gṛhaṃ yātaḥ kāmadhenurdivaṃ gatā |
punarāgatya duṣṭo'sau bāṇenaikena vakṣasi ॥21॥
jaghāna pitaraṃ kopādeka viṃśati sāyakaiḥ |
aparādhaṃ vinā māṃ ca tāḍayitvā gataḥ khalaḥ ॥22॥
tasmāttvayā'dya duṣṭasya nāśaḥ śīghraṃ vidhīyatām |
ekaviṃśati vāraṃ ca kuru niḥkṣatriyāṃ dharām ॥23॥
ekaviṃśati bāṇā me dehe tena yato'rpitāḥ |
anyadekaṃ vade putra tatkuruṣva vaco mama ॥24॥
saṃskāraṃ kuru nau tatra yatra ko'pi na dāhitāḥ |
dattātreyaṃ munivaramākārya sarva vedinam ॥25॥
kuru karmāntaraṃ nau tvaṃ trayodaśadināvadhi |
tadaiva gatimāpsyāvo nānyovaktā'sti tādṛśaḥ ॥26॥
ityuktvā reṇukā dehaṃ tyaktvā dhāmāpa durgamam|
rāmastatsarvamakarottayādiṣṭaṃ mahāmanāḥ ॥27॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe rāmopākhyāne'śītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥80॥

<adhyāya 81 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
rāmastu vapanaṃ kṛtvā snātvā samyavidhānataḥ |
utthāna śrāddhamakarod brāhmaṇaiḥ kathitaṃ yathā ॥1॥
viśrāntisthānamārabhya mantrāgnimubhayoradāt |
tadaiva prayayau taṃ tu dattātreyaṃ muniṃ prati ॥2॥
kuveṣaṃ śiṣya sahitaṃ śvahastaṃ malinaṃ kṛśam |
dhyānenālokya taṃ rāmo nanāma munipuṃgavam ॥3॥
baddhāṃjalipuṭo'tiṣṭhadyāmārdhaṃ tatpuro dvijaḥ |
dattātreyo'pi sarvajño buddhvā tasyāśayaṃ muniḥ ॥4॥
uvāca rāmamāmantrya jñātaṃ tava prayojanam |
athāpi pṛcche vijñātuṃ kimarthaṃ tvamihāgataḥ ॥5॥
ka uvāca |
sa uvāca tato rāmo vṛttāntamāditaḥ sphuṭam |
rāma uvāca |
kṛtavīryātmajo rājā piturāśramamāgataḥ ॥6
bhojitaḥ paramānnena nānāpakvānnaśālinā |
bhuktaḥ sa senayā sārdhaṃ kāmadhenumayācata ॥7॥
yadā na dattā pitrā me balānnetuṃ mano dadhe |
sā tāḍitā'tha suṣuve sainikānāṃ gaṇān bahūn ॥ 8॥
tairbhagno balibhī rājā sasainyo'gānnijālayam |
kāmadhenurgatā svargaṃ tato rājā ruṣānvitaḥ ॥9॥
jamadagnerhṛdi śaramahanad dṛḍhamojasā |
ekaviṃśatikān bāṇān māturaṃge'hanadruṣā ॥10॥
nāhaṃ tatra sthito brahman paścādāgatya dṛṣṭavān |
mantrāgniā tu dagdhau tau māturvāṃkyādihāgataḥ ॥11॥
dattātreyaṃ vinā nānyo vaktā kāryotra karmaṇi |
kṛte karmaṇi sarvasmiṃstrayodaśadināvadhi ॥12॥
paścādrājā nihaṃtavyaḥ kṛtavīryātmajo bali |
ekaviṃśativāraṃ ca kāryā niḥkṣatriyā mahī ॥13॥
itthamājñāpayanmahyaṃ jananī reṇukā mama |
ityarthamiha saṃprāptastasmātkuru kṛpāṃ mayi ॥14॥
brahmovāca |
iti rāmavacaḥ śrutvā reṇukāyāḥ sakhā muniḥ |
śokasaṃvignahṛdayo rāmamūca idaṃ vacaḥ ॥15॥
dattātreya uvāca |
yadgṛhe bhuktamatyaṃtaṃ tena neṣṭā virodhitā|
yadi duṣṭena sā'kāri draṣṭā tatphalamantikāt ॥16॥
idānīṃ tu tayoḥ samyagaurdhvadehikamācara |
brahmovāca |
tata āśramamāgatya dattātreyayutastu saḥ ॥17
rāmaścakāra bhaktyaiva pitroruttarakarma tat |
dattātreyoktamantraiḥ sa dvitīyadivasāvadhi ॥18॥
samāpte karmaṇi munirgantuṃ kolhāpuraṃ manaḥ |
cakre tadā'bravīdrāmaḥ kadāyāti bhavānpunaḥ ॥19॥
muniḥ proce yadā tvaṃ me smaraṇaṃ vai kariṣyasi |
ehi dattātreya iti tadā māṃ drakṣyase'nagha ॥20॥
kṛtvā karma vrajatyeva bhikṣituṃ sa dine dine |
aśaucino na bhoktavyamannamityabhicintayan ॥21॥
paṃcame divase karmasamāpta munipuṃgavaḥ |
gṛhītvā''jñāṃ gato yāvattāvad vyāghraḥ samāyayau ॥22॥
mātarmātaḥ kva yāmyadyetyevaṃ cukrośa tad bhayāt |
āvirābhūttadā mātā reṇukā mama vākyataḥ ॥23॥
asaṃpūrṇaśiromātrā putrasnehavaśena hi |
yadi dvādaśa rātrātsā''hūtā syād bhārgaveṇa tu ॥24॥
āgatā syātsā saṃpūrṇā sarvāvayavaśobhinī |
uvāca taṃ kimāhūtā vada bāla prayojanam ॥25॥
āliliṃga tadā snehādrāmaṃ snutapayodharā |
dattātreyaḥ punaragāt ṣaṣṭe ca divase muniḥ ॥26॥
apaśyadreṇukāṃ tatra tādṛśīṃ rāmamabravīt |
madhyaeva kimāhūtā nyūnadehā samāgatā | 27॥
sapiṃḍīkaraṇādūrdhvaṃ yadyāhūtā tadā'khilā|
āgatā syādreṇukeyaṃ tava snehāddvijarṣabha ॥28॥
rāma uvāca |
bālabhāvād bhayād brahman svabhāvānmātarityaham |
uktavān muniśārdūla dṛṣṭavānīdṛśīmimām ॥29॥
vṛṣotsargaṃ ca kṛtvānekādaśadine dvijaḥ |
sapiṃḍīkaraṇaṃ caiva dvādaśe kṛtavān dvayoḥ ॥30॥
tataḥ parehni pātheyaṃ śrāddhaṃ puṇyāhavācanam |
dadau dānānyanekāni brāhmaṇebhyo yathārhataḥ ॥31॥
jamadagnirdivyadeho brahmalokamathāgamat |
tasthau sā reṇukā bhūmau sthāne sthāne ca tādṛśī ॥32॥
pūrayatyakhilān kāmān janānāṃ bhaktikāriṇām |
māhātmyaṃ vistṛtaṃ tasyāḥ skānde proktaṃ viśeṣataḥ ॥33॥
ativistārabhītyā'tra na proktaṃ munisattama ॥34॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe rāmopākhyānaṃ ekāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥81॥

<adhyāya 82 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
kathaṃ rāmo bāla eva kṛtavīryasutena ha|
ekākī bahusainyena sahasrabhujaśālinā ॥1॥
ajayattaṃ mahāvīraṃ tanme kathaya vistarāt |
ka uvāca |
ekasmin divase rāmo mātaraṃ paripṛṣṭavān ॥2॥
rāma uvāca |
yasmādindrādayo devā bibhyati trāsakampitāḥ |
anantaṃ yasya sainyaṃ taccaturaṃgasamanvitam ॥3॥
kathaṃ vijeṣye taṃ mātarupāyaṃ me'khilaṃ vada |
ekaviṃśativāraṃ ca kathaṃ niḥkṣatriyāṃ dharām ॥4॥
kariṣye vada tatsarvaṃ tvatprasādājjayo bhavet|
atulā mama kīrtiśca sarvalokeṣu viśrutā ॥5॥
mātovāca |
vijayaste bhavetputra śaṃkarārādhanaṃ kuru |
tuṣṭe tasminmahādeve sarvaṃ setsyati vāṃchitam ॥6॥
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā rāmaḥ kailāsamāyayau |
praṇamya mātṛcaraṇāvāśiṣaṃ pratigṛhaya ca ॥7॥
tatrāpaśyanmahādevaṃ ratnasiṃhāsanesthitam |
baddhāṃjalipuṭo rāmo natvā tuṣṭāva taṃ tadā ॥8॥
rāma uvāca |
namo deva deveśa gaurīśa śambho |
namo viśvakartre namo viśvabhartre |
namo viśvahartre namo viśvabharte |
namo viśvadhāmne namaḥścandradhāmne ॥9॥
namo nirguṇāyāmalajñānahetornirākāra sākāra nityāya te'stu |
namo devavedāttaśāstrātigāya|
namo'vyaktavyaktātmane satsvarūpa ॥10॥
guṇatrayaprabodhāya guṇātītāya te namaḥ |
namaḥ prapaṃcaviduṣe prapaṃcarahitāya te ॥11॥
ka uvāca |
iti stotraṃ samākarṇya parituṣṭo maheśvaraḥ |
uvāca rāmamāmantrya tṛpto vākyāmṛtena te ॥12॥
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva mattastvaṃ yaṃ yaṃ kāmayase hṛdi |
jānāmi jāmadagnyaṃ tvāṃ reṇukātanayaṃ dvija ॥13॥
rāma uvāca |
kārtavīryeṇa duṣṭena kāmadhenumapekṣatāṃ |
jamadagtirhato roṣād aparādhaṃ vinā prabho ॥14॥
reṇukā tāḍitā bāṇaiḥ samantājjananī mama|
ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyākairbhuktvā'pi senayā saha ॥15॥
jahi taṃ duṣṭanṛpatimiti mātrā niyojitaḥ |
tvāmahaṃ śaraṇaṃ yāta upāyaṃ vada tadvadhe ॥16॥
triḥsaptavāraṃ tenaiva kuryāṃ niḥkṣatriyāṃ mahīm |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ viditatatvārtho mahādevo jagād tam ॥17॥
ālokya praṇidhānena jayopāyaṃ sukhāvaham |
ṣaḍakṣaraṃ mahāmantraṃ dviradānanatoṣakam ॥18॥
kathayāmāsa rāmāya japaṃ kuru prayatnataḥ |
lakṣamātraṃ ca homaṃ ca daśāṃśena samācāra ॥19॥
tarpaṇaṃ taddaśāṃśena taddaśāṃśena bhojanam |
brāhmaṇānāṃ mahābhaktyā prasanno bhavitā bhuvi ॥20॥
gajānano devadevaḥ sarvakāryaṃ kariṣyati |
iti tadvacanaṃ śrutvā praṇamya bhavamādarāt ॥21॥
ājñāṃ gṛhītvā rāmo'sāvacalāyāṃ bhramanbhraman |
kṛṣṇāyā uttare deśe'paśyatsthānamanuttamam ॥22॥
nānāvṛkṣalatājālairabhirāmaṃ susiddhidam |
tatrākarodanuṣṭhānaṃ yathoktaṃ tena śambhunā ॥23॥
indriyāṇāṃ ca manaso vṛttiṃ sthāpya gajānane |
āvartayan mahāmantramekāṃguṣṭhasthito dvijaḥ ॥24॥
juhāva tarpayāmāsa bhojayāmāsa vai dvijān |
daśāṃśena daśāṃśena daśāṃśena yathākramam ॥25॥
tataḥ prasanno bhagavānāvirāsīd gajānanaḥ |
caturbhujo mahākāyo mahāmāyo'ti sundaraḥ ॥26॥
nāgayajñopavītī ca nānālaṃkāra śobhitaḥ|
mukuṭī kuṃḍalī bhrājaccārugaṃḍo lasanmukhaḥ ॥27॥
muktāpravālamālābhirbhrājadvakṣā mahābhujaḥ |
paraśuṃ kamalaṃ dantaṃ modakāṃśca dadhadbhujaiḥ ॥28॥
puṣkaraṃ puṣkare dhṛtvā bhāmayan svecchayā vibhuḥ |
prabhayā bhāsayan sarvā diśo vidiśa eva ca ॥29॥
dadarśa rāmo'kasmāttaṃ nyamīlayata cākṣiṇī|
tattejasā pratihate tattastuṣṭāva taṃ dvijaḥ ॥30॥
rāma uvāca|
sahasrāditya saṃkāśa namaste jagadīśvara |
namaste sarvavidyeśa sarvasiddhipradāyaka ॥31॥
vighnānāṃ pataye tubhyaṃ vighnanivāraṇa |
sarvāntaryāmiṇe tubhyaṃ namaḥ sarvapriyaṃkara ॥32॥
bhaktapriyāya devāya namo jñānasvarūpiṇe |
namo viśvasyakartre te namastatpālakāya ca ॥33॥
nivāraya mahāvighnaṃ taponāśakaraṃ mama| ka uvāca |
iti stutiṃ samākarṇya saumyatejā gajānanaḥ ॥34॥
uvāca rāmaṃ saṃbhrāntaṃ svasyaiva tigmatejasā |
gaṇeśa uvāca |
yaṃ dhyāyasi divārātrau mantraṃ japtvā ṣaḍakṣaram ॥35॥
varaṃ dātuṃ samāyātaḥ so'haṃ rāma tavādhunā |
varaṃ vṛṇīṣva mattastvaṃ yadyad vāṃchasi cetasā ॥36॥
brahmāṃḍānāmanekānāṃ sṛṣṭā pātā'pahārakaḥ |
naiva jānnanti me rūpaṃ brahmādyā munayo'pi ca ॥37॥
rājarṣayaśca sarve'pi so'haṃ te darśanaṃ gatāḥ |
rāma uvāca |
yo'prameyo'khilādhāraḥ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakārakaḥ ॥38॥
yo na vedairna tapasā na yajñairvratasaṃcayaiḥ|
na dānairnaiva yogaiśca janānāṃ dṛṣṭigocaraḥ ॥39॥
so'haṃ tvaṃ hi mayā dṛṣṭo'nugrāhāttava vighnapa |
kimanyadvaraye deva bhaktiṃ dehi nijāṃ dṛḍhām ॥40॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
bhavitā mama bhaktiste dṛḍhā rāma dvijottam |
pralobhitasyāpi varairna te buddhiḥ suvihvalā ॥41॥
paraśuṃ me gṛhāṇa tvaṃ sarvaśatrunibarhaṇam |
nāma te paraśurāmeti tryalokye khyātimeṣyati ॥42॥
ka uvāca |
evaṃ tasmai varaṃ dattvā paraśuṃ ca gajānanaḥ |
paśyatāṃ sarvalokānāmantardhānaṃ yayau tadā ॥43॥
rāmo sthāpayāmāsa mahāgaṇapatiṃ tadā |
brāhmaṇairvedavedāṃgaśāstravidbhiḥ samaṃ mudā |
brāhmaṇānbhojayāmāsa dattvā dānānyanekaśaḥ ॥44॥
prāsādaṃ kārayāmāsa ratnastaṃbhayutaṃ dṛḍham |
saṃpūjya taṃ parikramya praṇipatya gajānanam ॥45॥
rāmaḥ prasannamanasā prayayau nijamandiram |
tato rāmo juhāvoccaiḥ kārtavīryaṃ dharāpatim ॥46॥
yuddhe tu śātayāmāsa rājño bāhusahasrakam |
niḥkṣatriyāṃ ca pṛthivīṃ cakre trisaptavārataḥ ॥47॥
brāhmaṇebhyo dadau pṛthvīṃ yajñaṃ kṛtvā sadakṣiṇam |
pupūjustaṃ tadā lokā jñātvā viṣṇuṃ tamīśvarama ॥48॥
dṛṣṭvā parākramaṃ tasya sarvadevātigaṃ dṛḍhama |
evaṃ nānāvidho brahman mahimā te nirūpitaḥ ॥49॥
gajānanasya devasya saṃkṣepeṇa mayā suta |
nikhilena na śeṣo'pi kṣamo vaktuṃ munīśvara ॥50॥
upāsanākhaṃḍamidaṃ śṛṇuyānmāsavo bhuvi |
sarvāṃnkāmānavāpnoti gaṇeśaṃ dhāma cāpnuyāt ॥51॥
yatheṣṭaṃ ramate tatra yāvadābhūta saṃplavam ॥52॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe rāmavaradānaṃ nāma dvayaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥82॥

<adhyāya 83 prāraṃbhaḥ>

muniruvāca |
kasmin sthāne tapo'kāri rāmeṇa paramādbhutam |
tanme kathaya lokeśa tṛptiṃ me nāsti śaṇvataḥ ॥1॥
ka uvāca |
mayūreśvaranāmnā yatkṣetraṃ khyātaṃ caturyuge |
yatrāvatīrṇo deveśo mayūrārūḍha eva saḥ ॥2॥
kamalāsura nāmānamahanad daityapuṃgavam |
yasmān mayūramārūḍho mayūreśa iti sphuṭam ॥3॥
saṃstuto devamunibhirloke tāṃ khyātimāgamat |
tatrānuṣṭhānamakarodavāpa paraśuṃ tataḥ ॥4॥
svayaṃ paraśurāmo'bhūnnāmnā khyātastriviṣṭape |
itihāsaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu taṃ munisattama ॥5॥
tārakonāma daityo'bhūnmahābala parākramaḥ |
divyavarṣasahasraṃ sa tapastepe sudāruṇam ॥6॥
tataḥ prasanno brahmā'smai dadau sarvāṃbhayaṃ dvija |
devarṣiyakṣagandharvoragarākṣasahastataḥ ॥7॥
tacchastrāstragaṇān mṛtyurna te kvāpi bhavediti |
utpatsyate yadā skandastataste maraṇaṃ bhaveta ॥8॥
iti tadvākyamākarṇya balagarvasamāyutaḥ |
tārako'pīḍayallokāṃstrailokye vasato mune ॥9॥
vedādhyayananiṣṭhāṃśca tapo'nuṣṭhānakāriṇaḥ |
agnihotraratānanyān kārāgāre'kṣipad dvijān ॥10॥
sarvān rājño'tha nāgāṃśca vaśe kṛtvā divaṃ yayau |
indrādayastadā devā himācala guhāṃ gatāḥ ॥11॥
tad bhayānnābhavat kvāpi yajanaṃ pūjanaṃ tathā|
ahameveśvaro devo brāhmaṇaḥ kuladevatā ॥12॥
ahaṃ namasya pūjyaśca nānyo jagati vartate |
yaścānyān praṇamet kvāpi pūjayedvā kadācana ॥13॥
sa daṇḍyastāḍanīyaḥ syād gacchedvā yamasādanam |
evaṃ sarveṣu lokeṣu khyāpayāmāsa dūtataḥ ॥14॥
tataḥ sarve janā riktā jātāḥ sajjanavarjitāḥ |
niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkārā yajñadānavivarjitāḥ ॥15॥
ucchinnakuladharmāṃśca svācārarahitāḥ khalāḥ |
munayaḥ sādhavaḥ sarve girigahvaramāśritāḥ ॥16॥
devaṃ te prārthayāmāsuḥ kathaṃ daityo vivarddhitaḥ |
vinā tvāṃ śaraṇaṃ śambho yāmaḥ kaṃ jagadīśvaram ॥17॥
nirmātā rakṣitā dhātā saṃhartā jagatāṃ vibho |
dāvānalo vanamiva dahate'smānsa darpataḥ ॥18॥
yadi te saṃjihīrṣā syāt saṃharasva svayaṃ jagat |
nocet saṃhara daityaṃ tvaṃ tārakaṃ sarvapīḍakam ॥19॥
evaṃ saṃprārthya tepuste duṣkaraṃ tapa uttamam |
patrabhakṣyā vāyubhakṣyā nirāhārā jalāśinaḥ ॥20॥
evaṃ sthiteṣu muniṣu tenājñāteṣu daityarāṭ |
aindraṃ padaṃ samāsthāya brahmāṇaṃ samatāḍayat ॥21॥
viṣṇustato'gamat kṣīrasāgaraṃ nidrituṃ mune |
tyaktvā kailāsamagamacchaṃkaropi guhāntaram ॥22॥
dikpālā diggajāścāpi nānāgahvaramāśritāḥ |
teṣāṃ sthāne'sthāpayatsa daityānanyāṃśca daityarāṭ ॥23॥
acalāmacalastiṣṭhan pālayāmāsa vai prajāḥ|
garjate svasvabhāvena kampate'tha triviṣṭapam ॥24॥
tata indrādayo devāstuṣṭavurgirigahvare |
giriśaṃ girijānāthaṃ girā gaṃbhīrayā mudā ॥25॥
devā ūcuḥ |
devādhibhūmigaganena śaśisvarūpa|
vāyvagnirūpa yajamāna jalasvarūpa |
tvaṃ sthāvarānatha carān sṛjase'vasīśa |
tvaṃ sarvameva harase nijayecchayeśa ॥26॥
tannocitaṃ tvayi pare paraduḥkhahārin|
svīyaṃ yaśaḥparagataṃ bahudhā'dya kartum |
tannāśayainamathavā sakalān munīṃśca |
devān dvayośca bhajanaṃ pariviṣṭacittān ॥27॥
kaṃ vā vrajema śaraṇaṃ tvadṛte girīśa |
kaṃ bā bhajema bhagavaṃstvadṛte maheśa |
kaṃ vā vadema vṛjinārdana pārvatīśa |
ko vā'vituṃ prabhavati tvadṛte'khileśa ॥28॥
ka uvāca |
evaṃ te tuṣṭuvuryāvacchuśruvustāvadeva hi |
nabhovāṇīṃ sarvadevā haraputro yadā bhavet ॥29॥
tadā'sya nāśo bhavitā yatnaṃ kurūta tatra vai |
nabhovāṇī samākarṇya sarve harṣasamanvitāḥ ॥30॥
kailāsaṃ devanilayamāpurindrādayaḥ surāḥ |
nāpaśyacchaṃkaraṃ tatra dadṛśuḥ purato'maraḥ ॥31॥
mūlaprakṛtirūpāṃ tāmumāṃ sarve vijijñapuḥ |
tārake tārakajñānaprade tāraya tārakāt ॥32॥
trailokyapīḍakād duṣṭāt padabhraṣṭān munīna surān |
tathā vicintyatāṃ mātastasya nāśo bhavedyathā ॥33॥
mātastvāṃ praṇamāma devajananīṃ trailokyarakṣākare |
śarvāṇi tripure parātparakale brahmādibhiḥ saṃstute |
tvaṃ vedairanirūpite kuru jagat kalyāṇamīśa priye |
svecchopāttasuvigrahe surahare vaiśvādi bhūte'naghe॥34॥
ka uvāca |
evaṃ saṃprārthitā devī viśvamātā jagāda tān|
nabhovāṇī mayā jñātā śaṃkaraḥ śaṃ kariṣyati ॥35॥
sahaiva yāntu sarve'pi mayā yatra sa śaṃkaraḥ |
paraṃ niyamamāsthāya kurute paramaṃ tapaḥ ॥36॥
ityuktvā sarvadevānsā bhillīveśamadhārayat |
yāṃ dṛṣṭvā paramo yogī kāmabāṇārdito bhavet ॥37॥
devā api tadā kejijjātā madanavihvalāḥ |
urvaśī menakā rambhā pūrvacittī ratistathā ॥38॥
lajjitā tāṃ nirīkṣyaiva sarvāvayavasundarām |
te devā sā ca girijā prāptā brahman śivāntikam ॥39॥
sthāṇuṃ taṃ sthāṇubhūtaṃ ca dhyānaniścalalocanam |
dhyāyantaṃ manasā brahma japantaṃ niṣparigraham ॥40॥
dadṛśuḥ sarvadevāste bhillī cāpi trilocanam |
tata ūce'khilāndevānumopāyaṃ sukhāvaham ॥41॥
dehātīta ivāste'sau taponiṣṭhaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
etasya dehabhāvārthaṃ kāmaṃ vijñāpayantu ca ॥42॥
teneṣuṇā yadā viddhaḥ ekaniṣṭhaḥ sadāśivaḥ |
yāsyate dehabhāvaṃ sa tadā kāryaṃ bhaviṣyati ॥43॥
tato devāḥ sarva eva kāmaṃ sasmarurutkaṭam |
samāgataṃ tamūcuste svakārya kṛtaniścayāḥ ॥44॥
prārthayāmāsurapare svakāryāya manobhavam |
vibhustvamasi lokānāṃ careṣu sthāvareṣu ca ॥45॥
tvayaiva jāyate sṛṣṭistvayā vyāptamidaṃ jagat |
abalā kāminaḥ sarve tvayaiva balavattarāḥ ॥46॥
tvayā vinā vṛthā sarvaṃ jagat sthāvarajaṃgamam |
atastvayaiva kartavyaṃ sarveṣāṃ kāryamutkaṭam ॥47॥
kāma uvāca |
yadyapyahaṃ visāmagrisyathā yuṣmat prasādataḥ |
kariṣyāmi vacaḥ kāryamādeha patanāvadhi ॥48॥
sumaṃ dhanurmarālājyeṣuḥ kaṭākṣo'tha yoṣitaḥ |
vijeṣye'thāpi sakalānamarān chaṃkarāvadhi ॥49॥
vasantaṃ pratilabhyaiva sahāyaṃ sakalaṃ surāḥ |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā gataḥ kāmo yatrāste'sau sadāśivaḥ ॥50॥
mohituṃ śaṃkaraṃ devaṃ devānāṃ kāryasiddhaye ॥51॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe tryaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥83॥

<adhyāya 84 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
evamuktvā gataḥ kāmaḥ surakāryārthasiddhaye |
apaśyacchaṃkaraṃ sthānaṃ vṛkṣavallīsamākulam ॥1॥
siṃha śārdūlajuṣṭaṃ ca pakṣiśvāpadasaṃyutam |
svayaṃ ca nirmame māyāvāṭikāṃ tatkṣaṇena saḥ ॥2॥
sarovarāṇi bhūrīṇi sudhārūpajalāni ca |
aneka kusumān vṛkṣā nāgavyūtisugandhibhiḥ ॥3॥
jambvāmrabadarī vṛkṣān supakvaphalaśālinaḥ |
tathaiva rambhā panasā nārikelīḥ sakharjurīḥ ॥4॥
elālavaṃga mārīca vṛkṣānanyānanekaśaḥ |
agṛhayamāṇo gandho'sau haranāsāpuraṃ yayau ॥5॥
uṣaḥkāle haro'paśyajjyotsnā jalayaṃ manoharam |
aneka phalapuṣpāḍhyaṃ kāmakānanamadbhutaṃ ॥6॥
tadaiva bhedayāmāsa mānasaṃ śūlinastu saḥ |
svīyāmaśokavanikāṃ dhikca ke manasā śivaḥ ॥7॥
dehabhāvaṃ gato devaścintayāmāsa kāraṇama |
kṛtaṃ kena tapovighnaṃ kṛtametadvanaṃ śubham ॥8॥
akasmādracitaṃ kena duṣṭenedaṃ gatāyuṣā |
ābaddhya bhrukuṭīṃ roṣādārakta nayanodaraḥ ॥9॥
kāmastu bhayasaṃtrasto līnaḥ kvāpi na gṛhayate |
indrādīnāṃ sa sasmāra smṛtāste nātra te yayuḥ ॥10॥
vimānāni samārūḍhāḥ kāryasiddhīcchayā surāḥ |
śivena dadṛśe tāvan madano'ti laghuḥ kṛśaḥ ॥11॥
udghāṭayaṃstṛtīyaṃ sa netraṃ dagdhaṃ manobhavam |
cakampe pṛthivī sarvā svargaḥ pātālameva ca ||12||
mā jahīti bruvandevā yāvattāvatsa netrajaḥ |
cakāra bhammasāt kāmaṃ bhasmamātrāvaśeṣitam ॥13॥
tato bhillī maheśānaṃ prārthayāmāsa sādaram |
namaskṛtvāṃjaliṃ baddhvā trailokyahitakāmyayā ॥14॥
trailokyadāhakaṃ vahnimupasaṃhara śaṃkara |
brahmalabdhavaro daityastārako'ti mahābalī॥15॥
ākrāntaṃ tena trailokyaṃ niḥsvādhyāyaṃ nirāhuti |
sthānacyutāḥ surā sarve tvāṃ dṛṣṭvā tapasi sthitam ॥16॥
tvarayā kāmamāmantrya dehabhāvāya te'nagha |
apreṣayansa vai bhasma jātaḥ śreṣṭhāparādhataḥ ॥17॥
idānīṃ deva rakṣāsmāṃstvāmeva śaraṇaṃ gatān |
vikhyātastriṣu lokeṣu śaraṇāgata pālakaḥ ॥18॥
kṣamasva kṛpaṇānāṃ tvaṃ devānāṃ śaraṇaiṣiṇām |
aparādhaṃ mahādeva karuṇākara śaṃkara ॥19॥
ka uvāja |
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā svāṅghristhaśiraso mune |
jagāda hāsyavadano vahniṃ saṃhṛtya śaṃkaraḥ ॥20॥
uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha devānāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ kṛtamadya me|
pādayoḥ patitāyāste vacasā praṇayena ca ॥21॥
tata āliṃgya sahasā bhillīmaṃkamathānayat |
vṛṣabhāruhya ca tayā sārddhaṃ kailāsamāgamat ॥22॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe kāmadahanaṃ nāma caturaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥84॥

<adhyāya 85 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
śivasyāliṃganād bhillī madanānaladīpitā |
nāpa kvāpi sukhaṃ devī nirjale śapharī yathā ॥1॥
patitā jalamadhye sā śītalośīraśālinī|
na śarma lebhe tatrāpi sthale nidrāṃ na cālabhat ॥2॥
karpuracandanaṃ tasyā adhikaṃ tāpamādadhe |
na śītalapadārtho'syāḥ ko'pi saṃtoṣamādadhe ॥3॥
evaṃ bahutithe kāle sāsthicarmāvaśoṣitā|
tato giriśamāgamya girijā giramabravīt ॥4॥
na tvaṃ mṛśasi māṃ deva kāmavasthāṃ gatā'smi ha |
dagdho'pi madano mahyamatyantaṃ pīḍayatyaho ॥5॥
nānopāyāḥ kṛtāstasya śāntaye na ca sā'bhavat |
yenopāyena śāntiḥ syātsa taṃ kuru mama prabho ॥6॥
iti vākyaṃ samākarṇya priyāyāḥ priyakṛddharaḥ |
rahasyetāṃ kare kṛtvā paryaṃke paryaveśayat ॥7॥
reme tayā yatheṣṭaṃ sa madanena vaśīkṛtaḥ|
mṛtenāpi kṛtaṃ tena mahatkāryaṃ sureritam ||8॥
anaṃgena samo naiva dhanvīti yaśa āṣtavān |
tayostu krīḍato yātāḥ ṣaṣṭisāhasravatsarāḥ ॥9॥
bhraṣṭasthānā munisurāḥ śrutvā madanaceṣṭitam |
kailāsaṃ punarāyātāstatra krīḍārataṃ haram॥10॥
jñātvā tūṣṇīṃ sthitāstatra cintāvyākulacetasaḥ |
tārakāsurabhītāste punargahvaramāgatā ॥11॥
kadā vadho bhavedasya kadā sthānāni yāma ca |
kariṣyati kadā śambhurasmākaṃ duḥkhanāśanam ॥12॥
iti cintārṇave magnā yāvad brahmādayaḥ surāḥ |
tāvadūce surācāryo vākyaṃ me śṛṇutānaghāḥ ॥13॥
preṣayantvakhilā vahniṃ rūpāntaradharaṃ haram |
sa haraṃ bodhayitvāśu bhavatkāryaṃ bhaviṣyati ॥14॥
tataste vahnimākārya tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stavaiḥ |
devā ūcuḥ |
tvatto yajñakriyā brahman saṃskārāḥ sarva eva hi ॥15॥
apāṃ tvamasi hetuśca devānāṃ mukhameva ca |
agnihotrapraṇetā tvaṃ gārhapatyādināmabhiḥ॥16॥
tvameva pibasīśābdhi vāri nityaṃ mahattaram |
tvameva pacase nṛṇām jaṭhare ṣaḍrasānapi ॥17॥
tvameva sarvajantūnāṃ sandhau sandhau viceṣṭase |
tvayā tyaktaṃ pretasaṃjñāṃ labhate dahyate tvayā ॥18॥
hetustvamasi deveśa jantūnāṃ prāṇadhāraṇe|
tvayādbhiśca vinā nānnaṃ paktuṃ śakyaṃ na ca kvacit |
tvameva brahmā rudraśca sūryaścāneka rūpadhṛk ॥19॥
tvameva jāyase mūlaṃ krodhasya jagadīśvara |
yatra tatra bhavettejastattadrūpaṃ tavaiva ca ॥20॥
atastvāṃ prārthayāmo'dya trilokyā upakāraka |
trailokyākramaṇaṃ tena tārakeṇa kṛtaṃ vibho ॥21॥
jānāsi tannabhovāṇīṃ kāmasyāpi ca tāṃ gatim |
pārvatīharayorbodhaṃ ciraṃkrīḍānimagnayoḥ ॥22॥
kuru gatvā'nyarūpeṇa bhikṣāṃ yācasva tatra vai|
evaṃ kṛte jagatkāryamasmākaṃ ca bhaviṣyati ॥23॥
brahmovāca |
iti devavacaḥ śrutvā kāṣāyavasano dvijaḥ |
bhūtvā śuṣmāyayau tatra yatrāstāṃ pārvatīśvarau ॥24॥
krīḍāsaktau bahiḥ sthitvā bhikṣāṃ dehītyuvāca saḥ |
trivāraṃ plutaśabdena dvābhyāmapi śrutaḥ svanaḥ ॥25॥
ūcaturvismitau tau tu parasparamubhau vacaḥ |
paridhattāṃ śuke sve sve kuto'trāyaṃ samāgataḥ ॥26॥
kimasmai ca pradātavyamiti cintāṃ ca cakratuḥ |
umāṃjalāvadhādvīryamaśaktā tasya dhāraṇe ॥27॥
jānatī bhāvinaṃ cārthaṃ dadau sā bhikṣukāya tat |
bhūmau tyaktaṃ dahedetat trailokyaṃ sacarācaram ॥28॥
haravīryamityapi yadvahniḥ śāpabhayāttayoḥ |
garbhavānanalo jāto lajjāvānapi tāpavān॥29॥
tatra yatra yayau vahniḥ śarma lebhe na tatra vai|
uṣasyutthāya gaṃgāyāṃ tulāsaṃsthe divākare ॥30॥
snātuṃ yāvatsa śaucādi kurute tāvadeva ha|
āgatāḥ ṣaṭ striyastatra snātumūrje samāhitāḥ ॥31॥
agninā yatsamutsṛṣṭaṃ gaṃgeyaṃ ca harapriyā |
dhārayiṣyati śaktā cejjalarūpā suśītalā ॥32॥
śivavīryaṃ ca tattābhiḥ prāśitaṃ ṣaḍvibhāgataḥ |
tasminneva kṣaṇe so'gnirantarddhi samapadyata ॥33॥
aṃkuśāni ca yātāni dūradeśaṃ gate'nale |
paridhāya svavastrāṃstāstato yātā nijaṃ gṛham ॥34॥
dadṛśuḥ patayastāsāṃ mukhānyatyujjvalāni ca |
garbhiṇya iti te jñātvā jñānadṛṣṭyā munīśvarāḥ ॥35॥
bahiścakrurgṛhāt sarvā na pradarśyaṃ mukhaṃ tvidam |
tāḥ sametya punargagāṃ tīre śara suśobhite ॥36॥
mumucuḥ sva sva garbhaṃ tāḥ śuddhāḥ snātā gṛhānyayuḥ |
ṣaṭsu tāsu prayātāsu svaṃ svaṃ garbhaṃ vimucya vai ॥37॥
ṣaṇmukho dvādaśabhujo bālastatra vyajāyata |
tasya huṃkāramātreṇa nipeturbhāni ravādbhuvi ॥38॥
cakampe dharaṇī sarvā śeṣaḥ pātālameva ca |
drumā unmīlitāḥ sūryo nīhārācchādito'bhavat ॥39॥
etasminnantare tatra nārado divyadarśanaḥ |
kailāsaṃ giriśaṃ dṛṣṭuṃ gacchanmārge dadarśa tam ॥40॥
atidīptataraṃ bālaṃ durdaśaṃ balavattaram |
praṇidhānena taṃ jñātvā tūṣṇīṃ kailāsamāyayau ॥41॥
uvāca pārvatīṃ śambhuṃ vṛttāṃtaṃ sarvameva ca |
jaharṣa pṛthivī sarvāṃ jñātvā taṃ giriśātmajam ॥42॥
devadundubhayo nedurgandharvāṃ jaguradbhutam |
nārada uvāca |
āgacchatā mayā dṛṣṭo gauri mārge tavātmajaḥ ॥43॥
ṣaṇmukho dvādaśabhujo sūryakoṭisamaprabhaḥ |
gaṃgātīre nipatitastyaktaste ṣaṇmukho nu kim ॥44॥
koṭikandarpaśobhāḍhyo garjitakṣobhitākhilaḥ |
kathaṃ niṣṭhuratā'kāri gauri sundarabālake ॥45॥
ka uvāca |
ityuktvā'ntarhite tasmingaurī bālakamabhyagāt ॥46॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe skandopākhyānaṃ nāma paṃcāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥85॥

<adhyāya 86 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
dṛṣṭvā sā tādṛśaṃ bālaṃ snehasnutapayodharā |
āliliṃga mudā bālaṃ taddhastahṛdayā satī ॥1॥
tāmūce mama bālo'yaṃ gaṃgāgaṃbhīranisvanā |
mamāyamiti tāṃ prāha vahnirbālo gireḥ sutām ॥2॥
kṛttikādyāśca tāṃ prāhurasmajjāto'yamarbhakaḥ |
asmākameva bālo'yamiti cukruśurāturāḥ ॥3॥
evaṃ vivadamānāstā yātā vahnipurogamāḥ|
kailāse devanilaye giriśaṃ candraśekharam ॥4॥
gaurī viveśa prathamaṃ kaṭinyasta svabālakāḥ |
śivo gṛhītvā taṃ svāṃke śirasyāghrāya mantrataḥ ॥5॥
ramayāmāsa parayā mudā devastrilocanaḥ |
gaṃgā vahniḥ ṣaṭstriyastā gṛhaṃ jagmuryathā gatam ॥6॥
tannāmakaraṇārthāya brahmaṇaṃ ca bṛhaspatim |
āhūya śiva ūce tau nāmāsya kriyatāmiti ॥7॥
brahmābṛhaspatī ūcatuḥ |
kārtike māsi jāto'yaṃ kārtikeya iti sphuṭam |
nāmāsya prathamaṃ deva pārvatīnandano'pi ca ॥8॥
śaradvīpe'yamutpannaḥ śarajanmā tato'pi ca |
kṛttikābhyo'pi jātatvāt kārtikeya iti smṛtaḥ ॥9॥
yato'sya mātaraṃ ṣaṭ tāḥ sa ṣāṇmātura ityapi |
ayaṃ putrastārakajittārakaṃ ca vijeṣyati ॥10॥
devasenāpatirbhāvī senānīriti śabditaḥ |
tata eva mahāsenaḥ ṣaṇmukhatvāt ṣaḍānanaḥ ॥11॥
skannaṃ trivāraṃ reto yat tena skando'yamucyate |
tayostu vadatorevaṃ śakrādyā āyayuḥ surāḥ॥12॥
ānananduśca nemustaṃ pupūjustuṣṭuvurjaguḥ |
vyānaśe rodasī divyaṃ vādyaśabdo rasātalam ॥13॥
tataste prārthayāmāsuḥ senānyaṃ praṇipatya ca |
trailokyakaṃṭakaṃ deva jahi tvaṃ tārakāsuram ॥14॥
nānābhiṣekasambhāraiḥ senāpatye'bhiṣicya tam | vaidikai
stāntrikairmantrairnānāmunisamīritaiḥ ॥15॥
tato'nujñāṃ gṛhītvā te devāḥ svasthānamāgaman |
ṛṣayo'pi nirudyogā spatasteṣu yathāṃ purā ॥16॥
senānyāṃ vidyamāne no na bhayaṃ vidyate kvacit |
sa bālo vavṛdhe'tyantaṃ śuklapakṣe yathā śaśī ॥17॥
ekadā bālabhāvātsa uḍḍīya śaśinaṃ yayau|
gṛhītuṃ brahmaṇā'vāri nedṛśaṃ sāhasaṃ kuru ॥18॥
buddhyā bṛhaspati śaktyā jigāyendraṃ ca so'rbhakaḥ |
ekadā sukhamāsīnaṃ pārvatyā sahitaṃ śivam ॥19॥
praṇamya paripapraccha sarvakāmārthasiddhaye|
skanda uvāca |
pitarmayā'śrāvi mahā kathā nānāvidhāḥ śubhāḥ ॥20॥
sarvasiddhikaraṃ deva putrasaṃpat pravardhanam |
sarvapāpaharaṃ caiva dharmārthakāmamokṣadam ॥21॥
trailokyasya gurostvattastṛptirnāsti tathāpi me |
vada me sarvaśatrūṇāṃ jayakāri vrataṃ śubhami ॥22॥
īśvara uvāca |
samyakpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā skanda sarvalokopakārakam |
vrataṃ te'haṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvasiddhikaraṃ nṛṇām ॥23॥
sarvapāpakṣayakaraṃ dharmārthakāmamokṣadam |
sarvaśatrukṣayakaraṃ putrasaṃpat pravardhanam ॥24॥
alakṣmīsaṃkaṭaharaṃ gaṇanāthasya toṣakṛta |
yaḥ karoti naro bhaktyā sapūjyastridaśairapi ॥25॥
icchāvihārī bhavati sṛṣṭisthityantakṛcca saḥ |
darśanāttasya cānyeṣāṃ mahāpāpaṃ layaṃ vrajet ॥26॥
nātyeṣāṃ skanda varada caturthī vratasāmyatā |
evamākarṇya vacanaṃ senānīḥ śaṃkareritam ॥27॥
punaḥ papraccha pitaraṃ mahimānaṃ vratasya saḥ |
skanda uvāca |
vistareṇa mama brūhi māhātmyaṃ vratasambhavam ॥28॥
kasminmāsi dine cāsya prārambhaḥ kriyate hara |
ko vidhiḥ kiṃ phalaṃ cāsya kasyā'bhūt pratyayo'sya ca ॥29॥
etanme sakalaṃ brūhi yadi tuṣṭo'si śaṃkara |
sarvalokopakārāya vratasyāsya prasiddhaye ॥30॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe skandopākhyāne ṣaḍaśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥86॥

<adhyāya 87 prāraṃbhaḥ>

śaṃkara uvāca |
ahaṃ te kathayiṣyāmi vratasyāsya vidhiṃ param |
śrāvaṇe tu site pakṣe caturthyāmārabhed vratam ॥1॥
tilāmalakakalkena prātaḥsnānaṃ vidhāya ca |
nityanaimittikaṃ sarvaṃ samāpya krodhavarjitaḥ ॥2॥
śucau maṇḍapikāṃ kṛtvā kadalīstambhamaṃḍitām |
ikṣucāmarapuṣpāḍhayāmādarśāṃ valiśobhitām ॥3॥
tanmadhye kalaśam sthāpya vastrayugmena veṣṭitam |
tatra cāṣṭadalaṃ padmaṃ kārayeccandanena tu॥4॥
gurorājñāṃ gṛhītvā ca pūjādravyāṇi prokṣayet |
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ pūjayed gaṇanāyakam ॥5॥
kāṃcanaṃ rājataṃ vāpi svasvaśaktyā vinirmitam |
ekaviṃśati pakvānnairekaviṃśati saṃkhyakaiḥ ॥6॥
gajānanāya devāya naivedyaṃ parikalpayet |
ekaviṃśati mudrāstu dakṣiṇārthaṃ nivedayet ॥7॥
sauvarṇī rājatīrvāpi vittaśāṭhyaṃ vivarjitaḥ |
ekaviṃśati dūrvāśca śvetā vā haritā api ॥8॥
arpayeddevadevāya mantrapuṣpāṃjaliṃ tathā |
brāhmaṇānvedaviduṣaḥ pūjayedekaviṃśatim ॥9॥
bhojayettādṛśānnena tāvaddānāni dāpayet |
namet kṣamāpayet paścādacchidramiti vācayet॥10॥
pārthivasya gaṇeśasya vidhānaṃ kathitaṃ purā |
pūjāprakāraḥ so'pyatra sarva eva udāhṛtaḥ ॥11॥
kathāṃ tāmeva śṛṇuyāt paścād bandhuyutaḥ svayam |
dhyāyan gaṇeśaṃ bhuṃjīta maunernopavaseta vā ॥12॥
evaṃ māsavrataṃ kuryāṃdyāvad bhādracaturthikā|
tasyāṃ mahotsavaḥ kāryo yathāvibhavamādarāt 13॥
pūrvoktena vidhānena pūjayed gaṇanāyakam |
rātrau jāgaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ gītavāditraniḥsvanaiḥ ॥14॥
purāṇaśravaṇairanyairnānākhyānavarairapi |
sahasranāmamantreṇa stuvīta dviradānanam |
prabhāte vimalaṃ snātvā pūjayed dviradānanam ॥15
brāhmaṇān bhojayed bhaktyā śataṃ caivaikaviṃśatim |
gobhūkāṃcana vastrāṇi bhūṣaṇāni dhanāni ca ||16||
tebhyo dadyādyathāśakti dīnāndhakṛpaṇeṣu ca |
aśaktau bhojayedekaṃ viṃśatyekaṃ tu vāḍavān ॥17॥
brāhmaṇāya pradātavyā mūrtiśceddhātunirmitāḥ |
anyathā paramotsāhairjalamadhye visarjayet ॥18॥
divyavāditranirghoṣairnarayānasthitāṃ ca tām |
chatradhvajapatākābhirvedagītādiniḥsvanaiḥ ॥19॥
bālānāṃ daṇḍayuddhaiśca tāṃ visṛjya gṛhaṃ vrajet |
vratamevaṃ tu yaḥ kuryādekavāraṃ ṣaḍānana ॥20॥
sarvān kāmānavāpyaiva gāṇeśaṃ padamāpnuyāt |
brahmaṇā'kāri sṛṣṭayarthaṃ ekārṇaṃ japatā manum |
pratyakṣo vikaṭastasya sāmarthyaṃ vividhaṃ dadau ॥21॥
viṣṇunā'prāpi śaktiśca pālane kurvatā vratam |
ṣaḍakṣaraṃ mahāmantraṃ japatā paramādbhutama ॥22॥
svecchāvatārasāmarthyaṃ tataḥ prāptaṃ ṣaḍānana |
mayā'pyakāri putraitajjapatā'ṣṭākṣaraṃ manum ॥23॥
vṛtraṃ ca śambaraṃ daityaṃ śakreṇa madanena ca |
jighāṃsatā'kāri vrataṃ tau tau jetuṃ ca śekatuḥ ॥24॥
nānāsāmarthyavānasmi saṃharāmi jagat trayam |
yakṣagandharvamunibhiḥ kinnaroragarākṣasaiḥ |
akāri sveṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ siddhacāraṇamānavaiḥ ॥25॥
etattvaṃ skanda varadacaturthīvratamācara |
bhaviṣyasi raṇe'jeyo vikhyāto bhuvanatraye ॥26॥
ṣaḍakṣaraṃ ca mantraṃ te dadāmi varadasya ha|
brahmovāca |
sumuhūrte dadau tasmai śivo dīkṣāṃ ṣaḍakṣarīm ॥27॥
tadaiva sa yayau skandastapastaptuṃ dvijottama |
sunirmalatare deśe vṛkṣavallīsamākule ॥28॥
bahumūlaphale ramye sarovāpīsuśobhite |
ekapādasthitaḥ skandastapastepe sudāruṇam ॥29॥
vrataṃ cakāra vidhivadyathoktaṃ śambhunā purā|
anuṣṭhānācca mantrasya vratasyāsya prabhāvataḥ ॥30॥
prasanno'bhūttadaivāsya paramātmā gajānanaḥ |
darśayāmāsa senānye yogidhyeyamanuttamam ॥31॥
nijaṃ rūpaṃ mahātejāścaturbhujavirājitam |
mahāmukuṭa saṃśobhi kuṃḍalāṃgadaśobhitam ॥32॥
ekadantaṃ bhālacandraṃ śuṇḍādaṇḍavirājitam |
pāśāṃkuśakaraṃ mālā dantahastaṃ suśobhanam ॥33॥
muktāmaṇigaṇopetaṃ sarparājayutodaram |
divyavastra parīdhānaṃ divyagandhānulepanam ॥34॥
anekasūryasaṃkāśaṃ tejojvālā sudīpitam |
dadarśa ṣaṇmukhastatra vismayotphullalocanaḥ ॥35॥
vyākulībhūtacitto'sau kimetadityacintayat |
mayā yaccintyate'nyadvā tadvā vindyāṃ na cāpyaham ॥36॥
tataḥ papraccha tūṣṇīṃ sa ko bhavānkiṃ ca nāma te |
gajānana uvāca |
cintyate yo divārātrau tvayaikāgreṇa cetasā ॥37|
so'haṃ prāpto varaṃ dātuṃ vada te hṛdi vāṃchitam |
dadāmi tuṣṭastapasā ṣaḍānana gajānanaḥ ॥38॥
skanda uvāca |
vidurna devā na ca śāstrakārā brahmādayaḥ śeṣamukhāśca nāgāḥ |
tava svarūpaṃ jagadīśa samyak dadarśi tanme dviradānanādya ॥39॥
tenaiva jātaḥ paripūrṇakāmaḥ tathāpi yāce tava devavākyāt |
parājayo me na kadāpi bhūyāt pratyakṣatāmehi vicintyamānaḥ ॥40॥
avismṛtiḥ syāt tava pādapadme sarveṣu deveṣu variṣṭhatā ca ॥41॥
alakṣyo lakṣyatāṃ yātaḥ tato lakṣavināyakaḥ |
nāmnā khyāto bhavātra tvaṃ bhaktakāma suradrumaḥ |
lakṣa uvāca |
sarvaṃ tadbhavitā skanda yadyat prāthitamadya me ॥42॥
avismṛtiśca sānnidhyaṃ cintyamānasya me bhavet |
parājayo ripūṇāṃ te devaśraiṣṭhyaṃ bhaviṣyati ॥43॥
brahmovāca |
evaṃ varān dadau devo mayūraṃ nijavāhanam|
dadau skandāya suprīto vinayācca tapobalāt ॥44॥
mayūradhvaja ityevaṃ nāma khyātaṃ tato'bhavat |
gajānana uvāca |
mṛtiṃ yāsyanti hastātte tārakādyā mahāsurāḥ ॥45॥
lakṣavināyaka iti nāmnā'haṃ bhaktavatsalaḥ |
bhaviṣyāmi cirādatra kṣetre tvadvākyato'nagha ॥46||
brahmovāca |
evamuktvā tu vikaṭastatraivāntarhito'bhavat |
tataḥ skando mahāmūrtiṃ kṛtvā sthāpya dvijaiḥ saha ॥47॥
lakṣavināyaka iti nāma cakre śubhaṃ tadā |
modakairlakṣasaṃkhyākaiḥ puṣpairdūrvāṃkurairapi ॥48॥
pūjayāmāsa tāṃ mūrtimanyairdravyastathāvidhaiḥ |
brāhmaṇān bhojayāmāsa tāvat saṃkhyā'yutān dvijān ॥49॥
stutvā natvā yayau skandaḥ śaṃkaraṃ lokaśaṃkaram |
mayūramāruhya tataḥ sarvaṃ cākathayacca tam ॥50॥
mayūradhvaja iti ca nāma devakṛtaṃ jagau |
tārakāsuranāśāya yayau skandaḥ śivājñayā॥51॥
smṛtvā gajānanaṃ devaṃ parigṛhya śivāśiṣaḥ |
devasenāpatitve'sāvabhiṣiktaḥ surarṣibhiḥ ||52॥
senānīstārakaṃ dṛṣṭvā yuddhaṃ cakre mahābalaḥ |
lakṣavarṣāntare taṃ sa mārayāmāsa śaktitaḥ ॥53॥
yuddhaṃ tad varṇituṃ śaktiḥ śeṣasyāpi na vai bhavet|
tārake nihate daitye nananduḥ sarvadevatāḥ ॥54॥
munayo lokapālāśca nāgāśca mānavāstathā |
mumucuḥ puṣpavarṣāṇi skandasyopari nirvṛtāḥ ॥55॥
sarvadevāśca lokāśca svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ yayustadā |
svāhāsvadhā vaṣaṭkārān yathāpūrvaṃ ca cakrire ॥56॥
brahmovāca | evaṃ prabhāvo devo'sau kathitaste gajānanaḥ |
vrataprabhāvo'pi mayā yathāvatte nirūpitaḥ ॥57॥
trayastriṃśat koṭisurairavadhyo'sau mahāsuraḥ |
skandena nihataḥ saṃkhye devavrata prabhāvataḥ ॥58॥
indrādidevatāvṛndaiḥ pūjanīyo'bhavacca saḥ |
vyāsa uvāca |
anuṣṭhānaṃ kṛtaṃ tena kasmin sthāne samādhinā ॥59॥
parameṇa viśākhena tanme brūhi prajāpate |
brahmovāca |
anuṣṭhānaṃ kṛtaṃ tena yatrāste ghṛṣaṇeśvaraḥ ॥60॥
prasiddho vartate nāmnā āsīllakṣavināyakaḥ |
elo'bhūttatra nagare paścād rājā'bhiviśrutaḥ |
tannāmnā nagaraṃ khyātaṃ tatastadabhavan mune ॥61॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe tārakavadho nāma saptāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥87॥

<adhyāya 88 prāraṃbhaḥ>

muniruvāca |
śrutamākhyānakaṃ brahman gajānanavratāśrayam |
dagdhaścenmadanastena śaṃkareṇa ruṣāgninā| ॥1॥
dṛśyate sarvalokeṣu madano'dyāpi tatkatham |
etatkathaya me sarvaṃ vistarāccaturānana ॥2॥
brahmovāca |
tṛtīyaṃ tu tadā netraṃ ruṣodghāṭitavānharaḥ |
aparādhaṃ tu vijñāya madanasya ratistadā ॥3॥
ākrandatī mṛtaṃ kāmaṃ harāntikamupāgamat |
sāṣṭāṃgapraṇipatyainaṃ tuṣṭāva ca yathāmati ॥4॥
ratiruvāca |
namāmi devaṃ girijāsahāyaṃ vṛṣadhvajaṃ bhālavilocanaṃ ca |
yaḥ pāti lokān khalu satvayukto nirmāti lokān rajasā guṇena ॥5॥
yaḥ svecchayā saṃharate'khileśo jagattamoviṣṭatanurmaheśaḥ |
yo nuḥ kapālaṃ vahate janānāṃ bhikṣāśanaḥ pūrayate'khilārthān ॥6॥
dīnānukampī bhagavān maheśo gatapriyāyāḥ śaraṇaṃ mamāstām |
kartuṃ tathā'kartumadīnasattvaḥ śakto'nyathā kartumapīha devaḥ ॥7॥
sa me vidhattāṃ śaraṇaṃ gatāyā saubhāgyamuccairmṛtajīvanena |
no cedahaṃ prāṇavisarjanena yaśaḥ kariṣye viparītamīśa ॥8॥
brahmovāca |
evaṃ tayā stutā śambhuḥ prasannastāmathābravīt |
śambhuruvāca |
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāge kāmapatni śubhānane ॥9॥
dadāmi tuṣṭaḥ stotreṇa sarvāṃnkāmān hṛdi sthitān |
itthaṃ śivavacaḥ śrutvā ratirhṛṣṭā praṇamya tam ॥10॥
saubhāgyakāmā taṃ devaṃ nijagāda bhṛśāturā |
ratiruvāca |
svāmin yadi prasannaścecchṛṇu me paramaṃ vacaḥ ॥11॥
rasāyāṃ divi bhūmau vā kāntāḥ syuḥ kāminīguṇāḥ |
mama lāvaṇyaleṣo na teṣu kāpi trilocana ॥12॥
māṃ dṛṣṭvā mumucuḥ śakramukhyā vīryamapatrapāḥ |
tato me mahatī lajjā jātāmetāmapākuru ॥13॥
lāvaṇyaṃ ca vṛthā jātaṃ vinā kāmena śaṃkara |
ayaśo dahate mahyaṃ ratiḥ sā vidhevati ca ॥14॥
bhartudānena deveśa māṃ jīvaya dayānidhe|
evaṃ tayā prārthito'sau śaṃkaro lokaśaṃkaraḥ ॥15॥
uvāca ślakṣṇayā vācā harṣayan kāmayoṣitam |
śiva uvāca |
cintā māṃ kuru kalyāṇi na lajjāṃ kartumarhasi ॥16॥
smṛtamātrastvayā bāle kāmaḥ syād dṛṣṭigocaraḥ|
manasā cintito vāpi manobhūriti saṃjñitaḥ ॥17॥
pūrayiṣyati te kāmān mānyā tvaṃ ca bhaviṣyasi |
viṣṇoḥ sakāśādutpattiṃ ramāyāṃ prāpsyate yadā ॥18॥
bhartā tava jane khyāto nāmnā pradyumnasaṃjñitaḥ |
bhaviṣyati mahābhāge gacchedānīṃ nijālayam ॥19॥
sā'gatā śivavākyena mandiraṃ bhṛśasuṃdaram |
sasmāra taṃ patiṃ sā tu puro'naṃgaḥ samāyayau ॥20॥
īśvarecchā vaśāttasyāḥ pratyakṣaṃ samajāyata |
itiścāścaryayuktā sā jahṛṣe patinā saha ॥21॥
tato'naṃgo yayau śaṃmbhuṃ praṇamyābhidadhe vacaḥ |
anaṃga uvāca |
vinā'parādhaṃ deveśa gamito'naṃgatāṃ katham ॥22॥
indrādidevatāvṛndaistārakāsura pīḍitaḥ|
munibhiśca viśākhasya tvatta utpattivedibhiḥ ॥23॥
prārthitastava niṣṭhāyā bhaṃgaṃ kartumahaṃ vibho |
upakārāya sarveṣāmakārṣa karma tādṛśam ॥24॥
upakārasamaṃ puṇyaṃ na cāsti bhuvanatraye |
viparītaṃ tu tajjātaṃ mama daivād sureśvara ॥25॥
trayastriṃśatkoṭideveṣvahaṃ cārutaraḥ purā|
upamā cārupuruṣe mamaiva dīyate'khilaiḥ ॥26॥
kathaṃ tiṣṭhāmi deveśa svāṃgahīnaḥ paretavat |
ato mayi mahādeva kṛpayā'nugrahaṃ kuru ॥27॥
brahmovāca |
tata ekākṣaraṃ mantraṃ gaṇeśasya dadau śivaḥ |
anaṃgāya praṇamate'nuṣṭhānaṃ cādiśacca tam| ॥28॥
tato'naṃgo yayau ramyaṃ janasthānaṃ susiddhidam|
tatrānuṣṭhānamakarodanaṃgaḥ śaṃkarājñayā ॥29॥
saṃvatsaraśataṃ pūrṇaṃ tatāpa sa tapo mahat |
ekākṣaraṃ japanmantraṃ gaṇeśadhyāna tatparaḥ ॥30॥
vāyumātrāśano nityaṃ sadā rati sahāyavān |
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān devadevo gajānanaḥ ॥31॥
āvirāsīddaśabhujo mahāmukuṭaśobhitaḥ |
jvaladratnaprabhācāru kuṃḍalāṃgada maṃḍitāḥ ॥32॥
koṭisūryapratīkāśo muktāmālā vibhūṣitaḥ |
divyamālyāṃbaradharo divyagandhānulepanaḥ ॥33॥
sindūrāruṇaśuṃḍāsyo daśāyudhalasatkaraḥ |
śeṣālaṃkṛtanābhiśca nānālaṃkārarājitaḥ ॥34॥
siṃhaskandho mahāśrīmāṃścītkāratrāsitākhilaḥ |
indrādayopi munaya āvirbhūte gajānane ॥35॥
āyayurapsarobhiśca yakṣagandharvakinnaraiḥ |
divyavāditranirghoṣaiḥ pupūjuste gajānanam ॥36॥
upacāraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhirbhaktyā devaṃ pṛthakpṛthak |
kāmo'pi tata utthāya praṇipatyākhilān surān ॥37॥
vavande devacaraṇau pūrvaṃ natvā munīnapi |
praśaśaṃsa tadā devaṃ vikaṭaṃ karuṇākaram ॥38॥
kāma uvāca |
dhanyastvamasi deveṣu parabrahmasvarūpavān |
nirākāro'pi sākāro jāto'si bhaktavatsala ॥39॥
adya dhanyaṃ mama janustapo dhanyataraṃ ca me |
yattavāṅaghriyugaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sarvaduḥkhavimocanam ॥40॥
kāraṇaṃ sarvasiddhīnāṃ dharmārthakāma mokṣadama |
dhanye netrayuge me'dya yābhyāṃ dṛṣṭaḥ paraḥ pumān ॥41॥
yaṃ na jānanti vedāratāḥ sāṃkhyāḥ pātaṃjalādayaḥ |
neti netyabhavaṃstūṣṇīṃ vedo'pi yatra kuṃṭhitaḥ ॥42॥
anantakoṭibrahmāṃḍa romakūpo'khileśvaraḥ |
yena mantreṇa so'darśi mantro dhanyataro'pi saḥ ॥43॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
samyaguktaṃ ratipate na māṃ brahmādayo viduḥ |
yadā sākāratāṃ yāmi te'pi jānanti māṃ tadā ॥44॥
mamaivānugrahātkāma dṛṣṭavānasi māṃ tadā|
yato'haṃ parituṣṭaste tapasā manunā tathā ॥45॥
vṛṇu matto'khilān kāmān kāme kāmaṃ dadāmi te |
brahmovāca |
iti devavacaḥ śrutvā punarūce manobhavaḥ ॥46॥
ānupūrvyeṇa sakalaṃ vṛtāntaṃ śivakāritam |
tasmai cākathayatkāmaḥ prasannārthapradāyine ॥47॥
anaṃgatāṃ svasya raterākrandaṃ manumeva ca | a
nuṣṭhānaṃ cirakṛtaṃ śivadattaṃ varaṃ tathā ॥48॥
vṛṇoti sma varaṃ tasmāt suprasannād gajānanāt |
kāma uvāca |
yadi prasanno bhagavāṃstanme dehi sadehatām ॥49॥
mānyatāṃ sarva deveṣu prāgvallāvaṇyameva ca |
bhaktiṃ dṛḍhāṃ tvaccaraṇe trailokyavijayaṃ tathā ॥50॥
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe aṣṭāśītitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥88॥

<adhyāya 89 prāraṃbhaḥ>

gaṇeśa uvāca |
yadyatte prārthitaṃ kāma tattatte bhavitā'khilam |
ramodarājjaniṃ prāpya sāṃgastvaṃ sarvasundaraḥ ॥1॥
bhavitā sarvamānyaśca trailokyavijayī tathā|
puppaṃ phalaṃ kisalayaṃ kāminyavayavā marut ॥2॥
udbodhakāni te santu jyotsnā candana nīraje |
marālāṇḍaja śabdaśca jetāsyetairharādikān ॥3॥
darśanāt smaraṇādeṣāṃ manasyapi bhaviṣyasi |
manobhūḥ smṛtibhūrevaṃ nāmāpi syājjaneṣu te ॥4॥
avismṛtirmaccaraṇe dṛḍhā bhaktiśca te bhavet |
smṛtā'haṃ purataḥ syāṃ te mahatkāryaupasthite ॥5॥
brahmovāca |
evaṃ tasmai varān dattvā kāmāyātha gajānanaḥ |
antardadhe mahābhāga paśyatsu ca surarṣiṣu ॥6॥
tatra kāmo mahāmūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya tathāvidhām |
saṃpādya sthāpayāmāsa pūjayāmāsa tāṃ tadā ॥7॥
ratinirmita pakvānnairmodakairlaḍḍukādibhiḥ |
mahotkaṭeti nāmāsya tejasvitvāttathā'karot ॥8॥
prāsādaṃ nirmame kāntaṃ ratnastambhavirājitam |
rukmiṇyā udarājjātastyakto daityena vāridhau ॥9॥
matsyena gilitastatra sa matsyo dhīvaraistataḥ |
śambarāyārpitenāpi māyāvatye niveditaḥ ॥10॥
vibhinne'smiṃstadudarānnirgato varddhitastayā|
nārado'kathayattasyai kāmo'yaṃ varddhitastvayā ॥11॥
upadiṣṭo'nekamāyāḥ śambaraṃ sa tato'vadhīt |
gaṇeśasya prasādena bahuneko vyajigyata ॥12॥
māyāvatyā śikṣitābhirmāyābhirbahubhiśca saḥ |
pradyumna iti vikhyātaḥ sarasvatyāḥ puraṃ yayau ॥13॥
sarvadeveṣu mānyaśca trailokyajayakārakaḥ |
gajānanaprasādena babhūvānandanirvṛtaḥ ॥14॥
rukmiṇiprabhṛtistrībhirdṛṣṭaḥ kṛṣṇa ivāparaḥ |
tata utthāya yātābhirlajjitābhiḥ samantataḥ ॥15॥
buddhvā putraṃ nāradasya vākyena mumuduśca tāḥ|
āliliṃgustamabhyetya praṇanāma ratiśca tāḥ ॥16॥
sarvā purī harṣayutā tasmiṃjñāte babhūva sā |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ janasthānagata gaṇeśasya mahāmune ॥17॥
mahimā kathito yatra rāmeṇācchedi nāsikā |
śūpaṇakhyā snatastattu nāmnā khyātaṃ tu nāsikam ॥18॥
tatrādyāpi hi dṛśyanta upalā modakā iva |
evaṃ nārādhitastena kāmenāsau gajānanaḥ ॥19॥
ṣaḍakṣareṇa mantreṇa śeṣeṇārādhito yathā |
ratiśca madanaścāpi nanandaturubhau yathā ॥20॥
vyāsa uvāca |
kathamārādhito brahman śeṣeṇāsau gajānanaḥ |
kimarthaṃ kiṃ ca tenāptaṃ suprasannād gajānanāt ॥21॥
etat savistaraṃ brūhi pṛcchataścaturānana |
praṣṭuḥ śrotuśca vaktuśca yataḥ puṇyaṃ vivardhate ॥22॥
brahmovāca |
samyakpṛṣṭaṃ tvayā brahman kathayāmi kathāmṛtam |
śṛṇuṣvāvahito bhūtvā sarvaṃ satyavatī suta ॥23॥
kasmiṃścitsamaye śambhuḥ pārvatyā sahito mune |
sukhāsīno girivare ramyasānuśiloccaye ॥24॥
nānādrumalatākīrṇe nirjharārāvaśabdite |
guñjanti bhramarā yatra rukmapaṃkajavāsinaḥ ॥25॥
campakāśoka bakula mālatī kusumānilaḥ |
ānandayati cittāni vasatāṃ yasya śekhare ॥26॥
āyayustatra gandharvā apsaro yakṣa kinnarāḥ |
devāśca munayo nāgāstaṃ dṛṣṭuṃ girijāpatim ॥27॥
kecittaṃ praṇipātena sāṣṭāṃgenābhyavādayan |
uccairjaguśca gandharvā nanṛtuścāpsarogaṇāḥ ॥28॥
pupūjustaṃ daśabhujaṃ vyāghrājinadharaṃ harami |
nandirbhṛṃgigaṇākīrṇaṃ bhālacandratriśūlinam ॥29॥
bhasmāṃgaṃ śeṣaśirasaṃ śaṃkaraṃ vṛṣabhadhvajam |
aparairarcitaṃ devamupacārairmanomayaiḥ ॥30॥
nimīlya nayane tasthuḥ kecid dhyānaparāyaṇāḥ |
vasiṣṭho vāmadevaśca jamadagnistrito dvijaḥ॥31॥
atriḥ kaṇvo bharadvājo gautamādyā munīśvarāḥ |
tuṣṭuvurvividhaiḥ stotraistatra taṃ pārvatīpatim ॥32॥
stuvastu teṣu muniṣu śeṣo garvaparaṃ yayau |
ahameva śreṣṭhataro lokeṣu triṣu nāparaḥ ॥33॥
yataḥ śreṣṭhataraḥ śambhu stasyāhaṃ śirasi sthitaḥ |
dharādharaṇasāmarthyaṃ mayi nānyatra kutracit ॥34॥
vāsuke rajjubhūtānmat kulīnādamṛtaṃ suraiḥ |
prāptaṃ tato'maratvaṃ ca tasmānnānyo'sti matparaḥ ॥35॥
