taddhṛdisthaṃ tadā garvaṃ jñātvā śambhū trilokakṛt |
vijñāyākhiladṛktūṣṇīmuttasthau sahasā śivaḥ ॥36॥
āsphālayaddharāpṛṣṭhe śeṣaṃ tādṛśagarvitam |
ekaiko mastakastasya daśadhā daśadhā'bhavat ॥37॥
mūrchitaḥ praharārddhaṃ sa gataprāṇa ivābhavat |
tataḥ prabhṛtyasau jātaḥ sahastraphaṇimaṃḍitaḥ ॥38॥
jīvaśeṣaḥ śuśocātha śeṣo'śeṣāhi bhūṣaṇaḥ |
alaṃkaraṇa bhūto'haṃ trailokyeśa harasya ha ॥39॥
imāmavasthāṃ saṃprāpto na jāne kena karmaṇā |
calituṃ naiva śaknomi pakṣahīna ivāṇḍajaḥ ॥40॥
kiṃ karomi kva gacchāmi ko me trātā'dhunā bhavet |
ko me nijapadaṃ prāptāvupāyaṃ kathayecchubham ॥41॥
kau vā me'panayedduḥkhamiti cintāparastu saḥ |
dadarśa tena mārgeṇa gacchantaṃ nāradaṃ munim ॥42॥
jaharṣa kiṃcinnāgendraḥ svapnaṃ prāpyeva bhikṣukaḥ |
atikaṣṭāmavasthāṃ tāṃ nārado'lokayatpuraḥ ॥43॥
niśceṣṭaṃ niranuśvāsaṃ dhyānaniṣṭhaṃ yathā munim|
aśeṣa viṣayajño'pi śeṣaṃ papraccha nāradaḥ ॥44॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe ekonanavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥89॥

<adhyāya 90 prāraṃbhaḥ>

nārada uvāca |
kimarthamevaṃ jāto'si nistejā duḥkhito bhṛśam |
kathaṃ bhagnaśirā kasyācaro'priyataraṃ mune ॥1॥
kiṃ vā kṣubdho girīśaste'kāri garva stvayā'tha kim |
vada yatkāraṇaṃ śeṣa pratīkāraṃ purā vade ॥2॥
tvāṃ vinā kārayetko nu carācarayutāṃ dharām |
anuktavati tasmiṃstu svayamevābravīnmuniḥ ॥3॥
upāyaṃ nāgarājasya svasthānaprāpakaṃ śubham |
nārada uvāca |
avadhāraya me vākyaṃ śeṣāśeṣakalā nidhe ॥4॥
suparvāṇaśca keśādyā yena syuḥ kiṅkarā iva |
dhariṣyase dharāṃ mūrdhni puṣpamālāmivārbhakaḥ ॥5॥
śeṣa uvāca |
kiṃcit purātanaṃ puṇyamāsīcceddarśanaṃ tava|
ajanya kasmādagre'pi samyaṅme syānna saṃśayaḥ ॥6॥
anyathā kṛtapuṇyānāṃ darśanaṃ tu kathaṃ bhavet|
ati vihvalagātratvādaśakto bhūmidhāraṇe ॥7॥
taṃ prayatnaṃ vada mune yenāhaṃ syāṃ yathā purā |
nārada uvāca |
bhaṇe mahāmanuṃ te'dya tasya devasya nāgarāṭ|॥8॥
yasya prasādādindrādyāstattat padamavāpnuvan |
keśā yadājñayā'śeṣa sṛṣṭisthityantakāriṇaḥ ॥9॥
yasmin prasanne sarveśe pūrvāvasthāmavāpsyasi |
kāruṇyaṃ tava dṛṣṭvaiva klinnaṃ me sarvamānasam ॥10॥
ataḥ ṣaḍakṣaraṃ mantraṃ gaṇeśasya dadāmi te |
asyānuṣṭhānamātreṇa pratyakṣaste gajānanaḥ ॥11॥
sarvān vidhāsyate kāmānyānyāṃstvaṃ vṛṇuṣe tataḥ ॥12॥
ka uvāca |
kṛtvopadeśaṃ śeṣāya nāradontarhito'bhavat |
śeṣo'pi tapase kṛtvā niścayaṃ paramaṃ śubham |
niruddhāśeṣakaraṇo dhyātvā devaṃ gajānanam ॥13॥
jajāpa paramaṃ mantraṃ sahasraparivatsaram |
tadante purato'paśyad devadevaṃ gajānanam ॥14॥
siṃhārūḍhaṃ trinetraṃ daśabhujamuragaṃ kuṃḍale cāṃgade ca |
muktāmālāṃ vahaṃtaṃ sulalitamukuṭaṃ ratnamudrākṣasūtram |
nānādevarṣivṛndairanugatamaniśaṃ vakratuṇḍaṃ gajāsyaṃ |
bhaktecchopāttadehaṃ suranaraparadaṃ cintayedekadantam ॥15॥
evaṃ gajānanastasya darśanārthaṃ samāyayau |
siddhibuddhiyutastādṛga yādṛg dhyāto'hināpurā ॥16॥
sahasrasūrya sadṛśo dīptyākrānta digantaraḥ |
tejasā tasya śeṣo'pi dharṣito'ndha ivābhavat ॥17॥
cakampe bhayasaṃvigno vyagracitto'tivihvalaḥ |
svastho muhūrtamātreṇa cintayāmāsa cetasi ॥18॥
kimidaṃ teja āyātaṃ pralayānalasannibham |
tacceddaheta sarvalokānidaṃ māmeva dhakṣyati ॥19||
kalyāṇe karmaṇi kathaṃ kriyamāṇe śubhaṃ bhavet |
athavā nāradenoktaṃ drakṣyāmi gaṇanāyakam ॥20॥
evaṃ cintāture tasminnuvāca dviradānana |
mā bhaiḥ kutarkakuśala varado'haṃ samāgataḥ ॥21॥
yaṃ dhyāyasi divārātrau vṛṇu yatte hṛdi sthitam |
ahameva jagatkartā pātā hartā'khileśvara ॥22॥
mattejasā bhāti candro vahniḥ sūryaśca bhāni ca |
parabrahmasvarūpo'pi tapasā tava toṣitaḥ ॥23॥
avirbhūto varaṃ dātuṃ lokopakṛtaye'pi ca |
tvaṃ tu matto varānbrūhi yānyān kāmayase'khilān ॥24॥
śeṣa uvāca |
tejasā dharṣito deva draṣṭuṃ vaktuṃ ca notsahe |
anugrahaśca pūrṇaste mayi saumyo bhavānagha ॥25॥
ka uvāca |
evaṃ saṃprārthitastena karuṇābdhirgajānanaḥ |
abhavat koṭicandrābhaḥ saumyatejāḥ sureśvara ॥26॥
tato vavre varān śeṣo natvā stutvā'khileśvaraḥ |
anādinidhanaṃ deva vande'haṃ gaṇanāyakam ॥27॥
sarvavyāpinamīśānaṃ jagatkāraṇakāraṇam|
sarvasvarupaṃ viśveśaṃ viśvavandyaṃ namāmyaham ॥28॥
gajānanaṃ gaṇādhyakṣaṃ garūḍeśastu taṃ vibhum |
guṇādhīśaṃ guṇātītaṃ gaṇādhīśaṃ namāmyaham ॥29॥
vidyānāmadhipaṃ devaṃ devadevaṃ surapriyam |
siddhibuddhipriyaṃ sarvaṃ siddhidaṃ bhuktimuktidam ॥30॥
sarvavighnaharaṃ devaṃ namāmi gaṇanāyakam |
ka uvāca |
evaṃ stutvā tu deveśaṃ varadaṃ dviradānananam ॥31॥
varayāmāsa yān kāmāṃstāṃ śṛṇuṣva mahāmune |
śeṣa uvāca |
adya dhanyaṃ tapo jñānaṃ pitā mātā janurmama ॥32॥
deho netrāṇi bhūrīṇi mastakāni bahūni ca |
tvāṃ stotuṃ saṃpravṛttā yā dhanyasyā rasanā mama|
kulaṃ śīlaṃ ca dhanyaṃ me tvaṅghriyugadarśanāt |
akhaṃḍitaṃ te bhajanaṃ dehi me'khaṇḍavikrama ॥34॥
duḥkhaṃ kiṃ kiṃ nu vaktavyaṃ sarvajñe tvayi vighnarāṭ |
akharvagarvakaraṇāt sphuṭitā mastakā mama ॥35॥
mahāruṣā maheśenā sphālitasya mahītale|
nāradasya prasādena dṛṣṭaṃ te caraṇāmbujam |
idānīṃ deva me dehi śreṣṭhatāṃ bhuvanatraye ॥36॥
pāṭavaṃ sarvaśirasi sāmarthyaṃ dhāraṇe bhuvaḥ|
acalaṃ dehi me sthānaṃ darśanaṃ te nirantaram॥37॥
sānnidhyaṃ śaṃkarasyāpi kulaśreṭhyaṃ śive ratima |
gaṇapatiruvāca|
yadi te daśadhā jāto mastako bhujagādhipa ॥38॥
tadā sahasravadanaḥ sahasraphaṇimaṇḍitaḥ |
bhaviṣyasi jane khyāto yāvaccandrārkatārakāḥ ॥39॥
dharādharaṇasāmarthyaṃ dṛḍhaṃ tava bhaviṣyati |
paṃcāsyapaṃcaśirasi matprasādāt sthitiṃ sthirām॥40॥
lapsyase bhujagaśreṣṭha matsānnidhyaṃ nirantaram |
anyatte vāṃchitaṃ śeṣa tadaśeṣaṃ bhaviṣyati ॥41॥
ka uvāca |
evaṃ varān dadau tasmai svodare taṃ babandha ha |
vyālabaddhodara iti nāma prāpa gajānanaḥ ॥42॥
śeṣasya mastake hastamabhayārthaṃ dadau vibhuḥ |
svayaṃ virāṭsvarūpaṃ svaṃ śeṣāyādarśayan bhuvā ॥43॥
śabdenāpūrayad vyoma bhūtalaṃ vidiśaṃ diśaḥ |
yasya pādatale bhūmirdiśaḥ śrotre'kṣiṇī raviḥ ॥44॥
oṣadhyo yasya romāṇi nakhā yasya dharādharāḥ |
meghāḥ svedodakaṃ yasya prajanaścaturānanaḥ ॥45॥
kukṣau yasya jagatsarvaṃ catvāraścaiva sāgarāḥ |
anantānana eko yo'pyanantanayanaḥ svarāṭ ॥46॥
anantarūpī cānanta śaktiratyanta dīptimān |
yadromakūpe bhānti sma brahmāṇḍāni sahasraśaḥ ॥47॥
dṛṣṭvā taṃ tādṛśaṃ śeṣo bhītyā bhrānta ivābhavat |
prārthayāmāsa vighneśaṃ punaḥ saumyo bhaveti ca ॥48॥
tato daśabhujo devaḥ siṃhārūḍho'bhavacca saḥ |
uvāca varado devo nedṛg dṛṣṭaḥ surairapi॥49॥
madanugrahato'darśi tvayā śeṣādya bhāgyataḥ |
acalaṃ mayi te sthānaṃ pātāle'pi śive'pi ca |
mayā dattaṃ prasannena dhara puṣpamivādya gām ॥50॥
śeṣa uvāca |
dhare dharāṃ svaśirasi dharaṇīdhara ityapi |
mamāpi te'pi vikhyātaṃ nāma loke'ti viśrutam ॥51॥
asminkṣetre sthiro bhūtvā bhaktakāmān prapūraya |
brahmovāca |
omiti tamathoktvā'sau vighneśo'ntardadhe svayam ॥52॥
śeṣo'pi tādṛśīṃ mūrtiṃ kṛtvā'sthāpayadādarāt |
akarod bahuratnāḍhayaṃ prāsādaṃ kāṃcanaṃ śubham ॥53॥
dharaṇīdhara ityetannāmāsya parikalpayat |
hareḥ śayanatāṃ prāyādavahat puṣpavaddharām ||54॥
sthiro'bhūnnābhipadme'pi vighnarājasya bhūṣaṇam |
evaṃ te kathito vyāsa vighneśamahimā'dbhutaḥ ॥55॥
pravālanagare khyāto gaṇeśo dharaṇīdharaḥ|
etadarthaṃ hi śeṣeṇa kṛtamārādhanaṃ vibhoḥ ॥56॥ 
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe śeṣopākhyāne navatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥90॥

<adhyāya 91 prāraṃbhaḥ>

vyāsa uvāca |
anyat kathaya me deva gaṇeśasya kathāntarama |
śṛṇvato me mano'tyantamutsukuṃ bhavati prabho ॥1॥
brahmovāca |
ekadā pralaye vṛtte'jñāpayanmāṃ gajānanaḥ |
sṛṣṭiṃ nānāvidhāṃ brahman kuru tvaṃ hi mamājñayā ॥2॥
ahaṃ prakalpayāmāsa putrān sapta hṛdā tadā |
teṣāṃ nāmāni te vacmi kaśyapo gotamo'pi ca ॥3॥
jamadagnirvasiṣṭhaśca bharadvājo'trireva ca|
viśvāmitraśca saptaitai sarvavidyāviśāradāḥ ॥4॥
sarve māmabruvan brahmannājñāpaya sureśvara |
mayā tu kaśyapo'jñāyi buddhimāṃsteṣu cādhikaḥ ॥5॥
matkāryaṃ vividhāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ kurvityājñāpitastadā |
asāvomiti māmuktvā jagāma tapase vanam ॥6॥
jajāpaikākṣaraṃ mantraṃ divyavarṣasahasrakam |
tataḥ prasanno bhagavān dviradānana īśvaraḥ ॥7॥
caturbhujo'ravindākṣo mahāmukuṭamaṇḍitaḥ |
pāśāṃkuśadharo mālādanta hastaḥ śubhāṃgadaḥ |
suvarṇamaṇiratnāḍhayaḥ muktāmālālasadgalaḥ ॥8॥
sarpodaraḥ koṭisūrya vilasaddīptimaṇḍalaḥ |
vikasannetra vibhrājaccāruśuṇḍālasanmukhaḥ ॥9॥
āvirāsīt kaśyapasya pura evaṃ gajānanaḥ |
kṣudraghaṇṭā nūpurāṇāṃ ravān mukharitāṅghriyuk ॥10॥
dṛṣṭvā nanarta taṃ devaṃ kaśyapo harṣanirbharaḥ|
natvā sampūjayāmāsa nānāmaṃgalavastubhiḥ ॥11॥
baddhāṃjaliruvācedaṃ suprasanno gajānanam |
dhanyo me janako mātā tapo jñānaṃ vapuśca dṛk ॥12॥
dhanyeyaṃ dharaṇī tāta vīrūd vṛkṣa phalāni ca |
dhanya ekākṣaro mantro yena dṛṣṭo'khileśvaraḥ ॥13॥
gajānanaḥ prasannātmā paramātmā parātparaḥ |
yatra kuṇṭhāścaturvedā vedāntā mūkatāṃ gataḥ ॥14॥
agocaro manastarkai so'yaṃ devo mayekṣitaḥ |
yasmādāvirbhavantīme hareśāgnimukhāḥ surāḥ ॥15॥
pātālāni ca saptaiva bhuvanāni caturdaśa |
yatra tannilayaṃ yānti soyaṃ devo mayekṣitaḥ ॥16॥
yo nirguṇo nirādhīśo gurugamyo nirākṛtiḥ |
brahmeti yaṃ viduḥ kecit soyaṃ devo mayekṣitaḥ ॥17॥
ka uvāca |
evaṃ vākyāmṛtarasaiḥ parituṣṭo gajānanaḥ |
uvāca kaśyapaṃ mamraṃ stuvantaṃ vividhaiḥ stavaiḥ ॥18॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
bhakteḥstavādanuṣṭhānāt prītaste'haṃ mune'dhunā|
varaṃ varaya mattastvaṃ yaṃ yaṃ svavanasecchasi ॥19॥
kaśyapa uvāca |
sṛṣṭiṃ nānāvidhāṃ kartuṃ sāmarthyaṃ dehi me prabho |
tvadaḍghrikamale bhaktimavismaraṇameva ca ॥20॥
yāyāḥ pratyakṣatā tatra smarettvāṃ yatra cāpyaham |
tādṛśaṃ dehi me putraṃ nāmnā kaśyapanandanam ॥21॥
gaṇeśa uvāca |
sarvaṃ te vāṃchitaṃ matto bhavitā''śu mahāmune |
bhaktirme'vismṛti syātte saṃkaṭe cāntikaṃ vraje ॥22॥
kariṣyasi vicitrāṃ tvaṃ sṛṣṭiṃ mama prasādataḥ |
ka uvāca |
evamuktvā muniṃ devastatraivāntaradhīyata |
kaśyapo'pi mudā yukto nijasthānaṃ tadā yayau ॥23॥
kadācit kaśyapo'kasmādaśarīra prapīḍitaḥ |
na cālabhat kvāpi śarma gṛhamadhyaṃ tato'gamat ॥24॥
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ karma dhyānaṃ ca nāsmarat |
taṃ tathā vihvalaṃ dṛṣṭvā yoṣito'sya caturdaśa ॥25॥
dityaditirdanuḥ kadrurvinatādyāḥ puraḥ sthitāḥ |
bubhuje kramaśastāstu nānāgāreṣu kaśyapaḥ ॥26॥
kāle yathokte suṣuve ditārdaityānanekaśaḥ|
aditirdevagandharvān danuśca dānavānatha ॥27॥
krameṇa kinnarā yakṣāḥ siddhacāraṇaguhayakaḥ |
paśavaśca tathāraṇyā grāmyāścāsannanekaśaḥ॥28॥
pṛthivī parvatā vṛkṣāḥ samudrāḥ sarito latā|
dhānyāni dhātavo ratnā muktāḥ kṛmi pipīlikāḥ॥29॥
sarpāḥ pakṣigaṇāstābhyaḥ sarvamāsīccarācaram |
evaṃ dṛṣṭvā sa santānaṃ jātaṃ nānāvidhāṃ tadā ॥30॥
jaharṣa kaśyapo dhīmānnānāmantrān samādiśat |
śodhāyitvā ṛṇadhanaṃ siddhāricakramīkṣya ca ॥31॥
ṣoḍaśārṇaṃ ca kasmaicittathā'ṣṭādaśavarṇakam |
ekākṣaraṃ ca kasmaicidanyasmai ca ṣaḍakṣaram ॥32॥
paṃcārṇamaṣṭākṣaraṃ ca dvādaśākṣarameva ca |
kasmaicicca mahāmantraṃ dadau sa munipuṃgavaḥ ॥33॥
kurvan tvanuṣṭhitaṃ tāvadyāvaddevo nirīkṣyate |
gajānano'khilādhāraḥ sarvasiddhipradāyakaḥ | ॥34॥
evamājñāpayattāṃśca te ca jagmustadaiva hi |
tapase bahuśo deśān jepuḥ svaṃ svaṃ manuṃ tadā ॥35||
āsane bhojane devaṃ nidrāyāṃ jāgare'pi ca |
ananyabhaktyā deveśaṃ sasmaruste gajānanam ॥36॥
divyavarṣasahasrānte parituṣṭo gajānanaḥ |
āvirāsidanekātmā tatpuraḥ karūṇānidhiḥ ॥37॥
yo yathā dhyātavāndevaṃ tādṛśastatparo'bhavat |
kasyacit purataścāsīn meghābho'ṣṭamahābhujaḥ ॥38॥ kasyacitpuratastvāsīcchaśivarṇaścaturbhujaḥ |
agre kasyacidāraktaḥ ṣaḍbhujo'sau gaṇeśvaraḥ ॥39॥
sahasranayanastāvad bhujo'sāvapi kasyacit |
bhāti bālasvarūpo'pi yuvā vṛddho'pi bhāsate ॥40॥
daśadvādaśadordaṇḍo dhūmravarṇo mahanmahaḥ |
aṣṭādaśabhujo'pyāsīt koṭisūryasamaprabhaḥ ॥41॥
tejorūpo mahākāya ākhuvāhanapṛṣṭagaḥ |
siṃhago barhigo vāpi gajāsyo naikavaktravān ॥42॥
dṛṣṭvā devaṃ tu te sarve naikadhā tuṣṭuvurmudā ||
baddhāṃjalipuṭā namrā bhaktyā devaṃ gajānanama ॥43॥
sarve ūcuḥ |
yato'nantaśakteranantāśca jīvā|
yato nirguṇādaprameyād guṇāste|
yato bhāti sarvaṃ tridhā bhedabhinnaṃ|
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ||44||
yataścāvirāsījjagat sarvaṃ vettu
stathā'bjāsano viśvago viśva goptā |
tathendrādayo daityasaṃghā manuṣyāḥ |
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ ॥45॥
yato vahni bhānū bhavo bhūrjalaṃ ca |
yataḥ sāgarāścandramā vyoma vāyuḥ |
yataḥ sthāvarā jaṃgamā vṛkṣasaṃghāḥ |
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ ॥46॥
yato dānavāḥ kinnarā yakṣasaṃghā |
yataścāraṇā vāraṇāḥ śvāpadāśca |
yataḥ pakṣikīṭā yato vīrudhaśca |
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ ॥47॥
yato buddhirajñānanāśo mumukṣo|
yataḥ sampado bhaktasaṃtoṣakaḥ syuḥ |
yato vighnanāśo yataḥ kāryasiddhiḥ |
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ ॥48॥
yataḥ yataḥ putrasampadyato vāṃchitārtho |
yato bhaktividyāstathā'nekarūpāḥ |
yataḥ śokamohau yataḥ kāma eva |
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ ॥49॥
yato'nantaśaktiḥ sa śeṣo babhūva |
dharādhāraṇe'nekarūpe ca śaktaḥ |
yato'nekadhā svargalokā hi nānā|
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ ॥50॥
yato vedavāco vikuṇṭhā manobhiḥ|
sadā neti netīti tā yaṃ gṛṇanti |
parabrahmarūpaṃ cidānandabhūtaṃ |
sadā taṃ gaṇeśaṃ namāmo bhajāmaḥ ॥51॥ 

<adhyāya 92 prāraṃbhaḥ>

ka uvāca |
evaṃ natvā ca stutvā ca sarve te tu gajānanam |
punarūcurgaṇādhyakṣamadha dhanyatamā vayam ॥1॥
dhanyaṃ tapo no dānaṃ ca jñānaṃ yajñaśca pūrvajāḥ |
adya no dṛṣṭayo dhanyā yābhirdṛṣṭo gajānanaḥ ॥2॥
evaṃ vākyāmṛtaisteṣāṃ stutibhiḥ paritoṣitaḥ |
uvāca śṛṇvantāṃ teṣāṃ gāmbhīryād dviradānanaḥ ॥3॥
naitādṛśaṃ mayā'darśi brahmaviṣṇuśivādiṣu |
yat sākṣād dṛśyate sarvaṃ rūpaṃ me nirguṇasya yat ॥4॥
atituṣṭo'nayā stutyā varān dātumihāgataḥ |
yadyadvo vāṃchitaṃ tattad vṛṇudhvamakhilaṃ tu yat ॥5॥
evamuktāstadā tena vikaṭena munīśvara |
yasya yadvāṃchitaṃ tattadvavrustasmād gajānanāt ॥6॥
asaṃkhyatvāttadvarāṇāṃ nāsti śaktiścaturmukhaiḥ |
gadituṃ sarvathā tasmāduktaṃ saṃkṣepato mayā ॥7॥
tāṃstān dadau varānyānyān sarve vavrurgajānanam |
punarapyavadat sarvānidaṃ stotraṃ mama priyam |
trisandhyaṃ yaḥ paṭhedetad vidyāvān putravān bhavet |
āyurārogyamaiśvaryaṃ yaśovṛddhi jayodayam |
prāpnuyādvāṃchitānarthānante ca paramaṃ padam |
punarūce gaṇādhīśaḥ stotra metajjapennaraḥ |
trisandhyaṃ tridinaṃ tasya sarva kāryaṃ bhaviṣyati ॥8॥
yo japedaṣṭadivasaṃ ślokāṣṭakamidaṃ śubham |
aṣṭavāraṃ caturthyāṃ tu so'ṣṭasiddhimavāpnuyāt ॥9॥
yaḥ paṭhenmāsamātraṃ tu daśavāraṃ dine dine |
sa mocayed bandhanāttaṃ rājabaddhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ ॥10॥
vidyākāmo labhet vidyāṃ putrārthī putramāpnuyāt |
vāṃchitaṃ labhate sarvamekaviṃśati vārataḥ ॥11॥
yo japet parayā bhaktyā gajānanaparo naraḥ |
evamuktvā tato devaḥ sarveṣāmeva paśyatām ॥12॥
antardadhe'khilādhāraḥ sumukho'sau gajānanaḥ |
te ca cakrustadā mūrtiṃ gaṇeśasya śubhānanām ॥13॥
pratiṣṭhāpya ca tāṃ sthūle prāsāde ratnanirmite |
sumukheti ca tasyāste nāma cakruḥ suviśrutam ॥14॥
saṃpūjya ca namaskṛtvā svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ yayuḥ surāḥ |
sarvai te munayo lokā sarvakāryaratāstadā ॥15
kecittasyā nāma cakrurekadanta iti sphuṭam |
gandharvai kinnarai ramyā sthāpitā mūrtiruttamā ॥16॥
prāsāde kāṃcane śreṣṭhe'nekadhā paripūjya ca |
kapileti ca nāmāsyāḥ sthāpayāmāsuruttamam ॥17॥
guhayakāścāraṇāḥ siddhā mūrtimanyā pracakrire |
mahālaye pratiṣṭhāpya nemuśca pupujuśca tām ॥18॥
gajakarṇeti nāmāsyāścakruste tu sphuṭārthakam |
tat prabhāvādvimānasthāḥ sarve te divamākramat ॥19॥
lambodareti nāmnā ca sthāpitā sarva mānavaiḥ |
śvāpadairakhilairanyā sthāpitā mūrtiruttamā ॥20॥
vikaṭeti ca nāmnā tāṃ pupūjustte yayurvanam|
girayaśca drumāścāṃnyāṃ mūrtiṃ sthāpya prapūjya ca॥21॥
vighnāśana ityevaṃ nāma kṛtvā sthitāstu kauṃ|
tatprasādaśca te khyātāḥ parvatāśca drumāstathā॥22॥
sarvaiḥ pakṣigaṇairmūrtiḥ sthāpitā ratnakāṃcanī|
gaṇādhipeti nāmnā taiḥ pūjitā ca namaskṛtā ॥23॥
sarvairviṣadharairekā sthāpitā gaṇanāyakī |
yasyā hūtiḥ kṛtā taistu dhūmaketuriti sphuṭā ॥24॥
sarvairjalāśayairekā pratimā sthāpitā śubhā|
gaṇādhyakṣeti nāmnā sā pūjitā paramotsavaiḥ ॥25॥
kṛmikīṭādinicayairvanaspatyoṣadhīgaṇaiḥ |
sthāpitā paramā mūrtirbhālacandreti viśrutā ॥26॥
anyaiḥ sacetanairanyā ratnaprāsādamadhyagā|
vaināyakī mahāmūrtiḥ pūjitā bhaktibhāvataḥ ॥27॥
gajānanākhyayā khyātā sarveṣāṃ sarvakāmadā |
te te jagattraye khyātāstattajjātyā babhūvire ॥28॥
sukhinaḥ svasvakāryeṣu dakṣā deva prasādataḥ |
pratyekanāmakathane na śaktirmama vartate ॥29॥
sāraṃ sāraṃ pragṛhyaiva kṛtaṃ nāmnā sahasrakam |
tato'pi sārabhūtāni proktāni dvādaśaiva tu॥30॥
samudramathanādyadvad ratnānīva caturdaśa |
evaṃ saṃkṣepato brahman mahimā te'bhivarṇitaḥ ॥31॥
vistarād gadituṃ śeṣā neśo neśopyahaṃ hariḥ|
indrādimaśakānāṃ ca jīvānāṃ yakṣarakṣasām ॥32॥
tatra kā gaṇanā kāryā mayā satyavatīsuta |
tasmāt sarveṣu kāryeṣu pūjanīyo gajānanaḥ ॥33॥
yo na pūjayate devaṃ devavighnavināśanam |
sa durātmā parityājyaścāṃḍāla iva dūrataḥ ॥34॥
muniruvāca |
anukrameṇa kathaya nāmāni dvādaśaiva me |
śravaṇāt paṭhanādeṣāṃ sarvaṃ nirvighnatāmiyāt ॥35॥
brahmovāca |
sumukhaścaikadantaśca kapilo gajakarṇakaḥ|
lambodaraśca vikaṭo vighnanāśo gaṇādhipaḥ ॥36॥
dhūmraketurgaṇādhyakṣo bhālacandro gajānanaḥ |
dvādaśaitāni nāmāni yaḥ paṭhecchṛṇuyādapi ॥37॥
vidyārambhe vivāhe ca praveśe nirgame tathā|
saṅgrāme saṃkaṭe caiva vighnastasya na jāyate ॥38॥
śuklāmbaradharaṃ devaṃ śaśivarṇaṃ caturbhujam |
prasannavadanaṃ dhyāyet sarvavighnopaśāntaye ॥39॥
koṭikanyāpradānāni koṭiyajñaṃ vratāni ca |
tapāṃsi yāni sarvāṇi tīrthānyāyatanāni ca ॥40॥
svarṇabhārasahasrāṇi koṭidānāni yānyapi |
kṛcchrāṇi taptakṛcchrāṇi parākenduvratāni ca ॥41॥
śatāṃśameṣāṃ puṇyasya tāni nāmnāṃ na yānti ca |
imāni prātarutthāya śucirbhūtvā samāhitaḥ ॥42॥
yaḥ paṭhenmānavo bhaktyā vighnā no yāṃti taṃ naram |
siddhyanti sarvakāryāṇi mokṣamante vrajatyasau ॥43॥
tasya darśanato lokā devāḥ pūtā bhavanti ca |
ataeva mune sarvāḥ śāktāḥ śaivāśca vaiṣṇavāḥ ॥44॥
uktvā dvādaśa nāmāni sarvakāryāṇi kurvate |
gāṇeśā api kurvanti kimatrāpi ca kautukam ॥45॥
na siddhyanti hi kāryāṇi dvādaśānyatamasya ha |
uccāraṇaṃ vinā brahmaṃstasmādekaṃ samuccaret ॥46॥
duṣṭānāṃ nāstikānāṃ ca kāryaṃ yadyaddhi siddhyati |
te'pi bījaṃ samuccārya maddhyānādapi kurvate ॥47॥
evaṃ te mahimā sarvaḥ kathito leśato mune |
upāsanāphalaṃ nānā yathāmati nirūpitam ॥48॥
viṣṇūnāṃ kathitaṃ yāvat tāvattava nirūpitam |
so'pi nāntaṃ jagāmāsya gaṇeśopāsanasya ha ॥49॥
gaṇeśanāmamahimā pūrṇo jñāto na tena ca |
bhṛguruvāca |
evaṃ te kathitā rājannadbhuto mahimā mayā ॥50॥
brahmaṇā suprasannena yo vyāsāya nirūpitaḥ |
upāsanākhaṃḍamidaṃ varṇitaṃ nṛpate mayā ॥51॥
yadi te śravaṇe śraddhā tadānyadapi varṇane |
caritaṃ gaṇanāthasya somakāṃtāghanāśanam ॥52॥
sūta uvāca |
iti vaḥ kathitaṃ nānākathāntarasamanvitam |
upāsanaṃ gaṇeśasya śaunakādyā maharṣayaḥ ॥53॥
vedavyāsāya munaye brahmaṇā yatsamīritam |
bhṛguṇā somakāntāya varṇitaṃ pāpanāśanam ॥54॥
śṛṇuyādyo gaṇeśasya purāṇamidamuttamam |
sa sarvāmāpadaṃ hitvā bhuktvā bhogānanekaśaḥ ॥55॥
putrapautrasamāyukto jñānavijñānasaṃyutaḥ |
labhate paramāṃ muktiṃ gaṇeśasya prasādataḥ ॥56॥
na tasya punarāvṛttiḥ kalpakoṭiśatairapi |
yaḥ śrāvayed mahābhaktyā yathoktaphalabhāg bhavet ॥57॥
yathāptaṃ somakāntena śṛṇvatā paramādarāt|
iti śrīgaṇeśapurāṇa upāsanākhaṃḍe brahmavyāsa bhṛgusomakānta saṃvāde gajānananāmanirūpaṇaṃ nāma dvinavatitamo'dhyāyaḥ ॥92॥ 
॥śrīmadgajānanārpaṇamastu |

॥ iti śrīgaṇeśamahāpurāṇe upāsanā khaṇḍaṃ samāptam ॥
